# Relume Web UI Implementation Plan (OUTLINE)

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use podium:team-spawn (recommended) or podium:team-fable to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.
>
> **Status: outline.** This file is Stage 1 at package level: every package has purpose, spec sections, files, interfaces, dependencies, acceptance and size. Two detail planners later split each package into tasks and add Stage 2 steps (test code, implementation code, commands). They must not rename any interface frozen here or in the spec.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-19-webui-design.md` (commits 11c72ad + 9fd15dc). "spec L<n>" = line n of that file at 9fd15dc. "core §N", "catalog §N", "build §N", "device §N", "ui §N", "ci §N", "xposed §N" = section N of spec Part 1–7.
**Requirements:** `docs/logs/2026-10-19-webui-contract.md` (requirements 1–4d, decisions, parking lot).
**Superseded part:** the dependency-app model below (`dependency_app`, `dependency_app_ids`, `catalog.item_dependencies`, `GET /api/dependency-apps`, OTA `dependency` steps) is replaced by followed projects: see `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-23-follow-project-design.md`. Code listings here keep the old names as history.
**UI source:** `docs/superpowers/mockups/final/` (7 screens, "Workbench").

**Goal:** Replace the per-org build scripts with one container-based web server ("Relume") that manages (source × app) items, pulls upstream patches on schedules, builds items with one generic runner, installs artifacts on phones over wireless ADB, notifies, and keeps GitHub Actions builds on the same code.

**Architecture:** One compose file, five containers; the builder container becomes `app` and runs a stdlib-only Python 3.12 server on port 47000 next to the three anti-Cloudflare sidecars, all inside an idle `netns` anchor's network namespace (spec L172–209, core §1). State = SQLite `/data/webui.db` + files under `/data` (core §4, spec L251–292). Four feature modules (core, catalog, build, device) plug into one router, one job queue (one serial worker per kind) and one event bus (core §3, §5, spec L218–307). The build runs `src/build/build-item.sh <job.json>` as a child process (build §2–§4, spec L666–791). CI runs the same modules headless (ci §2, spec L1239–1241). Frontend = static HTML/CSS/ES-module JS ported from the mockup (ui §1, spec L1046–1060).

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12 stdlib only (`http.server.ThreadingHTTPServer`, `sqlite3`, `hashlib.scrypt`, `secrets`, `hmac`, `threading`, `subprocess`, `urllib.request`, `json`, `zoneinfo`, `zipfile`, `socket`); bash 5 + java 21 + apksigner + jq + pup v0.4.0 + APKEditor 1.4.9 + distro `adb` in `ubuntu:24.04`; static HTML/CSS/vanilla JS (ES modules, `EventSource`, native Drag and Drop, `<dialog>`, `<input type="color">`); Docker Compose; GitHub Actions (`actions/checkout@v7.0.1`, `gh release upload`). No pip, no npm, no bundler (spec L211–217).

**Decisions (hard-to-reverse only):** all fixed by the spec (§0.5 L94–127, §0.6 L129–152). This plan adds only:
1. Build job service (`build.py`) moves into round 1 (spec L233 suggested round 2). Alternatives: (a) spec split, (b) round 1. Driver: catalog (`build.item_status`, `build.forget_item`) and device (`build.latest_artifact`) call it in round 1; with (b) every in-process function exists at the end of round 1 and the round-1 integration check is real. Drawback: round 1 has 6 implementers; build service codes against `catalog.job_input` by spec signature and fakes it in tests.
2. Names the spec gives only as field lists are fixed here (see "Plan-fixed names"). Changing them later = edit this file first.

Plan gate: `/Users/jhangyu/.claude/plugins/cache/jhangyu/podium/1.1.0/protocols/review.md` §L0 Plan gate.

## Global Constraints

- Server: Python 3.12 standard library only; no pip packages for the server (spec L213).
- Frontend: no npm, no bundler; every server string through `esc()`; log text via `textContent` only (spec L214, L1059).
- Port 47000; sidecars on `127.0.0.1:47191` (FlareSolverr), `:47800` (cfbypass), `:47900` (solver) (spec L178–182, L681).
- `RELUME_NETWORK_MODE` is the only platform difference; no per-platform code branch (spec L190–193; contract "Project policy").
- All writes under `/data`; repo mounted read-only at `/repo`; `HOME=/data/home`; `PYTHONPATH=/repo/local`; `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1` (spec L198–205).
- Times stored as UTC ISO-8601 text `2026-10-19T04:00:00Z` (spec L253).
- Mutating requests need `Content-Type: application/json`; `Origin` host must equal `Host` (spec L334).
- Input validation regexes exactly as spec L243–249; every subprocess = argv list, no shell string, no `eval`.
- Module contract: `ROUTES = [(method, pattern, handler)]`, optional `register()` (no thread, no IO) and `start()` (spec L236–241).
- Job kinds `build`/`upstream`/`ota`; statuses `queued`/`running`/`success`/`failed`/`cancelled`; triggers `manual`/`schedule`/`upstream`/`after_build` (spec L44).
- Event names and payloads exactly as spec L50–59.
- Schema files `local/webui/schema/00N_<owner>.sql`; a schema change during development = delete `local/data/webui.db` (spec L255).
- Builds: 1 at a time globally; runner timeout 3600 s; inspect timeout 120 s; OTA install timeout 15 min (spec L303, L726, L779, L977).
- Unreleased project: replaced modules are deleted with their tests and docs; no compatibility shims.
- Git in the shared tree: no `stash` (incl. `stash list`), `reset`, `checkout --`, `clean`, `add -A`; commit with explicit pathspec `git commit -m "<msg>" -- <own paths>`; no `;` in `-m` text.

### Test commands (used in every acceptance list)

The pre-existing image `relume-builder:latest` (ubuntu 24.04, Python 3.12.3, bash 5, java 21, jq) is the test image until P3's new image is built by the user.

- `T_PY <pattern>` = `docker run --rm --network none -v "$PWD:/repo:ro" -e PYTHONPATH=/repo/local -e PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 relume-builder python3 -m unittest discover -s /repo/local/webui/tests -t /repo/local -p '<pattern>'` (foreground, < 150 s, no network).
- `T_SH <script>` = `docker run --rm --network none -v "$PWD:/repo:ro" -w /repo relume-builder bash <script>` (foreground, no network).
- `T_JS <file>` = `node --check <file>` (host node 25, ESM syntax check).
- `T_COMPOSE <mode>` = `RELUME_NETWORK_MODE=<mode> docker compose -f local/docker-compose.yml config >/dev/null; echo RC=$?`.
- "user-run" = exceeds the 150 s agent foreground limit or needs a phone / APKMirror / GitHub Actions; the user runs it and pastes the result. APKMirror rate-limits the home IP: user-run builds are spaced, one item at a time.

#### Task P1.1: DB layer, core schema, events, test base

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/__init__.py` (empty), `local/webui/db.py`, `local/webui/events.py`, `local/webui/schema/001_core.sql`, `local/webui/tests/__init__.py` (empty), `local/webui/tests/helpers.py` (DataTestCase only in this task), `local/webui/tests/test_core_db.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `db.DATA`, `db.SCHEMA_DIR`, `db.SUBDIRS`, `db.init(data_dir=None)`, `db.conn()`, `db.tx()`, `db.migrate(schema_dir=None)`, `db.utcnow() -> str` (`%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ`); `events.subscribe(type, fn)` with `fn(payload)`, `events.emit(type, payload)`, `events.reset()`; `helpers.DataTestCase`.
- Consumes: nothing.

**Behavior:** one SQLite connection per thread, cached until the next `db.init()`; `tx()` uses `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` (no nesting); `migrate()` runs files `NNN_*.sql` with `NNN > user_version` in number order and sets `PRAGMA user_version = NNN` after each. Event subscribers run synchronously; an exception in one is printed with its traceback and swallowed; the others still run.

**Constraints:** stdlib only; no IO at import except reading env `RELUME_DATA`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_core_db.py'` exit 0 (tests below).

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `local/webui/tests/test_core_db.py`

```python
import pathlib, sqlite3, tempfile, threading, unittest
from webui import db, events
from webui.tests.helpers import DataTestCase


class DbTest(DataTestCase):
    def test_init_creates_subdirs(self):
        for d in db.SUBDIRS:
            self.assertTrue((self.data / d).is_dir(), d)

    def test_pragmas(self):
        c = db.conn()
        self.assertEqual(c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode').fetchone()[0], 'wal')
        self.assertEqual(c.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys').fetchone()[0], 1)
        self.assertEqual(c.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout').fetchone()[0], 5000)

    def test_core_tables_and_user_version(self):
        names = {r[0] for r in db.conn().execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
        self.assertTrue({'settings', 'sessions', 'jobs', 'schedules'} <= names)
        self.assertGreaterEqual(db.conn().execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone()[0], 1)

    def test_migrate_order_and_skip(self):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as d:
            d = pathlib.Path(d)
            (d / '002_b.sql').write_text('CREATE TABLE b (x INTEGER REFERENCES a(x));')
            (d / '001_a.sql').write_text('CREATE TABLE a (x INTEGER PRIMARY KEY);')
            with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as data:
                db.init(data)
                db.migrate(d)
                self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone()[0], 2)
                db.migrate(d)  # second run applies nothing, raises nothing
                (d / '003_c.sql').write_text('CREATE TABLE c (y);')
                db.migrate(d)
                self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone()[0], 3)

    def test_tx_rollback(self):
        with self.assertRaises(RuntimeError):
            with db.tx() as c:
                c.execute("INSERT INTO settings VALUES ('k','1')")
                raise RuntimeError('x')
        self.assertIsNone(db.conn().execute("SELECT 1 FROM settings WHERE key='k'").fetchone())

    def test_connection_per_thread(self):
        main = db.conn()
        other = []
        t = threading.Thread(target=lambda: other.append(db.conn()))
        t.start(); t.join()
        self.assertIsNot(main, other[0])

    def test_utcnow_format(self):
        self.assertRegex(db.utcnow(), r'^\d{4}-\d\d-\d\dT\d\d:\d\d:\d\dZ$')


class EventsTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        events.reset()

    def test_emit_runs_all_and_swallows(self):
        got = []
        events.subscribe('build.failed', lambda p: 1 / 0)
        events.subscribe('build.failed', got.append)
        events.emit('build.failed', {'item_id': 1})
        self.assertEqual(got, [{'item_id': 1}])

    def test_other_type_not_called(self):
        got = []
        events.subscribe('ota.done', got.append)
        events.emit('ota.failed', {})
        self.assertEqual(got, [])
```

`local/webui/db.py`:

```python
import datetime
import os
import pathlib
import sqlite3
import threading
from contextlib import contextmanager

SCHEMA_DIR = pathlib.Path(__file__).with_name('schema')
SUBDIRS = ('home', 'logs', 'work', 'artifacts', 'apk-cache', 'upstream', 'icons')
DATA = pathlib.Path(os.environ.get('RELUME_DATA', '/data'))
_local = threading.local()
_gen = 0


def utcnow():
    return datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')


def init(data_dir=None):
    """Set DATA, create its subdirectories, drop cached connections."""
    global DATA, _gen
    DATA = pathlib.Path(data_dir or os.environ.get('RELUME_DATA', '/data'))
    for d in SUBDIRS:
        (DATA / d).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    _gen += 1


def conn():
    c = getattr(_local, 'c', None)
    if c is None or _local.gen != _gen:
        c = sqlite3.connect(DATA / 'webui.db', timeout=5, isolation_level=None,
                            check_same_thread=False)
        c.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
        c.execute('PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL')
        c.execute('PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON')
        c.execute('PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000')
        _local.c, _local.gen = c, _gen
    return c


@contextmanager
def tx():
    c = conn()
    c.execute('BEGIN IMMEDIATE')
    try:
        yield c
    except BaseException:
        c.execute('ROLLBACK')
        raise
    c.execute('COMMIT')


def migrate(schema_dir=None):
    d = pathlib.Path(schema_dir or SCHEMA_DIR)
    c = conn()
    have = c.execute('PRAGMA user_version').fetchone()[0]
    for f in sorted(d.glob('[0-9][0-9][0-9]_*.sql')):
        n = int(f.name[:3])
        if n > have:
            c.executescript(f.read_text())
            c.execute(f'PRAGMA user_version={n}')
```

`local/webui/events.py`:

```python
import traceback

_subs = {}


def subscribe(type, fn):
    """fn(payload); runs in the emitting thread; must not do network IO."""
    _subs.setdefault(type, []).append(fn)


def emit(type, payload):
    for fn in list(_subs.get(type, ())):
        try:
            fn(payload)
        except Exception:
            traceback.print_exc()


def reset():
    _subs.clear()
```

```python
import pathlib
import tempfile
import unittest

from webui import db, events


class DataTestCase(unittest.TestCase):
    """Fresh RELUME_DATA dir + all schema files for every test."""
    schema_dir = None

    def setUp(self):
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        self.data = pathlib.Path(tmp.name)
        db.init(self.data)
        db.migrate(self.schema_dir)
        events.reset()
```

#### Task P1.2: Job queue, workers, log capture, run_inline, recovery, history cleanup

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/jobs.py`, `local/webui/tests/test_core_jobs.py`
- Modify: `local/webui/tests/helpers.py` (DataTestCase.setUp also calls `jobs.reset()`)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `jobs.OPTION_DEFAULTS`, `jobs.canon(v)`, `jobs.normalize(kind, options) -> dict`, `jobs.JobFailed(reason)`, `jobs.Job`, `jobs.register(kind, handler, detail=None)`, `jobs.enqueue(kind, target, trigger, schedule_id=None, options=None) -> int`, `jobs.run_inline(kind, target, trigger, options=None) -> dict`, `jobs.cancel(job_id) -> 'cancelled' | 'cancelling' | None`, `jobs.get(job_id) -> dict | None`, `jobs.to_json(row, with_detail=True) -> dict`, `jobs.recover()`, `jobs.start()` (idempotent), `jobs.stop()` (tests), `jobs.reset()` (tests), `jobs.log_condition(job_id) -> threading.Condition`; handler `(job, log)`; `log.line(text)`, `log.run(argv, cwd=None, env=None, timeout=None, on_line=None) -> int`.
- Consumes: `db`, `settings.get('job_history_count', 500)` (imported at call time; P1.6).

**Behavior:**
- `normalize`: keep only the keys of `OPTION_DEFAULTS[kind]`, fill defaults (spec L46): upstream `pull` true, `rebuild` false, `notify` true; build `notify` true; ota `retries` 0, `mode` `normal`, `package` null, `notify` true.
- `enqueue`: dedup on (kind, status `queued`, canon target, canon normalized options) → existing id; a `running` job does not block (spec L298).
- One worker thread per kind; claims the oldest `queued` row (`ORDER BY id`) in one transaction (status `running`, `started_at`).
- `_execute(job)` shared by worker and `run_inline`: handler returns → `success`; `JobFailed(r)` → `failed`, reason `r`; any other exception → `failed`, reason `"<ExcClass>: <first line>"`, traceback into the log; cancel event set at the end → `cancelled`, reason `cancelled`. Then history cleanup for the kind.
- `log.line`: strips ANSI (`\x1b\[[0-9;?]*[ -/]*[@-~]`), one `HH:MM:SS text` line per input line, flush, `notify_all` on the job's condition (SSE).
- `log.run`: `subprocess.Popen(argv, start_new_session=True, stdout=PIPE, stderr=STDOUT, stdin=DEVNULL, text=True, errors='replace')`; each output line → `log.line` and `on_line(stripped)`; a watcher thread (0.2 s tick) kills the group on cancel or deadline: `SIGTERM`, then `SIGKILL` after 10 s; timeout → `JobFailed('timeout')`; cancel → `JobFailed('cancelled')` (mapped to status `cancelled`).
- `cancel(id)`: queued → `cancelled` at once (never runs); running → sets the event, returns `cancelling`; else `None`.
- `recover()`: every `running` row → `failed`, reason `server restarted`, `finished_at` now (spec L305).
- Cleanup: per kind, finished jobs beyond the newest `job_history_count` are deleted one by one; `sqlite3.IntegrityError` (RESTRICT FK, e.g. artifacts) → skip that row; deleted rows lose `logs/<id>.log` (spec L307).

**Constraints:** `target`/`options` stored only as `canon(...)` text (Plan-fixed "job JSON"); subprocess argv list only.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_core_jobs.py'` exit 0 with the tests below (dedup ×3, serial per kind, parallel across kinds, cancel kills group, cancel queued, timeout, restart recovery, cleanup skips RESTRICT and deletes log, run_inline parity ×3, ANSI strip).

**Steps:**

```python
import json, os, sqlite3, threading, time, unittest
from webui import db, jobs, settings
from webui.tests.helpers import DataTestCase


def wait_status(job_id, statuses, timeout=10):
    end = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while time.monotonic() < end:
        j = jobs.get(job_id)
        if j and j['status'] in statuses:
            return j
        time.sleep(0.05)
    raise AssertionError(f'job {job_id} not in {statuses}: {jobs.get(job_id)}')


class EnqueueTest(DataTestCase):
    def test_dedup_same_target(self):
        a = jobs.enqueue('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'schedule', schedule_id=None)
        b = jobs.enqueue('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'manual')
        self.assertEqual(a, b)

    def test_force_reinstall_not_merged(self):
        a = jobs.enqueue('ota', {'device_id': 1}, 'manual')
        b = jobs.enqueue('ota', {'device_id': 1}, 'manual', options={'mode': 'force_reinstall', 'package': 'x.y'})
        self.assertNotEqual(a, b)

    def test_unknown_option_dropped_and_defaults(self):
        jid = jobs.enqueue('upstream', {'upstream_id': 3}, 'manual', options={'rebuild': True, 'bogus': 1})
        self.assertEqual(jobs.get(jid)['options'], {'pull': True, 'rebuild': True, 'notify': True})
        raw = db.conn().execute('SELECT options, target FROM jobs WHERE id=?', (jid,)).fetchone()
        self.assertEqual(raw['options'], '{"notify":true,"pull":true,"rebuild":true}')
        self.assertEqual(raw['target'], '{"upstream_id":3}')


class WorkerTest(DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        jobs.start()

    def test_serial_per_kind_parallel_across(self):
        running, peak, lock = [], {'build': 0, 'ota': 0}, threading.Lock()

        def h(job, log):
            with lock:
                running.append(job.kind)
                peak[job.kind] = max(peak[job.kind], running.count(job.kind))
                both = 'build' in running and 'ota' in running
            time.sleep(0.3)
            with lock:
                running.remove(job.kind)
            if both:
                log.line('overlap')

        jobs.register('build', h)
        jobs.register('ota', h)
        ids = [jobs.enqueue('build', {'item_id': i}, 'manual') for i in (1, 2)]
        ids.append(jobs.enqueue('ota', {'device_id': 1}, 'manual'))
        for i in ids:
            wait_status(i, {'success'})
        self.assertEqual(peak['build'], 1)
        logs = ''.join((db.DATA / 'logs' / f'{i}.log').read_text() for i in ids)
        self.assertIn('overlap', logs)

    def test_cancel_kills_process_group(self):
        pidfile = self.data / 'child.pid'

        def h(job, log):
            log.run(['sh', '-c', f'sleep 60 & echo $! > {pidfile}; wait'])

        jobs.register('build', h)
        jid = jobs.enqueue('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'manual')
        wait_status(jid, {'running'})
        end = time.monotonic() + 5
        while not pidfile.exists() and time.monotonic() < end:
            time.sleep(0.05)
        self.assertEqual(jobs.cancel(jid), 'cancelling')
        j = wait_status(jid, {'cancelled'}, timeout=15)
        self.assertEqual(j['reason'], 'cancelled')
        pid = int(pidfile.read_text())
        time.sleep(0.2)
        with self.assertRaises(ProcessLookupError):
            os.kill(pid, 0)

    def test_cancel_queued_never_runs(self):
        ran = []
        gate = threading.Event()
        jobs.register('build', lambda job, log: (ran.append(job.id), gate.wait(5)))
        first = jobs.enqueue('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'manual')
        wait_status(first, {'running'})
        second = jobs.enqueue('build', {'item_id': 2}, 'manual')
        self.assertEqual(jobs.cancel(second), 'cancelled')
        gate.set()
        wait_status(first, {'success'})
        time.sleep(0.3)
        self.assertEqual(ran, [first])
        self.assertEqual(jobs.get(second)['status'], 'cancelled')

    def test_timeout(self):
        jobs.register('build', lambda job, log: log.run(['sleep', '30'], timeout=0.5))
        j = wait_status(jobs.enqueue('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'manual'), {'failed'}, timeout=15)
        self.assertEqual(j['reason'], 'timeout')

    def test_ansi_stripped_and_on_line(self):
        seen = []
        jobs.register('build', lambda job, log: log.run(['printf', '\\033[32mok\\033[0m\\n::step::patch\\n'], on_line=seen.append))
        jid = jobs.enqueue('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'manual')
        wait_status(jid, {'success'})
        text = (db.DATA / 'logs' / f'{jid}.log').read_text()
        self.assertRegex(text, r'^\d\d:\d\d:\d\d ok\n')
        self.assertNotIn('\x1b', text)
        self.assertEqual(seen, ['ok', '::step::patch'])


class InlineTest(DataTestCase):
    def test_parity(self):
        def ok(job, log): pass
        def bad(job, log): raise jobs.JobFailed('patch_failed: x')
        def boom(job, log): raise ValueError('kaput\nmore')
        for h, status, reason in ((ok, 'success', None), (bad, 'failed', 'patch_failed: x'),
                                  (boom, 'failed', 'ValueError: kaput')):
            jobs.register('build', h)
            j = jobs.run_inline('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'manual')
            self.assertEqual((j['status'], j['reason']), (status, reason))


class RecoveryCleanupTest(DataTestCase):
    def test_recover(self):
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO jobs(kind,status,trigger,target,created_at) VALUES('build','running','manual','{}','x')")
            c.execute("INSERT INTO jobs(kind,status,trigger,target,created_at) VALUES('build','queued','manual','{}','x')")
        jobs.recover()
        rows = [tuple(r) for r in db.conn().execute('SELECT status, reason FROM jobs ORDER BY id')]
        self.assertEqual(rows, [('failed', 'server restarted'), ('queued', None)])

    def test_cleanup_skips_restrict_and_deletes_logs(self):
        settings.put('job_history_count', 1)
        db.conn().execute('CREATE TABLE keep (job_id INTEGER REFERENCES jobs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT)')
        jobs.register('build', lambda job, log: log.line('x'))
        a = jobs.run_inline('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'manual')['id']
        db.conn().execute('INSERT INTO keep VALUES (?)', (a,))
        b = jobs.run_inline('build', {'item_id': 2}, 'manual')['id']
        c = jobs.run_inline('build', {'item_id': 3}, 'manual')['id']
        ids = [r[0] for r in db.conn().execute('SELECT id FROM jobs ORDER BY id')]
        self.assertEqual(ids, [a, c])            # b deleted, a kept by RESTRICT
        self.assertFalse((db.DATA / 'logs' / f'{b}.log').exists())
```

```python
import json
import os
import re
import signal
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import threading
import time
import traceback

from . import db

OPTION_DEFAULTS = {
    'upstream': {'pull': True, 'rebuild': False, 'notify': True},
    'build': {'notify': True},
    'ota': {'retries': 0, 'mode': 'normal', 'package': None, 'notify': True},
}
ANSI = re.compile(r'\x1b\[[0-9;?]*[ -/]*[@-~]')
_handlers = {}
_running = {}
_conds = {}
_lock = threading.Lock()
_wake = threading.Condition()
_threads = []
_stop = threading.Event()


def canon(v):
    return json.dumps(v, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'))


def normalize(kind, options):
    o = options or {}
    return {k: o.get(k, d) for k, d in OPTION_DEFAULTS[kind].items()}


class JobFailed(Exception):
    def __init__(self, reason):
        super().__init__(reason)
        self.reason = reason


class Job:
    def __init__(self, row):
        self.id = row['id']
        self.kind = row['kind']
        self.target = json.loads(row['target'])
        self.options = json.loads(row['options'])
        self.trigger = row['trigger']
        self.schedule_id = row['schedule_id']
        self.cancel_event = threading.Event()


def log_condition(job_id):
    with _lock:
        return _conds.setdefault(job_id, threading.Condition())  # ponytail: never pruned, one small object per job


def _kill_group(p):
    try:
        os.killpg(p.pid, signal.SIGTERM)
    except ProcessLookupError:
        return
    try:
        p.wait(10)
    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
        try:
            os.killpg(p.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
        except ProcessLookupError:
            pass


class Log:
    def __init__(self, job):
        self.job = job
        self.f = open(db.DATA / 'logs' / f'{job.id}.log', 'a', encoding='utf-8')
        self.cond = log_condition(job.id)

    def line(self, text):
        stamp = time.strftime('%H:%M:%S ')
        for t in ANSI.sub('', str(text)).splitlines() or ['']:
            self.f.write(stamp + t + '\n')
        self.f.flush()
        with self.cond:
            self.cond.notify_all()

    def run(self, argv, cwd=None, env=None, timeout=None, on_line=None):
        p = subprocess.Popen(argv, cwd=cwd, env=env, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL,
                             stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                             start_new_session=True, text=True, errors='replace')
        done, timed_out = threading.Event(), threading.Event()
        deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout if timeout else None

        def watch():
            while not done.wait(0.2):
                cancel = self.job.cancel_event.is_set()
                if cancel or (deadline and time.monotonic() > deadline):
                    if not cancel:
                        timed_out.set()
                    _kill_group(p)
                    return

        threading.Thread(target=watch, daemon=True).start()
        for raw in p.stdout:
            s = ANSI.sub('', raw.rstrip('\n'))
            self.line(s)
            if on_line:
                on_line(s)
        rc = p.wait()
        done.set()
        if timed_out.is_set():
            raise JobFailed('timeout')
        if self.job.cancel_event.is_set():
            raise JobFailed('cancelled')
        return rc

    def close(self):
        self.f.close()
        with self.cond:
            self.cond.notify_all()


def register(kind, handler, detail=None):
    _handlers[kind] = (handler, detail)


def reset():
    _handlers.clear()


def enqueue(kind, target, trigger, schedule_id=None, options=None):
    t, o = canon(target), canon(normalize(kind, options))
    with db.tx() as c:
        r = c.execute("SELECT id FROM jobs WHERE kind=? AND status='queued' AND target=? AND options=?",
                      (kind, t, o)).fetchone()
        if r:
            return r['id']
        jid = c.execute('INSERT INTO jobs(kind,status,trigger,schedule_id,target,options,created_at) '
                        "VALUES (?,'queued',?,?,?,?,?)",
                        (kind, trigger, schedule_id, t, o, db.utcnow())).lastrowid
    with _wake:
        _wake.notify_all()
    return jid


def _execute(job):
    handler = _handlers[job.kind][0]
    log = Log(job)
    with _lock:
        _running[job.id] = job
    status, reason = 'success', None
    try:
        handler(job, log)
    except JobFailed as e:
        status, reason = 'failed', e.reason
    except Exception as e:
        first = (str(e).splitlines() or [''])[0]
        status, reason = 'failed', f'{type(e).__name__}: {first}' if first else type(e).__name__
        log.line(traceback.format_exc())
    finally:
        with _lock:
            _running.pop(job.id, None)
    if job.cancel_event.is_set():
        status, reason = 'cancelled', 'cancelled'
    with db.tx() as c:
        # a handler that returns normally may have written jobs.reason itself (OTA outcome, spec L914)
        c.execute('UPDATE jobs SET status=?, reason=COALESCE(?, reason), finished_at=? WHERE id=?',
                  (status, reason, db.utcnow(), job.id))
    log.close()
    _cleanup(job.kind)
    return status


def _claim(kind):
    with db.tx() as c:
        r = c.execute("SELECT * FROM jobs WHERE kind=? AND status='queued' ORDER BY id LIMIT 1",
                      (kind,)).fetchone()
        if r is None:
            return None
        c.execute("UPDATE jobs SET status='running', started_at=? WHERE id=?", (db.utcnow(), r['id']))
    return Job(r)


def _worker(kind):
    while not _stop.is_set():
        job = _claim(kind) if kind in _handlers else None
        if job is None:
            with _wake:
                _wake.wait(5)
            continue
        _execute(job)


def start():
    if _threads:
        return
    _stop.clear()
    for kind in OPTION_DEFAULTS:
        t = threading.Thread(target=_worker, args=(kind,), daemon=True, name=f'jobs-{kind}')
        t.start()
        _threads.append(t)


def stop():
    """Tests only: end the worker threads (a running handler finishes first)."""
    _stop.set()
    with _wake:
        _wake.notify_all()
    for t in _threads:
        t.join(15)
    _threads.clear()


def run_inline(kind, target, trigger, options=None):
    with db.tx() as c:
        jid = c.execute('INSERT INTO jobs(kind,status,trigger,target,options,created_at,started_at) '
                        "VALUES (?,'running',?,?,?,?,?)",
                        (kind, trigger, canon(target), canon(normalize(kind, options)),
                         db.utcnow(), db.utcnow())).lastrowid
    _execute(Job(db.conn().execute('SELECT * FROM jobs WHERE id=?', (jid,)).fetchone()))
    return get(jid)


def cancel(job_id):
    with db.tx() as c:
        n = c.execute("UPDATE jobs SET status='cancelled', reason='cancelled', finished_at=? "
                      "WHERE id=? AND status='queued'", (db.utcnow(), job_id)).rowcount
    if n:
        return 'cancelled'
    with _lock:
        job = _running.get(job_id)
    if job:
        job.cancel_event.set()
        return 'cancelling'
    return None


def recover():
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute("UPDATE jobs SET status='failed', reason='server restarted', finished_at=? "
                  "WHERE status='running'", (db.utcnow(),))


def _cleanup(kind):
    from . import settings
    keep = int(settings.get('job_history_count', 500))
    old = [r[0] for r in db.conn().execute(
        "SELECT id FROM jobs WHERE kind=? AND status NOT IN ('queued','running') "
        'ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT -1 OFFSET ?', (kind, keep))]
    for jid in old:
        try:
            with db.tx() as c:
                c.execute('DELETE FROM jobs WHERE id=?', (jid,))
        except sqlite3.IntegrityError:
            continue
        (db.DATA / 'logs' / f'{jid}.log').unlink(missing_ok=True)


def to_json(row, with_detail=True):
    d = dict(row)
    d['target'] = json.loads(d['target'])
    d['options'] = json.loads(d['options'])
    detail = _handlers.get(d['kind'], (None, None))[1]
    d['detail'] = detail(d['id']) if with_detail and detail else None
    return d


def get(job_id):
    r = db.conn().execute('SELECT * FROM jobs WHERE id=?', (job_id,)).fetchone()
    return to_json(r, with_detail=False) if r else None
```

#### Task P1.3: HTTP server, router, response types, validators, module loading (interface commit ends here)

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/server.py`, `local/webui/tests/test_core_router.py`
- Modify: `local/webui/tests/helpers.py` (add `serve`, `http`)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `helpers.fake_request(method, path, json, params, query, headers, session)`, `server.VERSION = "1.0"`, `server.STARTED_AT`, `server.MODULES`, `server.CORE = ("auth", "jobs", "scheduler", "settings")`, `server.load_modules(names=MODULES) -> [module]`, `server.make_server(mods, host="0.0.0.0", port=47000) -> ThreadingHTTPServer`, `server.HttpError(status, message)`, `server.FileResponse(path, download_name=None, cache_seconds=0)`, `server.StreamResponse(write_fn, download_name)`, `server.SSEResponse(iterator)`, `server.Request` (`method, path, params, query, json, headers, session`), validators `validate_slug(v, field='value')`, `validate_package(v, field='package')`, `validate_repo(v, field='repo')`, `validate_host(v, field='host')`, `validate_port(v, field='port')` (each returns `v`, raises `HttpError(400, 'invalid <field>')`), `safe_remote(v) -> str`; handler return `(status, obj)` or `(status, obj, headers_dict)`; `helpers.serve(tc, names=())`, `helpers.http(...)`.
- Consumes: `auth.session_token(cookie_header) -> str | None` and `auth.PUBLIC` (P1.4; until P1.4 lands, `server.py` imports `auth` lazily inside the guard, so the test for the guard is in P1.4).

**Behavior:**
- Route table = `ROUTES` of the four core modules (imported by name at `make_server` time, so no import cycle) plus `ROUTES` of `mods`. `pattern` `{name}` segments match `[^/]+`; values matching `^-?\d+$` become `int`.
- Request pipeline for `/api/*`: route lookup (404 unknown path, 405 known path with other method) → for `POST PUT PATCH DELETE`: `Content-Type` (before `;`) must be `application/json` else 415; `Origin` present and its `host[:port]` ≠ `Host` header → 403; body ≤ 1 MiB else 413; empty body = `{}`; invalid JSON → 400 → auth guard (route not in `auth.PUBLIC` → valid session else 401) → handler.
- Errors: `HttpError` → `{"error": message}` with its status; `sqlite3.OperationalError` containing `locked` → 503 `{"error":"database busy"}`; any other exception → 500 `{"error":"internal error"}` + traceback on stdout.
- Responses: JSON with `Content-Length`; `FileResponse` streams with `Content-Length`, `Content-Type` from `mimetypes.guess_type` (else `application/octet-stream`), `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="<name>"` only when `download_name` is set (else inline), `Cache-Control: max-age=<n>` when `cache_seconds` > 0; `StreamResponse` sends `Connection: close`, no length, calls `write_fn(wfile)`; `SSEResponse` sends `Content-Type: text/event-stream`, `Cache-Control: no-cache`, `Connection: close`, writes and flushes each yielded `bytes`, stops on `BrokenPipeError`/`ConnectionResetError`.
- Static files: Plan-fixed "static auth rule"; content types `.html text/html; charset=utf-8`, `.css text/css`, `.js text/javascript`, `.svg image/svg+xml`; `Cache-Control: no-cache`.
- `load_modules(names)`: `importlib.import_module('webui.' + n)` for each name, in order.

**Constraints:** `ThreadingHTTPServer`, `daemon_threads = True`, `protocol_version = 'HTTP/1.1'`.

```python
import sqlite3, sys, threading, types, unittest
from webui import server
from webui.tests.helpers import DataTestCase, serve, http


def fake_module(name, routes, register=None):
    m = types.ModuleType('webui.' + name)
    m.ROUTES = routes
    if register:
        m.register = register
    sys.modules['webui.' + name] = m
    return name


class PublicRoutesMixin:
    """Routes in auth.PUBLIC skip the session check; tests mark their fake routes public."""
    def public(self, method, pattern):
        from webui import auth
        auth.PUBLIC.add((method, pattern))
        self.addCleanup(auth.PUBLIC.discard, (method, pattern))


class RouterTest(PublicRoutesMixin, DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        def echo(req):
            return 200, {'params': req.params, 'json': req.json, 'q': req.query}
        def boom(req):
            raise RuntimeError('x')
        def locked(req):
            raise sqlite3.OperationalError('database is locked')
        def fail(req):
            raise server.HttpError(409, 'busy item')
        routes = [('GET', '/api/t/{id}/x/{name}', echo), ('PATCH', '/api/t/{id}', echo),
                  ('GET', '/api/boom', boom), ('GET', '/api/locked', locked), ('POST', '/api/fail', fail)]
        for m, p, _ in routes:
            self.public(m, p)
        self.base = serve(self, [fake_module('fakerouter', routes)])

    def test_int_params_and_query(self):
        st, _, body = http('GET', self.base + '/api/t/12/x/abc?k=v')
        self.assertEqual(st, 200)
        self.assertIn(b'"params": {"id": 12, "name": "abc"}', body)
        self.assertIn(b'"q": {"k": "v"}', body)

    def test_404_405(self):
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/api/nope')[0], 404)
        self.assertEqual(http('DELETE', self.base + '/api/t/1', body={})[0], 405)

    def test_patch_mutating_rules(self):
        self.assertEqual(http('PATCH', self.base + '/api/t/1', body={'a': 1})[0], 200)
        st, _, _ = http('PATCH', self.base + '/api/t/1', body={'a': 1}, headers={'Content-Type': 'text/plain'})
        self.assertEqual(st, 415)
        st, _, _ = http('PATCH', self.base + '/api/t/1', body={}, headers={'Origin': 'http://evil.example'})
        self.assertEqual(st, 403)

    def test_too_large(self):
        st, _, _ = http('PATCH', self.base + '/api/t/1', body={'a': 'x' * (1 << 20)})
        self.assertEqual(st, 413)

    def test_error_mapping(self):
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/api/boom')[:3:2], (500, b'{"error": "internal error"}'))
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/api/locked')[0], 503)
        self.assertEqual(http('POST', self.base + '/api/fail', body={})[2], b'{"error": "busy item"}')


class LoadModulesTest(DataTestCase):
    def test_empty(self):
        self.assertEqual(server.load_modules(()), [])

    def test_register_starts_no_thread(self):
        name = fake_module('fakereg', [], register=lambda: None)
        before = threading.active_count()
        for m in server.load_modules([name]):
            m.register()
        self.assertEqual(threading.active_count(), before)


class ValidatorTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_slug(self):
        for ok in ('morphe', 'youtube-music-arm64-v8a', 'morphe.keystore', '_x'):
            self.assertEqual(server.validate_slug(ok), ok)
        for bad in ('', '.x', '-x', '..', 'a/b', 'a b', 5):
            with self.assertRaises(server.HttpError):
                server.validate_slug(bad)

    def test_package_repo_host_port(self):
        server.validate_package('com.google.android.youtube')
        server.validate_repo('MorpheApp/morphe-desktop')
        server.validate_host('192.168.1.20')
        server.validate_host('pixel-7a.local')
        self.assertEqual(server.validate_port(5555), 5555)
        for f, v in ((server.validate_package, 'youtube'), (server.validate_repo, 'a/b/c'),
                     (server.validate_host, '-bad'), (server.validate_host, '1.2.3.4; rm'),
                     (server.validate_port, 0), (server.validate_port, 65536), (server.validate_port, True)):
            with self.assertRaises(server.HttpError):
                f(v)

    def test_safe_remote(self):
        self.assertEqual(server.safe_remote('../x y'), '_._x_y')
        self.assertEqual(server.safe_remote('-v1.2'), '_v1.2')
```

```python
import datetime
import importlib
import ipaddress
import json
import mimetypes
import pathlib
import re
import shutil
import sqlite3
import traceback
import urllib.parse
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer

VERSION = '1.0'
STARTED_AT = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')
MODULES = ('catalog', 'upstream', 'build', 'devices', 'ota', 'notify')
CORE = ('auth', 'jobs', 'scheduler', 'settings')
STATIC = pathlib.Path(__file__).with_name('static')
MUTATING = {'POST', 'PUT', 'PATCH', 'DELETE'}
MAX_BODY = 1 << 20
CTYPES = {'.html': 'text/html; charset=utf-8', '.css': 'text/css', '.js': 'text/javascript',
          '.svg': 'image/svg+xml'}
PAGE = re.compile(r'^/([a-z]+\.html)$')
ASSET = re.compile(r'^/static/([A-Za-z0-9_-]+\.(?:css|js|svg))$')

SLUG = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z0-9_][A-Za-z0-9._-]*$')
PACKAGE = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_]*(\.[A-Za-z0-9_]+)+$')
REPO = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+/[A-Za-z0-9_.-]+$')
LABEL = re.compile(r'^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9-]{0,62}$')


class HttpError(Exception):
    def __init__(self, status, message):
        super().__init__(message)
        self.status, self.message = status, message


class FileResponse:
    """download_name None = inline (e.g. icons); cache_seconds > 0 adds Cache-Control max-age."""
    def __init__(self, path, download_name=None, cache_seconds=0):
        self.path, self.download_name, self.cache_seconds = pathlib.Path(path), download_name, cache_seconds


class StreamResponse:
    def __init__(self, write_fn, download_name):
        self.write_fn, self.download_name = write_fn, download_name


class SSEResponse:
    def __init__(self, iterator):
        self.iterator = iterator


class Request:
    def __init__(self, method, path, params, query, headers):
        self.method, self.path, self.params, self.query, self.headers = method, path, params, query, headers
        self.json = {}
        self.session = None


def validate_slug(v, field='value'):
    if not isinstance(v, str) or not SLUG.match(v):
        raise HttpError(400, f'invalid {field}')
    return v


def validate_package(v, field='package'):
    if not isinstance(v, str) or not PACKAGE.match(v):
        raise HttpError(400, f'invalid {field}')
    return v


def validate_repo(v, field='repo'):
    if not isinstance(v, str) or not REPO.match(v):
        raise HttpError(400, f'invalid {field}')
    return v


def validate_host(v, field='host'):
    if isinstance(v, str):
        try:
            ipaddress.IPv4Address(v)
            return v
        except ValueError:
            labels = v.split('.')
            if len(v) <= 253 and all(LABEL.match(x) for x in labels):
                return v
    raise HttpError(400, f'invalid {field}')


def validate_port(v, field='port'):
    if isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, int) or not 1 <= v <= 65535:
        raise HttpError(400, f'invalid {field}')
    return v


def safe_remote(v):
    s = re.sub(r'[^A-Za-z0-9._-]', '_', str(v))
    return '_' + s[1:] if s[:1] in ('.', '-') else s


def load_modules(names=MODULES):
    return [importlib.import_module('webui.' + n) for n in names]


def _compile(pattern):
    return re.compile('^' + re.sub(r'\{(\w+)\}', r'(?P<\1>[^/]+)', pattern) + '$')


def _attachment(name):
    return 'attachment; filename="' + re.sub(r'["\\\r\n]', '_', name) + '"'


class Handler(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    protocol_version = 'HTTP/1.1'

    def log_message(self, fmt, *args):  # ponytail: no access log, server log stays readable
        pass

    def do_GET(self): self._dispatch('GET')
    def do_POST(self): self._dispatch('POST')
    def do_PUT(self): self._dispatch('PUT')
    def do_PATCH(self): self._dispatch('PATCH')
    def do_DELETE(self): self._dispatch('DELETE')

    def _dispatch(self, method):
        url = urllib.parse.urlsplit(self.path)
        try:
            if not url.path.startswith('/api/'):
                return self._static(url.path)
            self._send(self._api(method, url))
        except HttpError as e:
            self._json(e.status, {'error': e.message})
        except sqlite3.OperationalError as e:
            if 'locked' in str(e):
                self._json(503, {'error': 'database busy'})
            else:
                traceback.print_exc()
                self._json(500, {'error': 'internal error'})
        except (BrokenPipeError, ConnectionResetError):
            self.close_connection = True
        except Exception:
            traceback.print_exc()
            self._json(500, {'error': 'internal error'})

    def _api(self, method, url):
        from . import auth
        found = None
        path_known = False
        for m, rx, pattern, fn in self.server.routes:
            mt = rx.match(url.path)
            if mt:
                path_known = True
                if m == method:
                    found = (pattern, fn, mt)
                    break
        if not found:
            raise HttpError(405 if path_known else 404, 'method not allowed' if path_known else 'not found')
        pattern, fn, mt = found
        params = {k: int(v) if re.fullmatch(r'-?\d+', v) else urllib.parse.unquote(v)
                  for k, v in mt.groupdict().items()}
        query = dict(urllib.parse.parse_qsl(url.query))
        req = Request(method, url.path, params, query, self.headers)
        if method in MUTATING:
            if self.headers.get('Content-Type', '').split(';')[0].strip() != 'application/json':
                raise HttpError(415, 'content type must be application/json')
            origin = self.headers.get('Origin')
            if origin and urllib.parse.urlsplit(origin).netloc != self.headers.get('Host'):
                raise HttpError(403, 'origin mismatch')
            n = int(self.headers.get('Content-Length') or 0)
            if n > MAX_BODY:
                self.close_connection = True
                raise HttpError(413, 'body too large')
            raw = self.rfile.read(n) if n else b''
            try:
                req.json = json.loads(raw) if raw else {}
            except ValueError:
                raise HttpError(400, 'invalid json')
        if (method, pattern) not in auth.PUBLIC:
            req.session = auth.session_token(self.headers.get('Cookie'))
            if not req.session:
                raise HttpError(401, 'login required')
        return fn(req)

    def _json(self, status, obj, headers=None):
        body = json.dumps(obj).encode()
        self.send_response(status)
        self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/json')
        self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(body)))
        for k, v in (headers or {}).items():
            self.send_header(k, v)
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def _send(self, res):
        if isinstance(res, tuple):
            return self._json(*res)
        if isinstance(res, FileResponse):
            with open(res.path, 'rb') as f:
                size = res.path.stat().st_size
                self.send_response(200)
                self.send_header('Content-Type', mimetypes.guess_type(res.path.name)[0] or 'application/octet-stream')
                self.send_header('Content-Length', str(size))
                if res.download_name:
                    self.send_header('Content-Disposition', _attachment(res.download_name))
                if res.cache_seconds:
                    self.send_header('Cache-Control', f'max-age={int(res.cache_seconds)}')
                self.end_headers()
                shutil.copyfileobj(f, self.wfile, 1 << 20)
            return
        if isinstance(res, StreamResponse):
            self.close_connection = True
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header('Content-Type', 'application/zip')
            self.send_header('Content-Disposition', _attachment(res.download_name))
            self.send_header('Connection', 'close')
            self.end_headers()
            res.write_fn(self.wfile)
            return
        if isinstance(res, SSEResponse):
            self.close_connection = True
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header('Content-Type', 'text/event-stream')
            self.send_header('Cache-Control', 'no-cache')
            self.send_header('Connection', 'close')
            self.end_headers()
            for chunk in res.iterator:
                self.wfile.write(chunk)
                self.wfile.flush()
            return
        raise TypeError(f'bad handler result {type(res).__name__}')

    def _static(self, path):
        from . import auth
        if path == '/':
            name, public = 'index.html', False
        elif ASSET.match(path):
            name, public = ASSET.match(path).group(1), True
        elif PAGE.match(path):
            name = PAGE.match(path).group(1)
            public = name == 'login.html'
        else:
            raise HttpError(404, 'not found')
        f = STATIC / name
        if not f.is_file():
            raise HttpError(404, 'not found')
        if not public and not auth.session_token(self.headers.get('Cookie')):
            self.send_response(302)
            self.send_header('Location', '/login.html')
            self.send_header('Content-Length', '0')
            self.end_headers()
            return
        body = f.read_bytes()
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header('Content-Type', CTYPES[f.suffix])
        self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(body)))
        self.send_header('Cache-Control', 'no-cache')
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)


def make_server(mods, host='0.0.0.0', port=47000):
    srv = ThreadingHTTPServer((host, port), Handler)
    srv.daemon_threads = True
    srv.routes = [(m, _compile(p), p, fn)
                  for mod in load_modules(CORE) + list(mods) for (m, p, fn) in getattr(mod, 'ROUTES', [])]
    return srv
```

`local/webui/tests/helpers.py` additions:

```python
import json
import threading
import types
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

from webui import db, events, jobs, server


class _NoRedirect(urllib.request.HTTPRedirectHandler):
    def redirect_request(self, *a, **k):
        return None


_opener = urllib.request.build_opener(_NoRedirect)


def serve(tc, names=()):
    """Live server on 127.0.0.1:<free port>; names = module names or already-imported modules."""
    mods = [server.load_modules([n])[0] if isinstance(n, str) else n for n in names]
    srv = server.make_server(mods, '127.0.0.1', 0)
    threading.Thread(target=srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
    tc.addCleanup(srv.server_close)
    tc.addCleanup(srv.shutdown)
    return f'http://127.0.0.1:{srv.server_address[1]}'


def fake_request(method='GET', path='/', json=None, params=None, query=None, headers=None, session='test'):
    """Route-handler input without HTTP (auth, CSRF and body parsing are tested in test_core_*)."""
    return types.SimpleNamespace(method=method, path=path, json=json or {}, params=params or {},
                                 query=query or {}, headers=headers or {}, session=session)


def http(method, url, body=None, cookie=None, headers=None):
    data = json.dumps(body).encode() if body is not None else None
    h = {'Content-Type': 'application/json'} if method != 'GET' else {}
    if cookie:
        h['Cookie'] = cookie
    h.update(headers or {})
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=method, headers=h)
    try:
        with _opener.open(req, timeout=15) as r:
            return r.status, dict(r.headers), r.read()
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        return e.code, dict(e.headers), e.read()
```

#### Task P1.4: Auth — password, sessions, lockout, guard, static redirect

**Files:**
- Modify (replace stub): `local/webui/auth.py`
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_core_auth.py`
- Modify: `local/webui/tests/helpers.py` (add `login`; `DataTestCase.setUp` calls `auth.reset_lockout()`)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `auth.PUBLIC` (set of `(method, pattern)`: `('POST','/api/auth/login')`, `('GET','/api/health')`), `auth.COOKIE = 'relume_session'`, `auth.hash_password(pw, salt=None) -> str` (`scrypt$<salt hex>$<hash hex>`), `auth.verify_password(pw, stored) -> bool`, `auth.set_password(pw)`, `auth.new_password() -> str` (20 chars), `auth.ensure_password()`, `auth.create_session(remember) -> (token, set_cookie)`, `auth.session_token(cookie_header) -> str | None`, `auth.reset_lockout()`; routes `POST /api/auth/login`, `POST /api/auth/logout`, `POST /api/auth/password`; `helpers.login(base_url, password='correct-horse-42') -> 'relume_session=<token>'`.
- Consumes: `db`, `settings.get/put`, `server.HttpError`.

**Behavior (spec L326–336, L345):**
- scrypt `n=2**14, r=8, p=1`, 16-byte salt, 32-byte key, compare with `hmac.compare_digest`.
- `ensure_password()`: when `admin_password_hash` is missing, create `secrets.token_urlsafe(15)` (20 chars), store its hash, print `admin password: <pw>` once.
- Login body `{password, remember?}` (`remember` default true). Right → 200 `{"ok":true}` + `Set-Cookie: relume_session=<token>; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/` plus `; Max-Age=2592000` when `remember`; server `expires_at` now + 30 d (remember) or + 12 h. Expired sessions are deleted at each login.
- Wrong → 401 `{"error":"wrong password","attempts_left":N}` with N = 5 − failures in the last 300 s; the 5th failure in 300 s → 429 `{"error":"locked","retry_after":300}`; while locked every login (also a correct one) → 429 with the remaining seconds (ceil). In-memory counter (resets on restart, spec L335).
- Logout deletes the current session and returns `Set-Cookie: relume_session=; …; Max-Age=0`.
- `POST /api/auth/password {old,new}`: `new` < 10 chars → 400 `new password must have at least 10 characters`; wrong `old` → 403 `wrong password`; else store, delete every other session → 200 `{"signed_out":N}`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_core_auth.py'` exit 0 (tests below: hash verify, 401 without cookie, session expiry, remember true/false cookies and expiry, 4 → 1 attempts_left then 429 and locked correct password, logout, password change, static rule: `/login.html` and `/static/x.css` 200 without session, `/` and `/item.html` 302 without and 200 with session, `ensure_password` prints once).

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `local/webui/tests/test_core_auth.py`

```python
import contextlib, datetime, io, pathlib, tempfile, unittest
from webui import auth, db, server, settings
from webui.tests.helpers import DataTestCase, serve, http, login


class AuthTest(DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.base = serve(self)

    def test_hash_roundtrip(self):
        h = auth.hash_password('secret-pass-1')
        self.assertTrue(h.startswith('scrypt$'))
        self.assertTrue(auth.verify_password('secret-pass-1', h))
        self.assertFalse(auth.verify_password('other', h))
        self.assertFalse(auth.verify_password('x', None))

    def test_401_without_cookie(self):
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/api/jobs')[0], 401)

    def test_remember_true_and_false(self):
        auth.set_password('correct-horse-42')
        st, h, _ = http('POST', self.base + '/api/auth/login', {'password': 'correct-horse-42', 'remember': True})
        self.assertEqual(st, 200)
        self.assertIn('Max-Age=2592000', h['Set-Cookie'])
        self.assertIn('HttpOnly', h['Set-Cookie']); self.assertIn('SameSite=Strict', h['Set-Cookie'])
        st, h2, _ = http('POST', self.base + '/api/auth/login', {'password': 'correct-horse-42', 'remember': False})
        self.assertNotIn('Max-Age', h2['Set-Cookie'])
        rows = sorted(r[0] for r in db.conn().execute('SELECT expires_at FROM sessions'))
        now = datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc)
        exp = [datetime.datetime.strptime(x, '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ').replace(tzinfo=datetime.timezone.utc) - now for x in rows]
        self.assertAlmostEqual(exp[0].total_seconds(), 12 * 3600, delta=60)
        self.assertAlmostEqual(exp[1].total_seconds(), 30 * 86400, delta=60)

    def test_session_expiry(self):
        cookie = login(self.base)
        db.conn().execute("UPDATE sessions SET expires_at='2000-01-01T00:00:00Z'")
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/api/jobs', cookie=cookie)[0], 401)

    def test_lockout(self):
        auth.set_password('correct-horse-42')
        left = []
        for _ in range(4):
            st, _, body = http('POST', self.base + '/api/auth/login', {'password': 'nope'})
            self.assertEqual(st, 401)
            left.append(__import__('json').loads(body)['attempts_left'])
        self.assertEqual(left, [4, 3, 2, 1])
        st, _, body = http('POST', self.base + '/api/auth/login', {'password': 'nope'})
        self.assertEqual((st, __import__('json').loads(body)['error']), (429, 'locked'))
        st, _, body = http('POST', self.base + '/api/auth/login', {'password': 'correct-horse-42'})
        self.assertEqual(st, 429)
        self.assertGreater(__import__('json').loads(body)['retry_after'], 0)

    def test_logout(self):
        cookie = login(self.base)
        st, h, _ = http('POST', self.base + '/api/auth/logout', {}, cookie=cookie)
        self.assertEqual(st, 200)
        self.assertIn('Max-Age=0', h['Set-Cookie'])
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/api/jobs', cookie=cookie)[0], 401)

    def test_password_change(self):
        a = login(self.base)
        b = login(self.base)
        st, _, _ = http('POST', self.base + '/api/auth/password', {'old': 'correct-horse-42', 'new': 'short'}, cookie=a)
        self.assertEqual(st, 400)
        st, _, _ = http('POST', self.base + '/api/auth/password', {'old': 'bad', 'new': 'long-enough-pw'}, cookie=a)
        self.assertEqual(st, 403)
        st, _, body = http('POST', self.base + '/api/auth/password', {'old': 'correct-horse-42', 'new': 'long-enough-pw'}, cookie=a)
        self.assertEqual((st, body), (200, b'{"signed_out": 1}'))
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/api/jobs', cookie=b)[0], 401)
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/api/jobs', cookie=a)[0], 200)

    def test_ensure_password_prints_once(self):
        out = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
            auth.ensure_password()
            auth.ensure_password()
        self.assertRegex(out.getvalue(), r'^admin password: \S{20}\n$')


class StaticRuleTest(DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        d = pathlib.Path(tmp.name)
        for n in ('index.html', 'login.html', 'item.html', 'x.css'):
            (d / n).write_text(n)
        old = server.STATIC
        server.STATIC = d
        self.addCleanup(setattr, server, 'STATIC', old)
        self.base = serve(self)

    def test_rule(self):
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/login.html')[0], 200)
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/static/x.css')[0], 200)
        for p in ('/', '/item.html'):
            st, h, _ = http('GET', self.base + p)
            self.assertEqual((st, h.get('Location')), (302, '/login.html'))
        cookie = login(self.base)
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/', cookie=cookie)[2], b'index.html')
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/static/item.html')[0], 404)
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', self.base + '/static/../db.py')[0], 404)
```

```python
import datetime
import hashlib
import hmac
import http.cookies
import math
import secrets
import threading
import time

from . import db, settings
from .server import HttpError

COOKIE = 'relume_session'
PUBLIC = {('POST', '/api/auth/login'), ('GET', '/api/health')}
MAX_FAILS, WINDOW, LOCK = 5, 300, 300
REMEMBER, SHORT = 30 * 86400, 12 * 3600
_fails = []
_state = {'locked_until': 0.0}
_lock = threading.Lock()


def _scrypt(pw, salt):
    return hashlib.scrypt(pw.encode(), salt=salt, n=2 ** 14, r=8, p=1, dklen=32).hex()


def hash_password(pw, salt=None):
    salt = salt or secrets.token_bytes(16)
    return f'scrypt${salt.hex()}${_scrypt(pw, salt)}'


def verify_password(pw, stored):
    try:
        _, salt, h = stored.split('$')
        return hmac.compare_digest(_scrypt(pw, bytes.fromhex(salt)), h)
    except (AttributeError, ValueError):
        return False


def set_password(pw):
    settings.put('admin_password_hash', hash_password(pw))


def new_password():
    return secrets.token_urlsafe(15)


def ensure_password():
    if settings.get('admin_password_hash') is None:
        pw = new_password()
        set_password(pw)
        print(f'admin password: {pw}', flush=True)


def reset_lockout():
    with _lock:
        _fails.clear()
        _state['locked_until'] = 0.0


def create_session(remember):
    token = secrets.token_urlsafe(32)
    life = REMEMBER if remember else SHORT
    exp = (datetime.datetime.now(datetime.timezone.utc) + datetime.timedelta(seconds=life)).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE expires_at <= ?', (db.utcnow(),))
        c.execute('INSERT INTO sessions(token, expires_at) VALUES (?, ?)', (token, exp))
    cookie = f'{COOKIE}={token}; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/'
    return token, cookie + (f'; Max-Age={life}' if remember else '')


def session_token(cookie_header):
    if not cookie_header:
        return None
    jar = http.cookies.SimpleCookie()
    try:
        jar.load(cookie_header)
    except http.cookies.CookieError:
        return None
    m = jar.get(COOKIE)
    if not m or not m.value:
        return None
    r = db.conn().execute('SELECT token FROM sessions WHERE token=? AND expires_at > ?',
                          (m.value, db.utcnow())).fetchone()
    return r['token'] if r else None


def login(req):
    now = time.monotonic()
    with _lock:
        if now < _state['locked_until']:
            return 429, {'error': 'locked', 'retry_after': math.ceil(_state['locked_until'] - now)}
    pw = req.json.get('password')
    if not isinstance(pw, str):
        raise HttpError(400, 'password required')
    if verify_password(pw, settings.get('admin_password_hash')):
        reset_lockout()
        _, cookie = create_session(bool(req.json.get('remember', True)))
        return 200, {'ok': True}, {'Set-Cookie': cookie}
    with _lock:
        _fails[:] = [t for t in _fails if now - t < WINDOW] + [now]
        if len(_fails) >= MAX_FAILS:
            _fails.clear()
            _state['locked_until'] = now + LOCK
            return 429, {'error': 'locked', 'retry_after': LOCK}
        return 401, {'error': 'wrong password', 'attempts_left': MAX_FAILS - len(_fails)}


def logout(req):
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE token=?', (req.session,))
    return 200, {'ok': True}, {'Set-Cookie': f'{COOKIE}=; HttpOnly; SameSite=Strict; Path=/; Max-Age=0'}


def change_password(req):
    old, new = req.json.get('old'), req.json.get('new')
    if not isinstance(new, str) or len(new) < 10:
        raise HttpError(400, 'new password must have at least 10 characters')
    if not isinstance(old, str) or not verify_password(old, settings.get('admin_password_hash')):
        raise HttpError(403, 'wrong password')
    set_password(new)
    with db.tx() as c:
        n = c.execute('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE token != ?', (req.session,)).rowcount
    return 200, {'signed_out': n}


ROUTES = [
    ('POST', '/api/auth/login', login),
    ('POST', '/api/auth/logout', logout),
    ('POST', '/api/auth/password', change_password),
]
```

#### Task P1.5: Scheduler — spec forms, next-run, expansion, routes

**Files:**
- Modify (replace stub): `local/webui/scheduler.py`
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_core_scheduler.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `scheduler.OPTION_KEYS`, `scheduler.EXPAND` (dict kind → `fn(target) -> [atomic target]`; tests replace entries), `scheduler.NEEDS_BUILD` (list holding one function; tests replace `[0]`), `scheduler.next_run(spec, after, last_run=None, z=None) -> datetime`, `scheduler.next_runs(spec, after, n=5, last_run=None) -> [datetime]`, `scheduler.create(name, kind, target, spec, options=None, enabled=True) -> int`, `scheduler.fire(row, now) -> [job_id]`, `scheduler.tick(now=None)`, `scheduler.wake()`, `scheduler.start()`; routes `GET/POST /api/schedules`, `PUT/DELETE /api/schedules/{id}`, `POST /api/schedules/preview`, `POST /api/schedules/{id}/run`.
- Consumes: `catalog.resolve_upstreams(target)`, `catalog.resolve_items(target)`, `devices.resolve_devices(target)`, `build.needs_build(item_id)` (all via `importlib` at call time); `jobs.enqueue`; `server.HttpError`, `server.validate_slug`.

**Behavior (spec L309–324, L347):**
- Spec forms only: `{"every":"day","at":"HH:MM"}`, `{"every":"week","days":[0..6],"at":"HH:MM"}` (0 = Monday, non-empty, unique), `{"every":"hours","hours":1..168}`; anything else → 400.
- Targets: `{"all":true}` or exactly one list: upstream `source_ids` (slugs); build `group_ids` (ints ≥ 0, 0 = Ungrouped) or `item_ids` (ints ≥ 1); ota `device_ids` (ints ≥ 1); non-empty → else 400.
- Options: Plan-fixed "schedule option keys"; bool fields must be bool, `retries` int 0–5; upstream `rebuild` and `pull` default true when absent (spec L324, decision Q4); unknown key → 400.
- `next_run`: day/week → first local wall time strictly after `after` (TZ from env `TZ`, default UTC; a non-existent wall time → next existing minute; an ambiguous one → first occurrence); hours → `(last_run or after) + hours` (may be in the past = due now, the single catch-up).
- `fire(row, now)`: expand, apply `only_changed` (build: keep items where `build.needs_build`), enqueue one job per atomic target with `trigger='schedule'`, `schedule_id`, options without `only_changed`; empty expansion → one stdout line `schedule <id> (<name>): target expands to nothing`; then `last_run_at = now`, `next_run_at = next_run(spec, now, last_run=now)`.
- `tick(now)`: fire every enabled schedule with `next_run_at <= now` (an exception in one is printed and the rest continue). Thread: `tick()` at start (missed-run catch-up), then every 30 s or on `wake()`.
- Routes: `GET` → `{"schedules":[{id, name, kind, target, spec, options, enabled, last_run_at, next_run_at, next_runs:[5 ISO], last_counts:{status:n}}]}` (`next_runs` = `[]` when disabled; `last_counts` over jobs of that schedule with `created_at >= last_run_at`, `{}` when never run); `POST` body `{name, kind, target, spec, options?, enabled?}` → 201 `{"id":N}`; `PUT /{id}` same fields, missing ones keep their value, `kind` cannot change (400), recomputes `next_run_at`; `DELETE /{id}` → 200; `POST /preview {spec}` → `{"runs":[5 ISO]}`; `POST /{id}/run` → `{"job_ids":[…]}` via `fire`. Every write calls `wake()`. Unknown id → 404.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_core_scheduler.py'` exit 0 (tests below: day/week/hours next-run; Berlin spring-forward gap and fall-back overlap; missed run fires once; only_changed filter; empty target warning, no job; options validation incl. `mode` rejected for ota; routes incl. preview 5 runs, last_counts, run now).

**Steps:**

```python
import contextlib, datetime as dt, io, json, os, unittest
from unittest import mock
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo
from webui import db, jobs, scheduler
from webui.tests.helpers import DataTestCase, serve, http, login

U = dt.timezone.utc
def T(s): return dt.datetime.strptime(s, '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ').replace(tzinfo=U)
def S(d): return d.strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')


class NextRunTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_day_utc(self):
        z = ZoneInfo('UTC')
        self.assertEqual(S(scheduler.next_run({'every': 'day', 'at': '04:00'}, T('2026-10-19T03:59:00Z'), z=z)), '2026-10-19T04:00:00Z')
        self.assertEqual(S(scheduler.next_run({'every': 'day', 'at': '04:00'}, T('2026-10-19T04:00:00Z'), z=z)), '2026-10-20T04:00:00Z')

    def test_week(self):  # 2026-10-19 is a Monday
        z = ZoneInfo('UTC')
        r = scheduler.next_run({'every': 'week', 'days': [3], 'at': '09:30'}, T('2026-10-19T10:00:00Z'), z=z)
        self.assertEqual(S(r), '2026-10-22T09:30:00Z')

    def test_hours(self):
        spec = {'every': 'hours', 'hours': 6}
        self.assertEqual(S(scheduler.next_run(spec, T('2026-10-19T00:00:00Z'))), '2026-10-19T06:00:00Z')
        self.assertEqual(S(scheduler.next_run(spec, T('2026-10-19T09:00:00Z'), last_run=T('2026-10-19T01:00:00Z'))), '2026-10-19T07:00:00Z')

    def test_dst_berlin(self):
        z = ZoneInfo('Europe/Berlin')
        spec = {'every': 'day', 'at': '02:30'}
        # spring forward 2026-03-29: 02:30 does not exist -> 03:00 CEST = 01:00Z
        self.assertEqual(S(scheduler.next_run(spec, T('2026-03-28T12:00:00Z'), z=z)), '2026-03-29T01:00:00Z')
        self.assertEqual(S(scheduler.next_run(spec, T('2026-03-29T01:00:00Z'), z=z)), '2026-03-30T00:30:00Z')
        # fall back 2026-10-25: 02:30 happens twice -> first (CEST) = 00:30Z
        self.assertEqual(S(scheduler.next_run(spec, T('2026-10-24T12:00:00Z'), z=z)), '2026-10-25T00:30:00Z')

    def test_next_runs_five(self):
        runs = scheduler.next_runs({'every': 'hours', 'hours': 1}, T('2026-10-19T00:00:00Z'))
        self.assertEqual([S(r) for r in runs], [f'2026-10-19T0{h}:00:00Z' for h in range(1, 6)])


class FireTest(DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        patcher = mock.patch.dict(scheduler.EXPAND, {
            'build': lambda t: [{'item_id': i} for i in (1, 2, 3)],
            'upstream': lambda t: [],
            'ota': lambda t: [{'device_id': 1}]})
        patcher.start(); self.addCleanup(patcher.stop)

    def test_missed_run_fires_once(self):
        sid = scheduler.create('n', 'build', {'all': True}, {'every': 'day', 'at': '04:00'})
        db.conn().execute("UPDATE schedules SET next_run_at='2026-10-10T04:00:00Z'")
        now = T('2026-10-19T12:00:00Z')
        scheduler.tick(now)
        scheduler.tick(now)
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute("SELECT count(*) FROM jobs").fetchone()[0], 3)
        row = db.conn().execute('SELECT last_run_at, next_run_at FROM schedules WHERE id=?', (sid,)).fetchone()
        self.assertEqual(tuple(row), ('2026-10-19T12:00:00Z', '2026-10-20T04:00:00Z'))

    def test_only_changed(self):
        sid = scheduler.create('n', 'build', {'all': True}, {'every': 'day', 'at': '04:00'}, {'only_changed': True})
        with mock.patch.object(scheduler, 'NEEDS_BUILD', [lambda i: i == 2]):
            scheduler.fire(db.conn().execute('SELECT * FROM schedules WHERE id=?', (sid,)).fetchone(), T('2026-10-19T12:00:00Z'))
        rows = [json.loads(r[0]) for r in db.conn().execute('SELECT target FROM jobs')]
        self.assertEqual(rows, [{'item_id': 2}])

    def test_empty_target_warns(self):

        out = io.StringIO()

        self.assertIn('target expands to nothing', out.getvalue())
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute('SELECT count(*) FROM jobs').fetchone()[0], 0)

    def test_upstream_defaults_and_rejects(self):
        sid = scheduler.create('pull', 'upstream', {'all': True}, {'every': 'day', 'at': '09:00'})
        opts = json.loads(db.conn().execute('SELECT options FROM schedules WHERE id=?', (sid,)).fetchone()[0])
        self.assertEqual(opts, {'pull': True, 'rebuild': True})
        from webui.server import HttpError

                ('ota', {'device_ids': [1]}, {'every': 'day', 'at': '04:00'}, {'retries': 6}),
                ('build', {'item_ids': []}, {'every': 'day', 'at': '04:00'}, {}),
                ('build', {'all': True}, {'every': 'day', 'at': '24:00'}, {}),
                ('build', {'all': True}, {'every': 'hours', 'hours': 0}, {}),
                ('build', {'all': True}, {'every': 'week', 'days': [], 'at': '04:00'}, {}),
                ('build', {'all': True}, {'every': 'cron', 'expr': '* * * * *'}, {})):
            with self.assertRaises(HttpError):
                scheduler.create('x', kind, target, spec, options)
```
