- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/scheduler.py`

```python
import datetime as dt
import importlib
import json
import os
import re
import threading
import traceback
from zoneinfo import ZoneInfo

from . import db, jobs
from .server import HttpError, validate_slug

UTC = dt.timezone.utc
AT = re.compile(r'^([01]\d|2[0-3]):[0-5]\d$')
OPTION_KEYS = {
    'upstream': {'pull': bool, 'rebuild': bool, 'notify': bool},
    'build': {'only_changed': bool, 'notify': bool},
    'ota': {'retries': int, 'notify': bool},
}
TARGET_KEYS = {'upstream': ('source_ids',), 'build': ('group_ids', 'item_ids'), 'ota': ('device_ids',)}
_mod = lambda n: importlib.import_module('webui.' + n)
EXPAND = {
    'upstream': lambda t: [{'upstream_id': i} for i in _mod('catalog').resolve_upstreams(t)],
    'build': lambda t: [{'item_id': i} for i in _mod('catalog').resolve_items(t)],
    'ota': lambda t: [{'device_id': i} for i in _mod('devices').resolve_devices(t)],
}
NEEDS_BUILD = [lambda item_id: _mod('build').needs_build(item_id)]
_wake = threading.Event()


def _iso(d):
    return d.astimezone(UTC).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ') if d else None


def _parse(s):
    return dt.datetime.strptime(s, '%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ').replace(tzinfo=UTC) if s else None


def _tz():
    return ZoneInfo(os.environ.get('TZ') or 'UTC')


def _local_to_utc(naive, z):
    for _ in range(24 * 60):
        u = naive.replace(tzinfo=z).astimezone(UTC)
        if u.astimezone(z).replace(tzinfo=None) == naive:
            return u
        naive += dt.timedelta(minutes=1)
    raise ValueError('no valid local time')


def next_run(spec, after, last_run=None, z=None):
    if spec['every'] == 'hours':
        return (last_run or after) + dt.timedelta(hours=spec['hours'])
    z = z or _tz()
    hh, mm = map(int, spec['at'].split(':'))
    day0 = after.astimezone(z).date()
    for i in range(9):
        day = day0 + dt.timedelta(days=i)
        if spec['every'] == 'week' and day.weekday() not in spec['days']:
            continue
        u = _local_to_utc(dt.datetime(day.year, day.month, day.day, hh, mm), z)
        if u > after:
            return u
    raise ValueError('no next run')


def next_runs(spec, after, n=5, last_run=None):
    out, t, lr = [], after, last_run
    for _ in range(n):
        t = next_run(spec, t, lr)
        out.append(t)
        lr = t
    return out


def _check_spec(spec):
    ok = isinstance(spec, dict) and (
        (spec.get('every') == 'day' and set(spec) == {'every', 'at'} and isinstance(spec['at'], str) and AT.match(spec['at']))
        or (spec.get('every') == 'week' and set(spec) == {'every', 'days', 'at'} and isinstance(spec['at'], str)
            and AT.match(spec['at']) and isinstance(spec['days'], list) and spec['days']
            and len(set(spec['days'])) == len(spec['days'])
            and all(isinstance(d, int) and not isinstance(d, bool) and 0 <= d <= 6 for d in spec['days']))
        or (spec.get('every') == 'hours' and set(spec) == {'every', 'hours'} and isinstance(spec['hours'], int)
            and not isinstance(spec['hours'], bool) and 1 <= spec['hours'] <= 168))
    if not ok:
        raise HttpError(400, 'invalid spec')
    return spec


def _check_target(kind, t):
    if t == {'all': True}:
        return t
    if not isinstance(t, dict) or len(t) != 1 or next(iter(t)) not in TARGET_KEYS[kind]:
        raise HttpError(400, 'invalid target')
    key, ids = next(iter(t.items()))
    if not isinstance(ids, list) or not ids:
        raise HttpError(400, 'invalid target')
    for v in ids:
        if key == 'source_ids':
            validate_slug(v, 'source id')
        elif isinstance(v, bool) or not isinstance(v, int) or v < (0 if key == 'group_ids' else 1):
            raise HttpError(400, 'invalid target')
    return t


def _check_options(kind, o):
    if not isinstance(o, dict):
        raise HttpError(400, 'invalid options')
    for k, v in o.items():
        typ = OPTION_KEYS[kind].get(k)
        if typ is None or isinstance(v, bool) != (typ is bool) or not isinstance(v, typ):
            raise HttpError(400, f'invalid option {k}')
        if k == 'retries' and not 0 <= v <= 5:
            raise HttpError(400, 'invalid option retries')
    if kind == 'upstream':
        o = {'pull': True, 'rebuild': True, **o}
    return o


def _validate(body, cur=None):
    cur = cur or {}
    kind = body.get('kind', cur.get('kind'))
    if kind not in OPTION_KEYS or (cur and kind != cur['kind']):
        raise HttpError(400, 'invalid kind')
    name = body.get('name', cur.get('name'))
    if not isinstance(name, str) or not name.strip() or len(name) > 100:
        raise HttpError(400, 'invalid name')
    enabled = body.get('enabled', cur.get('enabled', True))
    if not isinstance(enabled, bool):
        raise HttpError(400, 'invalid enabled')
    return {'name': name.strip(), 'kind': kind,
            'target': _check_target(kind, body.get('target', cur.get('target'))),
            'spec': _check_spec(body.get('spec', cur.get('spec'))),
            'options': _check_options(kind, body.get('options', cur.get('options', {}))),
            'enabled': enabled}


def _now():
    return dt.datetime.now(UTC).replace(microsecond=0)


def create(name, kind, target, spec, options=None, enabled=True):
    v = _validate({'name': name, 'kind': kind, 'target': target, 'spec': spec,
                   'options': options or {}, 'enabled': enabled})
    nxt = _iso(next_run(v['spec'], _now())) if v['enabled'] else None
    with db.tx() as c:
        sid = c.execute('INSERT INTO schedules(name,kind,target,spec,options,enabled,next_run_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                        (v['name'], kind, jobs.canon(v['target']), jobs.canon(v['spec']),
                         jobs.canon(v['options']), int(v['enabled']), nxt)).lastrowid
    wake()
    return sid


def fire(row, now):
    kind, opts = row['kind'], json.loads(row['options'])
    targets = EXPAND[kind](json.loads(row['target']))
    if kind == 'build' and opts.get('only_changed'):
        targets = [t for t in targets if NEEDS_BUILD[0](t['item_id'])]
    if not targets:
        print(f"schedule {row['id']} ({row['name']}): target expands to nothing", flush=True)
    job_opts = {k: v for k, v in opts.items() if k != 'only_changed'}
    ids = [jobs.enqueue(kind, t, 'schedule', row['id'], job_opts) for t in targets]
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('UPDATE schedules SET last_run_at=?, next_run_at=? WHERE id=?',
                  (_iso(now), _iso(next_run(json.loads(row['spec']), now, last_run=now)), row['id']))
    return ids


def tick(now=None):
    now = now or _now()
    for row in db.conn().execute('SELECT * FROM schedules WHERE enabled=1 AND next_run_at IS NOT NULL '
                                 'AND next_run_at <= ? ORDER BY id', (_iso(now),)).fetchall():
        try:
            fire(row, now)
        except Exception:
            traceback.print_exc()


def wake():
    _wake.set()


def _loop():
    while True:
        try:
            tick()
        except Exception:
            traceback.print_exc()
        _wake.wait(30)
        _wake.clear()


def start():
    threading.Thread(target=_loop, daemon=True, name='scheduler').start()


def _row(sid):
    r = db.conn().execute('SELECT * FROM schedules WHERE id=?', (sid,)).fetchone()
    if r is None:
        raise HttpError(404, 'schedule not found')
    return r


def _to_json(r, now):
    d = dict(r)
    for k in ('target', 'spec', 'options'):
        d[k] = json.loads(d[k])
    d['enabled'] = bool(d['enabled'])
    d['next_runs'] = [_iso(x) for x in next_runs(d['spec'], now, 5, _parse(d['last_run_at']))] if d['enabled'] else []
    d['last_counts'] = {s: n for s, n in db.conn().execute(
        'SELECT status, count(*) FROM jobs WHERE schedule_id=? AND created_at >= ? GROUP BY status',
        (d['id'], d['last_run_at']))} if d['last_run_at'] else {}
    return d


def list_route(req):
    now = _now()
    return 200, {'schedules': [_to_json(r, now) for r in db.conn().execute('SELECT * FROM schedules ORDER BY id')]}


def create_route(req):
    b = req.json
    return 201, {'id': create(b.get('name'), b.get('kind'), b.get('target'), b.get('spec'),
                               b.get('options'), b.get('enabled', True))}


def update_route(req):
    r = _row(req.params['id'])
    cur = {k: json.loads(r[k]) for k in ('target', 'spec', 'options')}
    cur.update(name=r['name'], kind=r['kind'], enabled=bool(r['enabled']))
    v = _validate(req.json, cur)
    nxt = _iso(next_run(v['spec'], _now(), _parse(r['last_run_at']))) if v['enabled'] else None
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('UPDATE schedules SET name=?, target=?, spec=?, options=?, enabled=?, next_run_at=? WHERE id=?',
                  (v['name'], jobs.canon(v['target']), jobs.canon(v['spec']), jobs.canon(v['options']),
                   int(v['enabled']), nxt, r['id']))
    wake()
    return 200, _to_json(_row(r['id']), _now())


def delete_route(req):
    _row(req.params['id'])
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('DELETE FROM schedules WHERE id=?', (req.params['id'],))
    return 200, {'ok': True}


def preview_route(req):
    return 200, {'runs': [_iso(x) for x in next_runs(_check_spec(req.json.get('spec')), _now())]}


def run_route(req):
    return 200, {'job_ids': fire(_row(req.params['id']), _now())}


ROUTES = [
    ('GET', '/api/schedules', list_route),
    ('POST', '/api/schedules', create_route),
    ('POST', '/api/schedules/preview', preview_route),
    ('PUT', '/api/schedules/{id}', update_route),
    ('DELETE', '/api/schedules/{id}', delete_route),
    ('POST', '/api/schedules/{id}/run', run_route),
]
```

Note: `POST /api/schedules/preview` is listed before `/{id}` patterns; `preview` would not match `{id}/run` anyway, but the router takes the first match, so keep this order.

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/settings.py` (complete the file started in P1.2)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_core_settings.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `settings.get`, `settings.put`, `settings.TOKEN_PATH` (`pathlib.Path('/run/secrets/github_token')`), `settings.github_token()`, `settings.check_token_file()`, `settings.fetch_rate_limit(token) -> (status, body_dict, headers)` (tests replace it), `settings.github_status(recheck=False) -> dict`, `settings.server_info() -> dict`; routes `GET /api/health` (public), `GET /api/settings`, `PUT /api/settings`.
- Consumes: `build.run_retention()` (importlib at call time), `server.VERSION`, `server.STARTED_AT`, `server.HttpError`.

**Behavior (spec L284, L344, L348, L390; Plan-fixed rows "settings values from other owners", "token file check"):**
- `GET /api/health` → exactly `{"ok":true,"app":"Relume","version":"1.0","started_at":<ISO>}`.
- `GET /api/settings` → `{job_history_count (default 500), base_url (default ""), artifact_retention (default 3), github:{state, limit, remaining, reset_at, token_hint, path, expires_at, scopes}, server:{arch, tz, now, network_mode, disk:{total, free, artifacts, apk_cache, upstream, logs, artifacts_count}}}`; `?recheck=1` bypasses the 5-min GitHub cache.
- GitHub `state`: `absent` (token path not a regular file), `rejected` (HTTP 401), `unauthenticated` (empty token), `authenticated`; `token_hint` `****` + last 4 or null; `reset_at` ISO from `resources.core.reset`; `expires_at` = header `github-authentication-token-expiration` or null; `scopes` = header `X-OAuth-Scopes` or null. Network error → `{state, error:"<text>"}` with the other fields null, not cached.
- `PUT /api/settings` accepts only `job_history_count` (int 1–100000), `base_url` (`""` or `^https?://\S+$`, trailing `/` removed), `artifact_retention` (int 1–100); other keys → 400; a changed `artifact_retention` calls `build.run_retention()`; returns the GET body.
- `artifacts_count` = number of `*.apk` files under `DATA/artifacts`; sizes = sum of regular file sizes per directory; `disk.total/free` from `shutil.disk_usage(db.DATA)`.

```python
        self.assertEqual(st, 200)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/settings.py` (complete file)

```python
import datetime
import importlib
import json
import os
import pathlib
import platform
import re
import shutil
import threading
import time
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

from . import db

TOKEN_PATH = pathlib.Path('/run/secrets/github_token')
RATE_URL = 'https://api.github.com/rate_limit'
_gh_cache = {}
_gh_lock = threading.Lock()
LIMITS = {'job_history_count': (1, 100000), 'artifact_retention': (1, 100)}


def get(key, default=None):
    r = db.conn().execute('SELECT value FROM settings WHERE key=?', (key,)).fetchone()
    return json.loads(r['value']) if r else default


def put(key, value):
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('INSERT INTO settings(key, value) VALUES (?, ?) '
                  'ON CONFLICT(key) DO UPDATE SET value=excluded.value', (key, json.dumps(value)))


def github_token():
    try:
        return TOKEN_PATH.read_text().strip() if TOKEN_PATH.is_file() else ''
    except OSError:
        return ''


def check_token_file():
    if TOKEN_PATH.exists() and not TOKEN_PATH.is_file():
        print(f'github token path is not a file: {TOKEN_PATH}', flush=True)


def fetch_rate_limit(token):
    h = {'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json', 'User-Agent': 'relume'}
    if token:
        h['Authorization'] = f'Bearer {token}'
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(RATE_URL, headers=h), timeout=10) as r:
            return r.status, json.load(r), r.headers
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        return e.code, {}, e.headers


def github_status(recheck=False):
    with _gh_lock:
        hit = _gh_cache.get('v')
        if hit and not recheck and time.monotonic() - _gh_cache['at'] < 300:
            return hit
    present = TOKEN_PATH.is_file()
    token = github_token()
    v = dict.fromkeys(('limit', 'remaining', 'reset_at', 'expires_at', 'scopes'))
    v.update(path=str(TOKEN_PATH), token_hint='****' + token[-4:] if token else None)
    try:
        status, body, headers = fetch_rate_limit(token)
    except OSError as e:
        return {**v, 'state': 'absent' if not present else 'unknown', 'error': str(e)}
    core = (body or {}).get('resources', {}).get('core', {})
    v['state'] = ('absent' if not present else 'rejected' if status == 401
                  else 'unauthenticated' if not token else 'authenticated')
    v['limit'], v['remaining'] = core.get('limit'), core.get('remaining')
    if core.get('reset'):
        v['reset_at'] = datetime.datetime.fromtimestamp(core['reset'], datetime.timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ')
    v['expires_at'] = headers.get('github-authentication-token-expiration') if headers else None
    v['scopes'] = headers.get('X-OAuth-Scopes') if headers else None
    with _gh_lock:
        _gh_cache.update(v=v, at=time.monotonic())
    return v


def _size(p):
    return sum(f.stat().st_size for f in p.rglob('*') if f.is_file()) if p.exists() else 0


def server_info():
    d = db.DATA
    u = shutil.disk_usage(d)
    arts = d / 'artifacts'
    return {'arch': platform.machine(), 'tz': os.environ.get('TZ') or 'UTC', 'now': db.utcnow(),
            'network_mode': os.environ.get('RELUME_NETWORK_MODE', ''),
            'disk': {'total': u.total, 'free': u.free, 'artifacts': _size(arts), 'apk_cache': _size(d / 'apk-cache'),
                     'upstream': _size(d / 'upstream'), 'logs': _size(d / 'logs'),
                     'artifacts_count': sum(1 for _ in arts.rglob('*.apk')) if arts.exists() else 0}}


def _payload(recheck=False):
    return {'job_history_count': get('job_history_count', 500), 'base_url': get('base_url', ''),
            'artifact_retention': get('artifact_retention', 3),
            'github': github_status(recheck), 'server': server_info()}


def health(req):
    from .server import STARTED_AT, VERSION
    return 200, {'ok': True, 'app': 'Relume', 'version': VERSION, 'started_at': STARTED_AT}


def get_route(req):
    return 200, _payload(req.query.get('recheck') == '1')


def put_route(req):
    from .server import HttpError
    b = req.json
    if not isinstance(b, dict) or set(b) - {'job_history_count', 'base_url', 'artifact_retention'}:
        raise HttpError(400, 'unknown setting')
    for k, (lo, hi) in LIMITS.items():
        if k in b and (isinstance(b[k], bool) or not isinstance(b[k], int) or not lo <= b[k] <= hi):
            raise HttpError(400, f'invalid {k}')
    if 'base_url' in b and not (b['base_url'] == '' or (isinstance(b['base_url'], str) and re.match(r'^https?://\S+$', b['base_url']))):
        raise HttpError(400, 'invalid base_url')
    old_keep = get('artifact_retention', 3)
    for k, v in b.items():
        put(k, v.rstrip('/') if k == 'base_url' else v)
    if 'artifact_retention' in b and b['artifact_retention'] != old_keep:
        importlib.import_module('webui.build').run_retention()
    return 200, _payload()


ROUTES = [
    ('GET', '/api/health', health),
    ('GET', '/api/settings', get_route),
    ('PUT', '/api/settings', put_route),
]
```

#### Task P1.7: Jobs API and live log (SSE)

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/jobs.py` (add routes and `log_stream`)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_core_sse.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `jobs.log_stream(job_id, offset=0) -> iterator[bytes]`, `jobs.KEEPALIVE = 15`; routes `GET /api/jobs` → `{"jobs":[job JSON with detail]}` (query `kind`, `status`, `item_id`, `device_id`, `before_id`, `limit` ≤ 100, default 50, newest first), `GET /api/jobs/{id}` (with `detail`), `POST /api/jobs/{id}/cancel` → `{"status":"cancelled"|"cancelling"}` (409 when finished, 404 unknown), `GET /api/jobs/{id}/log` (SSE, `Last-Event-ID` resume).
- Consumes: `server.HttpError`, `server.SSEResponse`.

**Behavior (spec L346, L353, L355):** each SSE event = `id: <byte offset after chunk>` + one `data:` line per log line + blank line; only whole lines are sent while the job runs; after the job is finished the rest of the file is sent, then `event: end` / `data: <status>`, then the stream ends; the loop waits on the job's log condition (1 s max per wait) and sends `: keepalive` after 15 s without data. `item_id`/`device_id` filters use `json_extract(target,'$.item_id')` / `'$.device_id'`; non-integer values → 400.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_core_sse.py'` exit 0 (finished job: whole file + end; resume from `Last-Event-ID`; running job streams lines appended after connect then end; list filters `kind`, `status`, `item_id`, `device_id`, `before_id`, `limit`; list rows carry `detail` from the registered detail function; cancel 409/404).

```python
import json, threading, time, urllib.request
from webui import db, jobs
from webui.tests.helpers import DataTestCase, serve, http, login


def read_sse(url, cookie, last_id=None):
    h = {'Cookie': cookie}
    if last_id is not None:
        h['Last-Event-ID'] = str(last_id)
    with urllib.request.urlopen(urllib.request.Request(url, headers=h), timeout=20) as r:
        return r.read().decode()


class JobsApiTest(DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.base = serve(self)
        self.c = login(self.base)

    def test_finished_job_and_resume(self):
        jobs.register('build', lambda job, log: (log.line('one'), log.line('two')))
        jid = jobs.run_inline('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'manual')['id']
        text = read_sse(f'{self.base}/api/jobs/{jid}/log', self.c)
        self.assertRegex(text, r'id: \d+\ndata: \d\d:\d\d:\d\d one\ndata: \d\d:\d\d:\d\d two\n\n')
        self.assertTrue(text.endswith('event: end\ndata: success\n\n'))
        first_line = len((db.DATA / 'logs' / f'{jid}.log').read_text().splitlines()[0]) + 1
        text = read_sse(f'{self.base}/api/jobs/{jid}/log', self.c, last_id=first_line)
        self.assertNotIn(' one', text)
        self.assertIn(' two', text)

    def test_running_job_streams(self):
        go = threading.Event()
        def h(job, log):
            log.line('start')
            go.wait(5)
            log.line('later')
        jobs.register('build', h)
        jobs.start()
        jid = jobs.enqueue('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'manual')
        threading.Timer(1.0, go.set).start()
        text = read_sse(f'{self.base}/api/jobs/{jid}/log', self.c)
        self.assertIn(' start', text)
        self.assertIn(' later', text)
        self.assertTrue(text.endswith('event: end\ndata: success\n\n'))

    def test_list_filters_and_detail(self):
        jobs.register('build', lambda job, log: None, detail=lambda i: {'d': i})
        jobs.register('ota', lambda job, log: None)
        a = jobs.run_inline('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'manual')['id']
        b = jobs.run_inline('build', {'item_id': 2}, 'manual')['id']
        c = jobs.run_inline('ota', {'device_id': 9}, 'manual')['id']
        get = lambda q: [j['id'] for j in json.loads(http('GET', f'{self.base}/api/jobs?{q}', cookie=self.c)[2])['jobs']]
        self.assertEqual(get('kind=build'), [b, a])
        self.assertEqual(get('item_id=1'), [a])
        self.assertEqual(get('device_id=9'), [c])
        self.assertEqual(get(f'before_id={b}'), [a])
        self.assertEqual(get('limit=1'), [c])
        self.assertEqual(get('status=failed'), [])
        rows = json.loads(http('GET', f'{self.base}/api/jobs?kind=build', cookie=self.c)[2])['jobs']
        self.assertEqual(rows[0]['detail'], {'d': b})
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', f'{self.base}/api/jobs?item_id=x', cookie=self.c)[0], 400)

    def test_cancel_errors(self):
        jobs.register('build', lambda job, log: None)
        jid = jobs.run_inline('build', {'item_id': 1}, 'manual')['id']
        self.assertEqual(http('POST', f'{self.base}/api/jobs/{jid}/cancel', {}, cookie=self.c)[0], 409)
        self.assertEqual(http('POST', f'{self.base}/api/jobs/999/cancel', {}, cookie=self.c)[0], 404)
        self.assertEqual(http('GET', f'{self.base}/api/jobs/999', cookie=self.c)[0], 404)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — append to `local/webui/jobs.py`:

```python
KEEPALIVE = 15


def log_stream(job_id, offset=0):
    path = db.DATA / 'logs' / f'{job_id}.log'
    cond = log_condition(job_id)
    last = time.monotonic()
    while True:
        row = db.conn().execute('SELECT status FROM jobs WHERE id=?', (job_id,)).fetchone()
        done = row is None or row['status'] not in ('queued', 'running')
        chunk = b''
        if path.exists():
            with open(path, 'rb') as f:
                f.seek(offset)
                chunk = f.read()
            if not done:
                chunk = chunk[:chunk.rfind(b'\n') + 1]
        if chunk:
            offset += len(chunk)
            lines = chunk.decode('utf-8', 'replace').rstrip('\n').split('\n')
            yield (f'id: {offset}\n' + ''.join(f'data: {x}\n' for x in lines) + '\n').encode()
            last = time.monotonic()
        if done:
            yield f"event: end\ndata: {row['status'] if row else 'missing'}\n\n".encode()
            return
        with cond:
            cond.wait(1)
        if time.monotonic() - last >= KEEPALIVE:
            yield b': keepalive\n\n'
            last = time.monotonic()


def _int(q, key):
    from .server import HttpError
    try:
        return int(q[key])
    except ValueError:
        raise HttpError(400, f'invalid {key}')


def list_route(req):
    q, where, args = req.query, [], []
    for key in ('kind', 'status'):
        if key in q:
            where.append(f'{key}=?')
            args.append(q[key])
    for key in ('item_id', 'device_id'):
        if key in q:
            where.append(f"json_extract(target,'$.{key}')=?")
            args.append(_int(q, key))
    if 'before_id' in q:
        where.append('id<?')
        args.append(_int(q, 'before_id'))
    limit = min(_int(q, 'limit'), 100) if 'limit' in q else 50
    sql = 'SELECT * FROM jobs' + (' WHERE ' + ' AND '.join(where) if where else '') + ' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT ?'
    return 200, {'jobs': [to_json(r) for r in db.conn().execute(sql, (*args, limit))]}


def _row_or_404(job_id):
    from .server import HttpError
    r = db.conn().execute('SELECT * FROM jobs WHERE id=?', (job_id,)).fetchone()
    if r is None:
        raise HttpError(404, 'job not found')
    return r


def get_route(req):
    return 200, to_json(_row_or_404(req.params['id']))


def cancel_route(req):
    from .server import HttpError
    _row_or_404(req.params['id'])
    res = cancel(req.params['id'])
    if res is None:
        raise HttpError(409, 'job already finished')
    return 200, {'status': res}


def log_route(req):
    from .server import SSEResponse
    _row_or_404(req.params['id'])
    try:
        offset = int(req.headers.get('Last-Event-ID') or 0)
    except ValueError:
        offset = 0
    return SSEResponse(log_stream(req.params['id'], offset))


ROUTES = [
    ('GET', '/api/jobs', list_route),

]
```

#### Task P1.8: Start-up, CLI, reset-password

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/__main__.py`, `local/webui/tests/test_core_startup.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `webui.__main__.boot(start=False, names=None) -> [module]` (names `None` = `server.MODULES` read at call time), `webui.__main__.main(argv=None) -> int`; CLI `python3 -m webui [serve|reset-password|ci-plan|ci-build|export-seed]`; `ci.main(argv)` is called after `boot()` with `argv[0]` = the subcommand.
- Consumes: every module's optional `register()`, `start()`; `catalog.seed_import()`; `ci.main(argv)` (round 2).

**Behavior (spec L224, L241, L328–329; Plan-fixed "module loading", "token file check"):**
- `boot`: `db.init()` → `db.migrate()` → `load_modules(names)` → `catalog.seed_import()` when `catalog` is loaded → each `register()`; with `start=True`: `settings.check_token_file()`, `auth.ensure_password()`, `jobs.recover()`, `jobs.start()`, `scheduler.start()`, each module's `start()`.
- `serve`: `boot(start=True)`, `make_server(mods, '0.0.0.0', 47000)`, print `Relume listening on :47000`, `serve_forever()`.
- `reset-password`: `boot()`, new 20-char password, store hash, delete all sessions, print `admin password: <pw>`, exit 0.
- `ci-plan|ci-build|export-seed`: `boot()`, then `from . import ci` and `return ci.main(argv)`.
- Unknown command → usage on stderr, exit 2.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_core_startup.py'` exit 0 (boot without threads; seed hook order before register; boot start=True calls recover, workers, scheduler, then module start in that order; reset-password prints 20-char password and clears sessions; `webui.ci` not imported by boot; unknown command → 2).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `local/webui/tests/test_core_startup.py`

```python
import contextlib, io, sys, threading, types
from unittest import mock
from webui import db, server
from webui.tests.helpers import DataTestCase
import webui.__main__ as cli


class StartupTest(DataTestCase):
    def fake(self, name, calls):
        m = types.ModuleType('webui.' + name)
        m.ROUTES = []
        m.register = lambda: calls.append(name + '.register')
        m.start = lambda: calls.append(name + '.start')
        if name == 'catalog':
            m.seed_import = lambda: calls.append('catalog.seed_import')
        p = mock.patch.dict(sys.modules, {'webui.' + name: m}); p.start(); self.addCleanup(p.stop)
        return name

    def test_boot_order_no_threads(self):
        calls = []
        names = [self.fake('catalog', calls), self.fake('build', calls)]
        before = threading.active_count()
        with mock.patch.dict('os.environ', {'RELUME_DATA': str(self.data)}):
            cli.boot(names=names)
        self.assertEqual(calls, ['catalog.seed_import', 'catalog.register', 'build.register'])
        self.assertEqual(threading.active_count(), before)
        self.assertNotIn('webui.ci', sys.modules)

    def test_boot_start(self):
        calls = []
        names = [self.fake('devices', calls)]
        out = io.StringIO()
        with mock.patch.dict('os.environ', {'RELUME_DATA': str(self.data)}), contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), \
                mock.patch.object(cli.jobs, 'start', lambda: calls.append('jobs.start')), \
                mock.patch.object(cli.jobs, 'recover', lambda: calls.append('jobs.recover')), \
                mock.patch.object(cli.scheduler, 'start', lambda: calls.append('scheduler.start')):
            cli.boot(start=True, names=names)
        self.assertEqual(calls, ['devices.register', 'jobs.recover', 'jobs.start', 'scheduler.start', 'devices.start'])
        self.assertIn('admin password: ', out.getvalue())

    def test_reset_password(self):
        db.conn().execute("INSERT INTO sessions VALUES ('t','2999-01-01T00:00:00Z')")
        out = io.StringIO()
        with mock.patch.object(server, 'MODULES', ()), mock.patch.dict('os.environ', {'RELUME_DATA': str(self.data)}), \
                contextlib.redirect_stdout(out):
            self.assertEqual(cli.main(['reset-password']), 0)
        self.assertRegex(out.getvalue(), r'admin password: \S{20}\n')
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute('SELECT count(*) FROM sessions').fetchone()[0], 0)

    def test_unknown_command(self):
        err = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
            self.assertEqual(cli.main(['bogus']), 2)
        self.assertIn('usage', err.getvalue())
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run** — `T_PY 'test_core_startup.py'` → `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'webui.__main__'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/__main__.py`

```python
import sys

from . import auth, db, jobs, scheduler, server, settings

USAGE = 'usage: python3 -m webui [serve|reset-password|ci-plan|ci-build|export-seed]'


def boot(start=False, names=None):
    db.init()
    db.migrate()
    mods = server.load_modules(server.MODULES if names is None else names)
    by_name = {m.__name__.rsplit('.', 1)[1]: m for m in mods}
    if 'catalog' in by_name:
        by_name['catalog'].seed_import()
    for m in mods:
        if hasattr(m, 'register'):
            m.register()
    if start:
        settings.check_token_file()
        auth.ensure_password()
        jobs.recover()
        jobs.start()
        scheduler.start()
        for m in mods:
            if hasattr(m, 'start'):
                m.start()
    return mods


def main(argv=None):
    argv = list(sys.argv[1:] if argv is None else argv)
    cmd = argv[0] if argv else 'serve'
    if cmd in ('ci-plan', 'ci-build', 'export-seed'):
        boot()
        from . import ci
        return ci.main(argv)
    if cmd == 'reset-password':
        boot()
        pw = auth.new_password()
        auth.set_password(pw)
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute('DELETE FROM sessions')
        print(f'admin password: {pw}', flush=True)
        return 0
    if cmd != 'serve':
        print(USAGE, file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    mods = boot(start=True)
    srv = server.make_server(mods, '0.0.0.0', 47000)
    print('Relume listening on :47000', flush=True)
    srv.serve_forever()
    return 0


if __name__ == '__main__':
    sys.exit(main())
```


| **17 org scripts** | **56** | 55 `get_apk*` pairs + 1 `req` pair |
| `Xposed.sh` | 8 | :18,:26,:29,:36,:39,:46,:53,:56 |
| **total items** | **64** | |

Why preflight-patches said 55: it counted unique `get_patches_key` keys per script. `DeRevanced.sh` uses key `tiktok-derevanced` for two packages (:119 → :120, :123 → :124), so keys = 56 − 1 = 55. Both counts are right for their unit; the seed unit is the pair, so the seed has **64 items** (56 org + 8 Xposed). Spec L482 "≈ 55" is an estimate and needs no change.
Command that reproduces 55 + 1: `for f in src/build/*.sh; do case $f in *utils.sh|*Xposed.sh) continue;; esac; grep -hE '^\s*(get_apk|get_apkpure) ' $f | awk -v f=$f '{print f, $2}'; done | sort -u | wc -l` → `55`; `grep -cE '^\s*req ' src/build/Dropped-Patches-Revanced.sh` → `1`.
Seed quirk: `Dropped-Patches-Revanced.sh:23` calls `get_apk "com.teslacoilsw.launcher" "apk"` (2nd arg is the file stem `apk`, not `nova-launcher`), then `patch "nova-launcher"`; the seed maps the item by package and uses `nova-launcher` as key, and lists the quirk in the seed report.

#### Y2 Offline inspect inputs exist

`local/work/<org>-<run>/` (untracked, kept by user decision) holds the last downloaded CLI and patches of every source, e.g. `local/work/morphe-1/{morphe-desktop-1.18.1-all.jar,patches-1.47.0.mpp,APKEditor.jar}`; `biliroamingm--/` holds `BiliRoamingM-patches-1.23.3.8.jar`, `dropped-patches--/` holds `dropped-patches-1.4.2.jar` (no `.mpp`). `local/out/morphe/youtube-arm64-v8a-morphe.apk` is a real patched APK. So the parser fixtures, the APKEditor `info` capture, the `apksigner` capture and the seed-time inspect (catalog review S2) run **offline** in `relume-builder` with `--network none`, one `docker run` per source (each well under 150 s). No GitHub access is needed for the seed.

#### Y3 Commands used below

Same as the plan header, with `--network none` (catalog review S10, row X5):
- `T_PY <pattern>` = `docker run --rm --network none -v "$PWD:/repo:ro" -e PYTHONPATH=/repo/local -e PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 relume-builder python3 -m unittest discover -s /repo/local/webui/tests -t /repo/local -p '<pattern>'`
- `T_SH <script>` = `docker run --rm --network none -v "$PWD:/repo:ro" -w /repo relume-builder bash <script>`
- `T_COMPOSE <mode>` = `RELUME_NETWORK_MODE=<mode> docker compose --env-file /dev/null -f local/docker-compose.yml config >/dev/null; echo RC=$?`
- Commit: `git add <own files> && git commit -m "<msg>" -- <own files>` (no `;` in the message).

**Files:**

#### Task P3.2: Slim `utils.sh` and the test shims

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/build/utils.sh` (whole file rewritten: keep `_req`, `req`, `detect_version`, `_fs_get`, `_cfb_get`, `_cf_get`, `get_apk`, `get_apkpure`, `split_editor`, log functions; delete the rest)
- Create: `src/build/test-fixtures/bin/java`, `src/build/test-fixtures/bin/apksigner`, `src/build/test-fixtures/bin/wget`, `src/build/test-fixtures/bin/curl`, `src/build/test-fixtures/apkeditor-info.txt` (captured), `src/build/test-fixtures/apksigner-certs.txt` (captured), `src/build/test-fixtures/list-patches.txt` (captured, morphe 1.47.0, YouTube)
- Create: `src/build/test-build-item.sh` (harness + utils cases; runner cases are added in Task P3.3, inspect cases in Task P3.4)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing.
- Produces (bash functions, `source "$RELUME_REPO/src/build/utils.sh"` has no side effects):
  - `init_ua` → sets `user_agent`; offline fallback prints `[!] Using fixed user agent`.
  - `red_log <msg>` also sets global `LAST_ERR=<msg>`.
  - `get_apk <pkg> <name> <type> <arch> <dpi> <minver> <list_url> <example_url> <example_url2>` → `./download/<name>.apk` (or `<name>.apkm` / `<name>/` for `bundle_extract`); returns 1 on failure.
  - `get_apkpure <pkg> <name> <type> <download_url>` → same file rules; returns 1 on failure.
  - `emit_apkm_versions` → prints `::apkm_versions::<v1>,<v2>,…` once per run from `$html` (guard `APKM_EMITTED=1`).
  - `patch_variant <input_apk> <out_apk> <strip_abi or "">` using globals `engine`, `cli_jar`, `patches_files` (array), `keystore`, `RELUME_REPO`; returns 1 on failure; a failure that is not `patch_failed` sets global `FAIL_CODE` (the runner prints `::fail::${FAIL_CODE:-patch_failed}::$LAST_ERR`); `lspatch|npatch` → `FAIL_CODE=internal`, `LAST_ERR="engine not implemented"`, returns 1 (X14).
  - `apk_info <apk>` → prints three lines `package=<p>`, `version_code=<c>`, `version_name=<n>` parsed from `java -jar "$APKEditor" info -i <apk> -package -version-code -version-name`; returns 1 if the package is empty.
  - `apk_signer <apk>` → prints the lowercase SHA-256 hex of signer #1 from `apksigner verify --print-certs`; returns 1 if none.
  - Variables: `pup=pup`, `APKEditor=${RELUME_APKEDITOR:-/opt/APKEditor.jar}`; solver URLs `FLARESOLVERR_URL`, `CFBYPASS_URL`, `APKM_SOLVER_URL` (no `*_PORT`).
- Shims (test only, on `PATH` first): `java` handles `-jar <x> patch … --out=<f> … <in>` (copies `<in>` to `<f>` unless `SHIM_PATCH_FAIL=1`), `-jar <x> list-patches …` (cats `$SHIM_LIST_PATCHES`, default the captured fixture), `-jar <x> options-create … -o <f>` (writes `[{"patches":{}}]` unless `SHIM_EMPTY=1`), `-jar <x> info …` (cats `apkeditor-info.txt`), `-jar <x> m -i <in> -o <out>` (copies); `apksigner` cats `apksigner-certs.txt`; `wget` copies `https://fixture.invalid/<f>` from `$SHIM_SERVE` (a temp dir the test fills, e.g. a generated `input.apk`) to the `-O` target, else exits 4; `curl` exits 7. Every shim appends `<name> <argv>` to `$SHIM_LOG`.

**Behavior:** Spec build §7 L830–835. Deleted: lines 3–19 side effects, `dl_gh`, `dl_gl`, `get_patches_key`, `get_apk_chplay`, `telegram_dl`, `patch`, `patch_multi`, `lspatch`, `npatch`, `archs`/`libs`, `apk_editor`, `split_arch`. `get_apk`/`get_apkpure` take URLs as arguments (no `apps.json`). `get_apk` calls `emit_apkm_versions` right after it fetches the list page (first `_cf_get "$list_url"` and page 1 of the uploads loop), so `latest` builds and fallback lookups report versions with 0 extra requests (spec L784). `init_ua` uses `wget --timeout=10 --tries=1` and falls back to the fixed Firefox UA; the fallback message is `[!]` (not `[-]`) so it never becomes a failure detail.

**Constraints:**
- No `eval`; every expansion quoted; no `exit` in `utils.sh`.
- `apk_info`/`apk_signer` parse the captured real output files (Y2), not a guessed format.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `grep -nE '^(dl_gh|dl_gl|get_patches_key|get_apk_chplay|telegram_dl|apk_editor|patch|patch_multi|split_arch|lspatch|npatch)\(\)' src/build/utils.sh` = 0 lines; `grep -nw eval src/build/utils.sh` = 0; `grep -nE '^\s*exit' src/build/utils.sh` = 0; `grep -n '_PORT' src/build/utils.sh` = 0; `grep -n 'localhost' src/build/utils.sh` = 0; `grep -n apps.json src/build/utils.sh` = 0.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the shims**

`src/build/test-fixtures/bin/java`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Test stand-in for java (spec build §8): replays captured output, copies inputs.
echo "java $*" >> "${SHIM_LOG:-/dev/null}"
[ "$1" = -jar ] || exit 2
shift 2
case "$1" in
patch)
	out="" in=""
	for a in "$@"; do case $a in --out=*) out=${a#--out=} ;; esac; in=$a; done
	if [ "${SHIM_PATCH_FAIL:-0}" = 1 ]; then echo "SEVERE: shim patch failure"; exit 1; fi
	cp "$in" "$out"
	;;
list-patches)
	[ "${SHIM_EMPTY:-0}" = 1 ] && exit 0
	cat "${SHIM_LIST_PATCHES:-$SHIM_FIXTURES/list-patches.txt}"
	;;
options-create)
	out="" prev=""
	for a in "$@"; do [ "$prev" = -o ] && out=$a; prev=$a; done
	[ "${SHIM_EMPTY:-0}" = 1 ] && exit 0
	printf '[{"patches":{}}]\n' > "$out"
	;;
info) cat "$SHIM_FIXTURES/apkeditor-info.txt" ;;
m)
	in="" out="" prev=""
	for a in "$@"; do case $prev in -i) in=$a ;; -o) out=$a ;; esac; prev=$a; done
	out=$(realpath -m "$out")
	if [ -d "$in" ]; then (cd "$in" && zip -qr "$out" .); else cp "$in" "$out"; fi
	;;
*) echo "java shim: unknown command $1" >&2; exit 2 ;;
esac
```

`src/build/test-fixtures/bin/apksigner`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
echo "apksigner $*" >> "${SHIM_LOG:-/dev/null}"
cat "$SHIM_FIXTURES/apksigner-certs.txt"
```

`src/build/test-fixtures/bin/wget`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Serves https://fixture.invalid/<f> from $SHIM_SERVE; every other URL fails (offline test).
echo "wget $*" >> "${SHIM_LOG:-/dev/null}"
out="" url="" prev=""
for a in "$@"; do
	case $prev in -O) out=$a ;; esac
	case $a in https://*|http://*) url=$a ;; esac
	prev=$a
done
case $url in
https://fixture.invalid/*)
	src="$SHIM_SERVE/${url#https://fixture.invalid/}"
	[ -f "$src" ] || exit 8
	if [ -z "$out" ] || [ "$out" = - ]; then cat "$src"; else cp "$src" "$out"; fi
	;;
*) exit 4 ;;
esac
```

`src/build/test-fixtures/bin/curl`:
```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
echo "curl $*" >> "${SHIM_LOG:-/dev/null}"
exit 7
```

Run: `chmod +x src/build/test-fixtures/bin/*`

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the failing test harness `src/build/test-build-item.sh`**

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Runner self-check (spec build §8). Offline: wget/curl/java/apksigner are shims.
# Usage: bash src/build/test-build-item.sh   (exit 0 = all PASS)
set -o pipefail
REPO=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.." && pwd)
FIX=$REPO/src/build/test-fixtures
fails=0
pass() { echo "PASS $1"; }
fail() { echo "FAIL $1: $2"; fails=$((fails + 1)); }

new_env() {
	T=$(mktemp -d)
	export SHIM_LOG=$T/shim.log SHIM_SERVE=$T/serve SHIM_FIXTURES=$FIX
	export PATH="$FIX/bin:$PATH" RELUME_APKEDITOR=$T/APKEditor.jar RELUME_APK_CACHE=$T/cache
	unset SHIM_PATCH_FAIL SHIM_EMPTY SHIM_LIST_PATCHES
	mkdir -p "$SHIM_SERVE" "$T/work" "$T/up" "$T/cache" "$T/m"
	printf '<manifest/>' > "$T/m/AndroidManifest.xml"
	printf 'x' > "$T/m/classes.dex"
	(cd "$T/m" && zip -q "$SHIM_SERVE/input.apk" AndroidManifest.xml classes.dex)
	: > "$T/up/patches-1.0.0.mpp"
	: > "$T/up/morphe-desktop-1.0.0-all.jar"
	: > "$RELUME_APKEDITOR"
}

# make_job <jq filter>: fixture job.json with paths rewritten, plus options.json
make_job() {
	sed -e "s#@WORK@#$T/work#g" -e "s#@UP@#$T/up#g" "$FIX/job.json" | jq "$1" > "$T/job.json"
	printf '[{"patches":{}}]' > "$T/work/options.json"
}

# ---- utils.sh cases ----
new_env
d=$(mktemp -d)
(cd "$d" && source "$REPO/src/build/utils.sh")
if [ -z "$(ls -A "$d")" ]; then pass utils_source_no_side_effects; else fail utils_source_no_side_effects "$(ls -A "$d")"; fi

out=$(cd "$T/work" && source "$REPO/src/build/utils.sh" && init_ua && echo "UA=$user_agent")
if grep -q '\[!\] Using fixed user agent' <<<"$out" && grep -q 'UA=Mozilla/5.0' <<<"$out"; then pass init_ua_offline; else fail init_ua_offline "$out"; fi

out=$(source "$REPO/src/build/utils.sh" && apk_info "$SHIM_SERVE/input.apk")
if grep -qE '^package=[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9_.]+$' <<<"$out" && grep -qE '^version_code=[0-9]+$' <<<"$out" && grep -qE '^version_name=.+' <<<"$out"; then pass apk_info_capture; else fail apk_info_capture "$out"; fi

out=$(source "$REPO/src/build/utils.sh" && apk_signer "$SHIM_SERVE/input.apk")
if grep -qE '^[0-9a-f]{64}$' <<<"$out"; then pass apk_signer_capture; else fail apk_signer_capture "$out"; fi

out=$(cd "$T/work" && mkdir -p release && source "$REPO/src/build/utils.sh" &&
	engine=morphe cli_jar=$T/up/morphe-desktop-1.0.0-all.jar keystore=morphe.keystore RELUME_REPO=$REPO &&
	patches_files=("$T/up/patches-1.0.0.mpp") &&
	patch_variant "$SHIM_SERVE/input.apk" release/x.apk arm64-v8a && echo OK)
log=$(cat "$SHIM_LOG")
if [ "$out" = OK ] && [ -s "$T/work/release/x.apk" ] && grep -q -- "--striplibs arm64-v8a" <<<"$log" &&
	grep -q -- "--options-file options.json" <<<"$log" && grep -q -- "--keystore=$REPO/src/keystore/morphe.keystore" <<<"$log" &&
	grep -q -- "-p $T/up/patches-1.0.0.mpp" <<<"$log" && grep -q -- "--continue-on-error" <<<"$log"; then
	pass patch_variant_argv
else fail patch_variant_argv "$out / $log"; fi

out=$(cd "$T/work" && source "$REPO/src/build/utils.sh" && engine=lspatch &&
	{ patch_variant a b ""; echo "rc=$? code=$FAIL_CODE err=$LAST_ERR"; })
if grep -q 'rc=1 code=internal err=engine not implemented' <<<"$out"; then pass engine_stub; else fail engine_stub "$out"; fi

# ---- runner cases (Task P3.3) ----
# ---- inspect cases (Task P3.4) ----

[ "$fails" -eq 0 ] || { echo "$fails case(s) failed"; exit 1; }
echo "ALL PASS"
```

Run: `T_SH src/build/test-build-item.sh`
Expected: exit 1; `FAIL utils_source_no_side_effects` (old `utils.sh` creates `release`, `download`, `pup.zip`) and `FAIL apk_info_capture` (function not defined).

- [ ] **Step 5: Rewrite `src/build/utils.sh`**

```bash
#!/bin/bash
# Build library sourced by src/build/build-item.sh (spec build §2, §7).
# Sourcing has no side effects. Callers cd into the job work dir; ./download and ./release live there.
# shellcheck disable=SC2034  # variables are read by the runner

pup=pup
APKEditor=${RELUME_APKEDITOR:-/opt/APKEditor.jar}
user_agent='Mozilla/5.0 (Android 16; Mobile; rv:146.0) Gecko/146.0 Firefox/146.0'
LAST_ERR=""
FAIL_CODE=""
APKM_EMITTED=0

#################################################

# Colored output logs
green_log() {
	echo -e "\e[32m$1\e[0m"
}
red_log() {
	LAST_ERR=$1
	echo -e "\e[31m$1\e[0m"
}
yellow_log() {
	echo -e "\e[33m$1\e[0m"
}

# Latest Firefox Android user agent; offline -> keep the fixed one.
init_ua() {
	local ua
	ua=$(wget -qO- --timeout=10 --tries=1 https://www.whatismybrowser.com/guides/the-latest-user-agent/firefox | tr '\n' ' ' | sed 's#</tr>#\n#g' | grep 'Firefox (Standard)' | sed -n 's/.*<span class="code">\([^<]*Android[^<]*\)<\/span>.*/\1/p')
	if [[ -n "$ua" ]]; then user_agent=$ua; else yellow_log "[!] Using fixed user agent"; fi
}

#################################################

_req() {
	local cookie_args=()
	[[ -n "$FS_COOKIES" ]] && cookie_args=(--header "Cookie: $FS_COOKIES")
	local out="./download/$2"
	[[ "$2" = "-" ]] && out="-"
	wget -nv -O "$out" --header="User-Agent: $user_agent" --header="Content-Type: application/octet-stream" --header="Accept-Language: en-US,en;q=0.9" --header="Connection: keep-alive" --header="Upgrade-Insecure-Requests: 1" --header="Cache-Control: max-age=0" --header="Accept: text/html,application/xhtml+xml,application/xml;q=0.9,image/webp,image/apng,*/*;q=0.8" "${cookie_args[@]}" --keep-session-cookies --timeout=30 "$1" || { [[ "$out" != "-" ]] && rm -f "$out"; return 1; }
}
req() {
	_req "$1" "$2"
}

# Highest compatible version of package $1 across the *.mpp in the work dir.
detect_version() {
	if [[ -z "$version" && "$lock_version" != "1" ]]; then
		local jars=(morphe-desktop-*.jar) mpps=(*.mpp)
		[[ -f "${jars[0]}" && -f "${mpps[0]}" ]] || return 0
		version=$(java -jar "${jars[0]}" list-patches --with-packages --with-versions --with-options --patches "${mpps[@]}" | awk -v pkg="$1" '
			BEGIN { found = 0; printing = 0 }
			/^Index:/ { if (printing) exit; found = 0 }
			/Package name: / { if ($3 == pkg) found = 1 }
			/Compatible versions:/ { if (found) printing = 1; next }
			printing && $1 ~ /^[0-9]+\./ { print $1 }
		' | sort -V | tail -n1)
	fi
}

_fs_get() {
	local url=$1
	local max_retries=3
	local attempt
	for attempt in $(seq 1 $max_retries); do
		local response
		response=$(curl -s -X POST "${FLARESOLVERR_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:47191}/v1" \
			-H 'Content-Type: application/json' \
			-d "{\"cmd\":\"request.get\",\"url\":\"$url\",\"maxTimeout\":15000}")
		local status
		status=$(echo "$response" | jq -r '.status // empty' 2>/dev/null)
		if [[ "$status" == "ok" ]]; then
			html=$(echo "$response" | jq -r '.solution.response // empty')
			export FS_COOKIES
			FS_COOKIES=$(echo "$response" | jq -r '[.solution.cookies[] | .name + "=" + .value] | join("; ")')
			user_agent=$(echo "$response" | jq -r '.solution.userAgent // empty')
			return 0
		fi
		yellow_log "[!] FlareSolverr attempt $attempt/$max_retries failed: $url"
		sleep 5
	done
	red_log "[-] FlareSolverr failed after $max_retries attempts: $url"
	return 1
}

_cfb_get() {
	local url=$1
	local base=${CFBYPASS_URL:-http://127.0.0.1:47800}
	local max_retries=3
	local attempt
	for attempt in $(seq 1 $max_retries); do
		local response_file http_code
		response_file=$(mktemp)
		http_code=$(curl -s -o "$response_file" -w '%{http_code}' -G --data-urlencode "url=$url" --max-time 120 "$base/html")
		if [[ "$http_code" == "200" ]]; then
			html=$(cat "$response_file")
			rm -f "$response_file"
			if [[ -n "$html" ]]; then
				export FS_COOKIES=""
				local ck cfb_ck cfb_ua
				# /cookies JSON: {"cookies": {k: v}, "user_agent": "..."}
				ck=$(curl -sf --max-time 120 -G --data-urlencode "url=$url" "$base/cookies")
				cfb_ck=$(jq -r '.cookies // {} | to_entries | map(.key + "=" + (.value|tostring)) | join("; ")' <<<"$ck" 2>/dev/null)
				cfb_ua=$(jq -r '.user_agent // ""' <<<"$ck" 2>/dev/null)
				# empty UA -> 403, so only use cookies + UA together
				if [[ -n "$cfb_ck" && -n "$cfb_ua" ]]; then
					FS_COOKIES="$cfb_ck"
					user_agent="$cfb_ua"
				fi
				return 0
			fi
		else
			rm -f "$response_file"
			yellow_log "[!] CFB attempt $attempt/$max_retries: HTTP $http_code: $url"
		fi
	done
	red_log "[-] CFBypass failed after $max_retries attempts: $url"
	return 1
}

_FFS_FAILED=0

_cf_get() {
	if [[ "$_FFS_FAILED" -eq 0 ]]; then
		_fs_get "$@" && return 0
		yellow_log "[!] FlareSolverr failed, falling back to CFB"
		_FFS_FAILED=1
	fi
	_cfb_get "$@"
}

# Print ::apkm_versions:: once per run from the list page in $html (spec build §4 step 5d).
emit_apkm_versions() {
	[[ "$APKM_EMITTED" -eq 1 || -z "$html" ]] && return 0
	APKM_EMITTED=1
	local v
	v=$(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'h5.appRowTitle a.fontBlack json{}' | \
		jq -r '.[] | select(.text | test("(?i)beta|alpha") | not) | .text' 2>/dev/null | \
		awk '{print $NF}' | grep -E '^[0-9]' | awk '!seen[$0]++' | paste -sd, -)
	[[ -n "$v" ]] && echo "::apkm_versions::$v"
	return 0
}

# get_apk <pkg> <name> <type> <arch> <dpi> <minver> <list_url> <example_url> <example_url2>
get_apk() {
	local pkg_name=$1 apk_name=$2
	local pkg_type=${3:-apk} arch=${4:-} dpi=${5:-} minver=${6:-}
	local list_url=${7:-}
	local allow_near_version=${near_version:-0}
	unset near_version
	local base_url="https://www.apkmirror.com"
	html=""

	local example_urls=()
	[[ -n "${8:-}" ]] && example_urls+=("$8")
	[[ -n "${9:-}" ]] && example_urls+=("$9")

	if [[ -z "$list_url" ]]; then
		red_log "[-] Package $pkg_name has no APKMirror list URL"
		return 1
	fi

	local base_apk
	if [[ "$pkg_type" == "bundle" ]] || [[ "$pkg_type" == "bundle_extract" ]]; then
		base_apk="$apk_name.apkm"
	else
		base_apk="$apk_name.apk"
	fi

	detect_version "$pkg_name"
	export version

	green_log "[+] Detected version: ${version:-latest} [$pkg_name]"
	green_log "[+] Downloading $apk_name (type=$pkg_type arch=${arch:-any} dpi=$dpi)"

	local version_href=""

	if [[ ${#example_urls[@]} -gt 0 && -n "$version" ]]; then
		local example_url slug_ver target_ver
		for example_url in "${example_urls[@]}"; do
			version_href="${example_url#"$base_url"}"
			slug_ver=$(echo "$version_href" | grep -oP '\d+(-\d+)+' | tail -1)
			target_ver=$(echo "$version" | tr '.' '-' | grep -oP '\d+(-\d+)+')
			if [[ -n "$slug_ver" ]]; then
				version_href="${version_href/$slug_ver/$target_ver}"
			fi

			echo "$base_url$version_href"
			if _cf_get "$base_url$version_href" && [[ "$html" != *"Page Not Found"* && "$html" != *"404 Whoops"* ]]; then
				break
			fi
			version_href=""
		done
	fi

	if [[ -z "$version_href" ]]; then
		_cf_get "$list_url" || return 1
		emit_apkm_versions

		version_href=$(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'h5.appRowTitle a.fontBlack json{}' | \
			jq -r '.[] | select(.text | test("(?i)beta|alpha") | not) | .href' | head -1)
		[[ -z "$version_href" ]] && \
			version_href=$(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'h5.appRowTitle a.fontBlack attr{href}' | head -1)

		if [[ -z "$version_href" ]]; then
			red_log "[-] Could not find release on APKMirror"
			return 1
		fi

		if [[ -n "$version" ]]; then
			local slug_ver target_ver
			slug_ver=$(echo "$version_href" | grep -oP '\d+(-\d+)+' | tail -1)
			target_ver=$(echo "$version" | tr '.' '-' | grep -oP '\d+(-\d+)+')
			if [[ -n "$slug_ver" ]]; then
				version_href="${version_href/$slug_ver/$target_ver}"
			fi
		fi

		echo "$base_url$version_href"

		_cf_get "$base_url$version_href" || return 1
	fi

	if [[ "$html" == *"Page Not Found"* ]] || [[ "$html" == *"404 Whoops"* ]]; then
		yellow_log "[!] Version page not found, searching uploads pages..."
		local target_ver_dot="$version"
		local near_version_href="" near_version_value=""
		version_href=""
		local page_num
		for page_num in $(seq 1 10); do
			local page_url="$list_url"
			if [[ $page_num -gt 1 ]]; then
				page_url="${list_url%%\?*}/page/$page_num/?${list_url#*\?}"
			fi
			_cf_get "$page_url" || return 1
			[[ $page_num -eq 1 ]] && emit_apkm_versions
			if [[ -n "$target_ver_dot" ]]; then
				version_href=$(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'h5.appRowTitle a.fontBlack json{}' | \
					jq -r --arg v "$target_ver_dot" '.[] | select(.text | contains($v)) | .href' | head -1)
				if [[ -z "$version_href" && "$allow_near_version" == "1" ]]; then
					local release_text release_href release_version
					while IFS=$'\t' read -r release_text release_href; do
						release_version=$(echo "$release_text" | grep -oP '\d+(\.\d+)+' | tail -1)
						[[ -z "$release_version" ]] && continue
						if [[ "$(printf '%s\n' "$release_version" "$target_ver_dot" | sort -V | tail -n1)" == "$target_ver_dot" && "$release_version" != "$target_ver_dot" ]]; then
							if [[ -z "$near_version_value" || "$(printf '%s\n' "$near_version_value" "$release_version" | sort -V | tail -n1)" == "$release_version" ]]; then
								near_version_value="$release_version"
								near_version_href="$release_href"
							fi
						fi
					done < <(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'h5.appRowTitle a.fontBlack json{}' | \
						jq -r '.[] | select(.text | test("(?i)beta|alpha") | not) | [.text, .href] | @tsv')
				fi
			else
				version_href=$(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'h5.appRowTitle a.fontBlack json{}' | \
					jq -r '.[] | select(.text | test("(?i)beta|alpha") | not) | .href' | head -1)
			fi
			[[ -n "$version_href" ]] && break
		done
		if [[ -z "$version_href" && -n "$near_version_href" ]]; then
			version_href="$near_version_href"
			version="$near_version_value"
			export version
			yellow_log "[!] Exact version $target_ver_dot unavailable; using nearest lower version $version"
		fi
		if [[ -z "$version_href" ]]; then
			red_log "[-] Could not find version on APKMirror"
			return 1
		fi
		echo "$base_url$version_href"
		_cf_get "$base_url$version_href" || return 1
	fi

	local type_badge="APK"
	[[ "$pkg_type" == "bundle" || "$pkg_type" == "bundle_extract" ]] && type_badge="BUNDLE"

	local vtable_html rows variant_href=""
	vtable_html=$(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'div.variants-table')
	rows=$(echo "$vtable_html" | tr '\n' ' ' | sed 's/<div class="table-row/\n<div class="table-row/g')

	local dpi_fallback=("120-640dpi" "120-480dpi" "480-640dpi" "480dpi")
	local type_attempts=("$type_badge")
	if [[ "$type_badge" == "BUNDLE" ]]; then
		type_attempts+=("APK")
	else
		type_attempts+=("BUNDLE")
	fi

	local matched_type=""
	local variant_page_loaded=false
	local try_type
	for try_type in "${type_attempts[@]}"; do
		local filtered_rows
		filtered_rows=$(echo "$rows" | grep -iP "apkm-badge[^>]*>\s*$try_type\s*<")
		[[ -n "$arch" ]] && filtered_rows=$(echo "$filtered_rows" | grep -iF "$arch")
		[[ -n "$minver" ]] && filtered_rows=$(echo "$filtered_rows" | grep -iF "$minver")

		if [[ -n "$dpi" ]]; then
			local dpi_filtered fb_dpi
			dpi_filtered=$(echo "$filtered_rows" | grep -iF "$dpi")
			if [[ -z "$dpi_filtered" ]]; then
				for fb_dpi in "${dpi_fallback[@]}"; do
					dpi_filtered=$(echo "$filtered_rows" | grep -iF "$fb_dpi")
					[[ -n "$dpi_filtered" ]] && { yellow_log "[!] DPI fallback: $dpi -> $fb_dpi"; break; }
				done
			fi
			filtered_rows="$dpi_filtered"
		fi

		variant_href=$(echo "$filtered_rows" | grep -oP 'accent_color[^>]*href="\K[^"]+' | head -1)
		if [[ -n "$variant_href" ]]; then
			matched_type="$try_type"
			[[ "$try_type" != "$type_badge" ]] && yellow_log "[!] Type fallback: $type_badge -> $try_type"
			break
		fi
	done

	if [[ -z "$variant_href" ]]; then
		local direct_apk_href
		direct_apk_href=$(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'a.downloadButton attr{href}' | grep 'forcebaseapk' | head -1)
		if [[ -n "$direct_apk_href" && -z "$arch" && -z "$dpi" && -z "$minver" ]]; then
			matched_type="APK"
			variant_page_loaded=true
			[[ "$type_badge" != "APK" ]] && yellow_log "[!] Type fallback: $type_badge -> APK"
			yellow_log "[!] APKMirror redirected directly to the APK download page"
		else
			red_log "[-] Could not find variant (type=$type_badge arch=${arch:-any} dpi=$dpi)"
			return 1
		fi
	fi
	if [[ "$variant_page_loaded" == false ]]; then
		variant_href=$(echo "$variant_href" | sed 's/&amp;/\&/g')
		echo "$base_url$variant_href"
	fi

	if [[ "$matched_type" == "BUNDLE" ]]; then
		base_apk="$apk_name.apkm"
	else
		base_apk="$apk_name.apk"
	fi

	if [[ "$variant_page_loaded" == false ]]; then
		_cf_get "$base_url$variant_href" || return 1
	fi

	local dl_btn_href all_dl_btns
	all_dl_btns=$(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'a.downloadButton attr{href}')
	if [[ "$matched_type" == "BUNDLE" ]]; then
		dl_btn_href=$(echo "$all_dl_btns" | grep -v 'forcebaseapk' | head -1)
		[[ -z "$dl_btn_href" ]] && dl_btn_href=$(echo "$all_dl_btns" | head -1)
	else
		dl_btn_href=$(echo "$all_dl_btns" | grep 'forcebaseapk' | head -1)
		[[ -z "$dl_btn_href" ]] && dl_btn_href=$(echo "$all_dl_btns" | head -1)
	fi

	if [[ -z "$dl_btn_href" ]]; then
		red_log "[-] Could not find download button"
		return 1
	fi
	dl_btn_href=$(echo "$dl_btn_href" | sed 's/&amp;/\&/g')

	_cf_get "$base_url$dl_btn_href" || return 1

	local final_href
	final_href=$(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'a#download-link attr{href}' | head -1)
	[[ -z "$final_href" ]] && \
		final_href=$(echo "$html" | grep -oP 'id="download-link"[^>]*href="\K[^"]+' | head -1)

	if [[ -z "$final_href" ]]; then
		red_log "[-] Could not find final download link"
		return 1
	fi
	final_href=$(echo "$final_href" | sed 's/&amp;/\&/g')

	echo "$base_url$final_href"
	local cookie_args=()
	[[ -n "$FS_COOKIES" ]] && cookie_args=(--header "Cookie: $FS_COOKIES")
	wget -nv -O "./download/$base_apk" \
		--header="User-Agent: $user_agent" \
		--referer="$base_url$dl_btn_href" \
		"${cookie_args[@]}" \
		--timeout=120 \
		"$base_url$final_href"
	local wget_rc=$?

	if [[ $wget_rc -ne 0 && -n "${APKM_SOLVER_URL:-}" ]]; then
		yellow_log "[!] Download blocked, asking Turnstile solver"
		local sol
		sol=$(curl -sf --max-time 150 -G --data-urlencode "url=$base_url$final_href" \
			--data-urlencode "referer=$base_url$dl_btn_href" "$APKM_SOLVER_URL/clearance")
		if [[ -n "$sol" ]]; then
			wget -nv -O "./download/$base_apk" --header="User-Agent: $(jq -r .ua <<<"$sol")" \
				--header="Cookie: $(jq -r .cookies <<<"$sol")" --referer="$base_url$dl_btn_href" \
				--timeout=120 "$base_url$final_href"
			wget_rc=$?
		fi
	fi

	if [[ $wget_rc -eq 0 && -s "./download/$base_apk" ]]; then
		green_log "[+] Successfully downloaded $apk_name"
	else
		rm -f "./download/$base_apk"
		red_log "[-] Failed to download $apk_name"
		return 1
	fi

	if [[ "$matched_type" == "BUNDLE" ]]; then
		if [[ "$pkg_type" == "bundle_extract" && "$type_badge" == "BUNDLE" ]]; then
			unzip "./download/$base_apk" -d "./download/$(basename "$base_apk" .apkm)" > /dev/null 2>&1
		else
			green_log "[+] Merge splits apk to standalone apk"
			java -jar "$APKEditor" m -i "./download/$apk_name.apkm" -o "./download/$apk_name.apk" > /dev/null 2>&1
		fi
	fi
}

# get_apkpure <pkg> <name> <type> <download_url>
get_apkpure() {
	local pkg_name=$1 apk_name=$2 pkg_type=${3:-apk} base_download_url=${4:-}
	html=""

	if [[ -z "$base_download_url" ]]; then
		red_log "[-] Package $pkg_name has no APKPure URL"
		return 1
	fi

	detect_version "$pkg_name"
	export version

	local base_apk
	if [[ "$pkg_type" == "bundle" ]] || [[ "$pkg_type" == "bundle_extract" ]]; then
		base_apk="$apk_name.xapk"
	else
		base_apk="$apk_name.apk"
	fi

	local dl_page_url
	if [[ -n "$version" ]]; then
		dl_page_url="${base_download_url%/}/$version"
	else
		dl_page_url="$base_download_url"
	fi

	green_log "[+] Downloading $apk_name from APKPure (type=$pkg_type)"
	echo "$dl_page_url"

	_cf_get "$dl_page_url" || return 1

	if [[ -z "$version" ]]; then
		version=$(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'h2 text{}' | grep -oP '\d+(\.\d+)+' | head -1)
	fi
	green_log "[+] Version: $version"

	local download_url
	download_url=$(echo "$html" | "$pup" 'a#download_link attr{href}' | head -1)
	[[ -z "$download_url" ]] && \
		download_url=$(echo "$html" | grep -oP '<a[^>]+id="download_link"[^>]+href="\Khttps://[^"]+' | head -1)

	if [[ -z "$download_url" ]]; then
		red_log "[-] Could not find download link on APKPure"
		return 1
	fi
	echo "$download_url"

	local cookie_args=()
	[[ -n "$FS_COOKIES" ]] && cookie_args=(--header "Cookie: $FS_COOKIES")
	wget -nv -O "./download/$base_apk" \
		--header="User-Agent: $user_agent" \
		--referer="$dl_page_url" \
		"${cookie_args[@]}" \
		--timeout=120 \
		"$download_url"
	local wget_rc=$?

	if [[ $wget_rc -eq 0 && -s "./download/$base_apk" ]]; then
		green_log "[+] Successfully downloaded $apk_name"
	else
		rm -f "./download/$base_apk"
		red_log "[-] Failed to download $apk_name"
		return 1
	fi
	if [[ "$pkg_type" == "bundle" ]]; then
		if unzip -l "./download/$base_apk" 2>/dev/null | grep -q '\.apk$'; then
			green_log "[+] Merge splits apk to standalone apk"
			if ! java -jar "$APKEditor" m -i "./download/$apk_name.xapk" -o "./download/$apk_name.apk" > /dev/null 2>&1; then
				red_log "[-] Failed to merge $apk_name.xapk to standalone apk"
				return 1
			fi
		elif unzip -l "./download/$base_apk" 2>/dev/null | grep -q 'AndroidManifest.xml'; then
			green_log "[+] File is already a standalone APK, renaming"
			mv "./download/$base_apk" "./download/$apk_name.apk"
		else
			red_log "[-] Unknown file format for $base_apk"
			return 1
		fi
	elif [[ "$pkg_type" == "bundle_extract" ]]; then
		unzip "./download/$base_apk" -d "./download/$(basename "$base_apk" .xapk)" > /dev/null 2>&1
	fi
}

#################################################

# patch_variant <input_apk> <out_apk> <strip_abi or "">; globals: engine cli_jar patches_files keystore RELUME_REPO
patch_variant() {
	local in=$1 out=$2 strip=${3:-}
	case "$engine" in
	morphe)
		local args=(-jar "$cli_jar" patch) f
		for f in "${patches_files[@]}"; do args+=(-p "$f"); done
		args+=(--options-file options.json "--keystore=$RELUME_REPO/src/keystore/$keystore" --force --continue-on-error)
		[[ -n "$strip" ]] && args+=(--striplibs "$strip")
		args+=("--out=$out" "$in")
		java "${args[@]}" || { red_log "[-] Patch failed: $out"; return 1; }
		;;
	lspatch|npatch)
		FAIL_CODE=internal
		LAST_ERR="engine not implemented"
		return 1
		;;
	*)
		FAIL_CODE=internal
		LAST_ERR="unknown engine $engine"
		return 1
		;;
	esac
	[[ -s "$out" ]] || { red_log "[-] Patch produced no file: $out"; return 1; }
}

# apk_info <apk> -> package=, version_code=, version_name= lines (APKEditor info, layout from the captured fixture)
apk_info() {
	local raw pkg code name
	raw=$(java -jar "$APKEditor" info -i "$1" -package -version-code -version-name 2>/dev/null | tr -d '\r"')
	pkg=$(grep -iE '^[[:space:]]*package' <<<"$raw" | head -1 | sed -E 's/^[^=:]*[=:][[:space:]]*//')
	code=$(grep -iE 'version[-_ ]?code' <<<"$raw" | head -1 | sed -E 's/^[^=:]*[=:][[:space:]]*//')
	name=$(grep -iE 'version[-_ ]?name' <<<"$raw" | head -1 | sed -E 's/^[^=:]*[=:][[:space:]]*//')
	[[ -n "$pkg" ]] || return 1
	printf 'package=%s\nversion_code=%s\nversion_name=%s\n' "$pkg" "$code" "$name"
}

# apk_signer <apk> -> lowercase SHA-256 of signer #1
apk_signer() {
	apksigner verify --print-certs "$1" 2>/dev/null | \
		sed -nE 's/^Signer #1 certificate SHA-256 digest: ([0-9a-fA-F]+)$/\1/p' | head -1 | tr 'A-F' 'a-f' | grep .
}

split_editor() {
	if [[ -z "$3" || -z "$4" ]]; then
		green_log "[+] Merge splits apk to standalone apk"
		java -jar "$APKEditor" m -i "./download/$1" -o "./download/$1.apk" > /dev/null 2>&1
		return 0
	fi
	local include_files file filename basename_no_ext
	IFS=' ' read -r -a include_files <<< "$4"
	mkdir -p "./download/$2"
	for file in "./download/$1"/*.apk; do
		filename=$(basename "$file")
		basename_no_ext="${filename%.apk}"
		if [[ "$filename" == "base.apk" ]]; then
			cp -f "$file" "./download/$2/" > /dev/null 2>&1
			continue
		fi
		local listed=0
		case " ${include_files[*]} " in *" $basename_no_ext "*) listed=1 ;; esac
		if [[ "$3" == "include" && $listed -eq 1 ]] || [[ "$3" == "exclude" && $listed -eq 0 ]]; then
			cp -f "$file" "./download/$2/" > /dev/null 2>&1
		fi
	done

	green_log "[+] Merge splits apk to standalone apk"
	java -jar "$APKEditor" m -i "./download/$2" -o "./download/$2.apk" > /dev/null 2>&1
}
```

#### Task P3.3: Runner `build-item.sh`

**Files:**
- Create: `src/build/build-item.sh`
- Modify: `src/build/test-build-item.sh` (runner cases)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task P3.2 functions; `job.json` (Task P3.1 shape); `options.json` in the work dir (written by P4 for morphe items); env X10.
- Produces: CLI `bash src/build/build-item.sh <job.json>`; stdout markers `::step::version`, `::step::download`, `::step::patch::<i>/<n>`, `::step::verify`, `::version::<v>`, `::apkm_versions::…`, exactly one `::fail::<code>::<detail>` on failure (codes `version_unresolved`, `apk_not_listed`, `download_failed`, `merge_failed`, `patch_failed`, `internal`); `<work_dir>/result.json` = `{app_version, patches_version, cli_version, variants:[{name, abi, file, size, sha256, package, version_name, version_code, signer_sha256}]}` with `file` relative to `work_dir` (X11), `version_code` a JSON number; function `keystore_creds <file>` (round-1 stub, returns 1, X14).

**Behavior:** Spec build §4 step 5 L780–788. Steps: read `job.json` with `jq`; `cd` work dir; `init_ua`; symlink `cli_jar` and `patches_files` into the work dir; version (pinned → value; latest → empty + `lock_version=1`; auto → `detect_version`, empty → `version_unresolved`; `near` → `near_version=1`); download each distinct `download` object once as `download/in-<n>` (apkmirror without `list_url` → `apk_not_listed`; failure → `download_failed` with `LAST_ERR`); APK cache `$RELUME_APK_CACHE/<package>/<version>-<type>-<arch>-<dpi>.<apk|apkm>` used only when the version is non-empty and `near` is false; `versions_due=true` + apkmirror + not yet emitted → one extra list-page fetch after the download, failure only `[!]`; per variant optional `split_editor` (`[]` = merge all, list = exclude) → no APK = `merge_failed`; `patch_variant` → `release/<name>.apk`, missing = `patch_failed`; verify: `apk_info` (empty package → `patch_failed`), `apk_signer`, `sha256sum`, size; write `result.json`. `app_version` = resolved `version`, else the first variant's `version_name`; `::version::` printed as soon as it is known.

**Constraints:**
- `set -o pipefail`, no `set -u` (`utils.sh` reads unset vars), no `eval`, all expansions quoted.
- The runner never calls GitHub; it never reads `GITHUB_TOKEN`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_SH src/build/test-build-item.sh` additionally prints `PASS runner_ok`, `PASS runner_missing_input`, `PASS runner_no_list_page`, `PASS runner_patch_failed`, `PASS runner_version_unresolved`, `PASS runner_apk_not_listed`, `PASS runner_engine_stub`, `PASS runner_cache_hit`, exit 0.
- [ ] `shellcheck -S warning src/build/build-item.sh` exit 0; `grep -nw eval src/build/build-item.sh` = 0; `grep -n GITHUB_TOKEN src/build/build-item.sh` = 0.

**Steps:**

```bash
# ---- runner cases (Task P3.3) ----
run_runner() { (bash "$REPO/src/build/build-item.sh" "$T/job.json" > "$T/out.txt" 2>&1); echo $?; }
fail_lines() { grep -c '^::fail::' "$T/out.txt"; }

new_env; make_job '.'
rc=$(run_runner)
r=$T/work/result.json
if [ "$rc" = 0 ] && [ "$(jq '.variants|length' "$r")" = 2 ] &&
	[ "$(jq -r '.variants[0].file' "$r")" = release/universal.apk ] &&
	[ -s "$T/work/release/universal.apk" ] && [ -s "$T/work/release/arm64-v8a.apk" ] &&
	[ "$(jq -r '.app_version' "$r")" = 1.0.0 ] && [ "$(jq -r '.patches_version' "$r")" = v1.2.3 ] &&
	jq -e '.variants[0].package|length>0' "$r" >/dev/null && jq -e '.variants[0].version_code|type=="number"' "$r" >/dev/null &&
	jq -re '.variants[0].signer_sha256' "$r" | grep -qE '^[0-9a-f]{64}$' &&
	grep -q '^::step::patch::2/2$' "$T/out.txt" && grep -q '^::version::1.0.0$' "$T/out.txt" &&
	[ "$(grep -c -- '--striplibs arm64-v8a' "$SHIM_LOG")" = 1 ]; then
	pass runner_ok
else fail runner_ok "rc=$rc $(tail -5 "$T/out.txt")"; fi

new_env; make_job '.'; rm "$SHIM_SERVE/input.apk"
rc=$(run_runner)
if [ "$rc" != 0 ] && [ "$(fail_lines)" = 1 ] && grep -q '^::fail::download_failed::' "$T/out.txt"; then pass runner_missing_input; else fail runner_missing_input "rc=$rc $(tail -3 "$T/out.txt")"; fi

new_env; make_job '.'
rc=$(run_runner)
if [ "$rc" = 0 ] && ! grep -q 'apkmirror.com/uploads' "$SHIM_LOG"; then pass runner_no_list_page; else fail runner_no_list_page "rc=$rc"; fi

new_env; make_job '.'; export SHIM_PATCH_FAIL=1
rc=$(run_runner); unset SHIM_PATCH_FAIL
if [ "$rc" != 0 ] && [ "$(fail_lines)" = 1 ] && grep -q '^::fail::patch_failed::' "$T/out.txt"; then pass runner_patch_failed; else fail runner_patch_failed "rc=$rc"; fi

new_env; make_job '.version.mode="auto" | .version.value=null'; export SHIM_LIST_PATCHES=/dev/null
rc=$(run_runner); unset SHIM_LIST_PATCHES
if [ "$rc" != 0 ] && grep -q '^::fail::version_unresolved::' "$T/out.txt"; then pass runner_version_unresolved; else fail runner_version_unresolved "rc=$rc"; fi

new_env; make_job '.recipe.variants[].download.kind="apkmirror"'
rc=$(run_runner)
if [ "$rc" != 0 ] && grep -q '^::fail::apk_not_listed::' "$T/out.txt"; then pass runner_apk_not_listed; else fail runner_apk_not_listed "rc=$rc"; fi

new_env; make_job '.engine="lspatch"'
rc=$(run_runner)
if [ "$rc" != 0 ] && [ "$(fail_lines)" = 1 ] && grep -q '^::fail::internal::engine not implemented$' "$T/out.txt"; then pass runner_engine_stub; else fail runner_engine_stub "rc=$rc"; fi

new_env; make_job '.'
rc1=$(run_runner)
rm -rf "${T:?}/work" && mkdir -p "$T/work" && make_job '.' && rm "$SHIM_SERVE/input.apk"
rc2=$(run_runner)
if [ "$rc1" = 0 ] && [ "$rc2" = 0 ] && grep -q 'Input from cache' "$T/out.txt"; then pass runner_cache_hit; else fail runner_cache_hit "rc1=$rc1 rc2=$rc2"; fi
```

Expected: exit 1; `FAIL runner_ok` … `FAIL runner_cache_hit` (runner file missing: `bash: …/build-item.sh: No such file or directory`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/build/build-item.sh`**

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Build runner (spec build §2-§4): executes one job.json. No DB, queue or HTTP knowledge.
# Usage: bash src/build/build-item.sh <job.json>
# stdout markers: ::step::<name>[::i/n]  ::version::<v>  ::apkm_versions::<csv>  ::fail::<code>::<detail>
set -o pipefail
RELUME_REPO=$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.." && pwd)

fail() {
	echo "::fail::$1::$2"
	exit 1
}

job=$(realpath -e "${1:-/nonexistent}" 2>/dev/null) || fail internal "job.json missing: ${1:-}"
# shellcheck source=src/build/utils.sh
source "$RELUME_REPO/src/build/utils.sh"

j() { jq -r "$1 // empty" "$job"; }

# ponytail: round-1 stub; P8 (Xposed) fills the keystore credential table here
keystore_creds() {
	return 1
}

work=$(j .work_dir)
package=$(j .package)
engine=$(j .engine)
cli_jar=$(j .cli_jar)
keystore=$(j .keystore)
upstream_tag=$(j .upstream_tag)
cli_tag=$(j .cli_tag)
[[ -n "$work" && -d "$work" && -n "$package" && -n "$engine" && -n "$cli_jar" && -n "$keystore" ]] ||
	fail internal "job.json lacks work_dir, package, engine, cli_jar or keystore"
mapfile -t patches_files < <(jq -r '.patches_files[]' "$job")
mapfile -t variants < <(jq -c '.recipe.variants[]' "$job")
mapfile -t specs < <(jq -cS '[.recipe.variants[].download] | unique | .[]' "$job")
n_var=${#variants[@]}
[[ $n_var -ge 1 ]] || fail internal "recipe has no variants"

cd "$work" || fail internal "cannot enter $work"
mkdir -p download release
init_ua

# symlinks keep the detect_version globs (morphe-desktop-*.jar, *.mpp) working
ln -sf "$cli_jar" . || fail internal "cannot link $cli_jar"
for f in "${patches_files[@]}"; do ln -sf "$f" . || fail internal "cannot link $f"; done

# ---- version (rule: catalog §4.2) ----
echo "::step::version"
mode=$(j .version.mode)
value=$(j .version.value)
allow_near=0
[[ "$(jq -r '.version.near' "$job")" == true ]] && allow_near=1
version=""
lock_version=""
case "$mode" in
pinned) version=$value ;;
latest) lock_version=1 ;;
auto)
	detect_version "$package"
	[[ -n "$version" ]] || fail version_unresolved "no compatible version for $package in the patches"
	;;
*) fail internal "unknown version mode $mode" ;;
esac
printed_version=""
if [[ -n "$version" ]]; then
	echo "::version::$version"
	printed_version=$version
fi

# ---- download (one fetch per distinct download spec) ----
echo "::step::download"
list_url=$(j .apkmirror.list_url)
ex1=$(j .apkmirror.example_url)
ex2=$(j .apkmirror.example_url2)
apkpure_url=$(j .apkpure_url)

fetch_input() {
	local n=$1 d=$2 name="in-$1" kind type arch dpi minver url ext=apk
	kind=$(jq -r .kind <<<"$d")
	type=$(jq -r '.type // "apk"' <<<"$d")
	arch=$(jq -r '.arch // empty' <<<"$d")
	dpi=$(jq -r '.dpi // empty' <<<"$d")
	minver=$(jq -r '.minver // empty' <<<"$d")
	url=$(jq -r '.url // empty' <<<"$d")
	[[ $type == bundle_extract ]] && ext=apkm
	local spec_key cache_dir="${RELUME_APK_CACHE:-}/$package" use_cache=0
	spec_key=$(printf -- '-%s-%s-%s' "$type" "${arch:-any}" "${dpi:-any}" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_')
	local vkey cache_file
	vkey=$(printf '%s' "$version" | tr -c 'A-Za-z0-9._-' '_')
	cache_file="$cache_dir/$vkey$spec_key.$ext"
	[[ -n "${RELUME_APK_CACHE:-}" && -n "$version" && $allow_near -eq 0 ]] && use_cache=1
	if [[ $use_cache -eq 1 && -s "$cache_file" ]]; then
		green_log "[+] Input from cache: $(basename "$cache_file")"
		cp "$cache_file" "download/$name.$ext" || fail internal "cannot copy from cache"
		[[ $ext == apkm ]] && unzip -q "download/$name.apkm" -d "download/$name"
		return 0
	fi
	case "$kind" in
	apkmirror)
		[[ -n "$list_url" ]] || fail apk_not_listed "no APKMirror list URL for $package"
		[[ $allow_near -eq 1 ]] && near_version=1
		get_apk "$package" "$name" "$type" "$arch" "$dpi" "$minver" "$list_url" "$ex1" "$ex2" ||
			fail download_failed "${LAST_ERR:-download failed}"
		;;
	apkpure)
		get_apkpure "$package" "$name" "$type" "$apkpure_url" || fail download_failed "${LAST_ERR:-download failed}"
		;;
	url)
		req "$url" "$name.apk" || fail download_failed "download failed: $url"
		;;
	*) fail internal "unknown download kind $kind" ;;
	esac
	if [[ $use_cache -eq 1 && -s "download/$name.$ext" ]]; then
		mkdir -p "$cache_dir" && rm -f "$cache_dir"/*"$spec_key".* && cp "download/$name.$ext" "$cache_file"
	fi
	return 0
}

for k in "${!specs[@]}"; do fetch_input "$k" "${specs[$k]}"; done
if [[ -n "$version" && "$version" != "$printed_version" ]]; then
	echo "::version::$version"
	printed_version=$version
fi

# APKMirror versions for the pin list (spec build §4 step 5d): at most one extra list-page request
if [[ "$(j .apkmirror.versions_due)" == true && $APKM_EMITTED -eq 0 && -n "$list_url" ]] &&
	jq -e '[.recipe.variants[].download.kind] | index("apkmirror")' "$job" >/dev/null; then
	if _cf_get "$list_url"; then emit_apkm_versions; else yellow_log "[!] APKMirror version list unavailable"; fi
fi

spec_index() {
	local k
	for k in "${!specs[@]}"; do
		[[ "${specs[$k]}" == "$1" ]] && { echo "$k"; return 0; }
	done
	echo -1
}

# ---- patch each variant ----
i=0
for v in "${variants[@]}"; do
	i=$((i + 1))
	echo "::step::patch::$i/$n_var"
	name=$(jq -r .name <<<"$v")
	strip=$(jq -r '.strip_libs // empty' <<<"$v")
	excl=$(jq -c '.exclude_splits' <<<"$v")
	base="in-$(spec_index "$(jq -cS .download <<<"$v")")"
	input="download/$base.apk"
	if [[ "$excl" != null && "$excl" != '[]' ]]; then
		split_editor "$base" "v-$name" exclude "$(jq -r 'join(" ")' <<<"$excl")"
		input="download/v-$name.apk"
	elif [[ -d "download/$base" && ! -s "download/$base.apk" ]]; then
		split_editor "$base"
	fi
	[[ -s "$input" ]] || fail merge_failed "no input APK for variant $name"
	FAIL_CODE=""
	patch_variant "$input" "release/$name.apk" "$strip" || fail "${FAIL_CODE:-patch_failed}" "${LAST_ERR:-patch failed}"
done

# ---- verify and write result.json ----
echo "::step::verify"
: > variants.jsonl
first_vname=""
for v in "${variants[@]}"; do
	name=$(jq -r .name <<<"$v")
	abi=$(jq -r .abi <<<"$v")
	f="release/$name.apk"
	info=$(apk_info "$f") || fail patch_failed "cannot read the package of $f"
	vpkg=$(sed -n 's/^package=//p' <<<"$info")
	vcode=$(sed -n 's/^version_code=//p' <<<"$info")
	vname=$(sed -n 's/^version_name=//p' <<<"$info")
	[[ -z "$first_vname" ]] && first_vname=$vname
	signer=$(apk_signer "$f") || signer=""
	sha=$(sha256sum "$f" | cut -d' ' -f1)
	size=$(stat -c %s "$f")
	jq -nc --arg name "$name" --arg abi "$abi" --arg file "$f" --argjson size "$size" --arg sha "$sha" \
		--arg pkg "$vpkg" --arg vname "$vname" --arg vcode "$vcode" --arg signer "$signer" \
		'{name:$name, abi:$abi, file:$file, size:$size, sha256:$sha, package:$pkg, version_name:$vname,
		  version_code:($vcode|tonumber? // null), signer_sha256:(if $signer == "" then null else $signer end)}' \
		>> variants.jsonl || fail internal "cannot record variant $name"
done
app_version=${version:-$first_vname}
[[ "$app_version" != "$printed_version" ]] && echo "::version::$app_version"
jq -s --arg av "$app_version" --arg pv "$upstream_tag" --arg cv "$cli_tag" \
	'{app_version:$av, patches_version:$pv, cli_version:$cv, variants:.}' variants.jsonl > result.json ||
	fail internal "cannot write result.json"
green_log "[+] Build finished: $n_var variant(s)"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `src/build/inspect.sh`**

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Patch inspection for the catalog (spec build §3.1.1). No network, no APKMirror.
# Usage: bash src/build/inspect.sh <cli_jar> <package> <out_dir> <patches_files...>
# Writes <out_dir>/options.json and <out_dir>/list-patches.txt; exit 0 only when both are non-empty.
set -o pipefail
if [[ $# -lt 4 ]]; then
	echo "[-] usage: inspect.sh <cli_jar> <package> <out_dir> <patches_files...>"
	exit 2
fi
cli=$1 pkg=$2 out=$3
shift 3
mkdir -p "$out" || { echo "[-] cannot create $out"; exit 1; }
p_args=() lp_args=()
for f in "$@"; do p_args+=(-p "$f"); done
lp_args=(--patches "$@")
java -jar "$cli" options-create "${p_args[@]}" -f "$pkg" -o "$out/options.json"
oc_rc=$?
java -jar "$cli" list-patches --with-packages --with-versions --with-options -f "$pkg" "${lp_args[@]}" |
	sed -E 's/\x1b\[[0-9;]*m//g' > "$out/list-patches.txt"
lp_rc=$?
if [[ $oc_rc -ne 0 || $lp_rc -ne 0 || ! -s "$out/options.json" || ! -s "$out/list-patches.txt" ]]; then
	echo "[-] inspect failed for $pkg (options-create rc=$oc_rc, list-patches rc=$lp_rc)"
	exit 1
fi
echo "[+] inspected $pkg"
```
