
#### Task P3.5: Image, compose, `.env.example`, solver bind

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/Dockerfile`, `local/docker-compose.yml`, `local/Dockerfile.dockerignore`, `local/solver/solver.py:79`
- Create: `local/.env.example`, `local/test-compose.sh`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: spec core §1 L172–209, §12 L386–400; build §2.1 L679–681.
- Produces: services `netns`, `app`, `flaresolverr`, `cfbypass`, `apkmsolver`; image `relume-app` with `/usr/local/bin/pup` (v0.4.0), `/opt/APKEditor.jar` (1.4.9), `adb`, `tzdata`, `curl`, `apksigner`, java 21, jq, `python3`; env on `app`: `HOME=/data/home`, `TZ`, `RELUME_NETWORK_MODE`, `PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1`, `PYTHONPATH=/repo/local`, `FLARESOLVERR_URL=http://127.0.0.1:47191`, `CFBYPASS_URL=http://127.0.0.1:47800`, `APKM_SOLVER_URL=http://127.0.0.1:47900`; compose env names `RELUME_NETWORK_MODE`, `RELUME_WEB_PORT`, `RELUME_DATA_DIR` (default `./data`), `RELUME_UID`, `RELUME_GID`, `TZ`, `RELUME_GITHUB_TOKEN_FILE` (consumed by P7, ci review S8).

**Behavior:** `netns` = `busybox:1.38.0-musl` `tail -f /dev/null`, owns `network_mode` and `ports`; the other four use `network_mode: service:netns`; all `restart: unless-stopped`; `app` healthcheck `curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:47000/api/health`; data bind long syntax with `create_host_path: false`; repo `..:/repo:ro`. FlareSolverr `HOST=127.0.0.1`, cfbypass `--host 127.0.0.1`, solver binds `127.0.0.1`. Derived decision (catalog review N6): the `ggplay_dl` venv, `python3-venv`, `python-is-python3` and `git` leave the image because their only user (`get_apk_chplay`, `utils.sh:645-699`) is deleted (spec L832); the Dockerfile copies nothing from the context, so `Dockerfile.dockerignore` is `*` only. `.env.example` tells the user to create `local/data/` and the (possibly empty) token file before `up` (core review S10, catalog parking).

**Constraints:**
- pup arch from `dpkg --print-architecture` (`arm64`/`amd64`), so the image builds on amd64 GitHub runners.
- No per-platform branch besides `RELUME_NETWORK_MODE`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `bash local/test-compose.sh` prints `PASS compose_host`, `PASS compose_bridge`, `PASS compose_unset`, `PASS compose_shape` and exits 0.
- [ ] `git grep -n ggplay -- local/` = 0 lines; `grep -n '0.0.0.0' local/solver/solver.py` = 0 lines.
- [ ] User-run: `docker compose -f local/docker-compose.yml build app` (arm64) succeeds; `docker run --rm relume-app sh -c 'adb version | head -2; pup --version; java -jar /opt/APKEditor.jar 2>&1 | head -1; adb pair 127.0.0.1:1 000000'` shows adb ≥ 34, `0.4.0`, an APKEditor usage line, `Failed: Unable to start pairing client.` (device T6, spec L1024).
- [ ] User-run: `docker buildx build --platform linux/amd64 -f local/Dockerfile ..` succeeds (from `local/`, spec L207).

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing check `local/test-compose.sh`**

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Compose shape checks (spec core §1, §11). Runs `docker compose config` only; starts nothing.
set -o pipefail
cd "$(dirname "$0")/.." || exit 1
f=local/docker-compose.yml
fails=0
pass() { echo "PASS $1"; }
fail() { echo "FAIL $1: $2"; fails=$((fails + 1)); }

for m in host bridge; do
	if RELUME_NETWORK_MODE=$m docker compose --env-file /dev/null -f "$f" config > /dev/null 2>&1; then pass "compose_$m"; else fail "compose_$m" "config failed"; fi
done
if env -u RELUME_NETWORK_MODE docker compose --env-file /dev/null -f "$f" config > /dev/null 2>&1; then
	fail compose_unset "config passed without RELUME_NETWORK_MODE"
else pass compose_unset; fi

j=$(RELUME_NETWORK_MODE=bridge docker compose --env-file /dev/null -f "$f" config --format json 2>/dev/null) || j='{}'
before=$fails
check() { jq -e "$1" <<<"$j" > /dev/null || fail compose_shape "$1"; }
check '.services | keys == ["apkmsolver","app","cfbypass","flaresolverr","netns"]'
check '[.services[] | select(.network_mode == "service:netns")] | length == 4'
check '.services.netns.network_mode == "bridge"'
check '.services.netns.ports[0].target == 47000'
check '.services.flaresolverr.environment.HOST == "127.0.0.1"'
check '.services.cfbypass.command as $c | ($c | index("--host")) as $i | $i != null and $c[$i + 1] == "127.0.0.1"'
check '[.services.app.volumes[] | select(.target == "/data") | .bind.create_host_path] == [false]'
check '[.services.app.volumes[] | select(.target == "/repo") | .read_only] == [true]'
check '.services.app.healthcheck.test | join(" ") | test("curl -sf http://127.0.0.1:47000/api/health")'
check '.services.app.environment.HOME == "/data/home"'
check '.services.app.environment.FLARESOLVERR_URL == "http://127.0.0.1:47191"'
check '.services.app.environment.CFBYPASS_URL == "http://127.0.0.1:47800"'
check '.services.app.environment.APKM_SOLVER_URL == "http://127.0.0.1:47900"'
check '[.services[] | .restart] | all(. == "unless-stopped")'
for name in RELUME_GITHUB_TOKEN_FILE RELUME_UID RELUME_GID RELUME_DATA_DIR RELUME_NETWORK_MODE RELUME_WEB_PORT; do
	grep -q "$name" "$f" || fail compose_shape "missing env name $name"
done
[ "$fails" -eq "$before" ] && pass compose_shape
[ "$fails" -eq 0 ] || exit 1
```

Run: `chmod +x local/test-compose.sh && bash local/test-compose.sh; echo RC=$?`
Expected: `FAIL compose_unset` (the old file needs no variable) and `FAIL compose_shape …`, `RC=1`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Rewrite `local/docker-compose.yml`**

```yaml
# One compose file for macOS (RELUME_NETWORK_MODE=bridge) and Linux (host). Spec core §1.
# netns holds the network namespace; app and the three sidecars join it and reach each other on 127.0.0.1.
name: relume
services:
  netns:
    image: busybox:1.38.0-musl
    command: ["tail", "-f", "/dev/null"]
    network_mode: ${RELUME_NETWORK_MODE:?set RELUME_NETWORK_MODE in local/.env}
    ports: ["${RELUME_WEB_PORT:-47000}:47000"]
    restart: unless-stopped
  flaresolverr:
    image: ghcr.io/flaresolverr/flaresolverr:latest
    network_mode: service:netns
    depends_on: [netns]
    environment:
      HOST: "127.0.0.1"
      PORT: "47191"
    restart: unless-stopped
  cfbypass:
    build:
      # pinned upstream commit; its Dockerfile is multi-arch (ubuntu:rolling + CloakBrowser linux-arm64)
      context: https://github.com/sarperavci/CloudflareBypassForScraping.git#0f628b9ccbea9ab69ca61bc1c896c2ef766a0f06
    image: relume-cfbypass:0f628b9
    # upstream has CMD (no ENTRYPOINT); the entrypoint forwards args to server.py
    command: ["/app/docker-entrypoint.sh", "--host", "127.0.0.1", "--port", "47800"]
    shm_size: 2g
    network_mode: service:netns
    depends_on: [netns]
    restart: unless-stopped
  apkmsolver:
    # headed CloakBrowser Turnstile solver for APKMirror downloads; same image as cfbypass, own entrypoint starts Xvfb
    build:
      context: https://github.com/sarperavci/CloudflareBypassForScraping.git#0f628b9ccbea9ab69ca61bc1c896c2ef766a0f06
    image: relume-cfbypass:0f628b9
    entrypoint: ["sh", "/solver/xrun.sh"]
    command: ["/app/venv/bin/python3", "-u", "/solver/solver.py", "47900"]
    shm_size: 2g
    network_mode: service:netns
    depends_on: [netns, cfbypass]
    volumes:
      # only the solver code: the browser container must not see the repo (token file)
      - ./solver:/solver:ro
    restart: unless-stopped
  app:
    build:
      context: ..
      dockerfile: local/Dockerfile
    image: relume-app
    network_mode: service:netns
    depends_on: [netns, flaresolverr, cfbypass, apkmsolver]
    user: "${RELUME_UID:-0}:${RELUME_GID:-0}"
    environment:
      HOME: /data/home
      TZ: ${TZ:-UTC}
      RELUME_NETWORK_MODE: ${RELUME_NETWORK_MODE}
      PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE: "1"
      PYTHONPATH: /repo/local
      FLARESOLVERR_URL: http://127.0.0.1:47191
      CFBYPASS_URL: http://127.0.0.1:47800
      APKM_SOLVER_URL: http://127.0.0.1:47900
    volumes:
      - ..:/repo:ro
      # create_host_path: false: a missing dir would be created root-owned; the user creates it (.env.example)
      - type: bind
        source: ${RELUME_DATA_DIR:-./data}
        target: /data
        bind:
          create_host_path: false
      - type: bind
        source: ${RELUME_GITHUB_TOKEN_FILE:-../src/keystore/github_token}
        target: /run/secrets/github_token
        read_only: true
        bind:
          create_host_path: false
    working_dir: /repo
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "curl", "-sf", "http://127.0.0.1:47000/api/health"]
      interval: 30s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 3
    restart: unless-stopped
```

```dockerfile
# app image: web server + build toolchain + adb (spec core §1, build §2.1, device D1/D2). Code is bind-mounted.
FROM ubuntu:24.04
ENV DEBIAN_FRONTEND=noninteractive
RUN apt-get update && apt-get install -y --no-install-recommends \
      bash curl wget jq unzip zip ca-certificates tzdata adb \
      openjdk-21-jdk-headless python3 apksigner \
    && rm -rf /var/lib/apt/lists/*
RUN case "$(dpkg --print-architecture)" in arm64) a=arm64 ;; amd64) a=amd64 ;; *) echo "unsupported arch" >&2; exit 1 ;; esac \
    && wget -q -O /tmp/pup.zip "https://github.com/ericchiang/pup/releases/download/v0.4.0/pup_v0.4.0_linux_${a}.zip" \
    && unzip -q /tmp/pup.zip -d /usr/local/bin && rm /tmp/pup.zip && chmod 755 /usr/local/bin/pup \
    && wget -q -O /opt/APKEditor.jar https://github.com/REAndroid/APKEditor/releases/download/V1.4.9/APKEditor-1.4.9.jar
ENV HOME=/data/home PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 PYTHONPATH=/repo/local
WORKDIR /repo
CMD ["python3", "-m", "webui"]
```

# P2 Catalog

Order (one implementer, serial): P2.1 (schema lands early, device review S7; announce to impl-device and impl-build) → P2.2 → P2.3 → P2.4 → P2.5 → P2.6 → P2.7 → P2.8 → P2.9.
Owner files (round 1): `local/webui/{catalog,upstream}.py`, `local/webui/schema/003_catalog.sql`, `local/webui/catalog-seed.json`, `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_{store,upstream,inspect,seed}.py`, `local/webui/tests/fixtures/catalog/`, `docs/logs/2026-10-19-seed-report.md`. Scratch (never committed): `.claude/tmp/make_catalog_seed.py`, `.claude/tmp/seed_inspect.sh`, `.claude/tmp/seed-inspect/`.

Shared conventions for every P2 task:
- Tests subclass `helpers.DataTestCase` (P1).
- DB access through P1: `db.conn()` (rows are `sqlite3.Row`), `with db.tx() as c:`; paths from `db.DATA` (X1) at call time.
- Route handlers take `req` and return `(status, obj)` or raise `server.HttpError(status, message)`; validators `server.validate_slug/package/repo(v, field)` raise `HttpError(400, "invalid <field>")` and return `v` (planner-outline ask B).
- Seams for tests (module attributes): `catalog.BUILD` (None → `webui.build` imported at call time; tests set a fake with `item_status(ids)` and `forget_item(id)`), `upstream.http_get`, `upstream.http_download`, `upstream.INSPECT`, `upstream.APKSIGNER`.
- Times: `now_iso()` = `datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")` (defined once in `catalog.py`, imported by `upstream.py`).

#### Task P2.1: Catalog schema `003_catalog.sql`

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/schema/003_catalog.sql`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_store.py` (class `SchemaTest`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P1 `db.migrate`, `helpers.DataTestCase`.
- Produces: tables `upstream`, `source`, `app`, `grp`, `item`, `bundle_info`, `dependency_app`, `item_dependency` with the columns of spec L443–466 and L1442–1446. Column names used by P4/P5 read-only SQL: `item(id, source_id, package, key, group_id, position, enabled)`, `grp(id, position)`, `source(id, upstream_id, cli_upstream_id)`, `upstream(id, downloaded_tag)`.

**Behavior:** `item.group_id` → `grp(id) ON DELETE SET NULL` (group delete moves items to Ungrouped, spec L483). `item_dependency.item_id ON DELETE CASCADE`. `bundle_info` FK `upstream_id → upstream ON DELETE CASCADE`. `source.upstream_id`, `source.cli_upstream_id`, `item.source_id`, `item.package` plain FKs (RESTRICT by default; source delete is guarded by the route's 409). CHECKs: `upstream.kind`, `upstream.channel`, `source.engine`, `item.version_mode`; `grp.color` NULL or 7 chars starting `#`.

**Constraints:** SQLite only, no triggers.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_catalog_store.py'` runs `SchemaTest` green: the 8 tables exist; `UNIQUE(repo, channel, tag)` rejects a duplicate; deleting a `grp` sets `item.group_id` NULL; deleting an `item` removes its `item_dependency` rows.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_store.py`

```python
"""Catalog store tests (spec catalog §3, §6, §10)."""
import sqlite3
import unittest

from webui import db
from webui.tests import helpers


class SchemaTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()

    def test_tables(self):
        names = {r[0] for r in db.conn().execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")}
        self.assertLessEqual({"upstream", "source", "app", "grp", "item", "bundle_info",
                              "dependency_app", "item_dependency"}, names)

    def test_upstream_unique_with_null_tag(self):
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO upstream(kind, repo, channel) VALUES ('patches','a/b','stable')")
        with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
            with db.tx() as c:
                c.execute("INSERT INTO upstream(kind, repo, channel) VALUES ('patches','a/b','stable')")

    def test_group_delete_and_item_cascade(self):
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO upstream(id, kind, repo, channel) VALUES (1,'cli','c/c','stable'),(2,'patches','p/p','stable'),(3,'dependency','m/m','stable')")
            c.execute("INSERT INTO source(id, name, upstream_id, cli_upstream_id) VALUES ('s','S',2,1)")
            c.execute("INSERT INTO app(package) VALUES ('com.a.b')")
            c.execute("INSERT INTO grp(id, name, position) VALUES (1,'G',0)")
            c.execute("INSERT INTO item(id, source_id, package, key, group_id, position, variants) VALUES (1,'s','com.a.b','b',1,0,'[]')")
            c.execute("INSERT INTO dependency_app(id, upstream_id, package, display_name) VALUES (1,3,'x.y','X')")
            c.execute("INSERT INTO item_dependency(item_id, dependency_app_id) VALUES (1,1)")
            c.execute("DELETE FROM grp WHERE id=1")
        self.assertIsNone(db.conn().execute("SELECT group_id FROM item WHERE id=1").fetchone()[0])
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("DELETE FROM item WHERE id=1")
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM item_dependency").fetchone()[0], 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `local/webui/schema/003_catalog.sql`**

```sql
-- Catalog tables (spec catalog §3, xposed §4). Owner: catalog.
CREATE TABLE upstream (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('patches','cli','dependency','module')),
  repo TEXT NOT NULL,
  channel TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (channel IN ('stable','prerelease','tag')),
  tag TEXT,                       -- only for channel='tag'
  asset_regex TEXT,               -- NULL = default for the kind (upstream.DEFAULT_REGEX)
  latest_tag TEXT, latest_published_at TEXT, checked_at TEXT, check_error TEXT,
  downloaded_tag TEXT, notified_tag TEXT);
-- UNIQUE(repo, channel, tag) with NULL tags treated as equal
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX upstream_key ON upstream(repo, channel, ifnull(tag, ''));

CREATE TABLE source (
  id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,            -- slug, e.g. 'morphe'
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  upstream_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES upstream(id),
  cli_upstream_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES upstream(id),
  engine TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'morphe' CHECK (engine IN ('morphe','lspatch','npatch')),
  keystore TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'morphe.keystore');

CREATE TABLE app (
  package TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
  display_name TEXT,              -- NULL until the icon fetcher reads Play og:title
  icon_file TEXT, icon_fetched_at TEXT,
  apkmirror_list_url TEXT, apkmirror_example_url TEXT, apkmirror_example_url2 TEXT, apkpure_url TEXT,
  apkmirror_versions TEXT,        -- JSON array, newest first; NULL = never seen
  apkmirror_checked_at TEXT);

CREATE TABLE grp (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  color TEXT CHECK (color IS NULL OR (length(color) = 7 AND substr(color, 1, 1) = '#')),
  position INTEGER NOT NULL);

CREATE TABLE item (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  source_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES source(id),
  package TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES app(package),
  key TEXT NOT NULL,
  display_name TEXT,
  group_id INTEGER REFERENCES grp(id) ON DELETE SET NULL,   -- NULL = Ungrouped
  position INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  enabled INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,
  version_mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'auto' CHECK (version_mode IN ('auto','pinned','latest')),
  version_pin TEXT,
  allow_nearest INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  variants TEXT NOT NULL,                       -- JSON, build §3.1 variants[]
  patch_overrides TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',   -- JSON, catalog §4
  engine_options TEXT,                          -- JSON, xposed §4
  UNIQUE (source_id, package));

CREATE TABLE bundle_info (
  upstream_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES upstream(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  tag TEXT NOT NULL,
  package TEXT NOT NULL,
  patches TEXT NOT NULL,                        -- JSON, upstream.merge_bundle output
  PRIMARY KEY (upstream_id, tag, package));

CREATE TABLE dependency_app (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  upstream_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES upstream(id),
  package TEXT NOT NULL,
  display_name TEXT NOT NULL);

CREATE TABLE item_dependency (
  item_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES item(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  dependency_app_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES dependency_app(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  PRIMARY KEY (item_id, dependency_app_id));
```

#### Task P2.2: Groups, ordering, catalog view, target resolution

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/catalog.py` (first part)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/catalog_rows.py` (shared P2 test rows, `FakeBuild`, `FakeLog`, `CatalogCase`)
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_store.py` (class `GroupsTest`, `ResolveTest`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task P2.1 tables; `catalog.BUILD.item_status(item_ids) -> {item_id: {"last_build","last_success","active_job"}}` (P4, faked).
- Produces:
  - `catalog.ABIS = ("arm64-v8a", "armeabi-v7a", "x86", "x86_64", "universal")`
  - `catalog.now_iso() -> str`; `catalog.version_key(v) -> tuple` (sort key for version strings, newest first with `reverse=True`)
  - `catalog.REPO_ROOT` (`Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]`)
  - `catalog.resolve_items(target: dict) -> list[int]` — enabled items only, catalog order (group position, Ungrouped last, then item position, then id); targets `{"all":true}`, `{"group_ids":[…]}` (0 = Ungrouped), `{"item_ids":[…]}`; unknown shape → `[]`.
  - `catalog.resolve_upstreams(target: dict) -> list[int]` — `{"all":true}` → every upstream row id; `{"source_ids":[…]}` → those sources' `upstream_id` and `cli_upstream_id`, distinct, ascending.
  - `catalog.item_objects(where_sql="", args=()) -> list[dict]` (spec L575 item object) and `catalog.catalog_view() -> dict` (spec L574).
  - Route handlers: `get_catalog`, `list_groups`, `post_group`, `put_group`, `delete_group`, `put_groups_order`, `put_group_items_order`.

**Behavior:** Spec catalog §3.1–3.2 L479–488, §6 L573–575. Colour `^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$` or null (null clears), else 400 `invalid color`; Ungrouped has no colour. `POST /api/groups` appends at the end. `DELETE /api/groups/{id}` → its items go to Ungrouped, appended after existing Ungrouped items in their old order; remaining groups renumbered 0..n-1. `PUT /api/groups/order {ids}` must list exactly the existing group ids, else 400 `ids must list every group once`. `PUT /api/groups/{id}/items/order {item_ids}` (id 0 = Ungrouped): unknown group 404; unknown item 400; listed items get `group_id` (NULL for 0) and `position` 0..n-1; items already in that group but not listed keep their order after the listed ones. Item label = `item.display_name` or `"<app.display_name or package> · <source.name>"`; `icon_url` = `/api/apps/<pkg>/icon` when `app.icon_file` is set, else null; `abis` = variant `abi` list; `last_build/last_success/active_job` from one `BUILD.item_status` call for all ids of the response.

**Constraints:** stdlib + `webui` only; one transaction per mutating route.
- `UNIQUE(repo, channel, tag)` is implemented as `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX upstream_key ON upstream(repo, channel, ifnull(tag, ''))` because SQLite treats NULLs in a UNIQUE constraint as distinct.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_catalog_store.py'` green for: group create/update with `#1a2B3c` stored and returned by `GET /api/catalog`; `red` and `#123` → 400; `color: null` clears; group order rewrite 0..n-1; item reorder 0..n-1; cross-group move; delete group → items Ungrouped; `resolve_items` order and enabled filter; `resolve_upstreams` for `all` and `source_ids`.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the shared test rows** `local/webui/tests/catalog_rows.py`

```python
"""Shared rows and fakes for the catalog tests (plain SQL, no routes)."""
import json
import subprocess
import unittest

from webui import catalog, db
from webui.tests import helpers

DOWNLOAD = {"kind": "apkmirror", "type": "apk", "arch": None, "dpi": None, "minver": None, "url": None}
VARIANTS = [
    {"name": "universal", "abi": "universal", "download": dict(DOWNLOAD), "exclude_splits": None, "strip_libs": None},
    {"name": "arm64-v8a", "abi": "arm64-v8a", "download": dict(DOWNLOAD), "exclude_splits": None, "strip_libs": "arm64-v8a"},
]


def add_basic(c):
    """upstreams 1 cli, 2 patches stable, 3 patches prerelease; sources morphe, morphe-beta;
    groups 1 Morphe, 2 Beta; items 1 YouTube (g1), 2 Reddit (g1), 3 YouTube beta (g2)."""
    c.execute("INSERT INTO upstream(id, kind, repo, channel) VALUES (1,'cli','MorpheApp/morphe-desktop','stable')")
    c.execute("INSERT INTO upstream(id, kind, repo, channel) VALUES (2,'patches','MorpheApp/morphe-patches','stable')")
    c.execute("INSERT INTO upstream(id, kind, repo, channel) VALUES (3,'patches','MorpheApp/morphe-patches','prerelease')")
    c.execute("INSERT INTO source(id, name, upstream_id, cli_upstream_id) VALUES ('morphe','Morphe',2,1),('morphe-beta','Morphe Beta',3,1)")
    c.execute("INSERT INTO app(package, display_name) VALUES ('com.google.android.youtube','YouTube'),('com.reddit.frontpage',NULL)")
    c.execute("INSERT INTO grp(id, name, color, position) VALUES (1,'Morphe',NULL,0),(2,'Beta',NULL,1)")
    v = json.dumps(VARIANTS)
    c.execute("INSERT INTO item(id, source_id, package, key, group_id, position, variants) VALUES (1,'morphe','com.google.android.youtube','youtube',1,0,?)", (v,))
    c.execute("INSERT INTO item(id, source_id, package, key, group_id, position, variants) VALUES (2,'morphe','com.reddit.frontpage','reddit',1,1,?)", (v,))
    c.execute("INSERT INTO item(id, source_id, package, key, group_id, position, variants) VALUES (3,'morphe-beta','com.google.android.youtube','youtube',2,0,?)", (v,))


class FakeBuild:
    """Stands in for webui.build (P4) in catalog tests."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.active = {}
        self.forgotten = []

    def item_status(self, ids):
        return {i: {"last_build": None, "last_success": None, "active_job": self.active.get(i)} for i in ids}

    def forget_item(self, item_id):
        self.forgotten.append(item_id)


class FakeLog:
    """jobs log stand-in: runs children synchronously, records argv and lines."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []
        self.runs = []

    def line(self, text):
        self.lines.append(text)

    def run(self, argv, cwd, env, timeout=None, on_line=None):
        self.runs.append(list(argv))
        p = subprocess.run(argv, cwd=cwd, env=env, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=timeout)
        for text in (p.stdout + p.stderr).splitlines():
            self.lines.append(text)
            if on_line:
                on_line(text)
        return p.returncode


class CatalogCase(helpers.DataTestCase):
    """Temp DB + basic rows + FakeBuild; self.call(handler, json=…, **params)."""

    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.build = FakeBuild()
        catalog.BUILD = self.build
        self.addCleanup(setattr, catalog, "BUILD", None)
        with db.tx() as c:
            add_basic(c)

    def call(self, handler, json=None, method="POST", **params):
        return handler(helpers.fake_request(method, "/test", json=json, params=params))

    def status_of(self, handler, json=None, method="POST", **params):
        try:
            return self.call(handler, json=json, method=method, **params)[0]
        except Exception as e:  # server.HttpError carries .status
            return getattr(e, "status", repr(e))
```

```python
from webui import catalog
from webui.tests.catalog_rows import CatalogCase


class GroupsTest(CatalogCase):
    def test_colour_create_update_clear(self):
        st, g = self.call(catalog.post_group, json={"name": "Fav", "color": "#1a2B3c"})
        self.assertEqual(st, 201)
        view = self.call(catalog.get_catalog, method="GET")[1]
        self.assertEqual([x["color"] for x in view["groups"] if x["id"] == g["id"]], ["#1a2B3c"])
        for bad in ("red", "#123", 5):
            self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.put_group, json={"color": bad}, method="PUT", id=g["id"]), 400)
        self.call(catalog.put_group, json={"color": None}, method="PUT", id=g["id"])
        self.assertIsNone(catalog._group(g["id"])["color"])
        self.assertEqual(catalog._group(g["id"])["position"], 2)

    def test_group_order(self):
        self.call(catalog.put_groups_order, json={"ids": [2, 1]}, method="PUT")
        self.assertEqual([g["id"] for g in catalog._groups()], [2, 1])
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.put_groups_order, json={"ids": [1]}, method="PUT"), 400)

    def test_item_reorder_and_move(self):
        self.call(catalog.put_group_items_order, json={"item_ids": [2, 1]}, method="PUT", id=1)
        view = catalog.catalog_view()
        self.assertEqual([(i["id"], i["position"]) for i in view["groups"][0]["items"]], [(2, 0), (1, 1)])
        self.call(catalog.put_group_items_order, json={"item_ids": [3]}, method="PUT", id=1)
        view = catalog.catalog_view()
        self.assertEqual([(i["id"], i["position"]) for i in view["groups"][0]["items"]], [(3, 0), (2, 1), (1, 2)])
        self.assertEqual(view["groups"][1]["items"], [])
        self.call(catalog.put_group_items_order, json={"item_ids": [1]}, method="PUT", id=0)
        self.assertEqual([i["id"] for i in catalog.catalog_view()["ungrouped"]], [1])
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.put_group_items_order, json={"item_ids": [99]}, method="PUT", id=1), 400)
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.put_group_items_order, json={"item_ids": [1]}, method="PUT", id=42), 404)

    def test_delete_group_moves_items_to_ungrouped(self):
        self.call(catalog.delete_group, method="DELETE", id=1)
        view = catalog.catalog_view()
        self.assertEqual([i["id"] for i in view["ungrouped"]], [1, 2])
        self.assertEqual([(g["id"], g["position"]) for g in view["groups"]], [(2, 0)])

    def test_item_object_fields(self):
        it = catalog.catalog_view()["groups"][0]["items"][0]
        self.assertEqual(it["label"], "YouTube · Morphe")
        self.assertEqual(it["abis"], ["universal", "arm64-v8a"])
        self.assertIsNone(it["icon_url"])
        self.assertEqual(it["upstream"]["repo"], "MorpheApp/morphe-patches")
        self.assertIn("active_job", it)
        reddit = catalog.catalog_view()["groups"][0]["items"][1]
        self.assertEqual(reddit["label"], "com.reddit.frontpage · Morphe")


class ResolveTest(CatalogCase):
    def test_resolve_items(self):
        from webui import db
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE item SET enabled=0 WHERE id=2")
        self.assertEqual(catalog.resolve_items({"all": True}), [1, 3])
        self.assertEqual(catalog.resolve_items({"group_ids": [2]}), [3])
        self.assertEqual(catalog.resolve_items({"item_ids": [3, 1]}), [1, 3])
        self.assertEqual(catalog.resolve_items({}), [])
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE item SET group_id=NULL WHERE id=1")
        self.assertEqual(catalog.resolve_items({"group_ids": [0]}), [1])
        self.assertEqual(catalog.resolve_items({"all": True}), [3, 1])

    def test_resolve_upstreams(self):
        self.assertEqual(catalog.resolve_upstreams({"all": True}), [1, 2, 3])
        self.assertEqual(catalog.resolve_upstreams({"source_ids": ["morphe-beta"]}), [1, 3])
        self.assertEqual(catalog.resolve_upstreams({"source_ids": ["nope"]}), [])
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write `local/webui/catalog.py` (first part)**

```python
"""Catalog: sources, apps, items, groups, patches, job input, seed (spec Part 2)."""
import json
import re
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from webui import db, server

ABIS = ("arm64-v8a", "armeabi-v7a", "x86", "x86_64", "universal")
REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
COLOR_RE = re.compile(r"^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$")
BUILD = None  # test seam; None -> webui.build (imported at call time)

ITEM_SQL = """SELECT i.*, s.name AS source_name, s.engine, a.display_name AS app_name, a.icon_file,
  u.id AS u_id, u.repo AS u_repo, u.channel AS u_channel, u.downloaded_tag AS u_downloaded_tag,
  u.latest_tag AS u_latest_tag, u.checked_at AS u_checked_at, u.check_error AS u_check_error
  FROM item i JOIN source s ON s.id = i.source_id JOIN app a ON a.package = i.package
  JOIN upstream u ON u.id = s.upstream_id LEFT JOIN grp g ON g.id = i.group_id"""
ORDER_SQL = " ORDER BY g.position IS NULL, g.position, i.position, i.id"


def now_iso():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")


def version_key(v):
    """Sort key for version strings ('20.12.46', '1.48.0-dev.3'); non-numeric parts sort first."""
    return tuple(int(p) if p.isdigit() else -1 for p in re.split(r"[.\-+_]", v))


def _build():
    if BUILD is not None:
        return BUILD
    from webui import build
    return build


def _j(text):
    return None if text is None else json.loads(text)


# ---- target resolution (core §4) ----

def resolve_items(target):
    rows = db.conn().execute(
        "SELECT i.id, i.group_id, i.enabled FROM item i LEFT JOIN grp g ON g.id = i.group_id" + ORDER_SQL).fetchall()
    if target.get("all") is True:
        def keep(r):
            return True
    elif isinstance(target.get("group_ids"), list):
        gids = set(target["group_ids"])

        def keep(r):
            return (r["group_id"] or 0) in gids
    elif isinstance(target.get("item_ids"), list):
        iids = set(target["item_ids"])

        def keep(r):
            return r["id"] in iids
    else:
        return []
    return [r["id"] for r in rows if r["enabled"] and keep(r)]


def resolve_upstreams(target):
    c = db.conn()
    if target.get("all") is True:
        return [r[0] for r in c.execute("SELECT id FROM upstream ORDER BY id")]
    ids = target.get("source_ids")
    if not isinstance(ids, list):
        return []
    out = set()
    for sid in ids:
        r = c.execute("SELECT upstream_id, cli_upstream_id FROM source WHERE id = ?", (sid,)).fetchone()
        if r:
            out.update((r[0], r[1]))
    return sorted(out)


# ---- item object (catalog §6) ----

def _item_obj(r, st):
    variants = json.loads(r["variants"])
    return {
        "id": r["id"],
        "label": r["display_name"] or f'{r["app_name"] or r["package"]} · {r["source_name"]}',
        "display_name": r["display_name"], "source_id": r["source_id"], "source_name": r["source_name"],
        "engine": r["engine"], "package": r["package"], "key": r["key"], "enabled": bool(r["enabled"]),
        "group_id": r["group_id"], "position": r["position"],
        "icon_url": f'/api/apps/{r["package"]}/icon' if r["icon_file"] else None,
        "abis": [v["abi"] for v in variants],
        "upstream": {"id": r["u_id"], "repo": r["u_repo"], "channel": r["u_channel"],
                     "downloaded_tag": r["u_downloaded_tag"], "latest_tag": r["u_latest_tag"],
                     "checked_at": r["u_checked_at"], "check_error": r["u_check_error"]},
        "last_build": st.get("last_build"), "last_success": st.get("last_success"), "active_job": st.get("active_job"),
    }


def item_objects(where_sql="", args=()):
    sql = ITEM_SQL + (" WHERE " + where_sql if where_sql else "") + ORDER_SQL
    rows = db.conn().execute(sql, args).fetchall()
    status = _build().item_status([r["id"] for r in rows]) if rows else {}
    return [_item_obj(r, status.get(r["id"]) or {}) for r in rows]


def _groups():
    return [dict(r) for r in db.conn().execute("SELECT id, name, color, position FROM grp ORDER BY position, id")]


def _group(gid):
    r = db.conn().execute("SELECT id, name, color, position FROM grp WHERE id = ?", (gid,)).fetchone()
    if r is None:
        raise server.HttpError(404, "group not found")
    return dict(r)


def catalog_view():
    groups = [dict(g, items=[]) for g in _groups()]
    by_id = {g["id"]: g for g in groups}
    ungrouped = []
    for it in item_objects():
        (by_id[it["group_id"]]["items"] if it["group_id"] in by_id else ungrouped).append(it)
    return {"groups": groups, "ungrouped": ungrouped}


# ---- validation helpers ----

def _name(v, field="name"):
    if not isinstance(v, str) or not v.strip() or len(v) > 80:
        raise server.HttpError(400, f"invalid {field}")
    return v.strip()


def _check_color(v):
    if v is not None and not (isinstance(v, str) and COLOR_RE.match(v)):
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid color")
    return v


def _int_list(v, field):
    if (not isinstance(v, list) or len(set(map(str, v))) != len(v)
            or not all(isinstance(x, int) and not isinstance(x, bool) for x in v)):
        raise server.HttpError(400, f"invalid {field}")
    return v


# ---- group routes (catalog §3.1) ----

def get_catalog(req):
    return 200, catalog_view()


def list_groups(req):
    return 200, {"groups": _groups()}


def post_group(req):
    b = req.json or {}
    name, color = _name(b.get("name")), _check_color(b.get("color"))
    with db.tx() as c:
        pos = c.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(position) + 1, 0) FROM grp").fetchone()[0]
        gid = c.execute("INSERT INTO grp(name, color, position) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", (name, color, pos)).lastrowid
    return 201, {"id": gid}


def put_group(req):
    gid = req.params["id"]
    _group(gid)
    b = req.json or {}
    with db.tx() as c:
        if "name" in b:
            c.execute("UPDATE grp SET name = ? WHERE id = ?", (_name(b["name"]), gid))
        if "color" in b:
            c.execute("UPDATE grp SET color = ? WHERE id = ?", (_check_color(b["color"]), gid))
    return 200, _group(gid)


def delete_group(req):
    gid = req.params["id"]
    _group(gid)
    with db.tx() as c:
        start = c.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(position) + 1, 0) FROM item WHERE group_id IS NULL").fetchone()[0]
        moved = [r[0] for r in c.execute("SELECT id FROM item WHERE group_id = ? ORDER BY position, id", (gid,))]
        for n, iid in enumerate(moved):
            c.execute("UPDATE item SET group_id = NULL, position = ? WHERE id = ?", (start + n, iid))
        c.execute("DELETE FROM grp WHERE id = ?", (gid,))
        for n, (g,) in enumerate(c.execute("SELECT id FROM grp ORDER BY position, id").fetchall()):
            c.execute("UPDATE grp SET position = ? WHERE id = ?", (n, g))
    return 200, {"ok": True}


def put_groups_order(req):
    ids = _int_list((req.json or {}).get("ids"), "ids")
    with db.tx() as c:
        if set(ids) != {r[0] for r in c.execute("SELECT id FROM grp")}:
            raise server.HttpError(400, "ids must list every group once")
        for n, g in enumerate(ids):
            c.execute("UPDATE grp SET position = ? WHERE id = ?", (n, g))
    return 200, {"ok": True}


def put_group_items_order(req):
    gid = req.params["id"]
    if gid != 0:
        _group(gid)
    ids = _int_list((req.json or {}).get("item_ids"), "item_ids")
    target = None if gid == 0 else gid
    with db.tx() as c:
        if not set(ids) <= {r[0] for r in c.execute("SELECT id FROM item")}:
            raise server.HttpError(400, "unknown item id")
        rest = [r[0] for r in c.execute("SELECT id FROM item WHERE group_id IS ? ORDER BY position, id", (target,))
                if r[0] not in ids]
        for n, iid in enumerate(ids + rest):
            c.execute("UPDATE item SET group_id = ?, position = ? WHERE id = ?", (target, n, iid))
    return 200, {"ok": True}


ROUTES = [
    ("GET", "/api/catalog", get_catalog),
    ("GET", "/api/groups", list_groups),
    ("POST", "/api/groups", post_group),
    ("PUT", "/api/groups/order", put_groups_order),
    ("PUT", "/api/groups/{id}", put_group),
    ("DELETE", "/api/groups/{id}", delete_group),
    ("PUT", "/api/groups/{id}/items/order", put_group_items_order),
]
```

Note for the router: `PUT /api/groups/order` is listed before `PUT /api/groups/{id}`; P1's router must try literal segments first or keep list order (P1 router test "int params"); `order` is not an int, so a router that types `{id}` as int-only also works.

#### Task P2.3: Sources, apps, items CRUD, dependency apps

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/catalog.py`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_store.py` (class `CrudTest`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task P2.2; `jobs.enqueue` (P1); `BUILD.forget_item(item_id)`; `BUILD.item_status`.
- Produces:
  - `catalog.upstream_row(c, kind, repo, channel, tag, asset_regex) -> int` (inside a transaction cursor `c`; get-or-create by the unique key `(repo, channel, ifnull(tag,''))`; an existing row of another kind → 400 `upstream kind conflict`).
  - `catalog.validate_variants(variants) -> list` (raises `HttpError(400, …)`).
  - `catalog.item_dependencies(item_id) -> list[dict]` — keys `dependency_app_id, upstream_id, package, display_name, tag, path, signer_sha256` (Plan-fixed); `tag` = upstream `downloaded_tag`; `path` = absolute path of the single `.apk` in `db.DATA/upstream/<upstream_id>/<tag>/`; `signer_sha256` = content of `signer.sha256` there; `tag`/`path`/`signer_sha256` null when not downloaded.
  - Route handlers: `list_sources`, `post_source`, `put_source`, `delete_source`, `list_items`, `post_item`, `get_item`, `put_item`, `delete_item`, `put_app`, `list_dependency_apps`.

**Behavior:** Spec catalog §6 L576–578, §0.3 delete rules L48, core §3 validation L243–249. `POST /api/sources {id, name, engine, repo, channel, tag?, asset_regex?, keystore, cli_upstream_id}`: `id` slug; `repo` repo regex; `channel ∈ stable|prerelease|tag`, `tag` slug required iff `channel='tag'`; `asset_regex` compiles (default `\.mpp$` for patches); `keystore` slug and file exists under `<repo root>/src/keystore/`; `cli_upstream_id` exists with `kind='cli'`; `engine ∈ morphe|lspatch|npatch` (stored; Xposed-only rules are P8); upstream kind = `patches` for morphe, `module` otherwise; duplicate id → 409. `PUT` same fields minus `id`. `DELETE` → 409 `source has items` while items use it. `POST /api/items {source_id, package, group_id, display_name?, variants?, engine_options?}`: package regex; creates the `app` row if missing (`display_name` null until the icon fetcher fills it); `variants` default = `[universal, arm64-v8a]` with `download {kind:"apkmirror", type:"apk", arch:null, dpi:null, minver:null, url:null}` and `strip_libs` `arm64-v8a` on the second (Q5); `key` = last segment of the package lower-cased; appended at the end of its group; duplicate `(source_id, package)` → 409. After insert: when the source upstream is `kind='patches'` with a `downloaded_tag` and no `bundle_info(upstream_id, downloaded_tag, package)` → `jobs.enqueue('upstream', {"upstream_id":N}, 'manual', options={"rebuild": False})` (spec L577). `PUT /api/items/{id}` takes the same fields plus `enabled`, `dependency_app_ids` (replace links; unknown id → 400). `DELETE /api/items/{id}` → 409 `build queued or running` when `BUILD.item_status([id])[id]["active_job"]` is not null; else `BUILD.forget_item(id)`, then delete the row. `PUT /api/apps/{pkg} {display_name?, apkmirror_list_url?, apkmirror_example_url?, apkmirror_example_url2?, apkpure_url?}`: URLs `https://…` or null. Variant rules: list of 1–10 dicts; `name` slug unique; `abi ∈ ABIS`; `download.kind ∈ apkmirror|apkpure|url`; `download.type ∈ apk|bundle|bundle_extract`; `download.arch` matches `^[A-Za-z0-9 ._+-]*$` or null (derived decision: the seed has `arm64-v8a + armeabi-v7a`, `icysymmetra.sh:11`, `DeRevanced.sh:120`; it is only ever an argv value); `dpi` slug or null; `minver` `^[A-Za-z0-9 .+-]*$` or null; `url` `https://…` required iff `kind='url'`; `exclude_splits` null or list of slugs; `strip_libs` null or in `ABIS`.

**Constraints:** every value validated before it reaches a path or SQL; SQL parameters only.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_catalog_store.py'` green for: source create dedups upstream rows; bad repo / missing keystore / non-cli `cli_upstream_id` → 400; source delete with items → 409; item create defaults (2 variants, app row created); duplicate item → 409; `POST /api/items` enqueues one `upstream` job with `{"rebuild":false}` when `bundle_info` is missing and none when present; item delete → 409 with active job, else `forget_item` called and `item_dependency` rows gone; variant validation table (8 bad cases → 400); `item_dependencies` returns `upstream_id` and nulls when not downloaded, and the file path + signer when downloaded.

```python
from webui import db, jobs


class CrudTest(CatalogCase):

           "channel": "stable", "keystore": "morphe.keystore", "cli_upstream_id": 1}

        self.assertEqual(n, 1)
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.post_source, json=self.SRC), 409)

                    {"channel": "tag"}, {"asset_regex": "("}, {"engine": "other"}):

        self.call(catalog.post_source, json=self.SRC)

    def test_item_create_defaults_and_duplicate(self):

        self.assertEqual(st, 201)
        it = catalog.item_detail(body["id"])
        self.assertEqual([v["abi"] for v in it["variants"]], ["universal", "arm64-v8a"])
        self.assertEqual(it["variants"][1]["strip_libs"], "arm64-v8a")
        self.assertEqual(it["key"], "duolingo")
        self.assertEqual(it["position"], 2)

        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.post_item, json={"source_id": "morphe", "package": "bad"}), 400)

    def test_item_create_enqueues_pull_when_bundle_missing(self):

    def test_item_delete(self):

        del self.build.active[1]

            c.execute("INSERT INTO dependency_app(id, upstream_id, package, display_name) VALUES (1,9,'app.revanced.android.gms','MicroG RE')")
            c.execute("INSERT INTO item_dependency VALUES (1,1)")
        self.assertEqual(self.call(catalog.delete_item, method="DELETE", id=1)[0], 200)
        self.assertEqual(self.build.forgotten, [1])
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM item_dependency").fetchone()[0], 0)

    def test_variant_validation(self):

        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.put_item, json={"dependency_app_ids": [5]}, method="PUT", id=1), 400)

        self.assertEqual(a["display_name"], "Reddit")
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.put_app, json={"apkmirror_list_url": "http://x"}, method="PUT", pkg="com.reddit.frontpage"), 400)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add to `local/webui/catalog.py`** (after the group routes, before `ROUTES`), plus `from webui import jobs` in the imports

```python
KEYSTORE_DIR = REPO_ROOT / "src" / "keystore"
URL_RE = re.compile(r"^https://[^\s\"'<>]+$")
ARCH_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9 ._+-]*$")   # seed has 'arm64-v8a + armeabi-v7a' (icysymmetra.sh:11)
MINVER_RE = re.compile(r"^[A-Za-z0-9 .+-]*$")
DOWNLOAD_DEFAULT = {"kind": "apkmirror", "type": "apk", "arch": None, "dpi": None, "minver": None, "url": None}
APP_URL_FIELDS = ("apkmirror_list_url", "apkmirror_example_url", "apkmirror_example_url2", "apkpure_url")


def _opt(v, rx, field):
    if v is None:
        return None
    if not isinstance(v, str) or not rx.match(v):
        raise server.HttpError(400, f"invalid {field}")
    return v


def default_variants():
    return [
        {"name": "universal", "abi": "universal", "download": dict(DOWNLOAD_DEFAULT), "exclude_splits": None, "strip_libs": None},
        {"name": "arm64-v8a", "abi": "arm64-v8a", "download": dict(DOWNLOAD_DEFAULT), "exclude_splits": None, "strip_libs": "arm64-v8a"},
    ]


def validate_variants(variants):
    if not isinstance(variants, list) or not 1 <= len(variants) <= 10:
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid variants")
    out, names = [], set()
    for v in variants:
        if not isinstance(v, dict):
            raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid variant")
        name = server.validate_slug(v.get("name"), "variant name")
        if name in names:
            raise server.HttpError(400, "duplicate variant name")
        names.add(name)
        if v.get("abi") not in ABIS:
            raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid abi")
        d = v.get("download")
        if (not isinstance(d, dict) or d.get("kind") not in ("apkmirror", "apkpure", "url")
                or d.get("type", "apk") not in ("apk", "bundle", "bundle_extract")):
            raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid download")
        url = _opt(d.get("url"), URL_RE, "download url")
        if (d["kind"] == "url") != (url is not None):
            raise server.HttpError(400, "download url is required for kind url and only for it")
        dl = {"kind": d["kind"], "type": d.get("type", "apk"),
              "arch": _opt(d.get("arch"), ARCH_RE, "arch"),
              "dpi": None if d.get("dpi") is None else server.validate_slug(d["dpi"], "dpi"),
              "minver": _opt(d.get("minver"), MINVER_RE, "minver"), "url": url}
        ex = v.get("exclude_splits")
        if ex is not None:
            if not isinstance(ex, list):
                raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid exclude_splits")
            ex = [server.validate_slug(x, "exclude_splits") for x in ex]
        sl = v.get("strip_libs")
        if sl is not None and sl not in ABIS:
            raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid strip_libs")
        out.append({"name": name, "abi": v["abi"], "download": dl, "exclude_splits": ex, "strip_libs": sl})
    return out


def _engine_options(v):
    if v is not None and not isinstance(v, dict):
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid engine_options")
    return v  # Xposed rules: P8


def _display_name(v):
    return None if v is None else _name(v, "display_name")


def upstream_row(c, kind, repo, channel, tag, asset_regex):
    r = c.execute("SELECT id, kind FROM upstream WHERE repo = ? AND channel = ? AND ifnull(tag, '') = ifnull(?, '')",
                  (repo, channel, tag)).fetchone()
    if r is not None:
        if r["kind"] != kind:
            raise server.HttpError(400, "upstream kind conflict")
        return r["id"]
    return c.execute("INSERT INTO upstream(kind, repo, channel, tag, asset_regex) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                     (kind, repo, channel, tag, asset_regex)).lastrowid


# ---- sources (catalog §6) ----

def _source(sid):
    r = db.conn().execute("""SELECT s.*, u.repo, u.channel, u.tag, u.asset_regex FROM source s
        JOIN upstream u ON u.id = s.upstream_id WHERE s.id = ?""", (sid,)).fetchone()
    if r is None:
        raise server.HttpError(404, "source not found")
    return dict(r)


def _source_fields(b):
    engine = b.get("engine", "morphe")
    if engine not in ("morphe", "lspatch", "npatch"):
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid engine")
    channel = b.get("channel")
    if channel not in ("stable", "prerelease", "tag"):
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid channel")
    tag = b.get("tag")
    if channel == "tag":
        tag = server.validate_slug(tag, "tag")
    elif tag is not None:
        raise server.HttpError(400, "tag is only for channel tag")
    rx = b.get("asset_regex")
    if rx is not None:
        try:
            re.compile(rx)
        except (re.error, TypeError):
            raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid asset_regex")
    ks = server.validate_slug(b.get("keystore", "morphe.keystore"), "keystore")
    if not (KEYSTORE_DIR / ks).is_file():
        raise server.HttpError(400, "keystore not found")
    cli = b.get("cli_upstream_id")
    r = db.conn().execute("SELECT kind FROM upstream WHERE id = ?", (cli,)).fetchone() if isinstance(cli, int) else None
    if r is None or r["kind"] != "cli":
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid cli_upstream_id")
    return {"name": _name(b.get("name")), "engine": engine, "repo": server.validate_repo(b.get("repo"), "repo"),
            "channel": channel, "tag": tag, "asset_regex": rx, "keystore": ks, "cli_upstream_id": cli}


def list_sources(req):
    rows = db.conn().execute("SELECT id FROM source ORDER BY id").fetchall()
    return 200, {"sources": [_source(r[0]) for r in rows]}


def post_source(req):
    b = req.json or {}
    sid = server.validate_slug(b.get("id"), "id")
    f = _source_fields(b)
    with db.tx() as c:
        if c.execute("SELECT 1 FROM source WHERE id = ?", (sid,)).fetchone():
            raise server.HttpError(409, "source exists")
        uid = upstream_row(c, "patches" if f["engine"] == "morphe" else "module", f["repo"], f["channel"], f["tag"], f["asset_regex"])
        c.execute("INSERT INTO source(id, name, upstream_id, cli_upstream_id, engine, keystore) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)",
                  (sid, f["name"], uid, f["cli_upstream_id"], f["engine"], f["keystore"]))
    return 201, {"id": sid}


def put_source(req):
    sid = req.params["id"]
    _source(sid)
    f = _source_fields(req.json or {})
    with db.tx() as c:
        uid = upstream_row(c, "patches" if f["engine"] == "morphe" else "module", f["repo"], f["channel"], f["tag"], f["asset_regex"])
        c.execute("UPDATE source SET name = ?, upstream_id = ?, cli_upstream_id = ?, engine = ?, keystore = ? WHERE id = ?",
                  (f["name"], uid, f["cli_upstream_id"], f["engine"], f["keystore"], sid))
    return 200, _source(sid)


def delete_source(req):
    sid = req.params["id"]
    _source(sid)
    with db.tx() as c:
        if c.execute("SELECT 1 FROM item WHERE source_id = ?", (sid,)).fetchone():
            raise server.HttpError(409, "source has items")
        c.execute("DELETE FROM source WHERE id = ?", (sid,))
    return 200, {"ok": True}


# ---- items (catalog §6) ----

def _group_id(v):
    if v is None or v == 0:
        return None
    if not isinstance(v, int) or isinstance(v, bool):
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid group_id")
    _group(v)
    return v


def _default_key(package):
    key = re.sub(r"[^a-z0-9._-]", "-", package.rsplit(".", 1)[-1].lower()).lstrip(".-")
    return key or "app"


def item_detail(iid):
    objs = item_objects("i.id = ?", (iid,))
    if not objs:
        raise server.HttpError(404, "item not found")
    r = db.conn().execute("""SELECT i.version_mode, i.version_pin, i.allow_nearest, i.variants, i.engine_options,
        a.apkmirror_versions, a.apkmirror_checked_at FROM item i JOIN app a ON a.package = i.package WHERE i.id = ?""",
                          (iid,)).fetchone()
    deps = [x[0] for x in db.conn().execute(
        "SELECT dependency_app_id FROM item_dependency WHERE item_id = ? ORDER BY dependency_app_id", (iid,))]
    o = objs[0]
    o.update(version={"mode": r["version_mode"], "pin": r["version_pin"], "allow_nearest": bool(r["allow_nearest"])},
             variants=json.loads(r["variants"]), engine_options=_j(r["engine_options"]), dependency_app_ids=deps,
             apkmirror={"versions": _j(r["apkmirror_versions"]), "checked_at": r["apkmirror_checked_at"]})
    return o


def list_items(req):
    return 200, item_objects()


def get_item(req):
    return 200, item_detail(req.params["id"])


def post_item(req):
    b = req.json or {}
    src = _source(server.validate_slug(b.get("source_id"), "source_id")) if isinstance(b.get("source_id"), str) else None
    if src is None:
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid source_id")
    pkg = server.validate_package(b.get("package"), "package")
    gid = _group_id(b.get("group_id"))
    variants = validate_variants(b["variants"]) if b.get("variants") is not None else default_variants()
    eo = _engine_options(b.get("engine_options"))
    dn = _display_name(b.get("display_name"))
    with db.tx() as c:
        if c.execute("SELECT 1 FROM item WHERE source_id = ? AND package = ?", (src["id"], pkg)).fetchone():
            raise server.HttpError(409, "item exists")
        c.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO app(package) VALUES (?)", (pkg,))
        pos = c.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(position) + 1, 0) FROM item WHERE group_id IS ?", (gid,)).fetchone()[0]
        iid = c.execute("""INSERT INTO item(source_id, package, key, display_name, group_id, position, variants, engine_options)
            VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""", (src["id"], pkg, _default_key(pkg), dn, gid, pos, json.dumps(variants),
                                                 None if eo is None else json.dumps(eo))).lastrowid
        up = c.execute("SELECT kind, downloaded_tag FROM upstream WHERE id = ?", (src["upstream_id"],)).fetchone()
        need_pull = bool(up["kind"] == "patches" and up["downloaded_tag"] and not c.execute(
            "SELECT 1 FROM bundle_info WHERE upstream_id = ? AND tag = ? AND package = ?",
            (src["upstream_id"], up["downloaded_tag"], pkg)).fetchone())
    if need_pull:
        jobs.enqueue("upstream", {"upstream_id": src["upstream_id"]}, "manual", options={"rebuild": False})
    return 201, {"id": iid}


def put_item(req):
    iid = req.params["id"]
    cur = item_detail(iid)
    b = req.json or {}
    for fixed in ("source_id", "package"):
        if fixed in b and b[fixed] != cur[fixed]:
            raise server.HttpError(400, f"{fixed} cannot change")
    sets = {}
    if "display_name" in b:
        sets["display_name"] = _display_name(b["display_name"])
    if "variants" in b:
        sets["variants"] = json.dumps(validate_variants(b["variants"]))
    if "engine_options" in b:
        eo = _engine_options(b["engine_options"])
        sets["engine_options"] = None if eo is None else json.dumps(eo)
    if "enabled" in b:
        if not isinstance(b["enabled"], bool):
            raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid enabled")
        sets["enabled"] = int(b["enabled"])
    deps = None
    if "dependency_app_ids" in b:
        deps = _int_list(b["dependency_app_ids"], "dependency_app_ids")
        known = {r[0] for r in db.conn().execute("SELECT id FROM dependency_app")}
        if not set(deps) <= known:
            raise server.HttpError(400, "unknown dependency app")
    with db.tx() as c:
        if "group_id" in b:
            gid = _group_id(b["group_id"])
            if gid != cur["group_id"]:
                sets["group_id"] = gid
                sets["position"] = c.execute("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(position) + 1, 0) FROM item WHERE group_id IS ?", (gid,)).fetchone()[0]
        for col, val in sets.items():  # col comes from the fixed names above
            c.execute(f"UPDATE item SET {col} = ? WHERE id = ?", (val, iid))
        if deps is not None:
            c.execute("DELETE FROM item_dependency WHERE item_id = ?", (iid,))
            c.executemany("INSERT INTO item_dependency(item_id, dependency_app_id) VALUES (?, ?)", [(iid, d) for d in deps])
    return 200, item_detail(iid)


def delete_item(req):
    iid = req.params["id"]
    item_detail(iid)
    st = _build().item_status([iid]).get(iid) or {}
    if st.get("active_job"):
        raise server.HttpError(409, "build queued or running")
    _build().forget_item(iid)
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute("DELETE FROM item WHERE id = ?", (iid,))
    return 200, {"ok": True}


# ---- apps and dependency apps ----

def _app(pkg):
    r = db.conn().execute("SELECT package, display_name, " + ", ".join(APP_URL_FIELDS) +
                          " FROM app WHERE package = ?", (pkg,)).fetchone()
    if r is None:
        raise server.HttpError(404, "app not found")
    return dict(r)


def put_app(req):
    pkg = server.validate_package(req.params["pkg"], "package")
    _app(pkg)
    b = req.json or {}
    with db.tx() as c:
        if "display_name" in b:
            c.execute("UPDATE app SET display_name = ? WHERE package = ?", (_display_name(b["display_name"]), pkg))
        for f in APP_URL_FIELDS:  # f from the fixed tuple
            if f in b:
                c.execute(f"UPDATE app SET {f} = ? WHERE package = ?", (_opt(b[f], URL_RE, f), pkg))
    return 200, _app(pkg)


def list_dependency_apps(req):
    rows = db.conn().execute("""SELECT d.id, d.package, d.display_name, d.upstream_id, u.downloaded_tag AS tag
        FROM dependency_app d JOIN upstream u ON u.id = d.upstream_id ORDER BY d.id""").fetchall()
    return 200, {"dependency_apps": [dict(r) for r in rows]}


def item_dependencies(item_id):
    from webui import upstream
    rows = db.conn().execute("""SELECT d.id, d.upstream_id, d.package, d.display_name, u.downloaded_tag
        FROM item_dependency x JOIN dependency_app d ON d.id = x.dependency_app_id
        JOIN upstream u ON u.id = d.upstream_id WHERE x.item_id = ? ORDER BY d.id""", (item_id,)).fetchall()
    out = []
    for r in rows:
        tag, path, signer = r["downloaded_tag"], None, None
        if tag:
            d = upstream.tag_dir(r["upstream_id"], tag)
            apks = sorted(d.glob("*.apk"))
            path = str(apks[0]) if apks else None
            sf = d / "signer.sha256"
            signer = (sf.read_text().strip() or None) if sf.is_file() else None
        if path is None:
            tag = signer = None
        out.append({"dependency_app_id": r["id"], "upstream_id": r["upstream_id"], "package": r["package"],
                    "display_name": r["display_name"], "tag": tag, "path": path, "signer_sha256": signer})
    return out
```

#### Task P2.4: Release check (`upstream.check`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/upstream.py` (created with `tag_dir` in Task P2.3)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/fixtures/catalog/release-stable.json`, `releases-list.json`, `release-tag.json`, `release-module-two.json`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_upstream.py` (class `CheckTest`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task P2.1 tables; `settings.github_token()` (P1); `events.emit` (P1); `catalog.now_iso`.
- Produces:
  - `upstream.API = "https://api.github.com"`
  - `upstream.http_get(url, headers=None, timeout=30) -> tuple[int, dict, bytes]` (status, lower-cased headers, body; HTTP error statuses returned, network errors raise `OSError`)
  - `upstream.DEFAULT_REGEX = {"patches": r"\.mpp$", "cli": r"^morphe-desktop-.*-all\.jar$", "module": r"\.apk$", "dependency": r"\.apk$"}`
  - `upstream.TokenRejected(Exception)`, `upstream.RateLimited(Exception)`
  - `upstream.select_release(channel, data) -> dict | None`, `upstream.match_assets(row, release) -> list[dict]`
  - `upstream.check(upstream_id, job_id=None) -> dict` (the updated row as dict; raises `TokenRejected` / `RateLimited` after recording `check_error`; `job_id` goes into the `upstream.new_version` payload)
  - `upstream.check_all() -> list[dict]`
  - `upstream.affected_items(upstream_id) -> list[int]`
  - Route handlers `list_upstreams` (`GET /api/upstreams` → rows + `source_ids`), `post_check` (`POST /api/upstreams/check` → `{"upstreams":[…]}`).

**Behavior:** Spec catalog §5.1 L524–534, §9 L609–613. stable → `/repos/{repo}/releases/latest`; prerelease → `/releases?per_page=10`, newest `published_at` among non-drafts; tag → `/releases/tags/{tag}`. Asset filter: row `asset_regex` (or `DEFAULT_REGEX[kind]`), skip `\.asc$`, for `stable` skip names containing `dev`. Module row with ≠ 1 match → `check_error="ambiguous module asset (<n> matches)"` (ci review S7). Errors: 401 → `token rejected`, raise `TokenRejected`; 403/429 → `rate limited until <x-ratelimit-reset as ISO>` (or `rate limited`), raise `RateLimited`; 404 → `not found`; no match → `no matching asset`; network error → `network error: <first line>`. `check_all` stops at `TokenRejected`/`RateLimited` (rest keep their old state). Success writes `latest_tag`, `latest_published_at`, `checked_at`, `check_error=NULL`; when `latest_tag != notified_tag` emits `upstream.new_version {upstream_id, tag, item_ids: affected_items, job_id}` once and sets `notified_tag` (`job_id` = the calling upstream job, null for `post_check` and ci, spec L54). `affected_items`: items of sources whose `upstream_id` or `cli_upstream_id` is the row, plus items linked through `dependency_app` → `item_dependency`. Headers: `Accept: application/vnd.github+json`, `User-Agent: Relume`, `Authorization: Bearer <token>` only when the token is non-empty.

**Constraints:** `urllib.request` only; never log the token.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_catalog_upstream.py'` green for: stable pick + `dev` and `.asc` skipped; prerelease newer than stable picked; stable newer than prerelease picked; draft skipped; tag channel URL; no matching asset; module with two matches → `ambiguous module asset (2 matches)` and `downloaded_tag` unchanged; 401 stops `check_all` after the first row; 429 message carries the reset time; two checks with the same tag emit one `upstream.new_version` with `job_id` null.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing tests** `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_upstream.py`

```python
"""Release check and pull tests (spec catalog §5, §9, §10)."""
import json
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest import mock

from webui import db, events, settings, upstream
from webui.tests.catalog_rows import CatalogCase

FIX = Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "catalog"
API = upstream.API


def fix(name):
    return json.loads((FIX / name).read_text())


class FakeHTTP:
    """url -> (status, body object or bytes, headers)."""

    def __init__(self, routes):
        self.routes = routes
        self.calls = []

    def __call__(self, url, headers=None, timeout=30):
        self.calls.append((url, headers or {}))
        st, body, hdr = self.routes.get(url, (404, {}, {}))
        return st, hdr, body if isinstance(body, bytes) else json.dumps(body).encode()


class UpstreamCase(CatalogCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        p = mock.patch.object(settings, "github_token", return_value="")
        p.start()
        self.addCleanup(p.stop)
        self.emitted = []
        p2 = mock.patch.object(events, "emit", side_effect=lambda t, pl: self.emitted.append((t, pl)))
        p2.start()
        self.addCleanup(p2.stop)

    def http(self, routes):
        f = FakeHTTP(routes)
        p = mock.patch.object(upstream, "http_get", f)
        p.start()
        self.addCleanup(p.stop)
        return f


class CheckTest(UpstreamCase):
    def test_stable_pick_skips_dev_and_asc(self):
        self.http({f"{API}/repos/MorpheApp/morphe-patches/releases/latest": (200, fix("release-stable.json"), {})})
        row = upstream.check(2)
        self.assertEqual((row["latest_tag"], row["check_error"]), ("v1.47.0", None))
        rel = fix("release-stable.json")
        self.assertEqual([a["name"] for a in upstream.match_assets(row, rel)], ["patches-1.47.0.mpp"])

    def test_prerelease_newest_non_draft(self):
        self.http({f"{API}/repos/MorpheApp/morphe-patches/releases?per_page=10": (200, fix("releases-list.json"), {})})
        self.assertEqual(upstream.check(3)["latest_tag"], "v1.48.0-dev.3")

    def test_prerelease_when_stable_is_newer(self):
        rels = fix("releases-list.json")
        rels[1]["published_at"] = "2026-10-16T00:00:00Z"
        self.http({f"{API}/repos/MorpheApp/morphe-patches/releases?per_page=10": (200, rels, {})})
        self.assertEqual(upstream.check(3)["latest_tag"], "v1.47.0")

    def test_tag_channel_url_and_no_matching_asset(self):
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO upstream(id, kind, repo, channel, tag) VALUES (5,'patches','indrastorms/Dropped-Patches','tag','v1.4.2')")
        f = self.http({f"{API}/repos/indrastorms/Dropped-Patches/releases/tags/v1.4.2": (200, fix("release-tag.json"), {})})
        row = upstream.check(5)
        self.assertEqual(f.calls[0][0], f"{API}/repos/indrastorms/Dropped-Patches/releases/tags/v1.4.2")
        self.assertEqual(row["check_error"], "no matching asset")
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute(r"UPDATE upstream SET asset_regex='\.jar$' WHERE id=5")
        self.assertIsNone(upstream.check(5)["check_error"])

    def test_module_with_two_assets_is_ambiguous(self):
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO upstream(id, kind, repo, channel, downloaded_tag) VALUES (6,'module','revenge-mod/revenge-xposed','stable','1800')")
        self.http({f"{API}/repos/revenge-mod/revenge-xposed/releases/latest": (200, fix("release-module-two.json"), {})})
        row = upstream.check(6)
        self.assertEqual(row["check_error"], "ambiguous module asset (2 matches)")
        self.assertEqual(row["downloaded_tag"], "1800")

    def test_401_stops_check_all(self):
        f = self.http({f"{API}/repos/MorpheApp/morphe-desktop/releases/latest": (401, {}, {})})
        rows = {r["id"]: r for r in upstream.check_all()}
        self.assertEqual(rows[1]["check_error"], "token rejected")
        self.assertEqual(len(f.calls), 1)
        self.assertIsNone(rows[2]["checked_at"])

    def test_429_message(self):
        self.http({f"{API}/repos/MorpheApp/morphe-desktop/releases/latest": (429, {}, {"x-ratelimit-reset": "1792500000"})})
        with self.assertRaises(upstream.RateLimited):
            upstream.check(1)
        self.assertEqual(upstream._row(1)["check_error"], "rate limited until 2026-10-20T12:40:00Z")

    def test_new_version_event_once(self):
        self.http({f"{API}/repos/MorpheApp/morphe-patches/releases/latest": (200, fix("release-stable.json"), {})})
        upstream.check(2)
        upstream.check(2)
        evs = [e for e in self.emitted if e[0] == "upstream.new_version"]
        self.assertEqual(evs, [("upstream.new_version", {"upstream_id": 2, "tag": "v1.47.0", "item_ids": [1, 2], "job_id": None})])

    def test_token_header(self):
        f = self.http({})
        with mock.patch.object(settings, "github_token", return_value="tok"):
            upstream.check(2)
        self.assertEqual(f.calls[0][1]["Authorization"], "Bearer tok")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

```python
"""Upstream release tracking: check, pull, inspect (spec catalog §5)."""
import json
import re
import urllib.error
import urllib.request
from datetime import datetime, timezone

from webui import catalog, db, events, server, settings

API = "https://api.github.com"
DEFAULT_REGEX = {"patches": r"\.mpp$", "cli": r"^morphe-desktop-.*-all\.jar$", "module": r"\.apk$", "dependency": r"\.apk$"}


class TokenRejected(Exception):
    pass


class RateLimited(Exception):
    pass


class _CheckError(Exception):
    pass


def http_get(url, headers=None, timeout=30):
    """(status, lower-cased headers, body). HTTP errors are returned; network errors raise OSError."""
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers or {})
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r:
            return r.status, {k.lower(): v for k, v in r.headers.items()}, r.read()
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        return e.code, {k.lower(): v for k, v in e.headers.items()}, e.read()


def tag_dir(upstream_id, tag):
    return db.DATA / "upstream" / str(upstream_id) / server.safe_remote(tag)


def _gh_headers():
    h = {"Accept": "application/vnd.github+json", "User-Agent": "Relume"}
    token = settings.github_token()
    if token:
        h["Authorization"] = "Bearer " + token
    return h


def _row(upstream_id):
    r = db.conn().execute("SELECT * FROM upstream WHERE id = ?", (upstream_id,)).fetchone()
    if r is None:
        raise KeyError(upstream_id)
    return dict(r)


def _set(upstream_id, **cols):
    # column names come from this module only
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute("UPDATE upstream SET " + ", ".join(f"{k} = ?" for k in cols) + " WHERE id = ?",
                  (*cols.values(), upstream_id))


def select_release(channel, data):
    if channel == "prerelease":
        rels = [r for r in data if isinstance(r, dict) and not r.get("draft")]
        return max(rels, key=lambda r: r.get("published_at") or "", default=None)
    return None if not isinstance(data, dict) or data.get("draft") else data


def match_assets(row, release):
    rx = re.compile(row["asset_regex"] or DEFAULT_REGEX[row["kind"]])
    out = []
    for a in release.get("assets", []):
        name = a.get("name", "")
        if name.endswith(".asc") or not rx.search(name):
            continue
        if row["channel"] == "stable" and "dev" in name:
            continue
        out.append(a)
    return out


def _release_url(row):
    if row["channel"] == "stable":
        return f"{API}/repos/{row['repo']}/releases/latest"
    if row["channel"] == "prerelease":
        return f"{API}/repos/{row['repo']}/releases?per_page=10"
    return f"{API}/repos/{row['repo']}/releases/tags/{row['tag']}"


def _fetch(row):
    try:
        status, headers, body = http_get(_release_url(row), _gh_headers())
    except OSError as e:
        raise _CheckError(("network error: " + str(e).splitlines()[0]) if str(e) else "network error")
    if status == 401:
        raise TokenRejected("token rejected")
    if status in (403, 429):
        reset = headers.get("x-ratelimit-reset", "")
        if reset.isdigit():
            raise RateLimited("rate limited until " +
                              datetime.fromtimestamp(int(reset), timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ"))
        raise RateLimited("rate limited")
    if status == 404:
        raise _CheckError("not found")
    if status != 200:
        raise _CheckError(f"http {status}")
    rel = select_release(row["channel"], json.loads(body))
    if rel is None:
        raise _CheckError("no release")
    assets = match_assets(row, rel)
    if not assets:
        raise _CheckError("no matching asset")
    if row["kind"] == "module" and len(assets) != 1:
        raise _CheckError(f"ambiguous module asset ({len(assets)} matches)")
    return rel, assets


def affected_items(upstream_id):
    rows = db.conn().execute("""SELECT i.id FROM item i JOIN source s ON s.id = i.source_id
        WHERE s.upstream_id = ? OR s.cli_upstream_id = ?
        UNION SELECT x.item_id FROM item_dependency x JOIN dependency_app d ON d.id = x.dependency_app_id
        WHERE d.upstream_id = ? ORDER BY 1""", (upstream_id, upstream_id, upstream_id)).fetchall()
    return [r[0] for r in rows]


def _check(upstream_id, job_id=None):
    """Check one row. Returns (row, assets); assets is None when check_error was set.
    TokenRejected / RateLimited are recorded, then re-raised."""
    row = _row(upstream_id)
    now = catalog.now_iso()
    try:
        rel, assets = _fetch(row)
    except (TokenRejected, RateLimited) as e:
        _set(upstream_id, checked_at=now, check_error=str(e))
        raise
    except _CheckError as e:
        _set(upstream_id, checked_at=now, check_error=str(e))
        return _row(upstream_id), None
    tag = rel["tag_name"]
    _set(upstream_id, latest_tag=tag, latest_published_at=rel.get("published_at"), checked_at=now, check_error=None)
    if tag != row["notified_tag"]:
        _set(upstream_id, notified_tag=tag)
        events.emit("upstream.new_version", {"upstream_id": upstream_id, "tag": tag,
                                             "item_ids": affected_items(upstream_id), "job_id": job_id})
    return _row(upstream_id), assets


def check(upstream_id, job_id=None):
    return _check(upstream_id, job_id)[0]


def _all_rows():
    rows = [dict(r) for r in db.conn().execute("SELECT * FROM upstream ORDER BY id")]
    for r in rows:
        r["source_ids"] = [x[0] for x in db.conn().execute(
            "SELECT id FROM source WHERE upstream_id = ? OR cli_upstream_id = ? ORDER BY id", (r["id"], r["id"]))]
    return rows


def check_all():
    for (uid,) in db.conn().execute("SELECT id FROM upstream ORDER BY id").fetchall():
        try:
            check(uid)
        except (TokenRejected, RateLimited):
            break
    return _all_rows()


def list_upstreams(req):
    return 200, {"upstreams": _all_rows()}


def post_check(req):
    return 200, {"upstreams": check_all()}


ROUTES = [
    ("GET", "/api/upstreams", list_upstreams),
    ("POST", "/api/upstreams/check", post_check),
]
```

#### Task P2.5: Pull job (`upstream.pull`)

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/upstream.py`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_upstream.py` (class `PullTest`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task P2.4; `jobs.register`, `jobs.enqueue`, `jobs.JobFailed`, handler `(job, log)` with `log.line`, `log.run(argv, cwd, env, timeout=None, on_line=None)` (P1); `upstream.inspect_row(upstream_id, tag, log)` (Task P2.6; in this task a function that returns at once, replaced in P2.6).
- Produces:
  - `upstream.http_download(url, dest, headers=None, timeout=300) -> int`
  - `upstream.APKSIGNER = ["apksigner"]` (argv prefix; tests set `["bash", <fixture script>]`)
  - `upstream.tag_dir(upstream_id, tag) -> pathlib.Path` (`db.DATA/upstream/<id>/<server.safe_remote(tag)>`)
  - `upstream.pull(upstream_id, log, rebuild=False, job_id=None) -> None` (X9; raises `jobs.JobFailed`; `job_id` passed to the step 1 check so `upstream.new_version` carries it)
  - `upstream.register()` → `jobs.register('upstream', _handler)`; `_handler(job, log)` = `pull(job.target["upstream_id"], log, rebuild=bool(job.options.get("rebuild", False)), job_id=job.id)`
  - Route handler `post_pull` (`POST /api/upstreams/{id}/pull`, body = options) → `(200, {"job_id": N})`; unknown id 404.

**Behavior:** Spec catalog §5.2 L536–547, §9 L613–614, §3 L474. Step 1 check (errors → `JobFailed(check_error)`); up to date when `latest_tag == downloaded_tag` and (row is not `patches` or every package of its items has `bundle_info`) → log `up to date`, return. Step 2 download every matching asset to `<tag>.part/`, verify `size` and, when `digest` starts with `sha256:`, the hash; mismatch or error → delete `.part`, keep old tag, set `check_error="download failed: <name>"`, `JobFailed`. Rename to `<tag>/`, set `downloaded_tag`. Dependency rows: run `apksigner verify --print-certs <apk>` via `log.run`, write `signer.sha256` (lowercase hex of `Signer #1 certificate SHA-256 digest`). Step 3 (patches only): if the sources' CLI rows have no `downloaded_tag`, pull them first (steps 1–2); then `inspect_row`. Step 4 keep the two newest tag dirs by mtime, delete older ones (never the current one). Step 5 `rebuild` and a new tag downloaded → `jobs.enqueue('build', {"item_id":N}, 'upstream')` for every enabled item of sources whose `upstream_id` is this row (patches/module rows only; a new CLI tag rebuilds nothing).

**Constraints:** atomic rename on the same filesystem; no partial `<tag>/` ever visible.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_catalog_upstream.py'` green for: fresh pull writes `<tag>/` and `downloaded_tag`; size mismatch → `.part` deleted, previous tag dir and `downloaded_tag` kept, `JobFailed`; digest mismatch same; third tag pulled → only two tag dirs left; `rebuild=True` + new tag enqueues builds of enabled items only (disabled skipped); `rebuild=False` enqueues none; a new module tag with `rebuild` enqueues exactly that source's enabled items (ops X10); CLI row without `downloaded_tag` is pulled in the same job before inspect; `_handler` of job 77 emits `upstream.new_version` with `job_id` 77; dependency pull writes `signer.sha256` from the `apksigner` stub; `post_pull` stores the body as job options.
