- [ ] **Step 4: Add the pull to `local/webui/upstream.py`**

Add to the imports: `import hashlib`, `import os`, `import shutil` and change the `webui` import line to `from webui import catalog, db, events, jobs, server, settings`. Add after `check_all`:

```python
APKSIGNER = ["apksigner"]


def http_download(url, dest, headers=None, timeout=300):
    req = urllib.request.Request(url, headers=headers or {"User-Agent": "Relume"})
    n = 0
    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as r, open(dest, "wb") as f:
        while chunk := r.read(1 << 20):
            f.write(chunk)
            n += len(chunk)
    return n


def _sha256(path):
    h = hashlib.sha256()
    with open(path, "rb") as f:
        for chunk in iter(lambda: f.read(1 << 20), b""):
            h.update(chunk)
    return h.hexdigest()


def _child_env():
    return {k: os.environ[k] for k in ("PATH", "HOME", "LANG", "TZ") if k in os.environ}


def _download(row, tag, assets, log):
    final = tag_dir(row["id"], tag)
    part = final.with_name(final.name + ".part")
    shutil.rmtree(part, ignore_errors=True)
    part.mkdir(parents=True)
    try:
        for a in assets:
            dest = part / server.safe_remote(a["name"])
            log.line(f"[+] downloading {a['name']}")
            http_download(a["browser_download_url"], dest)
            if dest.stat().st_size != a.get("size"):
                raise ValueError(f"size mismatch: {a['name']}")
            digest = a.get("digest") or ""
            if digest.startswith("sha256:") and _sha256(dest) != digest[7:]:
                raise ValueError(f"hash mismatch: {a['name']}")
    except (OSError, ValueError) as e:
        shutil.rmtree(part, ignore_errors=True)
        _set(row["id"], check_error=f"download failed: {e}")
        raise jobs.JobFailed(f"download failed: {e}")
    shutil.rmtree(final, ignore_errors=True)
    part.rename(final)
    _set(row["id"], downloaded_tag=tag)
    log.line(f"[+] {row['repo']} {tag} downloaded")
    return final


def _write_signer(final, log):
    apks = sorted(final.glob("*.apk"))
    if not apks:
        return
    found = []

    def on_line(text):
        m = re.match(r"Signer #1 certificate SHA-256 digest: ([0-9a-fA-F]+)$", text.strip())
        if m:
            found.append(m.group(1).lower())

    log.run([*APKSIGNER, "verify", "--print-certs", str(apks[0])], cwd=str(final), env=_child_env(), on_line=on_line)
    if found:
        (final / "signer.sha256").write_text(found[0] + "\n")
    else:
        log.line(f"[!] no signer digest for {apks[0].name}")


def _prune(upstream_id):
    base = db.DATA / "upstream" / str(upstream_id)
    cur = _row(upstream_id)["downloaded_tag"]
    keep = [tag_dir(upstream_id, cur).name] if cur else []
    dirs = sorted((d for d in base.iterdir() if d.is_dir() and not d.name.endswith(".part")),
                  key=lambda d: d.stat().st_mtime, reverse=True)
    for d in dirs:
        if d.name in keep:
            continue
        if len(keep) < 2:
            keep.append(d.name)
        else:
            shutil.rmtree(d, ignore_errors=True)


def _packages(upstream_id):
    return [r[0] for r in db.conn().execute("""SELECT DISTINCT i.package FROM item i JOIN source s ON s.id = i.source_id
        WHERE s.upstream_id = ? ORDER BY 1""", (upstream_id,))]


def _inspected(row):
    if row["kind"] != "patches":
        return True
    have = {r[0] for r in db.conn().execute("SELECT package FROM bundle_info WHERE upstream_id = ? AND tag = ?",
                                            (row["id"], row["downloaded_tag"]))}
    return set(_packages(row["id"])) <= have


def _fetch_row(upstream_id, log, job_id=None):
    """Pull steps 1-2 for one row. True when a new tag was downloaded."""
    try:
        row, assets = _check(upstream_id, job_id)
    except (TokenRejected, RateLimited) as e:
        raise jobs.JobFailed(str(e))
    if assets is None:
        raise jobs.JobFailed(row["check_error"])
    if row["latest_tag"] == row["downloaded_tag"] and tag_dir(upstream_id, row["downloaded_tag"]).is_dir():
        return False
    final = _download(row, row["latest_tag"], assets, log)
    if row["kind"] == "dependency":
        _write_signer(final, log)
    _prune(upstream_id)
    return True


def inspect_row(upstream_id, tag, log):
    """Inspect step; the real body lands in Task P2.6."""
    log.line(f"[!] inspect of upstream {upstream_id} {tag} skipped")


def pull(upstream_id, log, rebuild=False, job_id=None):
    try:
        row = _row(upstream_id)
    except KeyError:
        raise jobs.JobFailed("upstream not found")
    log.line(f"[+] pull {row['repo']} ({row['channel']}{' ' + row['tag'] if row['tag'] else ''})")
    new = _fetch_row(upstream_id, log, job_id)
    row = _row(upstream_id)
    if not new and _inspected(row):
        log.line("[+] up to date")
        return
    if row["kind"] == "patches":
        for (cli_id,) in db.conn().execute("SELECT DISTINCT cli_upstream_id FROM source WHERE upstream_id = ?",
                                           (upstream_id,)).fetchall():
            if _row(cli_id)["downloaded_tag"] is None:
                log.line(f"[+] CLI upstream {cli_id} not downloaded, pulling it first")
                _fetch_row(cli_id, log, job_id)
        inspect_row(upstream_id, row["downloaded_tag"], log)
    if new and rebuild and row["kind"] in ("patches", "module"):
        ids = [r[0] for r in db.conn().execute("""SELECT i.id FROM item i JOIN source s ON s.id = i.source_id
            WHERE s.upstream_id = ? AND i.enabled = 1 ORDER BY i.id""", (upstream_id,))]
        for iid in ids:
            jobs.enqueue("build", {"item_id": iid}, "upstream")
        log.line(f"[+] rebuild enqueued for {len(ids)} item(s)")


def _handler(job, log):
    pull(job.target["upstream_id"], log, rebuild=bool((job.options or {}).get("rebuild", False)), job_id=job.id)


def register():
    jobs.register("upstream", _handler)


def post_pull(req):
    uid = req.params["id"]
    if not db.conn().execute("SELECT 1 FROM upstream WHERE id = ?", (uid,)).fetchone():
        raise server.HttpError(404, "upstream not found")
    opts = req.json if isinstance(req.json, dict) else {}
    return 200, {"job_id": jobs.enqueue("upstream", {"upstream_id": uid}, "manual", options=opts)}
```

```python
]
```

#### Task P2.6: Inspector, patches and version routes, job input

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/upstream.py` (inspect + parser), `local/webui/catalog.py` (patches/version routes, `job_input`, `set_apkmirror_versions`)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/fixtures/catalog/list-patches.txt` (captured), `local/webui/tests/fixtures/catalog/options.json` (captured), `local/webui/tests/fixtures/catalog/fake-inspect.sh`, `local/webui/tests/fixtures/catalog/fake-inspect-fail.sh`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_inspect.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task P2.5 `tag_dir`, `pull`; P3 `inspect.sh` contract (outline Plan-fixed row); `src/build/test-fixtures/job.json` (Task P3.1).
- Produces:
  - `upstream.INSPECT` (default `<repo root>/src/build/inspect.sh`)
  - `upstream.parse_list_patches(text) -> list[dict]` — each `{name, description, enabled (bool|None), options:[{key, title, type, default, values:[…]}], packages:{package: [versions]}}`
  - `upstream.merge_bundle(list_text, options_json_text, package) -> list[dict]` — each `{name, description, default_enabled, options:[{key, title, type, default, values}], compatible_versions}`
  - `upstream.inspect_row(upstream_id, tag, log) -> None` (writes `bundle_info` per package)
  - `catalog.UpstreamMissing(Exception)` (X6)
  - `catalog.job_input(item_id) -> dict` (keys = fixture keys − `{job_id, work_dir}`)
  - `catalog.set_apkmirror_versions(package, versions: list[str]) -> None`
  - `catalog.stale_overrides(item_id) -> list[str]` (override names absent from the downloaded tag's `bundle_info`; `[]` without `bundle_info`; P4 logs them as `[!] stale override dropped: <name>`, spec L615)
  - Route handlers `get_patches`, `put_patches`, `put_version`.

**Behavior:** Spec catalog §4 L495–521, §5.2 step 3 L539–542, §5.3 L548–556, §9 L614–616. `inspect_row`: packages = distinct `item.package` of sources whose `upstream_id` is the row; per package a temp out dir, `log.run(["bash", INSPECT, cli_jar, package, out, *files], cwd=out, env=<PATH,HOME,LANG,TZ only>, timeout=120)`; exit ≠ 0 or `JobFailed('timeout')` → log `[!] inspect failed for <pkg>` and continue (no `bundle_info`, files and tag kept); success → `INSERT OR REPLACE bundle_info`. `cli_jar` = the single `.jar` in the CLI row's tag dir. Parser (layout of `utils.sh:195-201`): blocks start at `Index:`; `Name:`, `Description:`, `Enabled:`; `Options:` sub-blocks start at `Title:` with `Key:`, `Type:`, `Default:`, `Possible values:` (following indented lines); `Package name: <pkg>` then `Compatible versions:` (following lines starting with a digit). `merge_bundle`: `default_enabled` and option defaults from `options.json` (`[0].patches[name]`), descriptions/types/values/versions from the text; patches absent from `options.json` keep `default_enabled` from `Enabled:`; `compatible_versions` = versions for `package`, newest first (version-tuple sort). `GET /api/items/{id}/patches` → spec L505–511 (`409 {"error":"not a patches item"}` is P8's; here `404` unknown item, and when `bundle_info` is missing → `200 {"tag", "files", "patches": [], "stale_overrides": [], "compatible_versions": [], "unavailable": true}`). `PUT /api/items/{id}/patches {"overrides": {...}}` replaces the entries of the current bundle's patches; stale entries stay server-side; `{}` resets the bundle entries (bundle missing → 409 `patch list unavailable`; a name not in the bundle → 400; unknown option key → 400; type check Boolean→bool, Int/Long→int, Float/Double→number, String→str, *Array/List→list, other types any JSON value; `null` allowed). `PUT /api/items/{id}/version {version_mode, version_pin, allow_nearest}`: mode enum; pin slug required iff `pinned`, else stored null. `job_input` raises `UpstreamMissing("<source upstream|cli upstream> <id> not downloaded")` when a `downloaded_tag` is null; `options_file_json` = one `{"patches": overrides minus stale}` per patches file for `engine='morphe'`, `None` otherwise; stale = names not in the downloaded tag's `bundle_info` (no `bundle_info` → nothing is stale); `versions_due` true when `apkmirror_checked_at` is null or older than 24 h. `set_apkmirror_versions` writes the JSON list and `apkmirror_checked_at=now`.

**Constraints:** the parser must pass on the captured real output (Y2), not only on hand-written text.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_catalog_inspect.py'` green for: parser on the captured fixture (patch count = `grep -c '^Index:'` of the fixture, every patch has a name, at least one patch with options, YouTube has ≥ 1 compatible version); `merge_bundle` takes defaults from `options.json`; override merge incl. empty overrides → `[{"patches":{}}]` and a stale override dropped from `options_file_json` but listed in `GET …/patches`; `PUT …/patches` validation cases; `PUT …/version` cases; `job_input` raises `UpstreamMissing` for a missing patches tag and for a missing CLI tag; `set(job_input(id)) == set(fixture) - {"job_id","work_dir"}` and `recipe.variants[0]` keys equal the fixture's; `versions_due` true at null and at 25 h, false at 1 h; `inspect_row` with `fake-inspect.sh` writes `bundle_info`, and with a failing script writes none and does not raise; bundle missing with unchanged tag → `pull` re-inspects.

```python
                                             {"key": "count", "title": "Count", "type": "Int", "default": "3", "values": []}],

        self.assertTrue(m2 and all(isinstance(p["default_enabled"], bool) for p in m2))

        self.assertEqual(self.call(catalog.put_patches, json={"overrides": ok}, method="PUT", id=1)[0], 200)
        stored = json.loads(db.conn().execute("SELECT patch_overrides FROM item WHERE id=1").fetchone()[0])
        self.assertEqual(stored, {"Gone": {"enabled": True}, "Hide ads": {"enabled": True, "options": {"mode": "lite", "count": 4}}})
        self.call(catalog.put_patches, json={"overrides": {}}, method="PUT", id=1)

                {"Hide ads": {"options": {"count": "4"}}}, {"Hide ads": {"enabled": "yes"}}, {"Hide ads": {"extra": 1}}]
        for bad in bads:
            self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.put_patches, json={"overrides": bad}, method="PUT", id=1), 400, bad)
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.put_patches, json={"overrides": {}}, method="PUT", id=2), 409)

    def test_put_version(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.put_version, json={"version_mode": "pinned"}, method="PUT", id=1), 400)
        st, it = self.call(catalog.put_version, json={"version_mode": "pinned", "version_pin": "20.12.46"}, method="PUT", id=1)
        self.assertEqual(it["version"], {"mode": "pinned", "pin": "20.12.46", "allow_nearest": False})
        st, it = self.call(catalog.put_version, json={"version_mode": "latest", "version_pin": "x", "allow_nearest": True}, method="PUT", id=1)
        self.assertEqual(it["version"], {"mode": "latest", "pin": None, "allow_nearest": True})
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.put_version, json={"version_mode": "auto", "allow_nearest": "x"}, method="PUT", id=1), 400)
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.put_version, json={"version_mode": "never"}, method="PUT", id=1), 400)
```

```python
INSPECT = str(catalog.REPO_ROOT / "src" / "build" / "inspect.sh")
KNOWN_KEYS = {"Index", "Name", "Description", "Enabled", "Options", "Title", "Required", "Key", "Default",
              "Possible values", "Type", "Compatible packages", "Package name", "Compatible versions"}


def parse_list_patches(text):
    """Parse `list-patches --with-packages --with-versions --with-options` text (layout of utils.sh:195-201)."""
    patches, cur, opt, pkg, mode, sub = [], None, None, None, None, None
    for raw in text.splitlines():
        line = raw.strip()
        if not line:
            continue
        key, sep, val = line.partition(":")
        key, val = key.strip(), val.strip()
        known = bool(sep) and key in KNOWN_KEYS
        if known and key == "Index":
            cur = {"name": None, "description": "", "enabled": None, "options": [], "packages": {}}
            patches.append(cur)
            opt = pkg = mode = sub = None
            continue
        if cur is None:
            continue
        if not known:
            if sub == "values" and opt is not None:
                opt["values"].append(line)
            elif sub == "versions" and pkg is not None and line[:1].isdigit():
                cur["packages"][pkg].append(line.split()[0])
            continue
        sub = None
        if key == "Options":
            mode = "options"
            continue
        if key in ("Compatible packages", "Package name"):
            mode = "packages"
            if key == "Package name":
                pkg = val
                cur["packages"].setdefault(pkg, [])
            continue
        if mode == "options":
            if opt is None or (key in ("Title", "Key") and opt[key.lower()] is not None):
                opt = {"key": None, "title": None, "type": None, "default": None, "values": []}
                cur["options"].append(opt)
            if key == "Title":
                opt["title"] = val
            elif key == "Key":
                opt["key"] = val
            elif key == "Type":
                opt["type"] = val
            elif key == "Default":
                opt["default"] = val or None
            elif key == "Possible values":
                sub = "values"
            continue
        if mode == "packages":
            if key == "Compatible versions" and pkg is not None:
                sub = "versions"
            continue
        if key == "Name":
            cur["name"] = val
        elif key == "Description":
            cur["description"] = val
        elif key == "Enabled":
            cur["enabled"] = val.lower() == "true"
    return patches


def merge_bundle(list_text, options_text, package):
    try:
        defaults = json.loads(options_text)[0].get("patches", {})
    except (ValueError, IndexError, KeyError, AttributeError, TypeError):
        defaults = {}
    out = []
    for p in parse_list_patches(list_text):
        if not p["name"]:
            continue
        d = defaults.get(p["name"]) if isinstance(defaults.get(p["name"]), dict) else {}
        opt_defaults = d.get("options", {}) if isinstance(d.get("options"), dict) else {}
        out.append({"name": p["name"], "description": p["description"],
                    "default_enabled": d.get("enabled", p["enabled"]),
                    "options": [dict(o, default=opt_defaults.get(o["key"], o["default"])) for o in p["options"]],
                    "compatible_versions": sorted(p["packages"].get(package, []), key=catalog.version_key, reverse=True)})
    return out


def inspect_row(upstream_id, tag, log):
    files = sorted(str(p) for p in tag_dir(upstream_id, tag).iterdir() if p.is_file())
    clis = [r[0] for r in db.conn().execute(
        "SELECT DISTINCT cli_upstream_id FROM source WHERE upstream_id = ? ORDER BY 1", (upstream_id,))]
    if not clis:
        return
    cli = _row(clis[0])
    jars = sorted(tag_dir(cli["id"], cli["downloaded_tag"]).glob("*.jar")) if cli["downloaded_tag"] else []
    if not jars:
        log.line("[!] inspect skipped: CLI not downloaded")
        return
    for pkg in _packages(upstream_id):
        with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as out:
            try:
                rc = log.run(["bash", INSPECT, str(jars[0]), pkg, out, *files], cwd=out, env=_child_env(), timeout=120)
            except jobs.JobFailed as e:
                if str(e) == "cancelled":
                    raise
                rc = str(e)
            if rc != 0:
                log.line(f"[!] inspect failed for {pkg}: {rc}")
                continue
            lp = Path(out, "list-patches.txt").read_text(errors="replace")
            oj = Path(out, "options.json").read_text(errors="replace")
        patches = merge_bundle(lp, oj, pkg)
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT OR REPLACE INTO bundle_info(upstream_id, tag, package, patches) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                      (upstream_id, tag, pkg, json.dumps(patches)))
        log.line(f"[+] inspected {pkg}: {len(patches)} patches")
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implement in `local/webui/catalog.py`**

Change the datetime import to `from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone`; add after `item_dependencies`:

```python
class UpstreamMissing(Exception):
    """A needed upstream has no downloaded tag (build fails upstream_missing)."""


def _item_row(iid):
    r = db.conn().execute("SELECT i.*, s.upstream_id, s.cli_upstream_id, s.engine FROM item i JOIN source s ON s.id = i.source_id WHERE i.id = ?",
                          (iid,)).fetchone()
    if r is None:
        raise server.HttpError(404, "item not found")
    return r


def _bundle(r):
    """(tag, file names, bundle patches or None) for the item's downloaded patches tag."""
    from webui import upstream
    tag = db.conn().execute("SELECT downloaded_tag FROM upstream WHERE id = ?", (r["upstream_id"],)).fetchone()[0]
    if not tag:
        return None, [], None
    d = upstream.tag_dir(r["upstream_id"], tag)
    files = sorted(p.name for p in d.iterdir() if p.is_file()) if d.is_dir() else []
    b = db.conn().execute("SELECT patches FROM bundle_info WHERE upstream_id = ? AND tag = ? AND package = ?",
                          (r["upstream_id"], tag, r["package"])).fetchone()
    return tag, files, (json.loads(b[0]) if b else None)


def get_patches(req):
    r = _item_row(req.params["id"])
    tag, files, bundle = _bundle(r)
    ov = json.loads(r["patch_overrides"])
    if bundle is None:
        return 200, {"tag": tag, "files": files, "patches": [], "stale_overrides": [], "compatible_versions": [], "unavailable": True}
    names = {p["name"] for p in bundle}
    patches = []
    for p in bundle:
        o = ov.get(p["name"], {})
        values = o.get("options", {})
        patches.append({"name": p["name"], "description": p["description"], "default_enabled": p["default_enabled"],
                        "enabled": o.get("enabled", p["default_enabled"]),
                        "options": [dict(x, value=values.get(x["key"], x["default"])) for x in p["options"]],
                        "compatible_versions": p["compatible_versions"]})
    union = sorted({v for p in bundle for v in p["compatible_versions"]}, key=version_key, reverse=True)
    return 200, {"tag": tag, "files": files, "patches": patches, "stale_overrides": sorted(n for n in ov if n not in names),
                 "compatible_versions": union, "unavailable": False}


def _type_ok(t, v):
    if v is None:
        return True
    t = (t or "").lower()
    if t.endswith("array") or t.endswith("list") or t.startswith("list"):
        return isinstance(v, list)
    if t in ("boolean", "bool"):
        return isinstance(v, bool)
    if t in ("int", "integer", "long"):
        return isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool)
    if t in ("float", "double"):
        return isinstance(v, (int, float)) and not isinstance(v, bool)
    if t == "string":
        return isinstance(v, str)
    return isinstance(v, (str, int, float, bool, list))


def put_patches(req):
    r = _item_row(req.params["id"])
    _, _, bundle = _bundle(r)
    if bundle is None:
        raise server.HttpError(409, "patch list unavailable")
    ov = (req.json or {}).get("overrides")
    if not isinstance(ov, dict):
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid overrides")
    by_name = {p["name"]: p for p in bundle}
    # stale entries (not in this bundle) stay server-side; the body replaces the bundle entries ({} = reset)
    clean = {n: v for n, v in json.loads(r["patch_overrides"]).items() if n not in by_name}
    for name, o in ov.items():
        if name not in by_name:
            raise server.HttpError(400, f"unknown patch: {name}")
        if not isinstance(o, dict) or set(o) - {"enabled", "options"}:
            raise server.HttpError(400, f"invalid override: {name}")
        e = {}
        if "enabled" in o:
            if not isinstance(o["enabled"], bool):
                raise server.HttpError(400, f"invalid enabled: {name}")
            e["enabled"] = o["enabled"]
        if "options" in o:
            if not isinstance(o["options"], dict):
                raise server.HttpError(400, f"invalid options: {name}")
            types = {x["key"]: x["type"] for x in by_name[name]["options"]}
            for k, v in o["options"].items():
                if k not in types:
                    raise server.HttpError(400, f"unknown option: {name}.{k}")
                if not _type_ok(types[k], v):
                    raise server.HttpError(400, f"invalid value: {name}.{k}")
            e["options"] = o["options"]
        clean[name] = e
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute("UPDATE item SET patch_overrides = ? WHERE id = ?", (json.dumps(clean, sort_keys=True), r["id"]))
    return get_patches(req)


def put_version(req):
    r = _item_row(req.params["id"])
    b = req.json or {}
    mode = b.get("version_mode")
    if mode not in ("auto", "pinned", "latest"):
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid version_mode")
    pin = server.validate_slug(b.get("version_pin"), "version_pin") if mode == "pinned" else None
    near = b.get("allow_nearest", False)
    if not isinstance(near, bool):
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid allow_nearest")
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute("UPDATE item SET version_mode = ?, version_pin = ?, allow_nearest = ? WHERE id = ?", (mode, pin, int(near), r["id"]))
    return 200, item_detail(r["id"])


def stale_overrides(item_id):
    r = _item_row(item_id)
    _, _, bundle = _bundle(r)
    if bundle is None:
        return []
    names = {p["name"] for p in bundle}
    return sorted(n for n in json.loads(r["patch_overrides"]) if n not in names)


def _versions_due(checked_at):
    if checked_at is None:
        return True
    t = datetime.strptime(checked_at, "%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ").replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    return t < datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=24)


def job_input(item_id):
    from webui import upstream
    r = db.conn().execute("""SELECT i.*, s.engine, s.keystore, s.upstream_id, s.cli_upstream_id,
        a.apkmirror_list_url, a.apkmirror_example_url, a.apkmirror_example_url2, a.apkpure_url, a.apkmirror_checked_at,
        u.downloaded_tag AS up_tag, c.downloaded_tag AS cli_tag
        FROM item i JOIN source s ON s.id = i.source_id JOIN app a ON a.package = i.package
        JOIN upstream u ON u.id = s.upstream_id JOIN upstream c ON c.id = s.cli_upstream_id WHERE i.id = ?""",
                          (item_id,)).fetchone()
    if r is None:
        raise UpstreamMissing(f"item {item_id} not found")
    if not r["up_tag"]:
        raise UpstreamMissing(f"source upstream {r['upstream_id']} not downloaded")
    if not r["cli_tag"]:
        raise UpstreamMissing(f"cli upstream {r['cli_upstream_id']} not downloaded")
    pdir, cdir = upstream.tag_dir(r["upstream_id"], r["up_tag"]), upstream.tag_dir(r["cli_upstream_id"], r["cli_tag"])
    files = sorted(str(p) for p in pdir.iterdir() if p.is_file() and p.name != "signer.sha256") if pdir.is_dir() else []
    jars = sorted(str(p) for p in cdir.glob("*.jar")) if cdir.is_dir() else []
    if not files or not jars:
        raise UpstreamMissing("downloaded files missing on disk")
    options = None
    if r["engine"] == "morphe":
        stale = set(stale_overrides(item_id))
        ov = {k: v for k, v in json.loads(r["patch_overrides"]).items() if k not in stale}
        options = [{"patches": ov} for _ in files]
    return {
        "item_id": r["id"], "source_id": r["source_id"], "package": r["package"], "key": r["key"],
        "engine": r["engine"], "engine_options": _j(r["engine_options"]),
        "upstream_tag": r["up_tag"], "cli_tag": r["cli_tag"], "patches_files": files, "cli_jar": jars[0],
        "options_file_json": options,
        "version": {"mode": r["version_mode"], "value": r["version_pin"], "near": bool(r["allow_nearest"])},
        "apkmirror": {"list_url": r["apkmirror_list_url"], "example_url": r["apkmirror_example_url"],
                      "example_url2": r["apkmirror_example_url2"], "versions_due": _versions_due(r["apkmirror_checked_at"])},
        "apkpure_url": r["apkpure_url"], "keystore": r["keystore"], "recipe": {"variants": json.loads(r["variants"])},
    }


def set_apkmirror_versions(package, versions):
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute("UPDATE app SET apkmirror_versions = ?, apkmirror_checked_at = ? WHERE package = ?",
                  (json.dumps(list(versions)), now_iso(), package))
```

#### Task P2.7: Icon fetcher and icon routes

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/catalog.py`
- Create: `local/webui/tests/fixtures/catalog/play-youtube.html`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_store.py` (class `IconTest`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `upstream.http_get` (Task P2.4); `server.FileResponse(path, download_name=None, cache_seconds=0)` (planner-outline ask A).
- Produces: `catalog.PLAY_URL = "https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id={}&hl=en"`; `catalog.fetch_icon(package) -> bool`; `catalog.start()` (daemon thread `catalog-icons`, waits on `catalog._icon_wake` up to 3600 s between scans); route handlers `get_icon`, `post_icon_refresh`.

**Behavior:** Spec catalog §3.2–3.3 L485–494. Scan = apps with `icon_fetched_at IS NULL`. `fetch_icon`: GET the Play page; `og:title` minus ` - Apps on Google Play` → `app.display_name` only when it is null; `og:image` URL (must be `https://`) → GET image, content type `image/png|webp|jpeg` → `icons/<package>.<png|webp|jpg>`; sets `icon_file` (file name) and `icon_fetched_at`. Any failure sets only `icon_fetched_at` (no retry until refresh) and returns False. `GET /api/apps/{pkg}/icon` → `FileResponse(path, cache_seconds=86400)`, 404 when no icon. `POST …/icon/refresh` clears `icon_fetched_at`, sets the wake event, returns `{"queued": true}`. Seed import and `register()` never fetch (ci §2).

**Constraints:** no APKMirror request (`grep apkmirror.com` = 0 in `catalog.py`/`upstream.py`).

```python
from pathlib import Path
from unittest import mock

from webui import upstream

PLAY = (Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "catalog" / "play-youtube.html").read_bytes()


class IconTest(CatalogCase):
    def fake_http(self, play_status=200):
        def get(url, headers=None, timeout=30):
            if url.startswith("https://play.google.com/"):
                return play_status, {}, PLAY if play_status == 200 else b""
            if url.startswith("https://play-lh.googleusercontent.com/"):
                return 200, {"content-type": "image/png"}, b"\x89PNG\r\n"
            return 404, {}, b""
        return mock.patch.object(upstream, "http_get", get)

    def test_fetch_sets_name_and_icon(self):
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE app SET display_name='Mine' WHERE package='com.google.android.youtube'")
        with self.fake_http():
            self.assertTrue(catalog.fetch_icon("com.reddit.frontpage"))
            self.assertTrue(catalog.fetch_icon("com.google.android.youtube"))
        r = db.conn().execute("SELECT display_name, icon_file, icon_fetched_at FROM app WHERE package='com.reddit.frontpage'").fetchone()
        self.assertEqual((r["display_name"], r["icon_file"]), ("YouTube", "com.reddit.frontpage.png"))
        self.assertTrue((self.data / "icons" / "com.reddit.frontpage.png").is_file())
        self.assertEqual(catalog.item_detail(2)["icon_url"], "/api/apps/com.reddit.frontpage/icon")
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute("SELECT display_name FROM app WHERE package='com.google.android.youtube'").fetchone()[0], "Mine")
        self.assertIsInstance(self.call(catalog.get_icon, method="GET", pkg="com.reddit.frontpage"), server.FileResponse)

    def test_fetch_failure_is_letter_tile(self):
        with self.fake_http(play_status=404):
            self.assertFalse(catalog.fetch_icon("com.reddit.frontpage"))
        r = db.conn().execute("SELECT icon_file, icon_fetched_at FROM app WHERE package='com.reddit.frontpage'").fetchone()
        self.assertIsNone(r["icon_file"])
        self.assertIsNotNone(r["icon_fetched_at"])
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(catalog.get_icon, method="GET", pkg="com.reddit.frontpage"), 404)

    def test_refresh_clears_fetched_at(self):

            catalog.fetch_icon("com.reddit.frontpage")
        catalog._icon_wake.clear()
        self.assertEqual(self.call(catalog.post_icon_refresh, json={}, pkg="com.reddit.frontpage")[1], {"ok": True})
        self.assertIsNone(db.conn().execute("SELECT icon_fetched_at FROM app WHERE package='com.reddit.frontpage'").fetchone()[0])
        self.assertTrue(catalog._icon_wake.is_set())
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Add to `local/webui/catalog.py`**

Add `import html`, `import threading`, `import traceback` to the imports, then:

```python
PLAY_URL = "https://play.google.com/store/apps/details?id={}&hl=en"
OG_RE = re.compile(r'<meta[^>]+property="og:(title|image)"[^>]+content="([^"]*)"')
ICON_EXT = {"image/png": "png", "image/webp": "webp", "image/jpeg": "jpg"}
_icon_wake = threading.Event()


def fetch_icon(package):
    """Play og:title / og:image (catalog §3.2-3.3). False on any failure (UI shows a letter tile)."""
    from webui import upstream
    now = now_iso()
    try:
        st, _, body = upstream.http_get(PLAY_URL.format(package), {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"}, timeout=20)
        if st != 200:
            raise ValueError(f"play http {st}")
        og = dict(OG_RE.findall(body.decode("utf-8", "replace")))
        title = html.unescape(og.get("title", "")).removesuffix(" - Apps on Google Play").strip()
        img = html.unescape(og.get("image", ""))
        if not img.startswith("https://"):
            raise ValueError("no og:image")
        st, headers, data = upstream.http_get(img, {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0"}, timeout=20)
        ext = ICON_EXT.get(headers.get("content-type", "").split(";")[0].strip())
        if st != 200 or not ext or not data:
            raise ValueError("bad icon response")
        d = db.DATA / "icons"
        d.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        name = f"{package}.{ext}"
        (d / name).write_bytes(data)
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE app SET icon_fetched_at = ? WHERE package = ?", (now, package))
        return False
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute("UPDATE app SET icon_file = ?, icon_fetched_at = ? WHERE package = ?", (name, now, package))
        if title:
            c.execute("UPDATE app SET display_name = ? WHERE package = ? AND display_name IS NULL", (title, package))
    return True


def _icon_loop():
    while True:
        try:
            for (pkg,) in db.conn().execute("SELECT package FROM app WHERE icon_fetched_at IS NULL ORDER BY package").fetchall():
                fetch_icon(pkg)
        except Exception:  # keep the fetcher alive; the error goes to the server log
            traceback.print_exc()
        _icon_wake.wait(3600)
        _icon_wake.clear()


def start():
    threading.Thread(target=_icon_loop, name="catalog-icons", daemon=True).start()


def get_icon(req):
    pkg = server.validate_package(req.params["pkg"], "package")
    r = db.conn().execute("SELECT icon_file FROM app WHERE package = ?", (pkg,)).fetchone()
    path = db.DATA / "icons" / r["icon_file"] if r and r["icon_file"] else None
    if path is None or not path.is_file():
        raise server.HttpError(404, "no icon")
    return server.FileResponse(str(path), cache_seconds=86400)


def post_icon_refresh(req):
    pkg = server.validate_package(req.params["pkg"], "package")
    _app(pkg)
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute("UPDATE app SET icon_fetched_at = NULL WHERE package = ?", (pkg,))
    _icon_wake.set()
    return 200, {"ok": True}
```

Append to `ROUTES`:

```python
    ("POST", "/api/apps/{pkg}/icon/refresh", post_icon_refresh),
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task P2.1–P2.3 tables; P1 `schedules` table (X2).
- Produces: `catalog.SEED_PATH`; `catalog.seed_import(path=None) -> bool` (True = imported); `catalog.export_seed() -> dict`; `catalog.seed_text() -> str` (X7). Seed format (top-level keys, every list sorted by its id / natural key): `upstreams[{id, kind, repo, channel, tag, asset_regex}]`, `sources[{id, name, upstream_id, cli_upstream_id, engine, keystore}]`, `apps[{package, display_name, apkmirror_list_url, apkmirror_example_url, apkmirror_example_url2, apkpure_url}]`, `groups[{id, name, color, position}]`, `items[{id, source_id, package, key, display_name, group_id, position, enabled, version_mode, version_pin, allow_nearest, variants, patch_overrides, engine_options}]`, `dependency_apps[{id, upstream_id, package, display_name}]`, `item_dependencies[[item_id, dependency_app_id]]`.

**Behavior:** Spec catalog §8 L596–603, ci §4.4 L1276–1277. Import only when `source` and `item` are empty; one transaction; explicit ids kept, so `export_seed()` after import equals the file. Adds the daily upstream schedule (X2) in the same transaction, only when no `schedules` row with `kind='upstream'` and name `Daily upstream` exists. `export_seed()` reads the catalog tables only (no state columns: no tags, icons, APKMirror versions).

**Constraints:** `json` stdlib; `seed_text()` byte-identical to the committed file.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_catalog_seed.py'` green for: import into an empty DB returns True, second call returns False and adds nothing; one enabled `upstream` schedule with spec `{"every":"day","at":"09:00"}`, target `{"all":true}`, options `{"rebuild":true}`, and none added by a second import; `seed_text()` after import equals the committed file bytes.

**Steps:**

```python
"""Seed import / export tests (spec catalog §8, §10, ci §4.4)."""
import json
import unittest

from webui import catalog, db
from webui.tests import helpers


class SeedImportTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()

    def test_import_once_with_daily_schedule(self):
        self.assertTrue(catalog.seed_import())
        self.assertFalse(catalog.seed_import())
        rows = db.conn().execute("SELECT name, kind, target, spec, options, enabled FROM schedules").fetchall()
        self.assertEqual(len(rows), 1)
        r = rows[0]
        self.assertEqual((r["name"], r["kind"], r["enabled"]), ("Daily upstream", "upstream", 1))
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(r["target"]), {"all": True})
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(r["spec"]), {"every": "day", "at": "09:00"})
        self.assertIs(json.loads(r["options"])["rebuild"], True)
        self.assertIsNotNone(db.conn().execute("SELECT next_run_at FROM schedules").fetchone()[0])
        seeded = json.loads(catalog.SEED_PATH.read_text())
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM item").fetchone()[0], len(seeded["items"]))

    def test_export_round_trip_bytes(self):
        catalog.seed_import()
        self.assertEqual(catalog.seed_text(), catalog.SEED_PATH.read_text())


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add to `local/webui/catalog.py`**

```python
SEED_PATH = Path(__file__).with_name("catalog-seed.json")
# seed key -> (table, columns, order) ; insert order follows the foreign keys
SEED_TABLES = {
    "upstreams": ("upstream", ("id", "kind", "repo", "channel", "tag", "asset_regex"), "id"),
    "apps": ("app", ("package", "display_name", "apkmirror_list_url", "apkmirror_example_url",
                     "apkmirror_example_url2", "apkpure_url"), "package"),
    "sources": ("source", ("id", "name", "upstream_id", "cli_upstream_id", "engine", "keystore"), "id"),
    "groups": ("grp", ("id", "name", "color", "position"), "id"),
    "items": ("item", ("id", "source_id", "package", "key", "display_name", "group_id", "position", "enabled",
                       "version_mode", "version_pin", "allow_nearest", "variants", "patch_overrides", "engine_options"), "id"),
    "dependency_apps": ("dependency_app", ("id", "upstream_id", "package", "display_name"), "id"),
}
JSON_COLS = {"variants", "patch_overrides", "engine_options"}
BOOL_COLS = {"enabled", "allow_nearest"}
DAILY_SCHEDULE = ("Daily upstream", "upstream", {"all": True}, {"every": "day", "at": "09:00"}, {"rebuild": True})


def _to_db(col, v):
    if col in JSON_COLS:
        return None if v is None else json.dumps(v, sort_keys=True)
    if col in BOOL_COLS:
        return int(bool(v))
    return v


def _from_db(col, v):
    if col in JSON_COLS:
        return None if v is None else json.loads(v)
    if col in BOOL_COLS:
        return bool(v)
    return v


def seed_import(path=None):
    """Import the seed only into empty catalog tables (catalog §8). True when imported."""
    data = json.loads(Path(path or SEED_PATH).read_text())
    with db.tx() as c:
        if c.execute("SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM source) OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM item)").fetchone()[0]:
            return False
        for key, (table, cols, _) in SEED_TABLES.items():  # names from the fixed table above
            for row in data.get(key, []):
                c.execute(f"INSERT INTO {table}({', '.join(cols)}) VALUES ({', '.join('?' * len(cols))})",
                          [_to_db(k, row.get(k)) for k in cols])
        c.executemany("INSERT INTO item_dependency(item_id, dependency_app_id) VALUES (?, ?)",
                      [tuple(x) for x in data.get("item_dependencies", [])])
    name, kind, target, spec, options = DAILY_SCHEDULE
    if not db.conn().execute("SELECT 1 FROM schedules WHERE kind = ? AND name = ?", (kind, name)).fetchone():
        from webui import scheduler
        scheduler.create(name, kind, target, spec, options)  # computes next_run_at (P1)
    return True


def export_seed():
    c = db.conn()
    out = {}
    for key, (table, cols, order) in SEED_TABLES.items():
        out[key] = [{k: _from_db(k, r[k]) for k in cols}
                    for r in c.execute(f"SELECT {', '.join(cols)} FROM {table} ORDER BY {order}")]
    out["item_dependencies"] = [list(r) for r in c.execute(
        "SELECT item_id, dependency_app_id FROM item_dependency ORDER BY item_id, dependency_app_id")]
    return out


def seed_text():
    """Exact bytes of catalog-seed.json; `python3 -m webui export-seed` prints this (ci §4.4)."""
    return json.dumps(export_seed(), indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n"
```

#### Task P2.9: Generate the real seed (scratch script) and the seed report

**Files:**
- Scratch (not committed): `.claude/tmp/seed_inspect.sh`, `.claude/tmp/make_catalog_seed.py`, `.claude/tmp/seed-inspect/<source_id>/list-patches.txt`
- Replace: `local/webui/catalog-seed.json`
- Create: `docs/logs/2026-10-19-seed-report.md`
- Modify: `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_seed.py` (committed-seed checks)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `upstream.parse_list_patches` (Task P2.6); repo inputs `local/build.sh:17-34`, `src/build/*.sh`, `src/patches/**`, `src/options/*.json` (incl. untracked `hushfacebook.json`, `piko-newx.json`, read only), `src/build/helper/apps.json`; `local/work/<org>-<run>/` patches files (Y2).
- Produces: committed `local/webui/catalog-seed.json` (format of Task P2.8, text = `catalog.seed_text()` bytes); `docs/logs/2026-10-19-seed-report.md`.

**Behavior:** Spec catalog §8 L594–603, §4.2 L520, xposed §4.1 L1455–1481. Rules (decided here):
- Sources: `local/build.sh` rows in order, minus `scrazzz-patches` (no script) = 17; `id` = column 1, `name` = column 2; plus the 3 Xposed sources of spec L1466 = 20.
- Upstreams: per script the `dl_gh <repo> <owner> <channel>` line whose repo is not `morphe-desktop`, `revanced-cli`, `revanced-integrations` → `kind='patches'`, channel `latest→stable`, `prerelease→prerelease`, else `tag`; `asset_regex` `\.mpp$`, except BiliRoamingM and Dropped-Patches `\.jar$` (their releases ship `.jar` patches, Y2; Q3 says the first build shows whether they work). One CLI row `MorpheApp/morphe-desktop` stable `^morphe-desktop-.*-all\.jar$` for all 17. Xposed rows of spec L1459–1464. Dependency row `MorpheApp/MicroG-RE` stable `^microg-[0-9.]+\.apk$`.
- Items: walk each script's uncommented lines in order with state (`get_patches_key K`, `version="v"`, `near_version="1"`; `prefer_version` ignored per spec L519). `get_apk`/`get_apkpure`/`req` record a download under its file stem; `split_editor IN OUT [exclude "L"]` records a derived input (`exclude_splits` = `[]` or the list); `patch NAME ORG` / `patch_multi NAME ORG` add a variant; `split_arch NAME ORG` inside `for i in {0..3}` adds 4 variants with `strip_libs` = `arm64-v8a, armeabi-v7a, x86_64, x86`. Item = (source, package); `key` = first `patch`/`split_arch` NAME of the item; variant `name` = `all-splits` when `exclude_splits == []` (spec L722), else the `abi`; `abi` = `strip_libs`, else `download.arch` if in `ABIS`, else the NAME suffix `-<abi>`, else `universal`. Q5: keep variants with abi `universal`/`arm64-v8a`; an item with neither keeps all and is listed. `req` package map: `tasker.joaoapps.com` → `net.dinglisch.android.taskerm` (download kind `url`).
- Version: `version=` before the item's download → `pinned`; `near_version="1"` → `allow_nearest`; else `auto`, except `latest` when the seed-time inspect of the source lists no compatible version for the package; no inspect output → `auto` and listed as "not inspected".
- Overrides (referenced dirs/files only, catalog review N5): options file `src/options/<ORG>.json` entries `[*].patches` (`meta` dropped), then `exclude-patches` lines → `{"enabled": false}`, `include-patches` lines (text before `|`) → `{"enabled": true}` (keeps options). Options-file entries whose patch is absent from the item's seed-time inspect are dropped and listed (catalog parking item).
- Apps from `apps.json` (`apkmirror[pkg]`, `apkpure[pkg].download_url`); Tasker without URLs.
- Groups: one per org source in order (name = source name), then `Xposed`; colour null.
- Dependency: `dependency_app` MicroG RE, package `app.revanced.android.gms` (unverified offline, listed in the report as a user check), linked to every item whose package is `com.google.android.youtube`, `com.google.android.apps.youtube.music`, `com.google.android.apps.photos`, and to hoo-dles `pl.solidexplorer2` (spec L474).
- Xposed items: the 8 rows of spec L1470–1479 verbatim (key, pin, download, abi, `engine_options`); `version_mode` `latest` except Facebook `pinned`.

**Constraints:** the scratch script imports `webui.upstream` for the parser (no copy); it never writes outside `.claude/tmp/`, the seed file and the report.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_catalog_seed.py'` green with the generated seed: 20 sources; 64 items; 8 Xposed items with Facebook `pinned` `580.0.0.51.74`; no variant abi other than `universal`/`arm64-v8a` except in items whose ids the report lists under "kept all ABIs"; every non-empty referenced `src/patches/<K>` line and every `src/options/<ORG>.json` patch present as an override of the items that reference them (minus the report's "dropped stale" list); `seed_text()` equals the file bytes.
- [ ] `python3 -c "import json;d=json.load(open('local/webui/catalog-seed.json'));print(len(d['sources']),len(d['items']))"` prints `20 64`.
- [ ] `grep -c '^| ' docs/logs/2026-10-19-seed-report.md` ≥ 64 and the report has sections `## Lines`, `## Items per source`, `## Kept all ABIs`, `## Version modes`, `## Dropped stale overrides`, `## Asset names old → new`, `## User checks` (`grep -c '^## ' ` = 7).

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the committed-seed checks** (append to `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_seed.py`, before the `__main__` guard) and see them fail on the minimal seed

```python
import re
from pathlib import Path

REPORT = catalog.REPO_ROOT / "docs" / "logs" / "2026-10-19-seed-report.md"


class CommittedSeedTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.d = json.loads(catalog.SEED_PATH.read_text())

    def test_counts(self):
        self.assertEqual(len(self.d["sources"]), 20)
        self.assertEqual(len(self.d["items"]), 64)
        xp = [i for i in self.d["items"] if i["source_id"] in ("revenge-lspatch", "nexalloy-lspatch", "nexalloy-npatch")]
        self.assertEqual(len(xp), 8)
        fb = [i for i in xp if i["package"] == "com.facebook.katana"][0]
        self.assertEqual((fb["version_mode"], fb["version_pin"]), ("pinned", "580.0.0.51.74"))

    def test_abis(self):
        kept_all = set(int(x) for x in re.findall(r"^- item (\d+)", REPORT.read_text().split("## Kept all ABIs")[1].split("\n## ")[0], re.M))
        for it in self.d["items"]:
            abis = {v["abi"] for v in it["variants"]}
            if it["id"] not in kept_all:
                self.assertLessEqual(abis, {"universal", "arm64-v8a"}, it["id"])

    def test_report_lines_match_items(self):
        mapped = re.findall(r"^\| `src/build/[^`]+:\d+` \| (\d+) \|", REPORT.read_text(), re.M)
        self.assertEqual(len({int(m) for m in mapped}), len(self.d["items"]))

    def test_overrides_present(self):
        root = catalog.REPO_ROOT
        # every non-empty referenced include/exclude line appears in some item's overrides
        refs = set()
        for f in (root / "src" / "build").glob("*.sh"):
            refs |= set(re.findall(r'^\s*get_patches_key "([^"]+)"', f.read_text(), re.M))
        names = {n for it in self.d["items"] for n in it["patch_overrides"]}
        for k in refs:
            for fn in ("include-patches", "exclude-patches"):
                p = root / "src" / "patches" / k / fn
                for line in (p.read_text().splitlines() if p.is_file() else []):
                    if line.strip():
                        self.assertIn(line.split("|")[0].strip(), names, f"{k}/{fn}")

    def test_round_trip(self):
        catalog.seed_import()
        self.assertEqual(catalog.seed_text(), catalog.SEED_PATH.read_text())
```

Run: `T_PY 'test_catalog_seed.py'` → FAIL `test_counts` (`1 != 20`), ERROR on the missing report.

Note: `src/patches` and `src/build/*.sh` are deleted in round 2 (P11); this test class reads them only while they exist. P11 deletes `test_overrides_present` and `test_report_lines_match_items` together with the inputs (they then have nothing to check), keeping `test_counts`, `test_abis`, `test_round_trip`. Tell planner-ops (P11 owner).

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the seed-time inspect script** `.claude/tmp/seed_inspect.sh` (scratch)

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Seed-time inspect (Task P2.9, catalog review S2): list-patches of one source from its files in local/work (offline).
# Usage (repo root): docker run --rm --network none -v "$PWD:/repo:ro" -v "$PWD/.claude/tmp/seed-inspect:/out" -w /repo relume-builder bash .claude/tmp/seed_inspect.sh <org>
set -o pipefail
org=$1
cli=local/work/morphe-1/morphe-desktop-1.18.1-all.jar
dir=$(ls -d local/work/"$org"-* 2>/dev/null | sort | head -1)
[ -n "$dir" ] || { echo "[-] no local/work dir for $org"; exit 1; }
mapfile -t files < <(ls "$dir"/*.mpp 2>/dev/null; ls "$dir"/*.jar 2>/dev/null | grep -vE 'morphe-desktop|revanced-cli|APKEditor')
[ ${#files[@]} -gt 0 ] || { echo "[-] no patches files in $dir"; exit 1; }
mkdir -p "/out/$org"
java -jar "$cli" list-patches --with-packages --with-versions --with-options --patches "${files[@]}" | sed -E 's/\x1b\[[0-9;]*m//g' > "/out/$org/list-patches.txt"
rc=$?
printf '%s\n' "${files[@]}" > "/out/$org/files.txt"
echo "[+] $org rc=$rc $(grep -c '^Index:' "/out/$org/list-patches.txt") patches"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the generator** `.claude/tmp/make_catalog_seed.py` (scratch)

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""One-off seed generator (spec catalog §8). Scratch, never committed.
Run (repo root): docker run --rm --network none -v "$PWD:/repo" -w /repo -e PYTHONPATH=/repo/local -e PYTHONDONTWRITEBYTECODE=1 relume-builder python3 .claude/tmp/make_catalog_seed.py
Writes local/webui/catalog-seed.json and docs/logs/2026-10-19-seed-report.md."""
import json
import shlex
from pathlib import Path

from webui import upstream as up

ROOT = Path.cwd()
ABIS = ("arm64-v8a", "armeabi-v7a", "x86", "x86_64", "universal")
SPLIT_ARCHS = ("arm64-v8a", "armeabi-v7a", "x86_64", "x86")  # utils.sh:878 archs[] at 9fd15dc
NOT_PATCHES = {"morphe-desktop", "revanced-cli", "revanced-integrations"}
JAR_SOURCES = {"biliroamingm", "dropped-patches"}
REQ_PACKAGES = {"tasker.joaoapps.com": "net.dinglisch.android.taskerm"}
MICROG_PACKAGES = {"com.google.android.youtube", "com.google.android.apps.youtube.music", "com.google.android.apps.photos"}
MICROG_EXTRA = {("hoo-dles", "pl.solidexplorer2")}
INSPECT = ROOT / ".claude/tmp/seed-inspect"
APPS = json.loads((ROOT / "src/build/helper/apps.json").read_text())
CHANNEL = {"latest": "stable", "prerelease": "prerelease"}

XPOSED_UPSTREAMS = [("cli", "JingMatrix/LSPatch", "stable", None, r"^lspatch-.*-release\.jar$"),
                    ("cli", "7723mod/NPatch", "tag", "v1.0.5", r"^jar-.*-release\.jar$"),
                    ("module", "revenge-mod/revenge-xposed", "stable", None, r"^app-release\.apk$"),
                    ("module", "gnadgnaoh/NexAlloy", "tag", "v1.1", r"^NexAlloy-.*\.apk$")]
XPOSED_SOURCES = [("revenge-lspatch", "Revenge", "JingMatrix/LSPatch", "revenge-mod/revenge-xposed", "lspatch", "lspatch-signing.p12"),
                  ("nexalloy-lspatch", "NexAlloy", "JingMatrix/LSPatch", "gnadgnaoh/NexAlloy", "lspatch", "lspatch-signing.p12"),
                  ("nexalloy-npatch", "NexAlloy", "7723mod/NPatch", "gnadgnaoh/NexAlloy", "npatch", "fiorenmas.ks")]
# source, package, key, pin, kind, type, arch, dpi, minver, abi, sigbypass, injectdex, Xposed.sh line, old asset name (spec L1470-1479)
XPOSED_ITEMS = [
    ("revenge-lspatch", "com.discord", "discord", None, "apkmirror", "bundle", None, None, None, "universal", 0, False, 18, "discord-revenge.apk"),
    ("nexalloy-lspatch", "com.facebook.katana", "facebook-arm64-v8a", "580.0.0.51.74", "apkmirror", "bundle", "arm64-v8a", "320-640dpi", "Android 11+", "arm64-v8a", 3, True, 26, "facebook-arm64-v8a-gnadgnaoh.apk"),
    ("nexalloy-lspatch", "com.facebook.orca", "messenger-arm64-v8a", None, "apkmirror", "apk", "arm64-v8a", "nodpi", "Android 9.0+", "arm64-v8a", 3, True, 29, "messenger-arm64-v8a-gnadgnaoh.apk"),
    ("nexalloy-lspatch", "com.instagram.android", "instagram-arm64-v8a", None, "apkmirror", "bundle", "arm64-v8a", "120-640dpi", "Android 9.0+", "arm64-v8a", 3, True, 36, "instagram-arm64-v8a-gnadgnaoh.apk"),
    ("nexalloy-lspatch", "com.instagram.barcelona", "threads-arm64-v8a", None, "apkmirror", "bundle", "arm64-v8a", "160-640dpi", "Android 9.0+", "arm64-v8a", 3, True, 39, "threads-arm64-v8a-gnadgnaoh.apk"),
    ("nexalloy-lspatch", "com.zing.zalo", "zalo", None, "apkpure", "bundle", None, None, None, "universal", 3, True, 46, "zalo-gnadgnaoh.apk"),
    ("nexalloy-npatch", "com.zhiliaoapp.musically", "tiktok", None, "apkmirror", "apk", None, None, None, "universal", 3, False, 53, "tiktok-gnadgnaoh.apk"),
    ("nexalloy-npatch", "com.ss.android.ugc.trill", "tiktok-asian", None, "apkmirror", "apk", None, None, None, "universal", 3, False, 56, "tiktok-asian-gnadgnaoh.apk"),
]


def org_rows():
    block = (ROOT / "local/build.sh").read_text().split('ORGS="', 1)[1].split('"', 1)[0]
    rows = []
    for line in block.strip().splitlines():
        org, name, script, _ = line.split("|")
        if (ROOT / "src/build" / script).is_file():  # scrazzz-patches has no script (local/build.sh:33)
            rows.append((org, name, script))
    return rows


def tokens(line):
    try:
        return shlex.split(line, comments=True)
    except ValueError:
        return []


def walk(script):
    """Items of one org script: {package: item}. Variants per patch/split_arch call."""
    st = {"key": None, "version": None, "near": False}
    downloads, derived, items, last = {}, {}, {}, None
    for no, raw in enumerate((ROOT / "src/build" / script).read_text().splitlines(), 1):
        t = tokens(raw)
        if not t:
            continue
        cmd, args = t[0], t[1:]
        where = f"src/build/{script}:{no}"
        if cmd.startswith("version="):
            st["version"] = cmd.split("=", 1)[1]
        elif cmd.startswith("near_version="):
            st["near"] = cmd.split("=", 1)[1] == "1"
        elif cmd == "get_patches_key":
            st["key"] = args[0]
        elif cmd in ("get_apk", "get_apkpure", "req"):
            if cmd == "req":
                pkg, stem = REQ_PACKAGES[args[0].split("/")[2]], args[1].removesuffix(".apk")
                dl = {"kind": "url", "type": "apk", "arch": None, "dpi": None, "minver": None, "url": args[0]}
            else:
                a = args + [""] * 6
                pkg, stem = a[0], a[1]
                apkm = cmd == "get_apk"
                dl = {"kind": "apkmirror" if apkm else "apkpure", "type": a[2] or "apk", "arch": (a[3] or None) if apkm else None,
                      "dpi": (a[4] or None) if apkm else None, "minver": (a[5] or None) if apkm else None, "url": None}
            downloads[stem] = {"pkg": pkg, "dl": dl, "line": where, "version": st["version"], "near": st["near"]}
            st["version"], st["near"], last = None, False, stem
        elif cmd == "split_editor":
            excl = args[3].split() if len(args) >= 4 and args[2] == "exclude" else []
            derived[args[1]] = (args[0], excl)
        elif cmd in ("patch", "patch_multi", "split_arch"):
            name, opt_org = args[0], args[1]
            stem, excl = derived.get(name, (name, None))
            d, quirk = downloads.get(stem), None
            if d is None:
                d, quirk = downloads[last], f"{where}: patch {name} has no download named {stem}; used {last}"
            it = items.setdefault(d["pkg"], {"package": d["pkg"], "key": name, "variants": [], "lines": [], "dl": d,
                                             "opt_orgs": [], "patch_keys": [], "quirks": [], "abi_from_name": []})
            if d["line"] not in it["lines"]:
                it["lines"].append(d["line"])
            for lst, v in (("opt_orgs", opt_org), ("patch_keys", st["key"])):
                if v and v not in it[lst]:
                    it[lst].append(v)
            if quirk:
                it["quirks"].append(quirk)
            for s in (SPLIT_ARCHS if cmd == "split_arch" else (None,)):
                vname = f"{name}-{s}" if s else name
                arch = d["dl"]["arch"]
                abi = s or (arch if arch in ABIS else next((x for x in ABIS if vname.endswith("-" + x)), "universal"))
                if not s and arch not in ABIS and abi != "universal":
                    it["abi_from_name"].append(vname)
                it["variants"].append({"name": "all-splits" if excl == [] else abi, "abi": abi, "download": dict(d["dl"]),
                                       "exclude_splits": excl, "strip_libs": s,
                                       "_old": f"{name}-{s}-{opt_org}.apk" if s else f"{name}-{opt_org}.apk"})
    return items


def upstream_of(script):
    for raw in (ROOT / "src/build" / script).read_text().splitlines():
        t = tokens(raw)
        if t[:1] == ["dl_gh"] and t[1] not in NOT_PATCHES:
            repo, owner, ch = t[1], t[2], t[3]
            return f"{owner}/{repo}", CHANNEL.get(ch, "tag"), None if ch in CHANNEL else ch
    raise SystemExit(f"no patches dl_gh in {script}")


def inspected(org):
    f = INSPECT / org / "list-patches.txt"
    if not f.is_file() or not f.read_text().strip():
        return None
    return up.parse_list_patches(f.read_text())


def overrides(it, inspect_list, report_dropped, item_id):
    ov = {}
    names = None
    if inspect_list is not None:
        names = {p["name"] for p in inspect_list if not p["packages"] or it["package"] in p["packages"]}
    for org in it["opt_orgs"]:
        f = ROOT / "src/options" / f"{org}.json"
        if not f.is_file():
            continue
        for bundle in json.loads(f.read_text()):
            for pname, val in bundle.get("patches", {}).items():
                if names is not None and pname not in names:
                    report_dropped.append(f"- item {item_id}: {pname} (from src/options/{org}.json)")
                    continue
                ov[pname] = {k: v for k, v in val.items() if k in ("enabled", "options")}
    for k in it["patch_keys"]:
        for fn, enabled in (("exclude-patches", False), ("include-patches", True)):
            p = ROOT / "src/patches" / k / fn
            for line in (p.read_text().splitlines() if p.is_file() else []):
                pname = line.split("|")[0].strip()
                if pname:
                    ov.setdefault(pname, {})["enabled"] = enabled
    return ov


def main():
    ups, up_ids = [], {}

    def uid(kind, repo, ch, tag, rx):
        k = (repo, ch, tag or "")
        if k not in up_ids:
            up_ids[k] = len(ups) + 1
            ups.append({"id": up_ids[k], "kind": kind, "repo": repo, "channel": ch, "tag": tag, "asset_regex": rx})
        return up_ids[k]

    cli = uid("cli", "MorpheApp/morphe-desktop", "stable", None, r"^morphe-desktop-.*-all\.jar$")
    sources, groups, items, apps, deps = [], [], [], {}, []
    lines_tbl, per_source, kept_all, modes, dropped, names_tbl, quirks = [], [], [], [], [], [], []
    for pos, (org, name, script) in enumerate(org_rows()):
        repo, ch, tag = upstream_of(script)
        u = uid("patches", repo, ch, tag, r"\.jar$" if org in JAR_SOURCES else r"\.mpp$")
        sources.append({"id": org, "name": name, "upstream_id": u, "cli_upstream_id": cli, "engine": "morphe", "keystore": "morphe.keystore"})
        groups.append({"id": pos + 1, "name": name, "color": None, "position": pos})
        insp = inspected(org)
        found = walk(script)
        per_source.append(f"| {org} | {len(found)} | {'yes' if insp is not None else 'no'} |")
        for ipos, it in enumerate(found.values()):
            iid = len(items) + 1
            keep = [v for v in it["variants"] if v["abi"] in ("universal", "arm64-v8a")]
            if not keep:
                keep = it["variants"]
                kept_all.append(f"- item {iid}: {org} {it['package']} ({', '.join(v['abi'] for v in keep)})")
            d = it["dl"]
            if d["version"]:
                mode, why = "pinned", f"version= before {d['line']}"
            elif insp is None:
                mode, why = "auto", "not inspected"
            else:
                compat = [v for p in insp for v in p["packages"].get(it["package"], [])]
                mode, why = ("auto", f"{len(compat)} compatible versions") if compat else ("latest", "inspect lists no compatible version")
            modes.append(f"| {iid} | {org} | {it['package']} | {mode} | {why} |")
            ov = overrides(it, insp, dropped, iid)
            for ln in it["lines"]:
                lines_tbl.append(f"| `{ln}` | {iid} | {org} | {it['package']} |")
            for v in keep:
                names_tbl.append(f"| {v.pop('_old')} | {org}-{it['key']}-{v['name']}.apk |")
            for v in it["variants"]:
                v.pop("_old", None)
            quirks += [f"- item {iid}: {q}" for q in it["quirks"]] + [f"- item {iid}: abi from name suffix: {n}" for n in it["abi_from_name"]]
            items.append({"id": iid, "source_id": org, "package": it["package"], "key": it["key"], "display_name": None,
                          "group_id": pos + 1, "position": ipos, "enabled": True, "version_mode": mode,
                          "version_pin": d["version"], "allow_nearest": d["near"], "variants": keep,
                          "patch_overrides": ov, "engine_options": None})
            apps.setdefault(it["package"], None)
    xg = len(groups) + 1
    groups.append({"id": xg, "name": "Xposed", "color": None, "position": len(groups)})
    xu = {repo: uid(kind, repo, ch, tag, rx) for kind, repo, ch, tag, rx in XPOSED_UPSTREAMS}
    for sid, name, cli_repo, mod_repo, engine, ks in XPOSED_SOURCES:
        sources.append({"id": sid, "name": name, "upstream_id": xu[mod_repo], "cli_upstream_id": xu[cli_repo], "engine": engine, "keystore": ks})
    for ipos, (sid, pkg, key, pin, kind, typ, arch, dpi, minver, abi, sig, inj, line, old) in enumerate(XPOSED_ITEMS):
        iid = len(items) + 1
        items.append({"id": iid, "source_id": sid, "package": pkg, "key": key, "display_name": None, "group_id": xg, "position": ipos,
                      "enabled": True, "version_mode": "pinned" if pin else "latest", "version_pin": pin, "allow_nearest": False,
                      "variants": [{"name": abi, "abi": abi, "download": {"kind": kind, "type": typ, "arch": arch, "dpi": dpi, "minver": minver, "url": None},
                                    "exclude_splits": None, "strip_libs": None}],
                      "patch_overrides": {}, "engine_options": {"sigbypass": sig, "injectdex": inj}})
        lines_tbl.append(f"| `src/build/Xposed.sh:{line}` | {iid} | {sid} | {pkg} |")
        modes.append(f"| {iid} | {sid} | {pkg} | {'pinned' if pin else 'latest'} | spec L1470-1479 |")
        names_tbl.append(f"| {old} | {sid}-{key}-{abi}.apk |")
        apps.setdefault(pkg, None)
    mg = uid("dependency", "MorpheApp/MicroG-RE", "stable", None, r"^microg-[0-9.]+\.apk$")
    deps.append({"id": 1, "upstream_id": mg, "package": "app.revanced.android.gms", "display_name": "MicroG RE"})
    links = [[i["id"], 1] for i in items if i["package"] in MICROG_PACKAGES or (i["source_id"], i["package"]) in MICROG_EXTRA]
    app_rows = []
    for pkg in sorted(apps):
        m, p = APPS["apkmirror"].get(pkg, {}), APPS["apkpure"].get(pkg, {})
        app_rows.append({"package": pkg, "display_name": None, "apkmirror_list_url": m.get("list_url"),
                         "apkmirror_example_url": m.get("example_url"), "apkmirror_example_url2": m.get("example_url2"),
                         "apkpure_url": p.get("download_url")})
    seed = {"upstreams": ups, "apps": app_rows, "sources": sources, "groups": groups, "items": items,
            "dependency_apps": deps, "item_dependencies": links}
    (ROOT / "local/webui/catalog-seed.json").write_text(json.dumps(seed, indent=2, sort_keys=True, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
    rep = ["# Seed report (Task P2.9)", "", f"Sources {len(sources)}, items {len(items)}, upstreams {len(ups)}, MicroG links {len(links)}.", "",
           "## Lines", "", "| line | item | source | package |", "|---|---|---|---|", *lines_tbl, "",
           "## Items per source", "", "| source | items | inspected |", "|---|---|---|", *per_source, "", *quirks, "",
           "## Kept all ABIs", "", *(kept_all or ["- none"]), "",
           "## Version modes", "", "| item | source | package | mode | reason |", "|---|---|---|---|---|", *modes, "",
           "## Dropped stale overrides", "", *(dropped or ["- none"]), "",
           "## Asset names old → new", "", "| old | new |", "|---|---|", *names_tbl, "",
           "## User checks", "", "- MicroG RE package `app.revanced.android.gms` is unverified offline: after the first MicroG pull run "
           "`docker compose -f local/docker-compose.yml exec app java -jar /opt/APKEditor.jar info -i <data>/upstream/<id>/<tag>/microg-*.apk -package` "
           "and fix the dependency app if it differs.", ""]
    (ROOT / "docs/logs/2026-10-19-seed-report.md").write_text("\n".join(rep))
    print(f"sources={len(sources)} items={len(items)}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

# P4 Build job service

Order: P4.1 first (schema lands early, device review S7). Then P4.2 → P4.3 → P4.4 → P4.5 (all `build.py`, serial).
Owner files (round 1): `local/webui/build.py`, `local/webui/schema/002_build.sql`, `local/webui/tests/test_build_service.py`, `local/webui/tests/test_build_zip.py`, `local/webui/tests/fixtures/build/`.

Shared conventions: as P2 (db, `db.DATA`, `HttpError`, validators). Seams: `build.CATALOG` (None → `webui.catalog` imported at call time; tests set a fake with `job_input`, `set_apkmirror_versions`, `resolve_items`, `UpstreamMissing`), `build.RUNNER` (default `<repo root>/src/build/build-item.sh`; tests point it at scripts in `tests/fixtures/build/`). Tests insert the minimal catalog rows (`upstream`, `source`, `app`, `item`) with plain SQL, because `build_jobs.item_id` and `artifacts.item_id` reference `item(id)`, and the `jobs` row with plain SQL (`kind='build', status='running'`).

#### Task P4.1: Build schema `002_build.sql`

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/schema/002_build.sql`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_build_service.py` (class `SchemaTest`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P1 `jobs` table, P2 `item` table (migration order 001 → 002 → 003: SQLite accepts a FK to a table created later, enforcement happens at write time).
- Produces: tables `build_jobs(job_id PK → jobs ON DELETE CASCADE, item_id → item ON DELETE CASCADE, fail_code, fail_detail, app_version, patches_version, cli_version)`, `artifacts(id PK, job_id → jobs ON DELETE RESTRICT, item_id → item ON DELETE CASCADE, variant, abi, package, version_name, version_code INTEGER, patches_version, signer_sha256, path, size INTEGER, sha256, created_at)`; indexes `build_jobs_item(item_id, job_id DESC)`, `artifacts_item(item_id, abi, created_at DESC)`; settings key `artifact_retention` (default 3, read with `settings.get("artifact_retention", 3)`).

**Behavior:** Spec build §3.3 L737–751. `abi` CHECK in `('arm64-v8a','armeabi-v7a','x86','x86_64','universal')`.

**Constraints:** none beyond SQLite.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_build_service.py'` `SchemaTest` green: deleting a `jobs` row with an `artifacts` row raises `sqlite3.IntegrityError`; without artifacts its `build_jobs` row goes; deleting the `item` removes both.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `local/webui/tests/test_build_service.py`

```python
"""Build job service tests (spec build §3-§6, §8)."""
import json
import os
import sqlite3
import subprocess
import threading
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from types import SimpleNamespace
from unittest import mock

from webui import build, db, events, jobs, settings
from webui.tests import helpers

FIX = Path(__file__).parent / "fixtures" / "build"
JOB_FIXTURE = json.loads((build.REPO_ROOT / "src" / "build" / "test-fixtures" / "job.json").read_text())
YT = "com.google.android.youtube"


def add_item(c, iid=1, package=YT, key="youtube", group_id=None):
    c.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO upstream(id, kind, repo, channel, downloaded_tag) VALUES (1,'cli','c/c','stable','v1.0.0'),(2,'patches','p/p','stable','v1.2.3')")
    c.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO source(id, name, upstream_id, cli_upstream_id) VALUES ('morphe','Morphe',2,1)")
    c.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO app(package) VALUES (?)", (package,))
    c.execute("INSERT INTO item(id, source_id, package, key, group_id, position, variants) VALUES (?, 'morphe', ?, ?, ?, 0, '[]')",
              (iid, package, key, group_id))


def add_job(c, item_id, status="running"):
    return c.execute("INSERT INTO jobs(kind, status, trigger, target, options, created_at) VALUES ('build', ?, 'manual', ?, '{}', '2026-10-19T00:00:00Z')",
                     (status, json.dumps({"item_id": item_id}, separators=(",", ":")))).lastrowid


class SchemaTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()

    def test_restrict_and_cascade(self):
        with db.tx() as c:
            add_item(c)
            j1, j2 = add_job(c, 1, "success"), add_job(c, 1, "failed")
            c.execute("INSERT INTO build_jobs(job_id, item_id) VALUES (?, 1), (?, 1)", (j1, j2))
            c.execute("INSERT INTO artifacts(job_id, item_id, variant, abi, path, created_at) VALUES (?, 1, 'universal', 'universal', '/x', 'now')", (j1,))
        with self.assertRaises(sqlite3.IntegrityError):
            with db.tx() as c:
                c.execute("DELETE FROM jobs WHERE id = ?", (j1,))
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("DELETE FROM jobs WHERE id = ?", (j2,))
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM build_jobs").fetchone()[0], 1)
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("DELETE FROM item WHERE id = 1")
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM artifacts").fetchone()[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM build_jobs").fetchone()[0], 0)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `local/webui/schema/002_build.sql` and the module head of `local/webui/build.py`**

```sql
-- Build tables (spec build §3.3). Owner: build. Settings key artifact_retention (default 3).
CREATE TABLE build_jobs (
  job_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES jobs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  item_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES item(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  fail_code TEXT, fail_detail TEXT,
  app_version TEXT, patches_version TEXT, cli_version TEXT);
CREATE INDEX build_jobs_item ON build_jobs(item_id, job_id DESC);
CREATE TABLE artifacts (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  job_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES jobs(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
  item_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES item(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  variant TEXT NOT NULL,
  abi TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (abi IN ('arm64-v8a','armeabi-v7a','x86','x86_64','universal')),
  package TEXT, version_name TEXT, version_code INTEGER, patches_version TEXT,
  signer_sha256 TEXT, path TEXT NOT NULL, size INTEGER, sha256 TEXT, created_at TEXT NOT NULL);
CREATE INDEX artifacts_item ON artifacts(item_id, abi, created_at DESC);
```

#### Task P4.2: Build handler — input, spawn, classification, failure events

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/build.py` (first part)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/fixtures/build/runner-ok.sh`, `local/webui/tests/fixtures/build/runner-fail.sh`, `local/webui/tests/fixtures/build/runner-nomarker.sh`, `local/webui/tests/fixtures/build/runner-noresult.sh`, `local/webui/tests/fixtures/build/runner-env.sh`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_build_service.py` (class `HandlerFailTest`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P2 `catalog.job_input(item_id) -> dict`, `catalog.UpstreamMissing`, `catalog.stale_overrides(item_id)`; P1 `jobs.register`, `jobs.JobFailed`, `log.run(argv, cwd, env, timeout, on_line)`, `log.line`, `events.emit`; P3 runner contract; `src/build/test-fixtures/job.json` (Task P3.1).
- Produces:
  - `build.RUNNER`, `build.CATALOG`, `build.FAIL_CODES = ("upstream_missing","version_unresolved","apk_not_listed","download_failed","merge_failed","patch_failed","timeout","internal")`
  - `build.runner_env() -> dict` (X10 allowlist)
  - `build.handler(job, log) -> None`; `build.detail(job_id) -> dict` (`build_jobs` row + `artifacts` list)
  - `build.register()` → `jobs.register('build', handler, detail)`; `build.start()` → deletes every `db.DATA/work/*` dir (catalog review N4)
  - `build._steps: dict[int, dict]` (current step per running job, read by Task P4.4)
  - `build._Markers(job_id, package)` with `feed(text)` (the `on_line` callback; `fail_code`, `fail_detail`, `last_err` attributes)

**Behavior:** Spec build §4 steps 3–8 L778–790, §5 L792–809. `job_input` → `UpstreamMissing` → `build_jobs` row with `fail_code='upstream_missing'`, `build.failed` event, `JobFailed("upstream_missing: <detail>")`. Write `work/<id>/job.json` (input + `job_id`, `work_dir`) and, for morphe items, `options.json` (= `options_file_json`); insert `build_jobs` with `patches_version=upstream_tag`, `cli_version=cli_tag`; precheck every `patches_files` + `cli_jar` exists, else `upstream_missing` without spawning. Spawn `["bash", RUNNER, <job.json>]`, `cwd=work`, `env=runner_env()`, `timeout=3600`, `on_line` parsing: `::step::<name>[::<i>/<n>]` → `_steps[job.id]`; `::version::<v>` → `UPDATE build_jobs SET app_version`; `::apkm_versions::<csv>` → `CATALOG.set_apkmirror_versions(package, list)`; `::fail::<code>::<detail>` remembered; `[-] …` lines remembered (last). `JobFailed('timeout')` from `log.run` → `fail_code='timeout'`. Cancel (`job.cancel_event` set after `log.run`) → delete work dir, emit nothing, return. Failure classification: rc = 0 + valid `result.json` → success path (Task P4.3); else the last `::fail::` code (unknown code → `internal`); no marker → `internal` with detail = last `[-]` line or `runner exited <rc> without result.json`. `jobs.reason` = `"<fail_code>: <fail_detail>"` via `JobFailed`. Work dir deleted in every outcome (`finally`).

**Constraints:** env never contains `GITHUB_TOKEN` (spec L779); argv list only.

**Acceptance criteria:**
