```python
class StreamLog:
    """jobs log stand-in that streams lines to on_line while the child runs."""

    def __init__(self):
        self.lines, self.env, self.argv = [], None, None

    def line(self, text):
        self.lines.append(text)

    def run(self, argv, cwd, env, timeout=None, on_line=None):
        self.argv, self.env = argv, env
        p = subprocess.Popen(argv, cwd=cwd, env=env, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT, text=True)
        for text in p.stdout:
            text = text.rstrip("\n")
            self.lines.append(text)
            if on_line:
                on_line(text)
        return p.wait()


class FakeCatalog:
    class UpstreamMissing(Exception):
        pass

    def __init__(self, up):
        self.up, self.missing, self.key, self.versions = up, False, "youtube", []

    def job_input(self, item_id):
        if self.missing:
            raise self.UpstreamMissing("source upstream 2 not downloaded")
        j = {k: v for k, v in JOB_FIXTURE.items() if k not in ("job_id", "work_dir")}
        j.update(item_id=item_id, key=self.key, patches_files=[str(self.up / "patches-1.0.0.mpp")],
                 cli_jar=str(self.up / "morphe-desktop-1.0.0-all.jar"))
        return j

    def stale_overrides(self, item_id):
        return ["Gone"]

    def set_apkmirror_versions(self, package, versions):
        self.versions.append((package, versions))

    def resolve_items(self, target):
        return [r[0] for r in db.conn().execute("SELECT id FROM item WHERE ifnull(group_id, 0) = ? ORDER BY id", (target["group_ids"][0],))]


class BuildCase(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.up = self.data / "up"
        self.up.mkdir()
        (self.up / "patches-1.0.0.mpp").write_bytes(b"p")
        (self.up / "morphe-desktop-1.0.0-all.jar").write_bytes(b"c")
        self.cat = FakeCatalog(self.up)
        for attr, val in (("CATALOG", self.cat), ("RUNNER", str(FIX / "runner-ok.sh"))):
            p = mock.patch.object(build, attr, val)
            p.start()
            self.addCleanup(p.stop)
        self.events = []
        p = mock.patch.object(events, "emit", side_effect=lambda t, pl: self.events.append((t, pl)))
        p.start()
        self.addCleanup(p.stop)
        with db.tx() as c:
            add_item(c)
        self.addCleanup(build._steps.clear)

    def job(self, item_id=1, status="running"):
        with db.tx() as c:
            jid = add_job(c, item_id, status)
        return SimpleNamespace(id=jid, target={"item_id": item_id}, options={}, cancel_event=threading.Event())

    def run_handler(self, runner=None, job=None):
        job = job or self.job()
        if runner:
            build.RUNNER = str(FIX / runner)
        log = StreamLog()
        try:
            build.handler(job, log)
            return job, log, None
        except jobs.JobFailed as e:
            return job, log, e

    def bj(self, job):
        return db.conn().execute("SELECT * FROM build_jobs WHERE job_id = ?", (job.id,)).fetchone()


class HandlerFailTest(BuildCase):
    def test_job_json_matches_runner_fixture(self):
        seen = {}
        real = StreamLog.run

        def spy(log, argv, cwd, env, timeout=None, on_line=None):
            seen.update(json.loads(Path(argv[2]).read_text()))
            seen["_options"] = json.loads((Path(cwd) / "options.json").read_text())
            return real(log, argv, cwd, env, timeout, on_line)

        with mock.patch.object(StreamLog, "run", spy):
            self.run_handler("runner-noresult.sh")
        self.assertEqual(set(seen) - {"_options"}, set(JOB_FIXTURE))
        self.assertEqual(set(seen["recipe"]["variants"][0]), set(JOB_FIXTURE["recipe"]["variants"][0]))
        self.assertEqual(seen["_options"], JOB_FIXTURE["options_file_json"])

    def test_upstream_missing_from_catalog(self):
        self.cat.missing = True
        job, log, err = self.run_handler()
        self.assertEqual(self.bj(job)["fail_code"], "upstream_missing")
        self.assertTrue(str(err).startswith("upstream_missing: "))
        self.assertIsNone(log.argv)

    def test_missing_file_is_upstream_missing(self):
        (self.up / "morphe-desktop-1.0.0-all.jar").unlink()
        job, log, err = self.run_handler()
        self.assertEqual(self.bj(job)["fail_code"], "upstream_missing")
        self.assertIsNone(log.argv)

    def test_each_fail_code(self):
        for code in ("version_unresolved", "apk_not_listed", "download_failed", "merge_failed", "patch_failed", "internal"):
            self.cat.key = code
            job, log, err = self.run_handler("runner-fail.sh")
            self.assertEqual((self.bj(job)["fail_code"], str(err)), (code, f"{code}: detail of {code}"))
        self.assertEqual(self.events[-1], ("build.failed", {"item_id": 1, "job_id": job.id, "fail_code": "internal", "fail_detail": "detail of internal"}))

    def test_no_marker_and_no_result(self):
        job, log, err = self.run_handler("runner-nomarker.sh")
        self.assertEqual((self.bj(job)["fail_code"], self.bj(job)["fail_detail"]), ("internal", "[-] last error line"))
        job, log, err = self.run_handler("runner-noresult.sh")
        self.assertEqual(self.bj(job)["fail_code"], "internal")
        self.assertFalse((self.data / "work" / str(job.id)).exists())

    def test_env_allowlist(self):
        with mock.patch.dict(os.environ, {"GITHUB_TOKEN": "secret", "FLARESOLVERR_URL": "http://127.0.0.1:47191"}):
            job, log, err = self.run_handler("runner-env.sh")
        keys = set(next(l for l in log.lines if l.startswith("ENVKEYS ")).split()[1:])
        allowed = {"PATH", "HOME", "TZ", "LANG", "FLARESOLVERR_URL", "CFBYPASS_URL", "APKM_SOLVER_URL", "RELUME_APK_CACHE",
                   "PWD", "SHLVL", "_", "OLDPWD"}  # the last four are set by bash itself
        self.assertLessEqual(keys, allowed)
        self.assertNotIn("GITHUB_TOKEN", keys)
        self.assertIn("FLARESOLVERR_URL", keys)
        self.assertNotIn("GITHUB_TOKEN", log.env)

    def test_markers(self):
        job = self.job()
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO build_jobs(job_id, item_id) VALUES (?, 1)", (job.id,))
        m = build._Markers(job.id, YT)
        m.feed("::version::20.1.0")
        self.assertEqual(self.bj(job)["app_version"], "20.1.0")
        m.feed("::apkm_versions::20.2.0,20.1.0")
        self.assertEqual(self.cat.versions, [(YT, ["20.2.0", "20.1.0"])])
        m.feed("::step::patch::2/3")
        self.assertEqual(build._steps[job.id], {"step": "patch", "step_index": 2, "step_count": 3})
        m.feed("::fail::bogus::x")
        self.assertEqual(m.fail_code, "internal")

    def test_cancel_emits_nothing(self):
        job = self.job()
        job.cancel_event.set()
        _, _, err = self.run_handler(job=job)
        self.assertIsNone(err)
        self.assertEqual(self.events, [])
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM artifacts").fetchone()[0], 0)

    def test_stale_override_logged(self):
        job, log, err = self.run_handler("runner-noresult.sh")
        self.assertIn("[!] stale override dropped: Gone", log.lines)

    def test_start_removes_stale_work_dirs(self):
        (self.data / "work" / "77").mkdir(parents=True)
        build.start()
        self.assertEqual(list((self.data / "work").iterdir()), [])
```

```python
"""Build job service (spec Part 3): job.json, runner child process, artifacts, retention, routes."""
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import threading
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

from webui import db, events, jobs, server, settings

REPO_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
RUNNER = str(REPO_ROOT / "src" / "build" / "build-item.sh")
CATALOG = None  # test seam; None -> webui.catalog (imported at call time)
FAIL_CODES = ("upstream_missing", "version_unresolved", "apk_not_listed", "download_failed",
              "merge_failed", "patch_failed", "timeout", "internal")
ENV_KEYS = ("PATH", "HOME", "TZ", "LANG", "FLARESOLVERR_URL", "CFBYPASS_URL", "APKM_SOLVER_URL")
_steps = {}
_steps_lock = threading.Lock()


def _cat():
    if CATALOG is not None:
        return CATALOG
    from webui import catalog
    return catalog


def now_iso():
    return datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")


def runner_env():
    """X10: no GITHUB_TOKEN, the runner executes third-party patch code (spec L779)."""
    env = {k: os.environ[k] for k in ENV_KEYS if k in os.environ}
    env["RELUME_APK_CACHE"] = str(db.DATA / "apk-cache")
    return env


class _Markers:
    """on_line callback: ::step::, ::version::, ::apkm_versions::, ::fail::, last [-] line (spec build §3.2)."""

    def __init__(self, job_id, package):
        self.job_id, self.package = job_id, package
        self.fail_code = self.fail_detail = self.last_err = None

    def feed(self, text):
        if text.startswith("::step::"):
            parts = text[8:].split("::")
            st = {"step": parts[0], "step_index": None, "step_count": None}
            if len(parts) > 1 and re.fullmatch(r"\d+/\d+", parts[1]):
                i, n = parts[1].split("/")
                st.update(step_index=int(i), step_count=int(n))
            with _steps_lock:
                _steps[self.job_id] = st
        elif text.startswith("::version::"):
            with db.tx() as c:
                c.execute("UPDATE build_jobs SET app_version = ? WHERE job_id = ?", (text[11:].strip(), self.job_id))
        elif text.startswith("::apkm_versions::"):
            versions = [v for v in text[17:].strip().split(",") if v]
            if versions:
                _cat().set_apkmirror_versions(self.package, versions)
        elif text.startswith("::fail::"):
            code, _, detail = text[8:].partition("::")
            self.fail_code = code if code in FAIL_CODES else "internal"
            self.fail_detail = detail.strip()
        elif text.startswith("[-]"):
            self.last_err = text.strip()


def _fail(job, item_id, code, detail):
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute("""INSERT INTO build_jobs(job_id, item_id, fail_code, fail_detail) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
            ON CONFLICT(job_id) DO UPDATE SET fail_code = excluded.fail_code, fail_detail = excluded.fail_detail""",
                  (job.id, item_id, code, detail))
    events.emit("build.failed", {"item_id": item_id, "job_id": job.id, "fail_code": code, "fail_detail": detail})
    raise jobs.JobFailed(f"{code}: {detail}")


def _read_result(work):
    """result.json when valid (spec build §3.2: present, ≥1 variant, every file non-empty inside work)."""
    try:
        r = json.loads((work / "result.json").read_text())
        variants = r["variants"]
        if not variants:
            return None
        for v in variants:
            f = (work / v["file"]).resolve()
            if work.resolve() not in f.parents or not f.is_file() or f.stat().st_size == 0:
                return None
        return r
    except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
        return None


def artifact_name(source_id, key, variant, app_version, upstream_tag):
    return f"{source_id}-{key}-{variant}-{server.safe_remote(app_version)}-{server.safe_remote(upstream_tag)}.apk"


def _register(job, item_id, inp, work, result):
    dest = db.DATA / "artifacts" / str(item_id) / str(job.id)
    dest.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    now, ids = now_iso(), []
    with db.tx() as c:
        for v in result["variants"]:
            target = dest / artifact_name(inp["source_id"], inp["key"], v["name"], result["app_version"], inp["upstream_tag"])
            shutil.move(str(work / v["file"]), target)
            ids.append(c.execute("""INSERT INTO artifacts(job_id, item_id, variant, abi, package, version_name, version_code,
                patches_version, signer_sha256, path, size, sha256, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)""",
                                 (job.id, item_id, v["name"], v["abi"], v.get("package"), v.get("version_name"),
                                  v.get("version_code"), inp["upstream_tag"], v.get("signer_sha256"), str(target),
                                  target.stat().st_size, v.get("sha256"), now)).lastrowid)
        c.execute("UPDATE build_jobs SET app_version = ?, patches_version = ?, cli_version = ? WHERE job_id = ?",
                  (result["app_version"], inp["upstream_tag"], inp["cli_tag"], job.id))
    events.emit("build.succeeded", {"item_id": item_id, "job_id": job.id, "app_version": result["app_version"],
                                    "patches_version": inp["upstream_tag"], "artifacts": ids})


def handler(job, log):
    item_id = job.target["item_id"]
    cat = _cat()
    work = db.DATA / "work" / str(job.id)
    try:
        try:
            inp = cat.job_input(item_id)
        except cat.UpstreamMissing as e:
            _fail(job, item_id, "upstream_missing", str(e))
        for name in cat.stale_overrides(item_id):
            log.line(f"[!] stale override dropped: {name}")
        work.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (work / "job.json").write_text(json.dumps(dict(inp, job_id=job.id, work_dir=str(work)), indent=2))
        if inp["options_file_json"] is not None:
            (work / "options.json").write_text(json.dumps(inp["options_file_json"]))
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO build_jobs(job_id, item_id, patches_version, cli_version) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)",
                      (job.id, item_id, inp["upstream_tag"], inp["cli_tag"]))
        missing = [p for p in [*inp["patches_files"], inp["cli_jar"]] if not Path(p).is_file()]
        if missing:
            _fail(job, item_id, "upstream_missing", f"missing file {Path(missing[0]).name}")
        m = _Markers(job.id, inp["package"])
        try:
            rc = log.run(["bash", RUNNER, str(work / "job.json")], cwd=str(work), env=runner_env(), timeout=3600, on_line=m.feed)
        except jobs.JobFailed as e:
            if str(e) == "timeout":
                _fail(job, item_id, "timeout", "runner exceeded 3600 s")
            raise
        if job.cancel_event.is_set():
            return
        result = _read_result(work) if rc == 0 else None
        if result is None:
            if m.fail_code:
                _fail(job, item_id, m.fail_code, m.fail_detail or "")
            _fail(job, item_id, "internal", m.last_err or f"runner exited {rc} without result.json")
        _register(job, item_id, inp, work, result)
    finally:
        shutil.rmtree(work, ignore_errors=True)
        with _steps_lock:
            _steps.pop(job.id, None)


def _artifact_obj(r):
    d = dict(r)
    path = d.pop("path")
    d.update(file_name=Path(path).name, download_url=f"/api/artifacts/{r['id']}/download")
    return d


def detail(job_id):
    b = db.conn().execute("SELECT * FROM build_jobs WHERE job_id = ?", (job_id,)).fetchone()
    arts = [_artifact_obj(r) for r in db.conn().execute("SELECT * FROM artifacts WHERE job_id = ? ORDER BY id", (job_id,))]
    with _steps_lock:
        st = dict(_steps.get(job_id, {}))
    return {**(dict(b) if b else {}), "step": st.get("step"), "step_index": st.get("step_index"),
            "step_count": st.get("step_count"), "artifacts": arts}


def register():
    jobs.register("build", handler, detail)


def start():
    """No job can run yet: stale work dirs go (spec build §6)."""
    w = db.DATA / "work"
    if w.is_dir():
        for d in w.iterdir():
            shutil.rmtree(d, ignore_errors=True)
```

#### Task P4.3: Success path — artifacts, retention, artifact lookups

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/build.py`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_build_service.py` (class `HandlerOkTest`, `RetentionTest`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task P4.2; P1 `settings.get`, `server.safe_remote`.
- Produces: `build.artifact_name(source_id, key, variant, app_version, upstream_tag) -> str`; `build.run_retention(item_id=None) -> None` (Plan-fixed); `build.forget_item(item_id) -> None`; `build.latest_artifact(item_id, abi) -> dict | None` (X8); event `build.succeeded {item_id, job_id, app_version, patches_version, artifacts:[ids]}`.

**Behavior:** Spec build §3.2 L734–735, §4 step 7–8 L789–790, §6 L818–824, §3.4 L765–768. Valid result = `result.json` parses, has ≥ 1 variant, every `file` (relative to work dir) exists and is non-empty. Move each to `db.DATA/artifacts/<item_id>/<job_id>/<artifact_name>`; name `<source_id>-<key>-<variant>-<safe(app_version)>-<safe(upstream_tag)>.apk`; insert `artifacts` rows; `build_jobs` versions from `result.json`; retention for the item; emit `build.succeeded`. `run_retention(item_id=None)`: N = `max(1, int(settings.get("artifact_retention", 3)))`; per item (all items with artifacts when None) keep artifacts of the newest N jobs that have artifacts (by `job_id`), delete older files and rows, then remove empty job dirs. `forget_item` deletes `db.DATA/artifacts/<item_id>/`. `latest_artifact`: newest `success` build job of the item that has artifacts → the row with `abi = <abi>`, else `abi = 'universal'`, else None.

**Constraints:** newest success never deleted; `shutil.move` within `/data`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_build_service.py'` green: `runner-ok.sh` → 2 artifacts named `morphe-youtube-universal-20.1.0-v1.2.3.apk` / `…-arm64-v8a-…`, rows with sha256/size/signer, `build_jobs` versions set, `build.succeeded` payload with both artifact ids; retention N=2 over 4 successful jobs leaves the newest 2 jobs' files and rows; N=0 setting still keeps the newest; `run_retention()` with no argument covers two items; `latest_artifact(item, "arm64-v8a")` → arm64 row, `"x86"` → universal row, no success → None; `forget_item` removes the item dir.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (append to `local/webui/tests/test_build_service.py`, before the `__main__` guard)

```python
class HandlerOkTest(BuildCase):
    def test_success_registers_artifacts(self):
        job, log, err = self.run_handler()
        self.assertIsNone(err)
        rows = db.conn().execute("SELECT * FROM artifacts WHERE job_id = ? ORDER BY id", (job.id,)).fetchall()
        self.assertEqual([Path(r["path"]).name for r in rows],
                         ["morphe-youtube-universal-20.1.0-v1.2.3.apk", "morphe-youtube-arm64-v8a-20.1.0-v1.2.3.apk"])
        self.assertTrue(all(Path(r["path"]).is_file() for r in rows))
        self.assertEqual([(r["size"], r["sha256"], r["signer_sha256"]) for r in rows], [(1, "aa", "bb"), (3, "cc", "bb")])
        b = self.bj(job)
        self.assertEqual((b["app_version"], b["patches_version"], b["cli_version"], b["fail_code"]), ("20.1.0", "v1.2.3", "v1.0.0", None))
        self.assertEqual(self.events[-1], ("build.succeeded", {"item_id": 1, "job_id": job.id, "app_version": "20.1.0",
                                                               "patches_version": "v1.2.3", "artifacts": [r["id"] for r in rows]}))
        self.assertFalse((self.data / "work" / str(job.id)).exists())
        self.assertEqual(len(build.detail(job.id)["artifacts"]), 2)


class RetentionTest(BuildCase):
    def build_n(self, n, item_id=1):
        jobs_ = []
        for _ in range(n):
            job, _, err = self.run_handler(job=self.job(item_id))
            self.assertIsNone(err)
            with db.tx() as c:
                c.execute("UPDATE jobs SET status = 'success' WHERE id = ?", (job.id,))
            jobs_.append(job.id)
        return jobs_

    def job_ids_with_files(self, item_id=1):
        return sorted({r[0] for r in db.conn().execute("SELECT job_id FROM artifacts WHERE item_id = ?", (item_id,))})

    def test_keep_n(self):
        with mock.patch.object(settings, "get", side_effect=lambda k, d=None: 2 if k == "artifact_retention" else d):
            ids = self.build_n(4)
        self.assertEqual(self.job_ids_with_files(), ids[2:])
        self.assertEqual(sorted(int(p.name) for p in (self.data / "artifacts" / "1").iterdir()), ids[2:])

    def test_zero_keeps_newest(self):
        with mock.patch.object(settings, "get", side_effect=lambda k, d=None: 0 if k == "artifact_retention" else d):
            ids = self.build_n(2)
        self.assertEqual(self.job_ids_with_files(), ids[-1:])

    def test_run_retention_all_items(self):
        with db.tx() as c:
            add_item(c, 2, "com.reddit.frontpage", "reddit")
        a, b = self.build_n(2, 1), self.build_n(2, 2)
        with mock.patch.object(settings, "get", side_effect=lambda k, d=None: 1 if k == "artifact_retention" else d):
            build.run_retention()
        self.assertEqual((self.job_ids_with_files(1), self.job_ids_with_files(2)), (a[-1:], b[-1:]))

    def test_latest_artifact_and_forget(self):
        self.assertIsNone(build.latest_artifact(1, "arm64-v8a"))
        self.build_n(1)
        self.assertEqual(build.latest_artifact(1, "arm64-v8a")["abi"], "arm64-v8a")
        self.assertEqual(build.latest_artifact(1, "x86")["abi"], "universal")
        build.forget_item(1)
        self.assertFalse((self.data / "artifacts" / "1").exists())
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add to `local/webui/build.py`** (after `_register`) and call retention

```python
def run_retention(item_id=None):
    """Keep the artifacts of the newest N jobs per item; the newest is never deleted (spec build §6)."""
    n = max(1, int(settings.get("artifact_retention", 3)))
    c = db.conn()
    items = [item_id] if item_id is not None else [r[0] for r in c.execute("SELECT DISTINCT item_id FROM artifacts")]
    for iid in items:
        job_ids = [r[0] for r in c.execute("SELECT DISTINCT job_id FROM artifacts WHERE item_id = ? ORDER BY job_id DESC", (iid,))]
        for old in job_ids[n:]:
            for r in c.execute("SELECT path FROM artifacts WHERE job_id = ?", (old,)).fetchall():
                Path(r["path"]).unlink(missing_ok=True)
            with db.tx() as t:
                t.execute("DELETE FROM artifacts WHERE job_id = ?", (old,))
            shutil.rmtree(db.DATA / "artifacts" / str(iid) / str(old), ignore_errors=True)


def forget_item(item_id):
    shutil.rmtree(db.DATA / "artifacts" / str(item_id), ignore_errors=True)


def _newest_success_job(item_id):
    r = db.conn().execute("""SELECT a.job_id FROM artifacts a JOIN jobs j ON j.id = a.job_id
        WHERE a.item_id = ? AND j.status = 'success' ORDER BY a.job_id DESC LIMIT 1""", (item_id,)).fetchone()
    return r[0] if r else None


def latest_artifact(item_id, abi):
    """X8: artifacts row of the newest success build with this ABI, else universal, else None."""
    jid = _newest_success_job(item_id)
    if jid is None:
        return None
    for want in (abi, "universal"):
        a = db.conn().execute("SELECT * FROM artifacts WHERE job_id = ? AND abi = ? ORDER BY id LIMIT 1", (jid, want)).fetchone()
        if a:
            return dict(a)
    return None
```

In `_register`, insert `run_retention(item_id)` on the line before `events.emit("build.succeeded", …)`.

#### Task P4.4: Item status and `needs_build`

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/build.py`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_build_service.py` (class `StatusTest`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P1 `jobs` table read-only with `json_extract(target,'$.item_id')` (X3); P2 tables `item`, `source`, `upstream` read-only.
- Produces: `build.item_status(item_ids) -> dict[int, dict]` (keys `last_build`, `last_success`, `active_job`, spec L765); `build.needs_build(item_id) -> bool`.

**Behavior:** Spec build §3.4 L765–766. `last_build {job_id, status, reason, fail_code}` = newest finished (`success|failed|cancelled`) build job; `last_success {job_id, app_version, patches_version, finished_at}`; `active_job {job_id, status, step, step_index, step_count}` = the running job, else the oldest queued (step fields null while queued). Absent → null; every requested id is a key. `needs_build`: true when no finished build, newest finished is not `success`, or source/CLI `downloaded_tag` ≠ newest success's `patches_version`/`cli_version`.

**Constraints:** at most 4 SQL queries per `item_status` call (App list loads all items at once).

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_build_service.py'` `StatusTest` green: never built → all null + `needs_build` true; failed newest → true; success with equal tags → false; patches tag changed → true; CLI tag changed → true; running job with step `patch::2/3` → `active_job.step == "patch"`, `step_index == 2`, `step_count == 3`; queued job → step fields null.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (append to `local/webui/tests/test_build_service.py`, before the `__main__` guard)

```python
class StatusTest(BuildCase):
    def finished(self, status, patches="v1.2.3", cli="v1.0.0"):
        with db.tx() as c:
            jid = add_job(c, 1, status)
            c.execute("UPDATE jobs SET finished_at = '2026-10-19T01:00:00Z' WHERE id = ?", (jid,))
            c.execute("INSERT INTO build_jobs(job_id, item_id, fail_code, app_version, patches_version, cli_version) VALUES (?, 1, ?, '20.1.0', ?, ?)",
                      (jid, "patch_failed" if status == "failed" else None, patches, cli))
        return jid

    def test_never_built(self):
        self.assertEqual(build.item_status([1]), {1: {"last_build": None, "last_success": None, "active_job": None}})
        self.assertTrue(build.needs_build(1))

    def test_success_and_tags(self):
        jid = self.finished("success")
        st = build.item_status([1])[1]
        self.assertEqual(st["last_build"], {"job_id": jid, "status": "success", "reason": None, "fail_code": None})
        self.assertEqual(st["last_success"]["patches_version"], "v1.2.3")
        self.assertFalse(build.needs_build(1))
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE upstream SET downloaded_tag = 'v1.3.0' WHERE id = 2")
        self.assertTrue(build.needs_build(1))
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE upstream SET downloaded_tag = 'v1.2.3' WHERE id = 2")
            c.execute("UPDATE upstream SET downloaded_tag = 'v1.1.0' WHERE id = 1")
        self.assertTrue(build.needs_build(1))

    def test_failed_newest(self):
        self.finished("success")
        self.finished("failed")
        self.assertEqual(build.item_status([1])[1]["last_build"]["fail_code"], "patch_failed")
        self.assertTrue(build.needs_build(1))

    def test_active_job_steps(self):
        queued = self.job(status="queued")
        st = build.item_status([1])[1]["active_job"]
        self.assertEqual(st, {"job_id": queued.id, "status": "queued", "step": None, "step_index": None, "step_count": None})
        running = self.job(status="running")
        build._Markers(running.id, YT).feed("::step::patch::2/3")
        st = build.item_status([1])[1]["active_job"]
        self.assertEqual(st, {"job_id": running.id, "status": "running", "step": "patch", "step_index": 2, "step_count": 3})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add to `local/webui/build.py`**

```python
STATUS_SQL = """SELECT j.id, j.status, j.reason, j.finished_at, json_extract(j.target, '$.item_id') AS item_id,
  b.fail_code, b.app_version, b.patches_version, b.cli_version
  FROM jobs j LEFT JOIN build_jobs b ON b.job_id = j.id
  WHERE j.kind = 'build' AND json_extract(j.target, '$.item_id') IN ({q})"""


def item_status(item_ids):
    """{item_id: {last_build, last_success, active_job}} in two queries (spec build §3.4)."""
    out = {i: {"last_build": None, "last_success": None, "active_job": None} for i in item_ids}
    if not item_ids:
        return out
    sql = STATUS_SQL.format(q=",".join("?" * len(item_ids)))
    c = db.conn()
    for r in c.execute(sql + " AND j.status IN ('success', 'failed', 'cancelled') ORDER BY j.id", list(item_ids)):
        o = out[r["item_id"]]
        o["last_build"] = {"job_id": r["id"], "status": r["status"], "reason": r["reason"], "fail_code": r["fail_code"]}
        if r["status"] == "success":
            o["last_success"] = {"job_id": r["id"], "app_version": r["app_version"], "patches_version": r["patches_version"],
                                 "cli_version": r["cli_version"], "finished_at": r["finished_at"]}
    # queued rows newest-first, then running: the last assignment wins = running job, else the oldest queued
    for r in c.execute(sql + " AND j.status IN ('queued', 'running') ORDER BY j.status = 'running', j.id DESC", list(item_ids)):
        with _steps_lock:
            st = _steps.get(r["id"], {}) if r["status"] == "running" else {}
        out[r["item_id"]]["active_job"] = {"job_id": r["id"], "status": r["status"], "step": st.get("step"),
                                          "step_index": st.get("step_index"), "step_count": st.get("step_count")}
    return out


def needs_build(item_id):
    st = item_status([item_id])[item_id]
    ls, lb = st["last_success"], st["last_build"]
    if ls is None or lb["status"] == "failed":
        return True
    r = db.conn().execute("""SELECT u.downloaded_tag AS p, c.downloaded_tag AS cl FROM item i JOIN source s ON s.id = i.source_id
        JOIN upstream u ON u.id = s.upstream_id JOIN upstream c ON c.id = s.cli_upstream_id WHERE i.id = ?""", (item_id,)).fetchone()
    return r is not None and (r["p"] != ls["patches_version"] or r["cl"] != ls["cli_version"])
```

`last_success` carries `cli_version` in addition to the spec fields (needed by `needs_build`; extra key, no consumer breaks).

#### Task P4.5: Build routes and group ZIP

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/build.py`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_build_zip.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P1 `jobs.enqueue`, `server.FileResponse`, `server.StreamResponse(write_fn, download_name)`; P2 `catalog.resolve_items({"group_ids":[id]})`.
- Produces: `build.ROUTES` = `POST /api/items/{id}/build` (`post_item_build` → `{"job_id"}`), `POST /api/builds` (`post_builds` → `{"job_ids"}`), `GET /api/items/{id}/artifacts` (`list_item_artifacts`), `GET /api/artifacts/{id}/download` (`download_artifact`), `GET /api/groups/{id}/download` (`download_group`); `build.group_zip_files(group_id) -> list[tuple[Path, str]]`.

**Behavior:** Spec build §3.4 L755–764. Item routes 404 for an unknown item; `POST /api/builds {item_ids}` needs a non-empty int list of existing items (else 400/404); dedup is core's (`jobs.enqueue`). Artifacts list newest first (`id DESC`), rows without `path` field leakage (path replaced by `download_url`). Download → `FileResponse(path, download_name=<file name>)`; missing file → 404. Group ZIP: items = `resolve_items` (enabled; 0 = Ungrouped); per item every artifact of its newest success job that has artifacts; none → 404 `no artifacts`; name `<slug(group name or "ungrouped")>-<YYYYMMDD>.zip`; `write_fn(f)` streams `zipfile.ZipFile(f, "w", ZIP_STORED, allowZip64=True)`, opens each file just before writing it and skips a file deleted meanwhile.

**Constraints:** no temp file; no `Content-Length` (core's `StreamResponse`).

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_build_zip.py'` green: two items, one without success → ZIP (written into `io.BytesIO`) lists only the other item's artifact names; `zipfile.ZipFile` reads it back and sizes match; empty group → 404; file name `my-group-<today>.zip` for group `My Group`; `post_item_build` twice → same `job_id` (queued dedup); unknown item → 404; `post_builds` returns one id per item; `download_artifact` on a deleted file → 404.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** `local/webui/tests/test_build_zip.py`

```python
"""Build routes and group ZIP tests (spec build §3.4, §8)."""
import io
import unittest
import zipfile
from datetime import datetime

from webui import build, db, server
from webui.tests import helpers
from webui.tests.test_build_service import BuildCase, add_item


class RoutesTest(BuildCase):
    def call(self, handler, json=None, method="POST", **params):
        return handler(helpers.fake_request(method, "/test", json=json, params=params))

    def status_of(self, handler, json=None, method="POST", **params):
        try:
            return self.call(handler, json=json, method=method, **params)[0]
        except server.HttpError as e:
            return e.status

    def success_build(self, item_id):
        job, _, err = self.run_handler(job=self.job(item_id))
        self.assertIsNone(err)
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("UPDATE jobs SET status = 'success' WHERE id = ?", (job.id,))
        return job

    def test_enqueue_dedup_and_404(self):
        a = self.call(build.post_item_build, json={}, id=1)[1]["job_id"]
        b = self.call(build.post_item_build, json={}, id=1)[1]["job_id"]
        self.assertEqual(a, b)
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(build.post_item_build, json={}, id=99), 404)
        with db.tx() as c:
            add_item(c, 2, "com.reddit.frontpage", "reddit")
        self.assertEqual(len(self.call(build.post_builds, json={"item_ids": [1, 2]})[1]["job_ids"]), 2)
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(build.post_builds, json={"item_ids": []}), 400)

    def test_artifacts_list_and_download(self):
        self.success_build(1)
        arts = self.call(build.list_item_artifacts, method="GET", id=1)[1]["artifacts"]
        self.assertEqual([a["variant"] for a in arts], ["arm64-v8a", "universal"])
        self.assertNotIn("path", arts[0])
        self.assertIsInstance(self.call(build.download_artifact, method="GET", id=arts[0]["id"]), server.FileResponse)
        p = db.conn().execute("SELECT path FROM artifacts WHERE id = ?", (arts[0]["id"],)).fetchone()[0]
        __import__("os").unlink(p)
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(build.download_artifact, method="GET", id=arts[0]["id"]), 404)

    def test_group_zip(self):
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO grp(id, name, position) VALUES (5, 'My Group', 0)")
            c.execute("UPDATE item SET group_id = 5 WHERE id = 1")
            add_item(c, 2, "com.reddit.frontpage", "reddit", group_id=5)
        self.success_build(1)
        resp = self.call(build.download_group, method="GET", id=5)
        self.assertIsInstance(resp, server.StreamResponse)
        self.assertEqual(resp.download_name, f"my-group-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.zip")
        buf = io.BytesIO()
        resp.write_fn(buf)
        z = zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(buf.getvalue()))
        names = sorted(z.namelist())
        self.assertEqual(names, ["morphe-youtube-arm64-v8a-20.1.0-v1.2.3.apk", "morphe-youtube-universal-20.1.0-v1.2.3.apk"])
        sizes = {Path_.name: Path_.stat().st_size for Path_ in (self.data / "artifacts" / "1").rglob("*.apk")}
        self.assertEqual({i.filename: i.file_size for i in z.infolist()}, sizes)
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO grp(id, name, position) VALUES (6, 'Empty', 1)")
        self.assertEqual(self.status_of(build.download_group, method="GET", id=6), 404)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add to `local/webui/build.py`** (add `import time` and `import zipfile` to the imports)

```python
def _item_exists(iid):
    if not db.conn().execute("SELECT 1 FROM item WHERE id = ?", (iid,)).fetchone():
        raise server.HttpError(404, "item not found")


def post_item_build(req):
    iid = req.params["id"]
    _item_exists(iid)
    return 200, {"job_id": jobs.enqueue("build", {"item_id": iid}, "manual")}


def post_builds(req):
    ids = (req.json or {}).get("item_ids")
    if not isinstance(ids, list) or not ids or not all(isinstance(i, int) and not isinstance(i, bool) for i in ids):
        raise server.HttpError(400, "invalid item_ids")
    for i in ids:
        _item_exists(i)
    return 200, {"job_ids": [jobs.enqueue("build", {"item_id": i}, "manual") for i in ids]}


def list_item_artifacts(req):
    iid = req.params["id"]
    _item_exists(iid)
    return 200, {"artifacts": [_artifact_obj(r) for r in db.conn().execute("SELECT * FROM artifacts WHERE item_id = ? ORDER BY id DESC", (iid,))]}


def download_artifact(req):
    r = db.conn().execute("SELECT path FROM artifacts WHERE id = ?", (req.params["id"],)).fetchone()
    if r is None or not Path(r["path"]).is_file():
        raise server.HttpError(404, "artifact not found")
    return server.FileResponse(r["path"], download_name=Path(r["path"]).name)


def group_zip_files(group_id):
    files = []
    for iid in _cat().resolve_items({"group_ids": [group_id]}):
        jid = _newest_success_job(iid)
        if jid is not None:
            files += [(Path(r[0]), Path(r[0]).name) for r in db.conn().execute("SELECT path FROM artifacts WHERE job_id = ? ORDER BY id", (jid,))]
    return files


def _slug(name):
    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9._-]+", "-", name).strip("-.").lower() or "group"


def download_group(req):
    gid = req.params["id"]
    if gid == 0:
        name = "ungrouped"
    else:
        r = db.conn().execute("SELECT name FROM grp WHERE id = ?", (gid,)).fetchone()
        if r is None:
            raise server.HttpError(404, "group not found")
        name = r[0]
    files = group_zip_files(gid)
    if not files:
        raise server.HttpError(404, "no artifacts")

    def write(f):
        # ZIP_STORED: APKs are already compressed; each file is opened right before it is written (spec build §3.4)
        with zipfile.ZipFile(f, "w", zipfile.ZIP_STORED, allowZip64=True) as z:
            for path, arc in files:
                try:
                    src = open(path, "rb")
                except FileNotFoundError:
                    continue
                with src, z.open(zipfile.ZipInfo(arc, time.localtime()[:6]), "w", force_zip64=True) as dst:
                    shutil.copyfileobj(src, dst, 1 << 20)

    return server.StreamResponse(write, f"{_slug(name)}-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.zip")


ROUTES = [
    ("POST", "/api/items/{id}/build", post_item_build),
    ("POST", "/api/builds", post_builds),
    ("GET", "/api/items/{id}/artifacts", list_item_artifacts),
    ("GET", "/api/artifacts/{id}/download", download_artifact),
    ("GET", "/api/groups/{id}/download", download_group),
]
```

**User-run steps (collected):**
1. Task P3.5 Step 7: `docker compose -f local/docker-compose.yml build app` (arm64) + in-image tool checks; amd64 `docker buildx build` (> 150 s, network).
2. Catalog review acceptance: on the running server `POST /api/upstreams/check` returns every upstream with `check_error` null (token present, GitHub).
3. Task P4.5 Step 6: live morphe YouTube pull + build + `apksigner verify`, and the override-in-CLI-log check (APKMirror, long).
4. Seed report "User checks": confirm the MicroG RE package name after the first MicroG pull.

**Uncertainties:**
- Morphe CLI 1.18.1 `list-patches -f <package>` and `options-create` flags: captured in Task P3.2 Step 1 / P2.6 Step 1; a rejection stops the task as BLOCKED (inspect contract depends on it).
- APKEditor 1.4.9 `info` output keys: `apk_info` greps are adjusted to the captured file if the first run fails (Task P3.2 Step 5 note).
- MicroG RE package `app.revanced.android.gms`: not verifiable offline (seed report user check).
- BiliRoamingM / Dropped-Patches ship revanced `.jar` bundles; seed regex `\.jar$`; inspect and build may fail (decision Q3 accepts).
- `resolve_upstreams({"all":true})` returns every upstream row incl. dependency rows (spec L290 names only source + CLI upstreams); chosen so the daily schedule also refreshes MicroG. Lead may revert to the literal reading (one line in Task P2.2).
- First check after the seed emits one `upstream.new_version` per upstream (`notified_tag` starts NULL, spec L534 literal).

**Self-review:** spec coverage — catalog §1–§12, build §2–§8, core §1/§12 container rows, xposed §4 seed rows map to tasks above; placeholder scan (acceptance 1 grep) = 0 hits; names in code match §X and the outline Plan-fixed table (`item_dependencies` incl. `upstream_id`, `run_retention`, `inspect.sh` file names, `job_input` keys checked against the P3 fixture by tests).

### Shared conventions for P5, P7, P8, P11

- Test commands `T_PY`, `T_SH`, `T_COMPOSE` are the ones defined in the plan's "Test commands" section. A single test file runs with `T_PY '<file name>'`.
- Test classes derive from P1's `helpers.DataTestCase` (fresh `db.DATA`, every schema file migrated, `events.reset()`, `jobs.reset()`). P5/P7 route handlers are called directly with `device_helpers.req(params=..., json=...)` (a `types.SimpleNamespace` with `method, path, params, query, headers, json`); P8 tests go through HTTP with `helpers.serve(self, ['catalog'])`, `helpers.login`, `helpers.http`. The HTTP layer (auth, CSRF) is P1's and is not re-tested here.
- Job handlers are tested by calling them directly with `job = types.SimpleNamespace(id=N, kind=..., target={...}, options={...}, trigger=..., cancel_event=threading.Event())` and a `FakeLog` (`line(text)` appends to `.lines`). A matching `jobs` row is inserted by SQL first, because `ota_jobs.job_id` and `ota_installs.job_id` reference `jobs(id)`.
- Catalog/build rows in tests are inserted by SQL with the column set of row X17.
- Every commit: `git add <own files> && git commit -m "<msg>" -- <own files>`; no `;` in the message; no `stash` (incl. `stash list`), `reset`, `checkout --`, `clean`, `add -A`.

---

### P5 Devices, OTA, notifications

Spec: device §1–§8 (L866–1034), §0.3 L44–63, §0.5 L103–107, L126–127. Owner files (round 1, impl-device): `local/webui/{devices,ota,notify}.py`, `local/webui/schema/004_device.sql`, `local/webui/tests/test_device_*.py`, `local/webui/tests/device_helpers.py`, `local/webui/tests/fixtures/device/`.

Module split: `devices.py` = adb runner + mDNS browser + device manager + device routes (everything that talks to adb about a phone's presence); `ota.py` = planner + executor + OTA job handler + recovery routes + after-build subscriber + `GET /api/devices` read model; `notify.py` = notifier. `ota.py` imports `devices`; `devices.py` never imports `ota`.

#### Task 5.1: Device schema and adb output parsers

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/schema/004_device.sql`
- Create: `local/webui/devices.py` (parsers part)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_device_adb.py`
- Create: `local/webui/tests/fixtures/device/adb-devices.txt`, `getprop.txt`, `pm-list.txt`
- Create: `local/webui/tests/device_helpers.py` (P5/P7 test helper, not a test module: `setup_db(tc)`, `add_job(...)`, `add_catalog_item(...)`, `add_artifact(...)`, `FakeLog`, `req(...)`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P1 `db.migrate`, `helpers.DataTestCase` (X4); P2 `003_catalog.sql` (`item` table), P1 `001_core.sql` (`jobs` table).
- Produces:
  - tables `devices`, `device_assignments`, `ota_jobs`, `ota_installs` (spec L897–917), index `ota_installs_lookup(device_id, kind, ref_id, id DESC)`.
  - `devices.parse_devices(text: str) -> dict[str, str]` (`"host:port" -> state`, states `device`/`offline`/`unauthorized`/other raw word).
  - `devices.parse_getprop(text: str) -> dict[str, str]` (`[key]: [value]` lines).
  - `devices.parse_packages(text: str) -> set[str]` (`package:<name>` lines).
  - `devices.install_error(text: str) -> str | None` (`None` when output contains a line equal to `Success`; else one error code of spec §5).
  - `devices.ERROR_CODES` dict `INSTALL_FAILED_* -> code`.

**Behavior:**
- `install_error`: `INSTALL_FAILED_UPDATE_INCOMPATIBLE` → `SIGNATURE_MISMATCH`; `INSTALL_FAILED_VERSION_DOWNGRADE` → `VERSION_DOWNGRADE`; `INSTALL_FAILED_INSUFFICIENT_STORAGE` → `INSUFFICIENT_STORAGE`; `INSTALL_FAILED_NO_MATCHING_ABIS` → `NO_MATCHING_ABI`; `INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED` and `INSTALL_FAILED_ABORTED` → `USER_REJECTED`; anything else without `Success` → `ADB_ERROR`.
- `parse_devices` ignores the header line `List of devices attached`, blank lines and `* daemon …` lines.
- Schema: `ota_jobs.device_id` and `ota_installs.device_id` are plain columns without FK (§0.3 delete rules), `device_assignments` cascades from both `devices` and `item`.

**Constraints:**
- stdlib only; parsers are pure functions (no IO).
- Schema file loads after `003_catalog.sql` (number order); P5 tests need P2's `003_catalog.sql` committed (outline review device S7: P2 lands its schema file in the early interface window). Until it lands, the implementer may run against a local uncommitted stub; the stub is never committed.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_device_adb.py'` exit 0 with tests `test_parse_devices_states`, `test_parse_getprop_facts`, `test_parse_packages`, `test_install_error_codes` (all 7 mappings + `Success` → None + unknown `Failure [X]` → `ADB_ERROR`), `test_schema_cascade` (delete device → assignments gone, `ota_installs` row stays; delete item → assignment gone).

```python
"""Shared helpers for the device (P5) and ci (P7) tests. Not a test module."""
import json
import pathlib
import threading
import types

from webui import db

FIXTURES = pathlib.Path(__file__).parent / 'fixtures' / 'device'
OTA_DEFAULTS = {'retries': 0, 'mode': 'normal', 'package': None, 'notify': True}


def canon(v):
    return json.dumps(v, sort_keys=True, separators=(',', ':'))


def req(params=None, body=None, query=None, method='POST', path='/'):
    return types.SimpleNamespace(method=method, path=path, params=params or {}, query=query or {},
                                 headers={}, json={} if body is None else body, session=None)


class FakeLog:
    def __init__(self):
        self.lines = []

    def line(self, text):
        self.lines.append(str(text))

    def run(self, argv, cwd=None, env=None, timeout=None, on_line=None):
        raise AssertionError('log.run is not used by device handlers')


def add_job(kind='ota', target=None, options=None, trigger='manual', status='running'):
    opts = dict(OTA_DEFAULTS, **(options or {})) if kind == 'ota' else (options or {})
    with db.tx() as c:
        return c.execute('INSERT INTO jobs(kind,status,trigger,target,options,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                         (kind, status, trigger, canon(target or {}), canon(opts), db.utcnow())).lastrowid


def job(jid, target=None, options=None, trigger='manual', kind='ota'):
    opts = dict(OTA_DEFAULTS, **(options or {})) if kind == 'ota' else (options or {})
    return types.SimpleNamespace(id=jid, kind=kind, target=target or {}, options=opts, trigger=trigger,
                                 schedule_id=None, cancel_event=threading.Event())


def add_group(gid, position):
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('INSERT INTO grp(id,name,color,position) VALUES (?,?,NULL,?)', (gid, f'g{gid}', position))


def add_catalog_item(item_id, package, source_id='src', group_id=None, position=0, enabled=1, key=None):
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO upstream(id,kind,repo,channel,tag,asset_regex,latest_tag,downloaded_tag) "
                  "VALUES (1,'patches','o/p','stable',NULL,'\\.mpp$','v1','v1')")
        c.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO upstream(id,kind,repo,channel,tag,asset_regex,latest_tag,downloaded_tag) "
                  "VALUES (2,'cli','o/cli','stable',NULL,'\\.jar$','v1','v1')")
        c.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO source(id,name,upstream_id,cli_upstream_id,engine,keystore) "
                  "VALUES (?,?,1,2,'morphe','morphe.keystore')", (source_id, source_id))
        c.execute('INSERT OR IGNORE INTO app(package,display_name) VALUES (?,?)', (package, package))
        c.execute('INSERT INTO item(id,source_id,package,key,display_name,group_id,position,enabled,version_mode,'
                  "version_pin,allow_nearest,variants,patch_overrides,engine_options) "
                  "VALUES (?,?,?,?,NULL,?,?,?,'auto',NULL,0,'[]','{}',NULL)",
                  (item_id, source_id, package, key or f'k{item_id}', group_id, position, enabled))


def add_device(host='192.168.1.20', port=37011, serial='SER1', abi='arm64-v8a,armeabi-v7a', after_build=0,
               mdns_name=None):
    with db.tx() as c:
        return c.execute('INSERT INTO devices(name,serial,mdns_name,host,connect_port,model,abi_list,sdk,'
                         'android_release,ota_after_build,last_seen_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,NULL)',
                         ('Pixel', serial, mdns_name, host, port, 'Pixel', abi, 35, '15', after_build)).lastrowid


def assign(device_id, *item_ids):
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.executemany('INSERT INTO device_assignments(device_id,item_id) VALUES (?,?)',
                      [(device_id, i) for i in item_ids])
```

```python
from webui import db, devices
from webui.tests import helpers
from webui.tests.device_helpers import FIXTURES, add_catalog_item, add_device, add_job, assign


class ParserTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def test_parse_devices_states(self):
        st = devices.parse_devices((FIXTURES / 'adb-devices.txt').read_text())
        self.assertEqual(st, {'192.168.1.20:37011': 'device', '192.168.1.21:40001': 'offline',
                              '192.168.1.22:41234': 'unauthorized'})

    def test_parse_getprop_facts(self):
        p = devices.parse_getprop((FIXTURES / 'getprop.txt').read_text())
        self.assertEqual(p['ro.serialno'], '32051JEHN09137')
        self.assertEqual(p['ro.product.cpu.abilist'], 'arm64-v8a,armeabi-v7a,armeabi')
        self.assertEqual(p['ro.build.version.sdk'], '35')

    def test_parse_packages(self):
        self.assertEqual(devices.parse_packages((FIXTURES / 'pm-list.txt').read_text()),
                         {'com.google.android.youtube', 'app.revanced.android.gms', 'com.android.settings'})

    def test_install_error_codes(self):
        cases = {
            'Failure [INSTALL_FAILED_UPDATE_INCOMPATIBLE: Package x signatures do not match]': 'SIGNATURE_MISMATCH',
            'Failure [INSTALL_FAILED_VERSION_DOWNGRADE]': 'VERSION_DOWNGRADE',
            'Failure [INSTALL_FAILED_INSUFFICIENT_STORAGE]': 'INSUFFICIENT_STORAGE',
            'Failure [INSTALL_FAILED_NO_MATCHING_ABIS: ...]': 'NO_MATCHING_ABI',
            'Failure [INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED: Install canceled by user]': 'USER_REJECTED',
            'Failure [INSTALL_FAILED_ABORTED: User rejected permissions]': 'USER_REJECTED',
            'Failure [INSTALL_FAILED_OTHER]': 'ADB_ERROR',
            'adb: failed to install x.apk': 'ADB_ERROR',
        }
        for text, code in cases.items():
            self.assertEqual(devices.install_error(text), code, text)
        self.assertIsNone(devices.install_error('Performing Streamed Install\nSuccess\n'))

    def test_schema_cascade(self):
        add_catalog_item(1, 'com.google.android.youtube')
        d = add_device()
        assign(d, 1)
        jid = add_job(target={'device_id': d})
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO ota_installs(job_id,device_id,kind,ref_id,package,result) "
                      "VALUES (?,?,'item',1,'com.google.android.youtube','installed')", (jid, d))
            c.execute('DELETE FROM devices WHERE id=?', (d,))
        c = db.conn()
        self.assertEqual(c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM device_assignments').fetchone()[0], 0)
        self.assertEqual(c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ota_installs').fetchone()[0], 1)
        d2 = add_device(serial='SER2')
        assign(d2, 1)
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute('DELETE FROM item WHERE id=1')
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM device_assignments').fetchone()[0], 0)
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the schema** — `local/webui/schema/004_device.sql`

```sql
-- device part (spec device §3.1). ota_* keep device_id without FK so OTA history outlives the device.
CREATE TABLE devices (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  name TEXT NOT NULL,
  serial TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE,
  mdns_name TEXT,
  host TEXT NOT NULL,
  connect_port INTEGER NOT NULL,
  model TEXT,
  abi_list TEXT,
  sdk INTEGER,
  android_release TEXT,
  ota_after_build INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
  last_seen_at TEXT
);
CREATE TABLE device_assignments (
  device_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES devices(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  item_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES item(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  PRIMARY KEY (device_id, item_id)
);
CREATE TABLE ota_jobs (
  job_id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES jobs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  device_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  item_ids TEXT,
  mode TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'normal' CHECK (mode IN ('normal','force_reinstall','downgrade')),
  outcome TEXT CHECK (outcome IN ('done','partial','failed','skipped_offline'))
);
CREATE TABLE ota_installs (
  id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
  job_id INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES jobs(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
  device_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  kind TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('item','dependency')),
  ref_id INTEGER NOT NULL,
  build_job_id INTEGER,
  upstream_tag TEXT,
  package TEXT NOT NULL,
  version_name TEXT,
  version_code INTEGER,
  result TEXT NOT NULL CHECK (result IN ('installed','up_to_date','failed')),
  error_code TEXT,
  error_text TEXT
);
CREATE INDEX ota_installs_lookup ON ota_installs(device_id, kind, ref_id, id DESC);
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the parsers** — `local/webui/devices.py` (first part; later tasks append)

```python
"""Device part: adb runner, mDNS browser, device manager and routes (spec device §2-§5)."""
import re

ERROR_CODES = {
    'INSTALL_FAILED_UPDATE_INCOMPATIBLE': 'SIGNATURE_MISMATCH',
    'INSTALL_FAILED_VERSION_DOWNGRADE': 'VERSION_DOWNGRADE',
    'INSTALL_FAILED_INSUFFICIENT_STORAGE': 'INSUFFICIENT_STORAGE',
    'INSTALL_FAILED_NO_MATCHING_ABIS': 'NO_MATCHING_ABI',
    'INSTALL_FAILED_USER_RESTRICTED': 'USER_REJECTED',
    'INSTALL_FAILED_ABORTED': 'USER_REJECTED',
}
_PROP = re.compile(r'^\[([^\]]+)\]: \[(.*)\]$')


def parse_devices(text):
    out = {}
    for line in text.splitlines():
        parts = line.split()
        if len(parts) >= 2 and not line.startswith(('*', 'List of devices')):
            out[parts[0]] = parts[1]
    return out


def parse_getprop(text):
    return {m.group(1): m.group(2) for m in map(_PROP.match, text.splitlines()) if m}


def parse_packages(text):
    return {line[8:].strip() for line in text.splitlines() if line.startswith('package:')}


def install_error(text):
    if any(line.strip() == 'Success' for line in text.splitlines()):
        return None
    for name, code in ERROR_CODES.items():
        if name in text:
            return code
    return 'ADB_ERROR'
```

#### Task 5.2: Adb runner with fake-adb seam

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/devices.py` (runner part)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/fixtures/device/fake-adb` (Python script, run as `python3 fake-adb …`)
- Modify: `local/webui/tests/device_helpers.py` (add `FakeAdb`)
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_device_adb.py` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `devices.ADB: list[str] = ["adb"]` (X14).
  - `devices.adb(args: list[str], *, serial: str | None = None, timeout: float = 60, cancel_event=None, log=None) -> tuple[int, str]` — runs `ADB + (["-s", serial] if serial else []) + args`, returns `(exit code, combined stdout+stderr text)`; one lock per `serial` (`None` = global lock); raises `devices.AdbTimeout` after `timeout` s or when `cancel_event` is set (child killed).
  - `devices.AdbTimeout(Exception)`.
  - `devices.start()` → `adb(["start-server"])`, logs a warning to stdout on failure, never raises.
  - test helper `tests/device_helpers.py`: `FakeAdb(tc)` (installs itself: sets `devices.ADB` and env `FAKE_ADB_DIR`, restores both via `tc.addCleanup`) with `.rule(*match: str, out: str = "", rc: int = 0, times: int | None = None, hang: bool = False, delay: float = 0) -> FakeAdb`, `.calls() -> list[list[str]]`.

**Behavior:**
- fake-adb reads `$FAKE_ADB_DIR/rules.json` (list of `{match, out, rc, times, hang, delay}`), appends its argv as one JSON line to `$FAKE_ADB_DIR/argv.log`, picks the first rule whose every `match` token is in argv, decrements `times` (rule removed at 0, file rewritten), blocks 60 s when `hang` or `delay` s, prints `out`, exits `rc`. No rule → exit 0, no output.
- `adb` binary missing (`FileNotFoundError`) → `(127, "adb not found")`.
- Server crash (spec L1002): output contains `cannot connect to daemon` or `daemon not running` → run `adb start-server` once, then retry the command once; return the retry result.
- `log` given → every output line is passed to `log.line("adb: " + line)`.

**Constraints:**
- argv list only, `subprocess.Popen(..., stdout=PIPE, stderr=STDOUT, text=True, start_new_session=True)`; never `shell=True`.
- Timeout and cancel are checked with `communicate(timeout=1)` steps; kill = `os.killpg(proc.pid, SIGKILL)`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_device_adb.py'` exit 0 incl. `test_adb_argv_and_serial` (argv log equals `["-s","1.2.3.4:5555","shell","getprop"]`), `test_adb_daemon_retry` (first call prints `cannot connect to daemon`, rc 1; calls = cmd, `start-server`, cmd; result rc 0), `test_adb_timeout_kills` (rule `hang=True` → `AdbTimeout` within 3 s at `timeout=1`), `test_adb_cancel_kills` (cancel event set → `AdbTimeout`), `test_adb_log_lines`, `test_adb_serialized_per_device` (two calls on one serial take ≥ 1.2 s with a 0.6 s fake delay; on two serials < 1.15 s), `test_adb_missing_binary`.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the fake adb and its helper**

`local/webui/tests/fixtures/device/fake-adb`:
```python
"""Fake adb for tests. Run as: python3 fake-adb <adb args>. Rules: see device_helpers.FakeAdb."""
import json
import os
import sys
import threading

d = os.environ['FAKE_ADB_DIR']
argv = sys.argv[1:]
with open(os.path.join(d, 'argv.log'), 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
    f.write(json.dumps(argv) + '\n')
path = os.path.join(d, 'rules.json')
with open(path, encoding='utf-8') as f:
    rules = json.load(f)
for i, r in enumerate(rules):
    if all(t in argv for t in r['match']):
        if r.get('times') is not None:
            r['times'] -= 1
            if r['times'] <= 0:
                rules.pop(i)
            with open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(rules, f)
        if r.get('hang'):
            threading.Event().wait(60)
        if r.get('delay'):
            threading.Event().wait(r['delay'])
        sys.stdout.write(r.get('out', ''))
        sys.exit(r.get('rc', 0))
sys.exit(0)
```

Append to `local/webui/tests/device_helpers.py`:
```python
import os
import sys
import tempfile

from webui import devices


class FakeAdb:
    """Points devices.ADB at fixtures/device/fake-adb for one test; restored on cleanup."""

    def __init__(self, tc):
        tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        tc.addCleanup(tmp.cleanup)
        self.dir = pathlib.Path(tmp.name)
        (self.dir / 'rules.json').write_text('[]')
        old_adb, old_env = devices.ADB, os.environ.get('FAKE_ADB_DIR')
        devices.ADB = [sys.executable, str(FIXTURES / 'fake-adb')]
        os.environ['FAKE_ADB_DIR'] = str(self.dir)

        def restore():
            devices.ADB = old_adb
            if old_env is None:
                os.environ.pop('FAKE_ADB_DIR', None)
            else:
                os.environ['FAKE_ADB_DIR'] = old_env
        tc.addCleanup(restore)

    def rule(self, *match, out='', rc=0, times=None, hang=False, delay=0):
        p = self.dir / 'rules.json'
        rules = json.loads(p.read_text())
        rules.append({'match': list(match), 'out': out, 'rc': rc, 'times': times, 'hang': hang, 'delay': delay})
        p.write_text(json.dumps(rules))
        return self

    def calls(self):
        p = self.dir / 'argv.log'
        return [json.loads(x) for x in p.read_text().splitlines()] if p.exists() else []
```

```python
import threading
import time

from webui.tests.device_helpers import FakeAdb, FakeLog


class RunnerTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def test_adb_argv_and_serial(self):
        fa = FakeAdb(self)
        fa.rule('getprop', out='[ro.serialno]: [X]\n')
        rc, out = devices.adb(['shell', 'getprop'], serial='1.2.3.4:5555')
        self.assertEqual((rc, out), (0, '[ro.serialno]: [X]\n'))
        self.assertEqual(fa.calls(), [['-s', '1.2.3.4:5555', 'shell', 'getprop']])

    def test_adb_daemon_retry(self):
        fa = FakeAdb(self)
        fa.rule('getprop', out='* cannot connect to daemon\n', rc=1, times=1)
        rc, out = devices.adb(['shell', 'getprop'])
        self.assertEqual(rc, 0)
        self.assertEqual(fa.calls(), [['shell', 'getprop'], ['start-server'], ['shell', 'getprop']])

    def test_adb_timeout_kills(self):
        fa = FakeAdb(self)
        fa.rule('install', hang=True)
        t = time.monotonic()
        with self.assertRaises(devices.AdbTimeout):
            devices.adb(['install', '-r', 'x.apk'], timeout=1)
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - t, 3)

    def test_adb_cancel_kills(self):
        fa = FakeAdb(self)
        fa.rule('install', hang=True)
        ev = threading.Event()
        threading.Timer(0.3, ev.set).start()
        with self.assertRaises(devices.AdbTimeout):
            devices.adb(['install', 'x.apk'], timeout=60, cancel_event=ev)

    def test_adb_log_lines(self):
        fa = FakeAdb(self)
        fa.rule('install', out='Performing Streamed Install\nSuccess\n')
        log = FakeLog()
        devices.adb(['install', 'x.apk'], log=log)
        self.assertEqual(log.lines, ['adb: Performing Streamed Install', 'adb: Success'])

    def test_adb_serialized_per_device(self):
        fa = FakeAdb(self)
        fa.rule('shell', delay=0.6)

        def two(serial_a, serial_b):
            ts = [threading.Thread(target=devices.adb, args=(['shell', 'x'],), kwargs={'serial': s})
                  for s in (serial_a, serial_b)]
            t = time.monotonic()
            for th in ts:
                th.start()
            for th in ts:
                th.join()
            return time.monotonic() - t
        self.assertGreaterEqual(two('1.2.3.4:5555', '1.2.3.4:5555'), 1.2)
        self.assertLess(two('1.2.3.4:5555', '5.6.7.8:5555'), 1.15)

    def test_adb_missing_binary(self):
        old = devices.ADB
        devices.ADB = ['/nonexistent/adb']
        self.addCleanup(setattr, devices, 'ADB', old)
        self.assertEqual(devices.adb(['devices']), (127, 'adb not found'))
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement the runner** — append to `local/webui/devices.py` (and add `import os`, `import signal`, `import subprocess`, `import threading`, `import time` to the import block at the top)

```python
ADB = ['adb']
_CRASH = ('cannot connect to daemon', 'daemon not running')
_locks = {}
_locks_guard = threading.Lock()


class AdbTimeout(Exception):
    pass


def _lock(serial):
    with _locks_guard:
        return _locks.setdefault(serial, threading.Lock())


def _exec(argv, timeout, cancel_event):
    try:
        p = subprocess.Popen(argv, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL, stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.STDOUT,
                             text=True, errors='replace', start_new_session=True)
    except FileNotFoundError:
        return 127, 'adb not found'
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    while True:
        try:
            out, _ = p.communicate(timeout=1)
            return p.returncode, out
        except subprocess.TimeoutExpired:
            if time.monotonic() > deadline or (cancel_event is not None and cancel_event.is_set()):
                try:
                    os.killpg(p.pid, signal.SIGKILL)
                except ProcessLookupError:
                    pass
                p.communicate()
                raise AdbTimeout(' '.join(argv[len(ADB):]))


def adb(args, *, serial=None, timeout=60, cancel_event=None, log=None):
    """Only entry point that runs adb. One lock per serial (None = global)."""
    argv = list(ADB) + (['-s', serial] if serial else []) + list(args)
    with _lock(serial):
        rc, out = _exec(argv, timeout, cancel_event)
        if any(s in out for s in _CRASH):
            _exec(list(ADB) + ['start-server'], 60, None)
            rc, out = _exec(argv, timeout, cancel_event)
    if log is not None:
        for line in out.splitlines():
            log.line('adb: ' + line)
    return rc, out


def start():
    try:
        rc, out = adb(['start-server'])
    except AdbTimeout as e:
        rc, out = 1, f'timeout: {e}'
    if rc != 0:
        print(f'warning: adb start-server failed: {out.strip()}', flush=True)
```

#### Task 5.3: mDNS browser (DNS-SD legacy unicast)

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/devices.py` (mDNS part)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_device_mdns.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `devices.DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT: float = 2.0` (X14).
  - `devices.build_query() -> bytes` (one DNS message, id 0, 2 PTR questions `_adb-tls-pairing._tcp.local`, `_adb-tls-connect._tcp.local`, QU bit off).
  - `devices.parse_packet(data: bytes) -> list[tuple[str, int, object]]` — `(name, rtype, rdata)`; rdata: PTR → str, SRV → `(port, target)`, A → dotted str; other types skipped; never raises.
  - `devices.browse(timeout: float | None = None) -> dict[str, dict]` — `instance -> {"service": "pairing"|"connect", "host": str, "port": int}`; also replaces the module map `devices.DISCOVERED` and stamps `last_seen`.
  - `devices._udp_socket() -> socket.socket` (patched in tests).

**Behavior:**
- Name decoding follows compression pointers; a pointer must point strictly before the start of the name being read and at most 32 jumps are allowed; a violation, truncated packet or bad label ends parsing of that packet: records parsed before it are returned, the rest skipped.
- Instance name = first label of the PTR target (e.g. `adb-ABC123-x1y2`); service from the PTR owner name.
- Only instances with PTR + SRV + A resolved are returned.
- `sendto` raising `OSError`, or `recvfrom` timing out with nothing received → empty dict (D4: macOS bridge path), never an exception.

**Constraints:**
- No bind to 5353, no multicast join (spec D3); socket bound to `("", 0)`, `settimeout` per remaining time.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_device_mdns.py'` exit 0 with `test_parse_compressed_reply` (PTR+SRV+A with compression → `{"adb-ABC123-x1y2": {"service":"connect","host":"192.168.1.20","port":37011}}`), `test_pointer_loop_returns` (self-referencing pointer → returns within 1 s, no exception), `test_truncated_packet` (packet cut mid-record → records before the cut only), `test_forward_pointer_rejected`, `test_sendto_oserror_empty` (patched socket raises `OSError` → `browse()` returns `{}`), `test_browse_collects_replies` (patched socket returns 2 packets then `socket.timeout`).

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `local/webui/tests/test_device_mdns.py`

```python
import socket
import struct
import time
import unittest
from unittest import mock

from webui import devices


def qname(name):
    return b''.join(bytes([len(p)]) + p.encode() for p in name.split('.')) + b'\0'


def ptr(off):
    return struct.pack('>H', 0xC000 | off)


def build_reply():
    """PTR + SRV + A for one connect service, every repeated name compressed."""
    b = bytearray(struct.pack('>HHHHHH', 0, 0x8400, 0, 1, 0, 2))
    svc_off = len(b)
    b += qname('_adb-tls-connect._tcp.local')
    local_off = svc_off + bytes(b[svc_off:]).index(b'\x05local')
    inst_rdata = bytes([15]) + b'adb-ABC123-x1y2' + ptr(svc_off)
    b += struct.pack('>HHIH', 12, 1, 120, len(inst_rdata))
    inst_off = len(b)
    b += inst_rdata
    srv_rdata = struct.pack('>HHH', 0, 0, 37011) + bytes([8]) + b'Pixel-7a' + ptr(local_off)
    b += ptr(inst_off) + struct.pack('>HHIH', 33, 1, 120, len(srv_rdata))
    tgt_off = len(b) + 6
    b += srv_rdata
    b += ptr(tgt_off) + struct.pack('>HHIH', 1, 1, 120, 4) + bytes([192, 168, 1, 20])
    return bytes(b)


class FakeSock:
    def __init__(self, packets=(), send_error=None):
        self.packets, self.send_error, self.sent = list(packets), send_error, []

    def __enter__(self):
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *a):
        return False

    def bind(self, addr):
        pass

    def settimeout(self, t):
        pass

    def sendto(self, data, addr):
        if self.send_error:
            raise self.send_error
        self.sent.append((data, addr))

    def recvfrom(self, n):
        if not self.packets:
            raise socket.timeout()
        return self.packets.pop(0), ('192.168.1.20', 5353)

    def close(self):
        pass


EXPECTED = {'adb-ABC123-x1y2': {'service': 'connect', 'host': '192.168.1.20', 'port': 37011}}


class MdnsTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_query_has_two_ptr_questions(self):
        q = devices.build_query()
        self.assertEqual(struct.unpack_from('>HHHHHH', q), (0, 0, 2, 0, 0, 0))
        self.assertIn(b'\x10_adb-tls-pairing\x04_tcp\x05local\x00\x00\x0c\x00\x01', q)

    def test_parse_compressed_reply(self):
        recs = devices.parse_packet(build_reply())
        self.assertEqual([r[1] for r in recs], [12, 33, 1])
        self.assertEqual(devices._merge(recs), EXPECTED)

    def test_pointer_loop_returns(self):
        pkt = struct.pack('>HHHHHH', 0, 0x8400, 0, 1, 0, 0) + ptr(12) + b'\x00' * 10
        t = time.monotonic()
        self.assertEqual(devices.parse_packet(pkt), [])
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - t, 1)

    def test_forward_pointer_rejected(self):
        pkt = struct.pack('>HHHHHH', 0, 0x8400, 0, 1, 0, 0) + ptr(40) + b'\x00' * 40
        self.assertEqual(devices.parse_packet(pkt), [])

    def test_truncated_packet(self):
        recs = devices.parse_packet(build_reply()[:-3])
        self.assertEqual([r[1] for r in recs], [12, 33])

    def test_sendto_oserror_empty(self):
        with mock.patch.object(devices, '_udp_socket', return_value=FakeSock(send_error=OSError('unreachable'))):
            self.assertEqual(devices.browse(timeout=0.2), {})

    def test_browse_collects_replies(self):
        sock = FakeSock(packets=[build_reply(), b'\x00\x01'])
        with mock.patch.object(devices, '_udp_socket', return_value=sock):
            self.assertEqual(devices.browse(timeout=0.2), EXPECTED)
        self.assertEqual(sock.sent[0][1], ('224.0.0.251', 5353))
        self.assertIn('last_seen', devices.DISCOVERED['adb-ABC123-x1y2'])
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — append to `local/webui/devices.py` (add `import socket`, `import struct` to the imports)

```python
DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT = 2.0
SERVICES = {'_adb-tls-pairing._tcp.local': 'pairing', '_adb-tls-connect._tcp.local': 'connect'}
DISCOVERED = {}
_MAX_JUMPS = 32


def _qname(name):
    return b''.join(bytes([len(p)]) + p.encode() for p in name.split('.')) + b'\0'


def build_query():
    """DNS-SD legacy unicast query (RFC 6762 6.7): id 0, two PTR questions, sent from an ephemeral port."""
    body = b''.join(_qname(n) + struct.pack('>HH', 12, 1) for n in SERVICES)
    return struct.pack('>HHHHHH', 0, 0, len(SERVICES), 0, 0, 0) + body


def _name(data, off):
    """Decode a (compressed) name; returns (name, offset after it). Pointers must go strictly backwards."""
    labels, end, start, jumps = [], None, off, 0
    while True:
        n = data[off]
        if n == 0:
            off += 1
            break
        if n & 0xC0 == 0xC0:
            target = ((n & 0x3F) << 8) | data[off + 1]
            if target >= start or jumps >= _MAX_JUMPS:
                raise ValueError('bad compression pointer')
            if end is None:
                end = off + 2
            jumps += 1
            off = start = target
            continue
        if n & 0xC0 or off + 1 + n > len(data):
            raise ValueError('bad label')
        labels.append(data[off + 1:off + 1 + n].decode('utf-8', 'replace'))
        off += 1 + n
    return '.'.join(labels), (off if end is None else end)


def parse_packet(data):
    """Records (name, type, rdata) for PTR/SRV/A; stops at the first malformed part, never raises."""
    out = []
    try:
        qd, an, ns, ar = struct.unpack_from('>HHHH', data, 4)
        off = 12
        for _ in range(qd):
            off = _name(data, off)[1] + 4
        for _ in range(an + ns + ar):
            name, off = _name(data, off)
            rtype, _cls, _ttl, rdlen = struct.unpack_from('>HHIH', data, off)
            rd, off = off + 10, off + 10 + rdlen
            if off > len(data):
                raise ValueError('truncated record')
            if rtype == 12:
                out.append((name, 12, _name(data, rd)[0]))
            elif rtype == 33:
                port = struct.unpack_from('>H', data, rd + 4)[0]
                out.append((name, 33, (port, _name(data, rd + 6)[0])))
            elif rtype == 1 and rdlen == 4:
                out.append((name, 1, socket.inet_ntoa(data[rd:rd + 4])))
    except (IndexError, ValueError, struct.error):
        pass
    return out


def _merge(records):
    srv = {n.lower(): v for n, t, v in records if t == 33}
    addr = {n.lower(): v for n, t, v in records if t == 1}
    out = {}
    for owner, t, inst in records:
        kind = SERVICES.get(owner.lower()) if t == 12 else None
        s = srv.get(inst.lower()) if kind else None
        if s and s[1].lower() in addr:
            out[inst.split('.')[0]] = {'service': kind, 'host': addr[s[1].lower()], 'port': s[0]}
    return out


def _udp_socket():
    return socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)


def browse(timeout=None):
    """One D3 query; {} when nothing answers or the network refuses (macOS bridge, spec D4)."""
    global DISCOVERED
    timeout = DISCOVERY_TIMEOUT if timeout is None else timeout
    records = []
    try:
        with _udp_socket() as s:
            s.bind(('', 0))
            s.sendto(build_query(), ('224.0.0.251', 5353))
            end = time.monotonic() + timeout
            while (left := end - time.monotonic()) > 0:
                s.settimeout(left)
                try:
                    data, _ = s.recvfrom(9000)
                except socket.timeout:
                    break
                records += parse_packet(data)
    except OSError:
        pass
    found = _merge(records)
    now = time.time()
    DISCOVERED = {k: dict(v, last_seen=now) for k, v in found.items()}
    return found
```

#### Task 5.4: Device manager and device routes

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/devices.py` (manager + `ROUTES` + `resolve_devices`)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_device_routes.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P1 `server.HttpError`, `server.validate_host(v)`, `server.validate_port(v)` (raise `HttpError(400)`), `db.tx()`, `db.conn()`.
- Produces:
  - `devices.validate_code(v) -> str` (`^[0-9]{6}$` on a `str`, else `HttpError(400, "code must be 6 digits")`).
  - `devices.address(dev: dict) -> str` = `"<host>:<connect_port>"`.
  - `devices.get(device_id: int) -> dict | None` (row as dict).
  - `devices.reconnect(dev: dict, *, cancel_event=None, log=None) -> str` — `"online" | "offline" | "unauthorized"`; on step 3 success updates `host`/`connect_port`; on online updates `last_seen_at`.
  - `devices.online_states() -> dict[str, str]` (`adb devices` once; any failure → `{}`).
  - `devices.resolve_devices(target: dict) -> list[int]`.
  - routes: `GET /api/devices/discovered`, `POST /api/devices/pair`, `POST /api/devices/{id}/connect`, `PATCH /api/devices/{id}`, `DELETE /api/devices/{id}`, `PUT /api/devices/{id}/assignments`.
  - responses: device object = row columns + `"online": bool` (no `assignments`; the page reloads `GET /api/devices`); pair, connect, PATCH, assignments → `200 {"device": device object}`; connect failure → `409 {"error":"DEVICE_OFFLINE"}` or `409 {"error":"UNAUTHORIZED"}`; pair failure → `400 {"error":"PAIR_FAILED: <last output line>"}`; DELETE → `200 {"deleted": id}`.

**Behavior:**
- Pair (spec L956–962): validate `host`, `pair_port`, `code`, optional `connect_port` before any adb call. Without `connect_port`: `browse()`, take the `connect` entry with the same host; none → `400 "connect_port required: not discovered"` and no adb call. `adb pair host:pair_port code`: output without `Successfully paired` → PAIR_FAILED even on rc 0. `adb connect host:port`: output with `connected to` → continue; output with `unauthorized` or `failed to authenticate` → 409 UNAUTHORIZED; else 409 DEVICE_OFFLINE. `adb -s host:port shell getprop` → facts; missing `ro.serialno` → 409 DEVICE_OFFLINE. Upsert by `serial`: new row `name` = model, `ota_after_build` 0; known serial updates `host`, `connect_port`, `mdns_name`, facts, `last_seen_at`, keeps id → assignments and `ota_installs` stay. `mdns_name` = instance name of the discovered `connect` entry with that host, else unchanged.
- Reconnect (spec L965–970): step 1 `adb devices` state of the address: `device` → online, `unauthorized` → unauthorized; step 2 `adb connect <address>` then `adb devices` again; step 3 if `mdns_name` in a fresh `browse()` with a different host/port → `adb connect` new address, `adb devices`; online → save it; else offline.
- `POST /{id}/connect {host?, connect_port?}`: validated values are saved before reconnect.
- `PATCH` accepts only `name` (str, 1–64 chars after strip) and `ota_after_build` (bool); other keys or types → 400.
- `DELETE`: `adb disconnect <address>` (result ignored), delete row; unknown id → 404.
- Assignments: `item_ids` list of int; unknown item → `400 "unknown item <id>"`; two items with one package → `400 "two items with package <pkg>"`; replace set in one transaction.
- `resolve_devices`: `{"all":true}` → every id ascending; `{"device_ids":[…]}` → those that exist, in given order; else `[]`.
- `GET /api/devices/discovered` → `{"pairing":[{name, host, port, last_seen}], "connect":[{name, host, port, device_id|null, last_seen}]}` (`last_seen` = epoch seconds from `devices.DISCOVERED`); `device_id` matched by `devices.mdns_name`.

**Constraints:**
- Every adb call goes through `devices.adb`. No value reaches argv before validation.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_device_routes.py'` exit 0 with: `test_pair_ok_upserts`, `test_pair_failed_exit0` (rc 0, `Failed: Unable to start pairing client.` → 400 PAIR_FAILED), `test_pair_port_from_mdns` (no `connect_port`, browse patched → `connect 192.168.1.20:37011` in argv log), `test_pair_no_port_400` (empty browse → 400, argv log empty), `test_repair_keeps_assignments_and_history`, `test_invalid_inputs_400` (host `-x`, `1.2.3.4;x`; port `0`, `65536`, `"80"`; code `12345`, `12345a` → each 400 and argv log empty), `test_reconnect_steps_order` (step 1 offline, step 2 fails, step 3 via mdns → argv order devices/connect/devices/connect new/devices; row saves new host/port), `test_unauthorized_state`, `test_patch_rejects_unknown_key`, `test_delete_disconnects_keeps_installs`, `test_assignments_same_package_400`, `test_resolve_devices`.

```python
from unittest import mock

from webui import db, devices
from webui.server import HttpError
from webui.tests import helpers
from webui.tests.device_helpers import (FIXTURES, FakeAdb, add_catalog_item, add_device, add_job, assign, req)

GETPROP = (FIXTURES / 'getprop.txt').read_text()
PAIR_BODY = {'host': '192.168.1.20', 'pair_port': 41000, 'code': '123456', 'connect_port': 37011}
DISC = {'adb-ABC123-x1y2': {'service': 'connect', 'host': '192.168.1.20', 'port': 37011}}


class DeviceRoutesTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.fa = FakeAdb(self)
```
