```python
        self.browse = p.start()
        self.addCleanup(p.stop)

    def pair_ok_rules(self):
        self.fa.rule('pair', out='Successfully paired to 192.168.1.20:41000 [guid=adb-ABC123]\n')
        self.fa.rule('connect', out='connected to 192.168.1.20:37011\n')
        self.fa.rule('getprop', out=GETPROP)

    def test_pair_ok_upserts(self):
        self.pair_ok_rules()
        st, body = devices.pair(req(body=dict(PAIR_BODY)))
        body = body['device']
        self.assertEqual(st, 200)
        self.assertEqual((body['serial'], body['name'], body['sdk'], body['online']), ('32051JEHN09137', 'Pixel 7a', 35, True))
        self.assertEqual(body['abi_list'], 'arm64-v8a,armeabi-v7a,armeabi')
        self.assertEqual(self.fa.calls(), [['pair', '192.168.1.20:41000', '123456'], ['connect', '192.168.1.20:37011'],
                                           ['-s', '192.168.1.20:37011', 'shell', 'getprop']])

    def test_pair_failed_exit0(self):
        self.fa.rule('pair', out='Failed: Unable to start pairing client.\n', rc=0)
        with self.assertRaises(HttpError) as cm:
            devices.pair(req(body=dict(PAIR_BODY)))
        self.assertEqual(cm.exception.status, 400)
        self.assertEqual(cm.exception.message, 'PAIR_FAILED: Failed: Unable to start pairing client.')

    def test_pair_port_from_mdns(self):
        self.browse.return_value = DISC
        self.pair_ok_rules()
        body = {k: v for k, v in PAIR_BODY.items() if k != 'connect_port'}
        dev = devices.pair(req(body=body))[1]['device']
        self.assertIn(['connect', '192.168.1.20:37011'], self.fa.calls())
        self.assertEqual(dev['mdns_name'], 'adb-ABC123-x1y2')

    def test_pair_no_port_400(self):

        with self.assertRaises(HttpError) as cm:

        self.assertEqual(cm.exception.status, 400)
        self.assertEqual(self.fa.calls(), [])

    def test_repair_keeps_assignments_and_history(self):
        add_catalog_item(1, 'com.google.android.youtube')
        d = add_device(host='192.168.1.9', port=5555, serial='32051JEHN09137')
        assign(d, 1)
        jid = add_job(target={'device_id': d})
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO ota_installs(job_id,device_id,kind,ref_id,package,result) "

        self.pair_ok_rules()
        dev = devices.pair(req(body=dict(PAIR_BODY)))[1]['device']
        self.assertEqual(dev['id'], d)
        self.assertEqual((dev['host'], dev['connect_port']), ('192.168.1.20', 37011))
        c = db.conn()
        self.assertEqual(c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM device_assignments WHERE device_id=?', (d,)).fetchone()[0], 1)
        self.assertEqual(c.execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ota_installs WHERE device_id=?', (d,)).fetchone()[0], 1)

    def test_invalid_inputs_400(self):
        bad = [('host', '-x'), ('host', '1.2.3.4;x'), ('pair_port', 0), ('pair_port', 65536), ('pair_port', '80'),
               ('connect_port', 0), ('code', '12345'), ('code', '12345a'), ('code', 123456)]

    def test_reconnect_steps_order(self):
        d = add_device(host='192.168.1.20', port=37011, mdns_name='adb-ABC123-x1y2')
        self.browse.return_value = {'adb-ABC123-x1y2': {'service': 'connect', 'host': '192.168.1.21', 'port': 40123}}
        self.fa.rule('devices', out='List of devices attached\n', times=2)
        self.fa.rule('connect', '192.168.1.20:37011', out='failed to connect to 192.168.1.20:37011\n')
        self.fa.rule('connect', '192.168.1.21:40123', out='connected to 192.168.1.21:40123\n')

        self.assertIsNotNone(row['last_seen_at'])

    def test_unauthorized_state(self):

        self.assertEqual(devices.reconnect(devices.get(d)), 'unauthorized')
        with self.assertRaises(HttpError) as cm:
            devices.connect(req(params={'id': d}))
        self.assertEqual((cm.exception.status, cm.exception.message), (409, 'UNAUTHORIZED'))

    def test_connect_offline_409(self):
        d = add_device()
        with self.assertRaises(HttpError) as cm:
            devices.connect(req(params={'id': d}, body={'connect_port': 40000}))
        self.assertEqual((cm.exception.status, cm.exception.message), (409, 'DEVICE_OFFLINE'))
        self.assertEqual(devices.get(d)['connect_port'], 40000)

    def test_patch_rejects_unknown_key(self):
        d = add_device()
        for body in ({'host': '1.2.3.4'}, {'name': ''}, {'ota_after_build': 1}, {}):
            with self.assertRaises(HttpError):
                devices.patch_device(req(params={'id': d}, body=body))
        dev = devices.patch_device(req(params={'id': d}, body={'name': ' Phone ', 'ota_after_build': True}))[1]['device']
        self.assertEqual((dev['name'], dev['ota_after_build']), ('Phone', True))

    def test_delete_disconnects_keeps_installs(self):
        add_catalog_item(1, 'com.google.android.youtube')
        d = add_device()
        assign(d, 1)
        jid = add_job(target={'device_id': d})
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute("INSERT INTO ota_installs(job_id,device_id,kind,ref_id,package,result) "
                      "VALUES (?,?,'item',1,'com.google.android.youtube','installed')", (jid, d))
        self.assertEqual(devices.delete_device(req(params={'id': d})), (200, {'deleted': d}))
        self.assertEqual(self.fa.calls(), [['disconnect', '192.168.1.20:37011']])
        self.assertIsNone(devices.get(d))
        self.assertEqual(db.conn().execute('SELECT COUNT(*) FROM ota_installs').fetchone()[0], 1)
        with self.assertRaises(HttpError) as cm:
            devices.delete_device(req(params={'id': d}))
        self.assertEqual(cm.exception.status, 404)

    def test_assignments_same_package_400(self):
        add_catalog_item(1, 'com.google.android.youtube', source_id='morphe')
        add_catalog_item(2, 'com.google.android.youtube', source_id='morphe-beta')
        add_catalog_item(3, 'com.reddit.frontpage', source_id='morphe')
        d = add_device()
        with self.assertRaises(HttpError) as cm:
            devices.put_assignments(req(params={'id': d}, body={'item_ids': [1, 2]}))
        self.assertEqual(cm.exception.message, 'two items with package com.google.android.youtube')
        with self.assertRaises(HttpError):
            devices.put_assignments(req(params={'id': d}, body={'item_ids': [99]}))
        st, body = devices.put_assignments(req(params={'id': d}, body={'item_ids': [1, 3]}))
        self.assertEqual((st, body['device']['id']), (200, d))
        self.assertEqual([r[0] for r in db.conn().execute('SELECT item_id FROM device_assignments ORDER BY item_id')], [1, 3])

    def test_resolve_devices(self):
        a, b = add_device(serial='A'), add_device(serial='B')
        self.assertEqual(devices.resolve_devices({'all': True}), [a, b])
        self.assertEqual(devices.resolve_devices({'device_ids': [b, 999]}), [b])
        self.assertEqual(devices.resolve_devices({'item_ids': [1]}), [])

    def test_discovered_route(self):
        add_device(mdns_name='adb-ABC123-x1y2')
        found = dict(DISC, **{'adb-ABC123-p': {'service': 'pairing', 'host': '192.168.1.20', 'port': 41000}})
        self.browse.side_effect = lambda: setattr(devices, 'DISCOVERED', {k: dict(v, last_seen=1.0) for k, v in found.items()})
        st, body = devices.discovered(req(method='GET'))
        self.assertEqual(body['pairing'], [{'name': 'adb-ABC123-p', 'host': '192.168.1.20', 'port': 41000, 'last_seen': 1.0}])
        self.assertEqual(body['connect'][0]['device_id'], 1)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — append to `local/webui/devices.py`; add `from . import db` and `from .server import HttpError, validate_host, validate_port` to the imports

```python
_CODE = re.compile(r'^[0-9]{6}$')


def validate_code(v):
    if not isinstance(v, str) or not _CODE.match(v):
        raise HttpError(400, 'code must be 6 digits')
    return v


def address(dev):
    return f"{dev['host']}:{dev['connect_port']}"


def get(device_id):
    r = db.conn().execute('SELECT * FROM devices WHERE id=?', (device_id,)).fetchone()
    return dict(r) if r else None


def _dev_or_404(device_id):
    dev = get(device_id)
    if dev is None:
        raise HttpError(404, 'device not found')
    return dev


def device_json(dev, states):
    return dict(dev, ota_after_build=bool(dev['ota_after_build']), online=states.get(address(dev)) == 'device')


def _last(text):
    lines = [x for x in text.splitlines() if x.strip()]
    return lines[-1].strip() if lines else 'no output'


def online_states():
    try:
        rc, out = adb(['devices'], timeout=10)
    except AdbTimeout:
        return {}
    return parse_devices(out) if rc == 0 else {}


def _state(addr):
    return online_states().get(addr)


def _connect(addr, log=None):
    try:
        return adb(['connect', addr], timeout=20, log=log)[1]
    except AdbTimeout:
        return 'timeout'


def _save_addr(dev, host, port):
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('UPDATE devices SET host=?, connect_port=? WHERE id=?', (host, port, dev['id']))
    dev.update(host=host, connect_port=port)


def reconnect(dev, *, cancel_event=None, log=None):
    """Spec device 4.2 steps 1-3; returns 'online' | 'offline' | 'unauthorized'."""
    st = _state(address(dev))
    if st not in ('device', 'unauthorized'):
        _connect(address(dev), log)
        st = _state(address(dev))
    if st not in ('device', 'unauthorized') and dev.get('mdns_name') and not (cancel_event and cancel_event.is_set()):
        e = browse().get(dev['mdns_name'])
        if e and e['service'] == 'connect' and (e['host'], e['port']) != (dev['host'], dev['connect_port']):
            new = f"{e['host']}:{e['port']}"
            _connect(new, log)
            st = _state(new)
            if st == 'device':
                _save_addr(dev, e['host'], e['port'])
    if st == 'device':
        with db.tx() as c:
            c.execute('UPDATE devices SET last_seen_at=? WHERE id=?', (db.utcnow(), dev['id']))
        return 'online'
    return 'unauthorized' if st == 'unauthorized' else 'offline'


def _discovered_connect(host):
    for name, e in sorted(browse().items()):
        if e['service'] == 'connect' and e['host'] == host:
            return name, e['port']
    return None, None


def pair(req):
    b = req.json or {}
    host = validate_host(b.get('host'))
    pair_port = validate_port(b.get('pair_port'), 'pair_port')
    code = validate_code(b.get('code'))
    connect_port = None if b.get('connect_port') is None else validate_port(b['connect_port'], 'connect_port')
    mdns_name, found_port = _discovered_connect(host)
    if connect_port is None:
        if found_port is None:
            raise HttpError(400, 'connect_port required: not discovered')
        connect_port = found_port
    out = adb(['pair', f'{host}:{pair_port}', code], timeout=30)[1]
    if 'Successfully paired' not in out:
        raise HttpError(400, 'PAIR_FAILED: ' + _last(out))
    addr = f'{host}:{connect_port}'
    out = _connect(addr)
    if 'unauthorized' in out or 'failed to authenticate' in out:
        raise HttpError(409, 'UNAUTHORIZED')
    if 'connected to' not in out:
        raise HttpError(409, 'DEVICE_OFFLINE')
    props = parse_getprop(adb(['shell', 'getprop'], serial=addr, timeout=20)[1])
    serial = props.get('ro.serialno') or props.get('ro.boot.serialno')
    if not serial:
        raise HttpError(409, 'DEVICE_OFFLINE')
    sdk = props.get('ro.build.version.sdk', '')
    facts = (props.get('ro.product.model'), props.get('ro.product.cpu.abilist'),
             int(sdk) if sdk.isdigit() else None, props.get('ro.build.version.release'))
    now = db.utcnow()
    with db.tx() as c:
        row = c.execute('SELECT id FROM devices WHERE serial=?', (serial,)).fetchone()
        if row:
            did = row['id']
            c.execute('UPDATE devices SET host=?, connect_port=?, mdns_name=COALESCE(?, mdns_name), model=?, '
                      'abi_list=?, sdk=?, android_release=?, last_seen_at=? WHERE id=?',
                      (host, connect_port, mdns_name, *facts, now, did))
        else:
            did = c.execute('INSERT INTO devices(name,serial,mdns_name,host,connect_port,model,abi_list,sdk,'
                            'android_release,ota_after_build,last_seen_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,0,?)',
                            (facts[0] or serial, serial, mdns_name, host, connect_port, *facts, now)).lastrowid
    return 200, {'device': device_json(get(did), {addr: 'device'})}


def connect(req):
    dev = _dev_or_404(req.params['id'])
    b = req.json or {}
    host = dev['host'] if b.get('host') is None else validate_host(b['host'])
    port = dev['connect_port'] if b.get('connect_port') is None else validate_port(b['connect_port'], 'connect_port')
    if (host, port) != (dev['host'], dev['connect_port']):
        _save_addr(dev, host, port)
    st = reconnect(dev)
    if st != 'online':
        raise HttpError(409, 'UNAUTHORIZED' if st == 'unauthorized' else 'DEVICE_OFFLINE')
    return 200, {'device': device_json(get(dev['id']), {address(dev): 'device'})}


def patch_device(req):
    dev = _dev_or_404(req.params['id'])
    b = req.json
    if not isinstance(b, dict) or not b or set(b) - {'name', 'ota_after_build'}:
        raise HttpError(400, 'only name and ota_after_build can change')
    sets = {}
    if 'name' in b:
        name = b['name'].strip() if isinstance(b['name'], str) else ''
        if not 1 <= len(name) <= 64:
            raise HttpError(400, 'name must be 1-64 characters')
        sets['name'] = name
    if 'ota_after_build' in b:
        if not isinstance(b['ota_after_build'], bool):
            raise HttpError(400, 'ota_after_build must be true or false')
        sets['ota_after_build'] = int(b['ota_after_build'])
    with db.tx() as c:  # keys come from the fixed whitelist above
        c.execute(f"UPDATE devices SET {', '.join(k + '=?' for k in sets)} WHERE id=?", (*sets.values(), dev['id']))
    return 200, {'device': device_json(get(dev['id']), online_states())}


def delete_device(req):
    dev = _dev_or_404(req.params['id'])
    try:
        adb(['disconnect', address(dev)], timeout=10)
    except AdbTimeout:
        pass
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('DELETE FROM devices WHERE id=?', (dev['id'],))
    return 200, {'deleted': dev['id']}


def put_assignments(req):
    dev = _dev_or_404(req.params['id'])
    ids = (req.json or {}).get('item_ids')
    if not isinstance(ids, list) or not all(isinstance(i, int) and not isinstance(i, bool) for i in ids):
        raise HttpError(400, 'item_ids must be a list of integers')
    ids = list(dict.fromkeys(ids))
    seen = {}
    for i in ids:
        r = db.conn().execute('SELECT package FROM item WHERE id=?', (i,)).fetchone()
        if r is None:
            raise HttpError(400, f'unknown item {i}')
        if r['package'] in seen:
            raise HttpError(400, f"two items with package {r['package']}")
        seen[r['package']] = i
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('DELETE FROM device_assignments WHERE device_id=?', (dev['id'],))
        c.executemany('INSERT INTO device_assignments(device_id,item_id) VALUES (?,?)', [(dev['id'], i) for i in ids])
    return 200, {'device': device_json(get(dev['id']), online_states())}


def resolve_devices(target):
    c = db.conn()
    if target.get('all') is True:
        return [r['id'] for r in c.execute('SELECT id FROM devices ORDER BY id')]
    ids = target.get('device_ids')
    if isinstance(ids, list):
        have = {r['id'] for r in c.execute('SELECT id FROM devices')}
        return [i for i in ids if i in have]
    return []


def discovered(req):
    browse()
    found = DISCOVERED
    known = {r['mdns_name']: r['id'] for r in
             db.conn().execute('SELECT id, mdns_name FROM devices WHERE mdns_name IS NOT NULL')}
    pairing = [{'name': n, 'host': e['host'], 'port': e['port'], 'last_seen': e['last_seen']}
               for n, e in sorted(found.items()) if e['service'] == 'pairing']
    connect_ = [{'name': n, 'host': e['host'], 'port': e['port'], 'device_id': known.get(n), 'last_seen': e['last_seen']}
                for n, e in sorted(found.items()) if e['service'] == 'connect']
    return 200, {'pairing': pairing, 'connect': connect_}


ROUTES = [
    ('GET', '/api/devices/discovered', discovered),
    ('POST', '/api/devices/pair', pair),
    ('POST', '/api/devices/{id}/connect', connect),
    ('PATCH', '/api/devices/{id}', patch_device),
    ('DELETE', '/api/devices/{id}', delete_device),
    ('PUT', '/api/devices/{id}/assignments', put_assignments),
]
```

#### Task 5.5: OTA planner and device read model

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/ota.py` (planner + read model + `GET /api/devices`, `GET /api/devices/{id}/plan`)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_device_ota.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P4 `build.latest_artifact(item_id, abi) -> dict | None` (X5); P2 `catalog.item_dependencies(item_id)` (plan-fixed); `devices.get`, `devices.online_states`, `devices.address`, `devices.adb`, `devices.parse_packages`.
- Produces:
  - `ota.assigned_items(device_id: int, item_ids: list[int] | None = None) -> list[dict]` — `[{item_id, package}]` in catalog order (X6), restricted to `item_ids` when given; disabled items included.
  - `ota.last_installs(device_id: int) -> tuple[dict[int, dict], dict[int, dict]]` — `(items: item_id -> {build_job_id, version_name, at}, deps: upstream_id -> {upstream_tag, at})` from the newest `ota_installs` row with result `installed`/`up_to_date`; `at` = `jobs.finished_at` of that row's job (null while running).
  - `ota.first_abi(dev: dict) -> str` (first of `abi_list` split on `,`; empty → `universal`).
  - `ota.plan(items, artifacts, deps_by_item, item_installs, dep_installs, present) -> list[dict]` — pure; see Behavior.
  - `ota._latest_artifact(item_id, abi)`, `ota._item_dependencies(item_id)` (call-time imports of `build`/`catalog`; tests patch these two).
  - routes `GET /api/devices` → `200 {"devices": [device object + "assignments" + "dependencies" (spec L926 shape)]}` (X18), `GET /api/devices/{id}/plan` → `{"online": bool, "steps": [...]}`.

**Behavior:**
- `plan` inputs: `items` = `assigned_items(...)`; `artifacts` = `{item_id: artifact dict | None}`; `deps_by_item` = `{item_id: item_dependencies(item_id)}`; `item_installs`/`dep_installs` from `last_installs`; `present` = set of packages on the phone or `None` (unknown → every package counts as present).
- Output steps in order; each item step `{"kind":"item","ref_id":item_id,"package","action","artifact","reason","needs":[upstream_id of every dependency of the item]}`; `action` ∈ `install` (reason `new build` or `missing on phone`), `up_to_date`, `error` (reason `NO_MATCHING_ARTIFACT`). D7: install when `artifact.job_id != item_installs[item].build_job_id` or package not in `present`.
- Before the first item step with action `install`, each of its dependencies that D8 says needs install is inserted once per plan: `{"kind":"dependency","ref_id":upstream_id,"dependency_app_id","package","path","tag","action":"install","reason"}`; D8: package not present or `tag != dep_installs[upstream_id].upstream_tag`; `path` may be null (not downloaded; the executor pulls, Task 5.6).
- `GET /api/devices` calls `online_states()` once (failure → every device offline) and no other adb command; `pending` = plan with `present=None` has an install step for the item.

**Constraints:**
- `plan` does no IO. The read model never calls adb except the one `adb devices`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_device_ota.py'` exit 0 with `test_plan_abi_choice_via_latest_artifact` (fake `latest_artifact` called with the phone's first ABI), `test_plan_d7_build_job` (same `build_job_id` → `up_to_date`; different → install), `test_plan_missing_on_phone`, `test_plan_d8_tag_and_dedup` (two items share MicroG → one dependency step, before the first item), `test_plan_no_artifact_error`, `test_assigned_items_catalog_order_incl_disabled` (group positions 1,0 + Ungrouped, one disabled item → order group0 items, group1 items, ungrouped; disabled present), `test_get_devices_no_adb` (fake adb rule rc 1 for every call → 200, `pending` true for a new build, `online` false), `test_plan_route`.

**Steps:**

```python
from webui import db, ota

MICROG = {'dependency_app_id': 1, 'upstream_id': 7, 'package': GMS, 'display_name': 'MicroG RE',
          'tag': 'v7.2.1', 'path': '/data/upstream/7/v7.2.1/microg-7.2.1.apk', 'signer_sha256': None}


def art(item_id, job_id, abi='arm64-v8a', path='/x.apk'):

            'version_name': '19.47.53', 'version_code': 1, 'patches_version': 'v1', 'signer_sha256': 'aa',
            'path': path, 'size': 1, 'sha256': 'bb', 'created_at': '2026-10-19T04:00:00Z'}


class PlanTest(helpers.DataTestCase):

    def test_plan_d7_build_job(self):
        steps = ota.plan(self.items()[:1], {1: art(1, 5)}, {1: []}, {1: {'build_job_id': 5}}, {}, {YT})
        self.assertEqual([s['action'] for s in steps], ['up_to_date'])
        steps = ota.plan(self.items()[:1], {1: art(1, 6)}, {1: []}, {1: {'build_job_id': 5}}, {}, {YT})
        self.assertEqual((steps[0]['action'], steps[0]['reason']), ('install', 'new build'))

    def test_plan_missing_on_phone(self):

        self.assertEqual((steps[0]['action'], steps[0]['reason']), ('install', 'missing on phone'))

    def test_plan_d8_tag_and_dedup(self):
        steps = ota.plan(self.items(), {1: art(1, 5), 2: art(2, 6)}, {1: [MICROG], 2: [MICROG]}, {}, {}, {YT})
        self.assertEqual([(s['kind'], s['ref_id']) for s in steps], [('dependency', 7), ('item', 1), ('item', 2)])
        self.assertEqual(steps[1]['needs'], [7])
        same = ota.plan(self.items(), {1: art(1, 5), 2: art(2, 6)}, {1: [MICROG], 2: [MICROG]}, {},
                        {7: {'upstream_tag': 'v7.2.1'}}, {YT, GMS})
        self.assertEqual([s['kind'] for s in same], ['item', 'item'])

    def test_plan_no_artifact_error(self):

        self.assertEqual([(s['kind'], s['action'], s['reason']) for s in steps],

    def test_assigned_items_catalog_order_incl_disabled(self):
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/ota.py`

```python
"""OTA part: planner, read model, OTA job handler, recovery routes, after-build OTA (spec device 3-4)."""
import importlib

from . import db, devices
from .server import HttpError


def _item_dependencies(item_id):
    return importlib.import_module('webui.catalog').item_dependencies(item_id)


def _latest_artifact(item_id, abi):
    return importlib.import_module('webui.build').latest_artifact(item_id, abi)


def first_abi(dev):
    abis = [a.strip() for a in (dev.get('abi_list') or '').split(',') if a.strip()]
    return abis[0] if abis else 'universal'


def assigned_items(device_id, item_ids=None):
    """Assigned items in catalog order (groups by position, Ungrouped last); disabled items included."""
    rows = db.conn().execute(
        'SELECT item.id AS item_id, item.package FROM device_assignments da JOIN item ON item.id = da.item_id '
        'LEFT JOIN grp ON grp.id = item.group_id WHERE da.device_id = ? '
        'ORDER BY grp.position IS NULL, grp.position, item.position, item.id', (device_id,)).fetchall()
    items = [dict(r) for r in rows]
    if item_ids is not None:
        items = [i for i in items if i['item_id'] in set(item_ids)]
    return items


def last_installs(device_id):
    items, deps = {}, {}
    rows = db.conn().execute(
        "SELECT oi.kind, oi.ref_id, oi.build_job_id, oi.upstream_tag, oi.version_name, j.finished_at "
        "FROM ota_installs oi JOIN jobs j ON j.id = oi.job_id "
        "WHERE oi.device_id = ? AND oi.result IN ('installed','up_to_date') ORDER BY oi.id", (device_id,))
    for r in rows:  # oldest first: newer rows overwrite
        if r['kind'] == 'item':
            items[r['ref_id']] = {'build_job_id': r['build_job_id'], 'version_name': r['version_name'],
                                  'at': r['finished_at']}
        else:
            deps[r['ref_id']] = {'upstream_tag': r['upstream_tag'], 'at': r['finished_at']}
    return items, deps


def plan(items, artifacts, deps_by_item, item_installs, dep_installs, present):
    """Pure D7/D8 planner. present=None means 'unknown': every package counts as present."""
    def has(pkg):
        return present is None or pkg in present
    steps, planned = [], set()
    for it in items:
        iid, pkg, a = it['item_id'], it['package'], artifacts.get(it['item_id'])
        deps = deps_by_item.get(iid, [])
        step = {'kind': 'item', 'ref_id': iid, 'package': pkg, 'artifact': a, 'needs': [d['upstream_id'] for d in deps]}
        if a is None:
            steps.append(dict(step, action='error', reason='NO_MATCHING_ARTIFACT'))
            continue
        last = item_installs.get(iid)
        if not has(pkg):
            action, reason = 'install', 'missing on phone'
        elif last is None or last['build_job_id'] != a['job_id']:
            action, reason = 'install', 'new build'
        else:
            action, reason = 'up_to_date', 'same build installed'
        if action == 'install':
            for d in deps:
                if d['upstream_id'] in planned:
                    continue
                prev = dep_installs.get(d['upstream_id'])
                if not has(d['package']):
                    why = 'missing on phone'
                elif prev is None or prev['upstream_tag'] != d['tag']:
                    why = 'new release'
                else:
                    continue
                planned.add(d['upstream_id'])
                steps.append({'kind': 'dependency', 'ref_id': d['upstream_id'], 'dependency_app_id': d['dependency_app_id'],
                              'package': d['package'], 'path': d['path'], 'tag': d['tag'], 'action': 'install',
                              'reason': why})
        steps.append(dict(step, action=action, reason=reason))
    return steps


def _plan_for(dev, item_ids, present):
    abi = first_abi(dev)
    items = assigned_items(dev['id'], item_ids)
    item_inst, dep_inst = last_installs(dev['id'])
    arts = {i['item_id']: _latest_artifact(i['item_id'], abi) for i in items}
    deps = {i['item_id']: _item_dependencies(i['item_id']) for i in items}
    return items, arts, deps, item_inst, dep_inst, plan(items, arts, deps, item_inst, dep_inst, present)


def _device_view(dev, states):
    items, arts, deps, item_inst, dep_inst, steps = _plan_for(dev, None, None)
    pending = {s['ref_id'] for s in steps if s['kind'] == 'item' and s['action'] == 'install'}
    assignments = []
    for i in items:
        a, inst = arts[i['item_id']], item_inst.get(i['item_id'])
        assignments.append({
            'item_id': i['item_id'],
            'installed': inst and {'version_name': inst['version_name'], 'build_job_id': inst['build_job_id'],
                                   'at': inst['at']},
            'available': a and {'version_name': a['version_name'], 'build_job_id': a['job_id']},
            'pending': i['item_id'] in pending})
    dmap = {}
    for iid, ds in deps.items():
        for d in ds:
            e = dmap.setdefault(d['upstream_id'], {
                'dependency_app_id': d['dependency_app_id'], 'display_name': d['display_name'], 'package': d['package'],
                'installed_tag': (dep_inst.get(d['upstream_id']) or {}).get('upstream_tag'),
                'available_tag': d['tag'], 'required_by': []})
            e['required_by'].append(iid)
    return dict(devices.device_json(dev, states), assignments=assignments, dependencies=list(dmap.values()))


def list_devices(req):
    states = devices.online_states()
    rows = db.conn().execute('SELECT * FROM devices ORDER BY id').fetchall()
    return 200, {'devices': [_device_view(dict(r), states) for r in rows]}


def _present(dev, cancel_event=None, log=None):
    try:
        rc, out = devices.adb(['shell', 'pm', 'list', 'packages'], serial=devices.address(dev), timeout=60,
                              cancel_event=cancel_event, log=None)
    except devices.AdbTimeout:
        return None
    return devices.parse_packages(out) if rc == 0 else None


def plan_route(req):
    dev = devices.get(req.params['id'])
    if dev is None:
        raise HttpError(404, 'device not found')
    online = devices.online_states().get(devices.address(dev)) == 'device'
    steps = _plan_for(dev, None, _present(dev) if online else None)[-1]
    return 200, {'online': online, 'steps': steps}


ROUTES = [
    ('GET', '/api/devices', list_devices),
    ('GET', '/api/devices/{id}/plan', plan_route),
]
```

```bash
git add local/webui/ota.py local/webui/tests/test_device_ota.py && git commit -m "feat(webui): OTA planner (D7/D8), catalog install order and device read model" -- local/webui/ota.py local/webui/tests/test_device_ota.py
```

#### Task 5.6: OTA job handler, recovery routes, after-build

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/ota.py` (handler, `detail`, `register`, routes `POST /api/devices/{id}/ota`, `POST /api/ota/{job_id}/force-reinstall`, `POST /api/ota/{job_id}/downgrade`)
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_device_otajob.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P1 `jobs.register`, `jobs.enqueue`, `jobs.get` (X2), `jobs.JobFailed`, `events.emit`, `events.subscribe`; Task 5.5 functions; `devices.reconnect`, `devices.adb`, `devices.install_error`, `devices.AdbTimeout`.
- Produces:
  - `ota.RETRY_WAIT = 60`, `ota.INSTALL_TIMEOUT = 900` (X14).
  - `ota.handle(job, log) -> None` (registered `jobs.register('ota', ota.handle, ota.detail)`).
  - `ota.detail(job_id: int) -> dict` = `{"device_id", "item_ids", "mode", "outcome", "installs": [ota_installs rows]}` or `{}`.
  - `ota.on_build_succeeded(payload: dict) -> None` (subscriber of `build.succeeded`).
  - `ota.register()`; routes as listed; `POST /api/devices/{id}/ota {item_ids?}` → `200 {"job_id": N}`; recovery routes → `200 {"job_id": N}`.
  - events `ota.done {job_id, device_id, outcome}`, `ota.failed {job_id, device_id, reason}`, `device.offline {job_id, device_id}`.

**Behavior:**
- Order in `handle`: (1) `mode != "normal"` and `trigger != "manual"` → `JobFailed("recovery requires manual trigger")` (defence for X1). (2) device row missing → `JobFailed("device removed")`. (3) insert `ota_jobs` row. (4) reconnect: attempts = 1 + `options.retries` for triggers `schedule`/`after_build`, 1 for `manual`; between attempts `job.cancel_event.wait(RETRY_WAIT)`; cancel set → return (core marks `cancelled`). `unauthorized` → outcome `failed`, reason `failed: UNAUTHORIZED`, `ota.failed`. Offline after attempts: manual → outcome `failed`, reason `failed: DEVICE_OFFLINE`, `ota.failed`; scheduled/after-build → outcome `skipped_offline`, reason `skipped_offline`, `device.offline`; both raise `JobFailed(reason)` after the event. (5) present = `parse_packages(adb shell pm list packages)`. (6) mode `normal`: plan over `assigned_items(device, target.item_ids)`; mode `force_reinstall`/`downgrade`: the one assigned item with `options.package`, else the dependency (of an assigned item) with that package, as a single `install` step; neither → `JobFailed("package not assigned")`. (7) execute steps in order (below). (8) outcome: rows ok = `installed`/`up_to_date`; all ok (or no rows) → `done`, some → `partial`, none → `failed`; write `ota_jobs.outcome`, `jobs.reason` = outcome; `done`/`partial` → `ota.done`, return; `failed` → `ota.failed` with reason `failed`, raise `JobFailed("failed")`.
- Dependency step: `path` null → `jobs.enqueue('upstream', {"upstream_id": ref_id}, job.trigger, options={"rebuild": False, "notify": job.options.get("notify", True)})`, wait: loop `row = jobs.get(id)` until `status` ∈ success/failed/cancelled, with `job.cancel_event.wait(1)` between reads (cancel set → stop waiting and return from the handler), re-read `catalog.item_dependencies` for that dependency; still null → row `failed`, `DEPENDENCY_FAILED`, text `dependency not downloaded` (X7). Install `adb -s <addr> install -r <path>`; row kind `dependency`, `upstream_tag` = tag.
- Item step: any dependency of the item failed in this job → row `failed` `DEPENDENCY_FAILED`, no adb call. `error` → row `failed` `NO_MATCHING_ABI`, text `NO_MATCHING_ARTIFACT`. `up_to_date` → row `up_to_date`. `install` → re-resolve `latest_artifact(item, abi)`; `None` or path missing → resolve once more; still missing → row `failed` `NO_MATCHING_ABI`, text `artifact file missing`. Mode `force_reinstall` → `adb -s <addr> uninstall <pkg>` first (result logged, not checked); install argv `install -r` plus `-d` for `downgrade`, timeout `INSTALL_TIMEOUT`, `AdbTimeout` → `TIMEOUT`; result via `install_error`; row with `build_job_id`, `version_name`, `version_code` from the artifact; `error_text` = last 2048 chars of output on failure.
- Recovery routes: `ota_installs` row by `install_id` whose `job_id` equals the route `job_id`, else `404 "install not found"`; enqueue `ota` target `{"device_id": row.device_id}` options `{"mode": <mode>, "package": row.package}` trigger `manual`.
- `POST /api/devices/{id}/ota`: unknown device → 404; `item_ids` present → list of int, each assigned to the device, else 400; target `{"device_id":id}` or with `item_ids`; trigger `manual`, options `{}`.
- `on_build_succeeded`: for every device with `ota_after_build = 1` and `payload.item_id` assigned → `jobs.enqueue('ota', {"device_id": d, "item_ids": [item_id]}, 'after_build')`.

**Constraints:**
- The handler is the only writer of `ota_jobs`/`ota_installs`. Subscriber does no IO besides the DB read and `jobs.enqueue`.
- `ponytail:` comment on the re-resolve: no file-descriptor pinning; an install whose file is retention-deleted between re-resolve and adb open fails `ADB_ERROR` and the next OTA retries.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_device_otajob.py'` exit 0 with `test_ota_installs_in_order_with_dependency`, `test_dependency_failure_marks_items` (`DEPENDENCY_FAILED`, item not attempted, other item installed → outcome `partial`, `ota.done`), `test_all_fail_outcome_failed`, `test_manual_offline_fails_device_offline`, `test_scheduled_offline_retries_then_skipped` (`retries=1`, `RETRY_WAIT=0` → two reconnect rounds, outcome `skipped_offline`, status-raise `JobFailed`, event `device.offline`, no `ota.failed`), `test_unauthorized`, `test_device_removed`, `test_recovery_needs_manual_trigger`, `test_force_reinstall_argv` (argv log: `uninstall <pkg>` then `install -r <path>`, no other install), `test_downgrade_argv` (`install -r -d`), `test_package_not_assigned`, `test_recovery_route_wrong_job_404`, `test_after_build_enqueues_only_opted_devices` (2 devices, one with `ota_after_build`, both assigned → one job target `{"device_id":1,"item_ids":[X]}` trigger `after_build`), `test_artifact_reresolved_once` (first fake result has a missing path, second exists → one install), `test_missing_dependency_pulled` (dependency path null → `jobs.enqueue('upstream', …)` called with `{"rebuild": False, …}`, the fake `jobs.enqueue` marks the pulled job `success` in the `jobs` table, second `item_dependencies` returns a path → dependency installed), `test_missing_dependency_still_missing` (→ `DEPENDENCY_FAILED`), `test_install_signature_mismatch_code`, `test_install_timeout_code`, `test_ota_now_route`.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `local/webui/tests/test_device_otajob.py`

```python
from unittest import mock

from webui import db, events, jobs, ota
from webui.server import HttpError
from webui.tests import helpers
from webui.tests.device_helpers import (FakeAdb, FakeLog, add_catalog_item, add_device, add_job, assign, job, req)

YT, YTM, GMS = 'com.google.android.youtube', 'com.google.android.apps.youtube.music', 'app.revanced.android.gms'
ADDR = '192.168.1.20:37011'
ONLINE = f'List of devices attached\n{ADDR}\tdevice\n'


def art(item_id, job_id, path):
    return {'id': job_id * 10, 'job_id': job_id, 'item_id': item_id, 'variant': 'arm64-v8a', 'abi': 'arm64-v8a',
            'package': YT, 'version_name': '19.47.53', 'version_code': 1, 'patches_version': 'v1',
            'signer_sha256': 'aa', 'path': path, 'size': 3, 'sha256': 'bb', 'created_at': '2026-10-19T04:00:00Z'}


class OtaJobTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.fa = FakeAdb(self)
        self.ev = []
        for name in ('ota.done', 'ota.failed', 'device.offline'):
            events.subscribe(name, lambda p, n=name: self.ev.append((n, p)))
        self.yt, self.ytm, self.gms = (db.DATA / 'yt.apk', db.DATA / 'ytm.apk', db.DATA / 'microg.apk')
        for f in (self.yt, self.ytm, self.gms):
            f.write_bytes(b'apk')
        self.microg = {'dependency_app_id': 1, 'upstream_id': 7, 'package': GMS, 'display_name': 'MicroG RE',
                       'tag': 'v7.2.1', 'path': str(self.gms), 'signer_sha256': None}
        add_catalog_item(1, YT)
        add_catalog_item(2, YTM)
        self.d = add_device()
        assign(self.d, 1, 2)
        self.arts = {1: art(1, 6, str(self.yt)), 2: art(2, 7, str(self.ytm))}
        self.deps = {1: [self.microg]}
        for name, fn in (('_latest_artifact', lambda i, abi: self.arts.get(i)),
                         ('_item_dependencies', lambda i: self.deps.get(i, []))):
            p = mock.patch.object(ota, name, side_effect=fn)
            p.start()
            self.addCleanup(p.stop)
        for name, value in (('RETRY_WAIT', 0), ('INSTALL_TIMEOUT', 900)):
            self.addCleanup(setattr, ota, name, getattr(ota, name))
            setattr(ota, name, value)

    def online(self, pm='package:com.android.settings\n'):
        self.fa.rule('devices', out=ONLINE)
        self.fa.rule('pm', out=pm)

    def run_job(self, target=None, options=None, trigger='manual'):
        target = target or {'device_id': self.d}
        jid = add_job(target=target, options=options, trigger=trigger)
        try:
            ota.handle(job(jid, target, options, trigger), FakeLog())
            return jid, None
        except jobs.JobFailed as e:
            return jid, e.reason

    def installs(self, jid):
        return [(r['kind'], r['package'], r['result'], r['error_code']) for r in
                db.conn().execute('SELECT * FROM ota_installs WHERE job_id=? ORDER BY id', (jid,))]

    def adb_cmds(self, verb):
        return [c[2:] for c in self.fa.calls() if len(c) > 2 and c[2] == verb]

    def outcome(self, jid):
        r = db.conn().execute('SELECT o.outcome, j.reason FROM ota_jobs o JOIN jobs j ON j.id=o.job_id WHERE o.job_id=?',
                              (jid,)).fetchone()
        return r['outcome'], r['reason']

    def test_ota_installs_in_order_with_dependency(self):
        self.online()

        jid, err = self.run_job()

                                                    ['install', '-r', str(self.ytm)]])

        self.assertEqual(self.outcome(jid), ('done', 'done'))
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — append to `local/webui/ota.py`; change the import lines at the top to `import importlib`, `import json`, `import os` and `from . import db, devices, events, jobs`

```python
RETRY_WAIT = 60
INSTALL_TIMEOUT = 900
_DONE = ('success', 'failed', 'cancelled')


def _finish(job, device_id, outcome, reason, event, extra):
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('UPDATE ota_jobs SET outcome=? WHERE job_id=?', (outcome, job.id))
        c.execute('UPDATE jobs SET reason=? WHERE id=?', (reason, job.id))
    events.emit(event, dict(extra, job_id=job.id, device_id=device_id))


def _fail(job, device_id, reason, event='ota.failed', extra=None, outcome='failed'):
    _finish(job, device_id, outcome, reason, event, {'reason': reason} if extra is None else extra)
    raise jobs.JobFailed(reason)


def _record(job, device_id, step, result, error_code=None, error_text=None, a=None):
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('INSERT INTO ota_installs(job_id,device_id,kind,ref_id,build_job_id,upstream_tag,package,version_name,'
                  'version_code,result,error_code,error_text) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                  (job.id, device_id, step['kind'], step['ref_id'], a and a['job_id'],
                   step.get('tag') if step['kind'] == 'dependency' else None, step['package'],
                   a and a['version_name'], a and a['version_code'], result, error_code,
                   error_text[-2048:] if error_text else None))
    return result in ('installed', 'up_to_date')


def _install(job, dev, path, package, log):
    """-> (error_code or None, output). Mode force_reinstall uninstalls first (D6, manual only)."""
    serial, mode = devices.address(dev), job.options.get('mode', 'normal')
    try:
        if mode == 'force_reinstall':
            devices.adb(['uninstall', package], serial=serial, timeout=120, cancel_event=job.cancel_event, log=log)
        argv = ['install', '-r'] + (['-d'] if mode == 'downgrade' else []) + [path]
        rc, out = devices.adb(argv, serial=serial, timeout=INSTALL_TIMEOUT, cancel_event=job.cancel_event, log=log)
    except devices.AdbTimeout:
        return ('ADB_ERROR', 'cancelled') if job.cancel_event.is_set() else ('TIMEOUT', 'install timed out')
    code = devices.install_error(out)
    return (code or ('ADB_ERROR' if rc != 0 else None)), out


def _pull_dependency(job, dev, step, log):
    """User decision: a dependency that was never downloaded is pulled by its upstream job, then installed."""
    log.line(f"dependency {step['package']} not downloaded: pulling upstream {step['ref_id']}")
    jid = jobs.enqueue('upstream', {'upstream_id': step['ref_id']}, job.trigger,
                       options={'rebuild': False, 'notify': job.options.get('notify', True)})
    while True:
        row = jobs.get(jid)
        if row is None or row['status'] in _DONE:
            break
        if job.cancel_event.wait(1):
            return None
    for i in assigned_items(dev['id']):
        for d in _item_dependencies(i['item_id']):
            if d['upstream_id'] == step['ref_id'] and d['path']:
                return d
    return None


def _run_dependency(job, dev, step, log, failed):
    if not step['path']:
        d = _pull_dependency(job, dev, step, log)
        if d is None:
            failed.add(step['ref_id'])
            return _record(job, dev['id'], step, 'failed', 'DEPENDENCY_FAILED', 'dependency not downloaded')
        step = dict(step, path=d['path'], tag=d['tag'])
    code, out = _install(job, dev, step['path'], step['package'], log)
    if code:
        failed.add(step['ref_id'])
        return _record(job, dev['id'], step, 'failed', code, out)
    return _record(job, dev['id'], step, 'installed')


def _run_item(job, dev, step, abi, log, failed):
    if failed.intersection(step['needs']):
        return _record(job, dev['id'], step, 'failed', 'DEPENDENCY_FAILED', 'dependency install failed', step['artifact'])
    if step['action'] == 'error':
        return _record(job, dev['id'], step, 'failed', 'NO_MATCHING_ABI', 'NO_MATCHING_ARTIFACT')
    if step['action'] == 'up_to_date':
        return _record(job, dev['id'], step, 'up_to_date', a=step['artifact'])
    # ponytail: re-resolve right before install, once more if the file is gone; no fd pinning. A file deleted
    # by retention between this check and adb's open fails ADB_ERROR and the next OTA retries.
    a = None
    for _ in range(2):
        a = _latest_artifact(step['ref_id'], abi)
        if a and os.path.exists(a['path']):
            break
        a = None
    if a is None:
        return _record(job, dev['id'], step, 'failed', 'NO_MATCHING_ABI', 'artifact file missing')
    code, out = _install(job, dev, a['path'], step['package'], log)
    if code:
        return _record(job, dev['id'], step, 'failed', code, out, a)
    return _record(job, dev['id'], step, 'installed', a=a)


def _recovery_steps(dev, package, abi):
    items = assigned_items(dev['id'])
    for i in items:
        if i['package'] == package:
            a = _latest_artifact(i['item_id'], abi)
            return [{'kind': 'item', 'ref_id': i['item_id'], 'package': package, 'artifact': a, 'needs': [],
                     'action': 'install' if a else 'error', 'reason': 'recovery' if a else 'NO_MATCHING_ARTIFACT'}]
    for i in items:
        for d in _item_dependencies(i['item_id']):
            if d['package'] == package:
                return [{'kind': 'dependency', 'ref_id': d['upstream_id'], 'dependency_app_id': d['dependency_app_id'],
                         'package': package, 'path': d['path'], 'tag': d['tag'], 'action': 'install',
                         'reason': 'recovery'}]
    return None


def handle(job, log):
    mode = job.options.get('mode', 'normal')
    if mode != 'normal' and job.trigger != 'manual':
        raise jobs.JobFailed('recovery requires manual trigger')
    dev = devices.get(job.target.get('device_id'))
    if dev is None:
        raise jobs.JobFailed('device removed')
    item_ids = job.target.get('item_ids')
    with db.tx() as c:
        c.execute('INSERT OR REPLACE INTO ota_jobs(job_id,device_id,item_ids,mode,outcome) VALUES (?,?,?,?,NULL)',
                  (job.id, dev['id'], None if item_ids is None else json.dumps(item_ids), mode))
    abi = first_abi(dev)
    recovery = None
    if mode != 'normal':
        recovery = _recovery_steps(dev, job.options.get('package'), abi)
        if recovery is None:
            _fail(job, dev['id'], 'package not assigned')
    attempts = 1 + (int(job.options.get('retries') or 0) if job.trigger in ('schedule', 'after_build') else 0)
    for n in range(attempts):
        state = devices.reconnect(dev, cancel_event=job.cancel_event, log=log)
        if state != 'offline' or n == attempts - 1:
            break
        log.line(f'device offline, retry {n + 1} of {attempts - 1} in {RETRY_WAIT} s')
        if job.cancel_event.wait(RETRY_WAIT):
            return
    if job.cancel_event.is_set():
        return
    if state == 'unauthorized':
        _fail(job, dev['id'], 'failed: UNAUTHORIZED', extra={'reason': 'UNAUTHORIZED'})
    if state == 'offline':
        if job.trigger == 'manual':
            _fail(job, dev['id'], 'failed: DEVICE_OFFLINE', extra={'reason': 'DEVICE_OFFLINE'})
        _fail(job, dev['id'], 'skipped_offline', event='device.offline', extra={}, outcome='skipped_offline')
    steps = recovery or _plan_for(dev, item_ids, _present(dev, job.cancel_event))[-1]
    ok, failed = [], set()
    for step in steps:
        if job.cancel_event.is_set():
            return
        log.line(f"{step['kind']} {step['package']}: {step['action']} ({step['reason']})")
        if step['kind'] == 'dependency':
            ok.append(_run_dependency(job, dev, step, log, failed))
        else:
            ok.append(_run_item(job, dev, step, abi, log, failed))
    outcome = 'done' if all(ok) else 'partial' if any(ok) else 'failed'
    if outcome == 'failed':
        _fail(job, dev['id'], 'failed')
    _finish(job, dev['id'], outcome, outcome, 'ota.done', {'outcome': outcome})


def detail(job_id):
    c = db.conn()
    r = c.execute('SELECT * FROM ota_jobs WHERE job_id=?', (job_id,)).fetchone()
    if r is None:
        return {}
    return {'device_id': r['device_id'], 'item_ids': json.loads(r['item_ids']) if r['item_ids'] else None,
            'mode': r['mode'], 'outcome': r['outcome'],
            'installs': [dict(x) for x in c.execute('SELECT * FROM ota_installs WHERE job_id=? ORDER BY id', (job_id,))]}


def on_build_succeeded(payload):
    item_id = payload.get('item_id')
    rows = db.conn().execute('SELECT d.id FROM devices d JOIN device_assignments da ON da.device_id = d.id '
                             'WHERE d.ota_after_build = 1 AND da.item_id = ? ORDER BY d.id', (item_id,)).fetchall()
    for r in rows:
        jobs.enqueue('ota', {'device_id': r['id'], 'item_ids': [item_id]}, 'after_build')


def ota_now(req):
    dev = devices.get(req.params['id'])
    if dev is None:
        raise HttpError(404, 'device not found')
    ids = (req.json or {}).get('item_ids')
    target = {'device_id': dev['id']}
    if ids is not None:
        if not isinstance(ids, list) or not ids or not all(isinstance(i, int) and not isinstance(i, bool) for i in ids):
            raise HttpError(400, 'item_ids must be a non-empty list of integers')
        if not set(ids) <= {i['item_id'] for i in assigned_items(dev['id'])}:
            raise HttpError(400, 'item not assigned to this device')
        target['item_ids'] = ids
    return 200, {'job_id': jobs.enqueue('ota', target, 'manual')}


def _recovery(req, mode):
    install_id = (req.json or {}).get('install_id')
    if not isinstance(install_id, int) or isinstance(install_id, bool):
        raise HttpError(400, 'install_id must be an integer')
    r = db.conn().execute('SELECT device_id, package FROM ota_installs WHERE id=? AND job_id=?',
                          (install_id, req.params['job_id'])).fetchone()
    if r is None:
        raise HttpError(404, 'install not found')
    jid = jobs.enqueue('ota', {'device_id': r['device_id']}, 'manual', options={'mode': mode, 'package': r['package']})
    return 200, {'job_id': jid}


def force_reinstall(req):
    return _recovery(req, 'force_reinstall')


def downgrade(req):
    return _recovery(req, 'downgrade')


def register():
    jobs.register('ota', handle, detail)
    events.subscribe('build.succeeded', on_build_succeeded)


ROUTES += [
    ('POST', '/api/devices/{id}/ota', ota_now),
    ('POST', '/api/ota/{job_id}/force-reinstall', force_reinstall),
    ('POST', '/api/ota/{job_id}/downgrade', downgrade),
]
```

#### Task 5.7: Notifier

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/notify.py`
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_device_notify.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P1 `settings.get('notify')`, `settings.put`, `settings.get('base_url')`, `jobs.get` (X2), `events.subscribe`, `server.HttpError`.
- Produces:
  - `notify.EVENTS = ("upstream.new_version","build.succeeded","build.failed","ota.done","ota.failed","device.offline")`; `notify.DEFAULT_EVENTS` = all but `device.offline`.
  - `notify.TELEGRAM_API = "https://api.telegram.org"` (tests point it at the stub).
  - `notify.register()` (subscribes `notify.enqueue` to every name in `EVENTS`, no thread), `notify.start()` (starts one daemon thread running `notify.worker()`).
  - `notify.enqueue(type: str, payload: dict) -> None` (only `queue.put`); in practice one closure per event name binds `type`.
  - `notify.format_message(type, payload) -> str`; `notify.send(cfg: dict, text: str) -> tuple[bool, int | None, str]` (ok, HTTP status, body or error text).
  - `notify.mask(cfg: dict) -> dict`.
  - routes `GET /api/notify`, `PUT /api/notify`, `POST /api/notify/test` → `200 {"ok": bool, "status": int|null, "detail": str}`.

**Behavior:**
- Settings value `notify` = `{type, enabled, config, events, last_test}`; absent → GET returns `{"type": null, "enabled": false, "config": {}, "events": DEFAULT_EVENTS, "last_test": null}`.
- PUT: `type` ∈ telegram/discord/ntfy; `enabled` bool; config keys per type: telegram `bot_token`, `chat_id`; discord `webhook_url`; ntfy `server_url`, `topic`, optional `token`; unknown keys → 400; URL fields must parse with scheme `http`/`https` and a host, else 400; `events` ⊂ `EVENTS` (absent → `DEFAULT_EVENTS`). Secrets `bot_token`, `webhook_url`, `token`: absent or equal to the current masked value → keep the stored value (X15). `last_test` is kept, never taken from the body.
- `mask`: secret → `"****" + value[-4:]` (`"****"` when shorter than 5 chars).
- Worker: per event, skip when channel disabled, event not in `events`, or the payload has `job_id` and `jobs.get(job_id)["options"].get("notify") is False`. This covers `upstream.new_version` too: its `job_id` is the upstream job (Task P2.5); `job_id` null (`POST /api/upstreams/check`) = sent.
- Text: one line per event (examples in Step code), then `\n<base_url><path>` when `base_url` non-empty (build → `/builds.html?job=<job_id>`, ota/device → `/devices.html`, upstream → `/`); whole text truncated to 2000 chars keeping the link.
- Send: telegram `POST {TELEGRAM_API}/bot<token>/sendMessage` JSON `{chat_id, text}`; discord `POST <webhook_url>` JSON `{content}`; ntfy `POST <server_url>/<topic>` body = UTF-8 text cut to 4096 bytes, header `Title: Relume`, `Authorization: Bearer <token>` when set. Timeout 10 s, 3 attempts; HTTP 429 waits Telegram `parameters.retry_after` / Discord `retry_after` / `Retry-After` header (capped at 60 s), else waits 1 s then 2 s; 2xx = ok. Failure logged to stdout with event type.
- Test route sends `"Relume test message"` synchronously and stores `last_test {at, ok, detail}`.

**Constraints:**
- Subscribers only `queue.put` (spec §4.4); `events.emit` must return in < 100 ms while the worker is blocked.
- Waits use `notify._pause(seconds)` (module function wrapping the stdlib wait) so tests replace it.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_device_notify.py'` exit 0 with `test_telegram_shape`, `test_discord_shape`, `test_ntfy_shape_and_auth`, `test_truncate_2000_keeps_link`, `test_retry_after_honoured` (stub 429 `{"ok":false,"parameters":{"retry_after":3}}` then 200 → `_pause(3)` called, ok), `test_emit_not_blocked_by_stalled_provider` (stub blocks on an Event; `events.emit` returns < 0.1 s), `test_skip_notify_false_job`, `test_upstream_new_version_follows_job_notify` (scheduled upstream job with `notify:false` → nothing sent; manual upstream job → sent; `job_id` null → sent), `test_mask_and_keep_secret_on_put` (`GET` returns `****1234`; `PUT` with `****1234` keeps the stored token), `test_default_events_exclude_offline`, `test_put_validation` (bad type, unknown config key, `ftp://` URL → 400), `test_test_route_stores_last_test`, `test_register_starts_no_thread`.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `local/webui/tests/test_device_notify.py`

```python
import json
import threading
import time
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, ThreadingHTTPServer
from unittest import mock

from webui import events, notify, settings
from webui.server import HttpError
from webui.tests import helpers
from webui.tests.device_helpers import add_catalog_item, add_job, req


class Stub:
    """Local HTTP endpoint: records requests, answers from `responses` (the last one repeats)."""

    def __init__(self, tc, responses=((200, {}, b'{"ok":true}'),)):
        self.requests, self.responses, self.gate = [], list(responses), None
        outer = self

        class H(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
            def do_POST(self):
                n = int(self.headers.get('Content-Length') or 0)
                outer.requests.append((self.path, dict(self.headers), self.rfile.read(n)))
                if outer.gate is not None:
                    outer.gate.wait(30)
                st, hd, body = outer.responses.pop(0) if len(outer.responses) > 1 else outer.responses[0]
                self.send_response(st)
                for k, v in hd.items():
                    self.send_header(k, v)
                self.send_header('Content-Length', str(len(body)))
                self.end_headers()
                self.wfile.write(body)

            def log_message(self, *a):
                pass

        self.srv = ThreadingHTTPServer(('127.0.0.1', 0), H)
        self.srv.daemon_threads = True
        threading.Thread(target=self.srv.serve_forever, daemon=True).start()
        tc.addCleanup(self.srv.server_close)
        tc.addCleanup(self.srv.shutdown)
        tc.addCleanup(lambda: self.gate and self.gate.set())
        self.url = f'http://127.0.0.1:{self.srv.server_address[1]}'


def discord(url, events_=None):
    return {'type': 'discord', 'enabled': True, 'config': {'webhook_url': url + '/api/webhooks/1/x'},
            'events': events_ or list(notify.DEFAULT_EVENTS), 'last_test': None}


class NotifyTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        self.pauses = []
        p = mock.patch.object(notify, '_pause', side_effect=self.pauses.append)
        p.start()
        self.addCleanup(p.stop)

    def test_telegram_shape(self):
        stub = Stub(self)
        self.addCleanup(setattr, notify, 'TELEGRAM_API', notify.TELEGRAM_API)
        notify.TELEGRAM_API = stub.url
        cfg = {'type': 'telegram', 'enabled': True, 'config': {'bot_token': '123:ABC', 'chat_id': '42'}}
        self.assertEqual(notify.send(cfg, 'hi')[:2], (True, 200))
        path, headers, body = stub.requests[0]
        self.assertEqual(path, '/bot123:ABC/sendMessage')
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(body), {'chat_id': '42', 'text': 'hi'})
        self.assertEqual(headers['Content-Type'], 'application/json')

    def test_discord_shape(self):
        stub = Stub(self, [(204, {}, b'')])
        self.assertEqual(notify.send(discord(stub.url), 'hi')[:2], (True, 204))
        self.assertEqual((stub.requests[0][0], json.loads(stub.requests[0][2])), ('/api/webhooks/1/x', {'content': 'hi'}))

    def test_ntfy_shape_and_auth(self):
        stub = Stub(self)
        cfg = {'type': 'ntfy', 'enabled': True, 'config': {'server_url': stub.url, 'topic': 'rv', 'token': 'tk_secret1'}}
        self.assertTrue(notify.send(cfg, 'hi')[0])
        path, headers, body = stub.requests[0]
        self.assertEqual((path, body, headers['Title'], headers['Authorization']), ('/rv', b'hi', 'Relume', 'Bearer tk_secret1'))

    def test_truncate_2000_keeps_link(self):
        add_catalog_item(1, 'com.google.android.youtube')
        settings.put('base_url', 'http://nas:47000/')
        text = notify.format_message('build.failed', {'item_id': 1, 'job_id': 5, 'fail_code': 'patch_failed',
                                                      'fail_detail': 'x' * 5000})
        self.assertEqual(len(text), 2000)
        self.assertTrue(text.endswith('\nhttp://nas:47000/builds.html?job=5'))
        self.assertTrue(text.startswith('Build failed: com.google.android.youtube · src: patch_failed: x'))

    def test_retry_after_honoured(self):
        stub = Stub(self, [(429, {}, b'{"ok":false,"parameters":{"retry_after":3}}'), (200, {}, b'{"ok":true}')])
        self.addCleanup(setattr, notify, 'TELEGRAM_API', notify.TELEGRAM_API)
        notify.TELEGRAM_API = stub.url
        cfg = {'type': 'telegram', 'enabled': True, 'config': {'bot_token': 't', 'chat_id': '1'}}
        self.assertTrue(notify.send(cfg, 'hi')[0])
        self.assertEqual(self.pauses, [3.0])
        self.assertEqual(len(stub.requests), 2)

    def test_emit_not_blocked_by_stalled_provider(self):
        stub = Stub(self)
        stub.gate = threading.Event()
        settings.put('notify', discord(stub.url))
        notify.register()
        threading.Thread(target=notify.worker, daemon=True).start()
        t = time.monotonic()
        events.emit('ota.done', {'job_id': None, 'device_id': 1, 'outcome': 'done'})
        self.assertLess(time.monotonic() - t, 0.1)
        deadline = time.monotonic() + 5
        while not stub.requests and time.monotonic() < deadline:
            threading.Event().wait(0.05)
        self.assertEqual(len(stub.requests), 1)

    def test_skip_notify_false_job(self):
        stub = Stub(self, [(204, {}, b'')])
        settings.put('notify', discord(stub.url))
        add_catalog_item(1, 'com.google.android.youtube')
        quiet = add_job(kind='build', target={'item_id': 1}, options={'notify': False}, status='failed')
        loud = add_job(kind='build', target={'item_id': 1}, options={'notify': True}, status='failed')
        p = {'item_id': 1, 'fail_code': 'patch_failed', 'fail_detail': 'x'}
        self.assertIsNone(notify.deliver('build.failed', dict(p, job_id=quiet)))
        self.assertEqual(stub.requests, [])
        self.assertTrue(notify.deliver('build.failed', dict(p, job_id=loud)))
        self.assertIsNone(notify.deliver('device.offline', {'job_id': loud, 'device_id': 1}))

    def test_upstream_new_version_follows_job_notify(self):
        stub = Stub(self, [(204, {}, b'')])
        settings.put('notify', discord(stub.url))
        quiet = add_job(kind='upstream', target={'upstream_id': 2}, options={'notify': False}, trigger='schedule', status='success')
        manual = add_job(kind='upstream', target={'upstream_id': 2}, options={'notify': True}, trigger='manual', status='success')
        p = {'upstream_id': 2, 'tag': 'v1.48.0', 'item_ids': [1]}
        self.assertIsNone(notify.deliver('upstream.new_version', dict(p, job_id=quiet)))
        self.assertEqual(stub.requests, [])
        self.assertTrue(notify.deliver('upstream.new_version', dict(p, job_id=manual)))
        self.assertTrue(notify.deliver('upstream.new_version', dict(p, job_id=None)))
        self.assertEqual(len(stub.requests), 2)

    def test_mask_and_keep_secret_on_put(self):
        body = {'type': 'telegram', 'enabled': True, 'config': {'bot_token': '123456:ABCDEFGH1234', 'chat_id': '42'}}
        notify.put_notify(req(body=body))
        st, got = notify.get_notify(req(method='GET'))
        self.assertEqual(got['config']['bot_token'], '****1234')
        notify.put_notify(req(body={'type': 'telegram', 'enabled': True,
                                    'config': {'bot_token': '****1234', 'chat_id': '43'}}))
        self.assertEqual(settings.get('notify')['config'], {'bot_token': '123456:ABCDEFGH1234', 'chat_id': '43'})
        notify.put_notify(req(body={'type': 'telegram', 'enabled': True, 'config': {'chat_id': '44'}}))
        self.assertEqual(settings.get('notify')['config']['bot_token'], '123456:ABCDEFGH1234')

    def test_default_events_exclude_offline(self):
        st, got = notify.get_notify(req(method='GET'))
        self.assertEqual(got['events'], ['upstream.new_version', 'build.succeeded', 'build.failed', 'ota.done', 'ota.failed'])
        notify.put_notify(req(body={'type': 'discord', 'enabled': True, 'config': {'webhook_url': 'https://d.example/x'}}))
        self.assertNotIn('device.offline', settings.get('notify')['events'])

    def test_put_validation(self):
        bad = [{'type': 'slack', 'config': {}},
               {'type': 'discord', 'config': {'webhook_url': 'https://d.example/x', 'x': 1}},
               {'type': 'discord', 'config': {}},
               {'type': 'ntfy', 'config': {'server_url': 'ftp://n.example', 'topic': 't'}},
               {'type': 'discord', 'enabled': 'yes', 'config': {'webhook_url': 'https://d.example/x'}},
               {'type': 'discord', 'config': {'webhook_url': 'https://d.example/x'}, 'events': ['nope']}]
        for body in bad:
            with self.assertRaises(HttpError, msg=str(body)) as cm:
                notify.put_notify(req(body=body))
            self.assertEqual(cm.exception.status, 400)

    def test_test_route_stores_last_test(self):
        stub = Stub(self, [(500, {}, b'boom')])
        settings.put('notify', discord(stub.url))
        st, body = notify.test_notify(req())
        self.assertEqual((body['ok'], body['status'], body['detail']), (False, 500, 'boom'))
        self.assertEqual(len(stub.requests), 3)
        last = settings.get('notify')['last_test']
        self.assertEqual((last['ok'], last['detail']), (False, '500: boom'))
        self.assertTrue(last['at'].endswith('Z'))

    def test_register_starts_no_thread(self):
        n = threading.active_count()
        notify.register()
        self.assertEqual(threading.active_count(), n)
```

Expected: `ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'webui.notify'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/notify.py`

```python
"""Notifier (spec device 4.4, 6): subscribers only queue; one thread sends to the one configured channel."""
import json
import queue
import threading
import urllib.error
import urllib.parse
import urllib.request

from . import db, events, jobs, settings
from .server import HttpError

EVENTS = ('upstream.new_version', 'build.succeeded', 'build.failed', 'ota.done', 'ota.failed', 'device.offline')
DEFAULT_EVENTS = tuple(e for e in EVENTS if e != 'device.offline')
TELEGRAM_API = 'https://api.telegram.org'
CONFIG_KEYS = {'telegram': {'bot_token': True, 'chat_id': True}, 'discord': {'webhook_url': True},
               'ntfy': {'server_url': True, 'topic': True, 'token': False}}
SECRETS = ('bot_token', 'webhook_url', 'token')
URL_KEYS = ('webhook_url', 'server_url')
LIMIT = 2000
_q = queue.Queue()


def _pause(seconds):
    threading.Event().wait(seconds)


def enqueue(type_, payload):
    _q.put((type_, payload))


def register():
    for name in EVENTS:
        events.subscribe(name, lambda payload, n=name: enqueue(n, payload))


def start():
    threading.Thread(target=worker, name='notifier', daemon=True).start()


def worker():
    while True:
        t, p = _q.get()
        try:
            deliver(t, p)
        except Exception as e:  # never let one event kill the notifier thread
            print(f'notify: {t}: {type(e).__name__}: {e}', flush=True)


def deliver(t, p):
    cfg = settings.get('notify')
    if not cfg or not cfg.get('enabled') or t not in cfg.get('events', DEFAULT_EVENTS):
        return None
    if p.get('job_id') is not None:
        row = jobs.get(p['job_id'])
        if row and row['options'].get('notify') is False:
            return None
    ok, status, detail = send(cfg, format_message(t, p))
    if not ok:
        print(f'notify: {t} failed: {status} {detail[:200]}', flush=True)
    return ok


def _one(sql, args, default):
    r = db.conn().execute(sql, args).fetchone()
    return r[0] if r and r[0] is not None else default


def _label(item_id):
    return _one("SELECT COALESCE(item.display_name, app.display_name || ' · ' || source.name) FROM item "
                'JOIN app ON app.package = item.package JOIN source ON source.id = item.source_id WHERE item.id = ?',
                (item_id,), f'item {item_id}')


def _device(device_id):
    return _one('SELECT name FROM devices WHERE id = ?', (device_id,), f'device {device_id}')


def format_message(t, p):
    if t == 'build.succeeded':
        text = f"Build succeeded: {_label(p['item_id'])} {p.get('app_version')} (patches {p.get('patches_version')})"
        path = f"/builds.html?job={p['job_id']}"
    elif t == 'build.failed':
        text = f"Build failed: {_label(p['item_id'])}: {p.get('fail_code')}: {p.get('fail_detail')}"
        path = f"/builds.html?job={p['job_id']}"
    elif t == 'upstream.new_version':
        repo = _one('SELECT repo FROM upstream WHERE id = ?', (p['upstream_id'],), f"upstream {p['upstream_id']}")
        text, path = f"New upstream version: {repo} {p.get('tag')} ({len(p.get('item_ids') or [])} items)", '/'
    elif t == 'ota.done':
        text, path = f"OTA {p.get('outcome')}: {_device(p['device_id'])}", '/devices.html'
    elif t == 'ota.failed':
        text, path = f"OTA failed: {_device(p['device_id'])}: {p.get('reason')}", '/devices.html'
    else:
        text, path = f"Device offline, scheduled OTA skipped: {_device(p['device_id'])}", '/devices.html'
    base = (settings.get('base_url') or '').rstrip('/')
    link = f'\n{base}{path}' if base else ''
    return text[:LIMIT - len(link)] + link


def _request(cfg, text):
    c, t = cfg['config'], cfg['type']
    if t == 'telegram':
        return urllib.request.Request(f"{TELEGRAM_API}/bot{c['bot_token']}/sendMessage", method='POST',
                                      data=json.dumps({'chat_id': c['chat_id'], 'text': text}).encode(),
                                      headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
    if t == 'discord':
        return urllib.request.Request(c['webhook_url'], method='POST', data=json.dumps({'content': text}).encode(),
                                      headers={'Content-Type': 'application/json'})
    headers = {'Title': 'Relume'}
    if c.get('token'):
        headers['Authorization'] = f"Bearer {c['token']}"
    body = text.encode()[:4096].decode('utf-8', 'ignore').encode()
    return urllib.request.Request(f"{c['server_url'].rstrip('/')}/{urllib.parse.quote(c['topic'])}", method='POST',
                                  data=body, headers=headers)


def _retry_after(headers, body):
    try:
        j = json.loads(body)
        v = (j.get('parameters') or {}).get('retry_after', j.get('retry_after'))
        if v is not None:
            return float(v)
    except (ValueError, AttributeError):
        pass
    try:
        return float(headers.get('Retry-After'))
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None


def send(cfg, text):
    """-> (ok, http status or None, response body or error text). 3 attempts, timeout 10 s each."""
    status, detail = None, ''
    for attempt in range(3):
        wait = None
        try:
            with urllib.request.urlopen(_request(cfg, text), timeout=10) as r:
                return True, r.status, r.read(2000).decode('utf-8', 'replace')
        except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
            status, detail = e.code, e.read(2000).decode('utf-8', 'replace')
            if e.code == 429:
                wait = _retry_after(e.headers, detail)
        except OSError as e:
            status, detail = None, str(e)
        if attempt < 2:
            _pause(min(wait, 60.0) if wait is not None else float(attempt + 1))
    return False, status, detail


def _mask_value(v):
    return None if not v else '****' + (v[-4:] if len(v) > 4 else '')


def mask(cfg):
    out = json.loads(json.dumps(cfg))
    for k in SECRETS:
        if out.get('config', {}).get(k):
            out['config'][k] = _mask_value(out['config'][k])
    return out


def _current():
    return settings.get('notify') or {'type': None, 'enabled': False, 'config': {},
                                      'events': list(DEFAULT_EVENTS), 'last_test': None}


def get_notify(req):
    return 200, mask(_current())


def put_notify(req):
    b = req.json
    if not isinstance(b, dict) or set(b) - {'type', 'enabled', 'config', 'events', 'last_test'}:
        raise HttpError(400, 'unknown field')
    t = b.get('type')
    if t not in CONFIG_KEYS:
        raise HttpError(400, 'type must be telegram, discord or ntfy')
    enabled = b.get('enabled', False)
    if not isinstance(enabled, bool):
        raise HttpError(400, 'enabled must be true or false')
    given = b.get('config') or {}
    if not isinstance(given, dict) or set(given) - set(CONFIG_KEYS[t]):
        raise HttpError(400, f'config keys for {t}: ' + ', '.join(CONFIG_KEYS[t]))
    cur = _current()
    old = cur['config'] if cur.get('type') == t else {}
    config = {}
    for k, required in CONFIG_KEYS[t].items():
        v = given.get(k)
        if k in SECRETS and (v is None or v == _mask_value(old.get(k))):
            v = old.get(k)
        if k == 'chat_id' and isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool):
            v = str(v)
        if v in (None, ''):
            if required:
                raise HttpError(400, f'{k} required')
            continue
        if not isinstance(v, str):
            raise HttpError(400, f'{k} must be a string')
        if k in URL_KEYS:
            u = urllib.parse.urlparse(v)
            if u.scheme not in ('http', 'https') or not u.netloc:
                raise HttpError(400, f'{k} must be an http(s) URL')
        config[k] = v
    ev = b.get('events', list(DEFAULT_EVENTS))
    if not isinstance(ev, list) or not set(ev) <= set(EVENTS):
        raise HttpError(400, 'events must be a list of: ' + ', '.join(EVENTS))
    value = {'type': t, 'enabled': enabled, 'config': config, 'events': ev, 'last_test': cur.get('last_test')}
    settings.put('notify', value)
    return 200, mask(value)


def test_notify(req):
    cur = settings.get('notify')
    if not cur or not cur.get('type'):
        raise HttpError(400, 'no channel configured')
    ok, status, detail = send(cur, 'Relume test message')
    cur['last_test'] = {'at': db.utcnow(), 'ok': ok, 'detail': '' if ok else f'{status}: {detail[:500]}'}
    settings.put('notify', cur)
    return 200, {'ok': ok, 'status': status, 'detail': detail[:500]}


ROUTES = [
    ('GET', '/api/notify', get_notify),
    ('PUT', '/api/notify', put_notify),
    ('POST', '/api/notify/test', test_notify),
]
```

### P6 Frontend shell — shared CSS/JS, Login, App list

Spec: ui §1 L1046–1060, ui §2 Global L1063–1072, Login L1073–1085, App list L1086–1107, ui §3 rows L1218, L1222; xposed §8 L1564, L1568; core §7 L326–336; build §3.4 L762; §0.6 R1, R2, R5, R6.
Mockup files are read from `docs/superpowers/mockups/final/` (`M/` below). Response shapes: `docs/superpowers/plans/parts/ui-api-shapes.md` (frozen in the Plan-fixed table after the merge).

**Frontend rules for P6, P9, P10 (all pages):**
- HTML is built only with the tagged template `html\`…\`` from `app.js`; it escapes every interpolation unless the value is itself an `html` result (or an array of them). `innerHTML`/`insertAdjacentHTML` receive only `html\`…\`` or `''`. Plain text goes through `textContent`. This is the mechanical form of spec L1059 (review core S7).
- Pages: `/` = `index.html`, `/item.html?id=N`, `/builds.html[?job=N][&item=N]`, `/schedules.html`, `/devices.html`, `/settings.html`, `/login.html`; assets `/static/<name>.css|js`.
- Each page `<head>`: `<meta charset>`, viewport, `<title>`, `<link rel="stylesheet" href="/static/app.css">`, the page's own `<style>` (from its mockup second style block), one inline theme line `<script>document.documentElement.dataset.theme=localStorage.getItem('relume-theme')||(matchMedia('(prefers-color-scheme: dark)').matches?'dark':'light')</script>`, `<script type="module" src="/static/<page>.js"></script>`.
- Port rules for mockup markup: sidebar nav links become `/`, `/builds.html`, `/schedules.html`, `/devices.html`, `/settings.html`; the sign-out `<a href="login.html">` becomes `<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-icon btn-sm" type="button" data-logout aria-label="Sign out" title="Sign out">` (same svg); every `onclick="toggleTheme()"` becomes `data-theme-toggle`; every `data-toast="…"` sample attribute is removed; sample rows are removed and replaced by an empty container with an `id`; the sidebar status `<div … aria-label="Server status">` gets `data-shell-status` and empty content; the sidebar user block keeps "Admin"; the `#toast` element is kept.

#### Task P6.1: Shared stylesheet, shared module, static checks

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/static/app.css`, `local/webui/static/app.js`, `local/webui/tests/test_ui_static.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (`app.js` exports): `esc(v)`, `html\`…\``, `$(sel, root)`, `$$(sel, root)`, `api(method, path, body) -> Promise<data>` (throws `Error` with `.status`, `.data`; 401 outside `/login.html` → `location.href = '/login.html'`), `act(method, path, body, okMsg)` (api + toast on success/error), `toast(msg, kind='ok'|'error')`, `logStream(jobId, onText, onEnd) -> EventSource`, `fmt.{abs, rel, bytes, gb, duration}`, `every(ms, fn) -> stop()` (runs `fn` only while `document.visibilityState === 'visible'`), `shell()`, `tabs(root)`, `ask(dialogEl) -> Promise<returnValue>`, `itemStatus(item) -> 'run'|'fail'|'upd'|'new'|'ok'`, `STATUS` (label + badge class per status), `iconTile(item, size)`.
- Consumes: `GET /api/jobs?status=running|queued`, `GET /api/settings`, `POST /api/auth/logout`.

**Behavior:** `app.css` = mockup lines `M/apps.html:3-147` verbatim (the first `<style>` block, identical in 6 pages) plus three rules appended: `.toast[data-kind=error]{border-color:hsl(var(--destructive))}`, `.group[data-color]{border-left:4px solid var(--gc)}`, `.gdot{width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--gc);display:inline-block}`. `app.js` ports the mockup global script (`M/apps.html:158-171`: theme toggle, toast, nav drawer, dropdown close, Escape, tab roving focus) as module code plus the helpers above. `shell()` marks the current nav link `aria-current="page"`, wires tabs, fills `[data-shell-status]` ("Worker busy · N running · M queued" or "Idle · 0 running · M queued", "Disk <used> / <total> GB · <arch>") every 15 s while visible, and fills `[data-host]` with `location.hostname`.
`itemStatus`: `active_job` → `run`; `last_build.status === 'failed'` → `fail`; `upstream.latest_tag && upstream.latest_tag !== upstream.downloaded_tag` → `upd`; no `last_success` → `new`; else `ok`.

```python
import pathlib, re, unittest
from webui import server
from webui.tests.helpers import DataTestCase, serve, http, login

STATIC = pathlib.Path(server.__file__).with_name('static')
UNSAFE = re.compile(r'(?:innerHTML|outerHTML)\s*=(?!\s*(?:html`|\'\'|""|``))|insertAdjacentHTML\([^,]+,(?!\s*html`)')
NAV = ('href="/"', 'href="/builds.html"', 'href="/schedules.html"', 'href="/devices.html"', 'href="/settings.html"')


def violations(text):
    return [m.group(0) for m in UNSAFE.finditer(text)]


class StaticRulesTest(unittest.TestCase):
    def test_negative_fixture_is_flagged(self):
        self.assertTrue(violations('el.innerHTML = `<b>${name}</b>`'))
        self.assertTrue(violations("el.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', '<i>' + x)"))
        self.assertFalse(violations('el.innerHTML = html`<b>${name}</b>`'))
        self.assertFalse(violations("el.innerHTML = ''"))

    def test_js_uses_html_template_only(self):
        for f in sorted(STATIC.glob('*.js')):
            self.assertEqual(violations(f.read_text()), [], f.name)
        for f in sorted(STATIC.glob('*.js')):
            if f.name != 'app.js':
                self.assertNotIn('new Safe', f.read_text(), f.name)

    def test_no_network_refs_or_mockup_names(self):
        for f in sorted(STATIC.iterdir()):
            t = f.read_text()
            self.assertNotRegex(t, r'(?:src|href)=["\']https?://|url\(["\']?https?://|fetch\(["\'`]https?://', f.name)
            self.assertNotRegex(t, r'href="(apps|app-detail|build-log|index)\.html', f.name)
            self.assertNotIn('data-toast=', t, f.name)

    def test_pages_have_nav_and_logout(self):
        for f in sorted(STATIC.glob('*.html')):
            t = f.read_text()
            if f.name == 'login.html':
                continue
            for n in NAV:
                self.assertIn(n, t, f'{f.name} {n}')
            self.assertIn('data-logout', t, f.name)
            self.assertIn('id="toast"', t, f.name)


class StaticServeTest(DataTestCase):
    def test_every_page_and_asset(self):
        base = serve(self)
        pages = sorted(STATIC.glob('*.html'))
        self.assertTrue(pages)
        for f in pages:
            path = '/' if f.name == 'index.html' else '/' + f.name
            st, h, _ = http('GET', base + path)
            if f.name == 'login.html':
                self.assertEqual(st, 200)
            else:
                self.assertEqual((st, h.get('Location')), (302, '/login.html'), f.name)
            for ref in re.findall(r'(?:src|href)="(/static/[^"]+)"', f.read_text()):
                self.assertEqual(http('GET', base + ref)[0], 200, f'{f.name} {ref}')
        cookie = login(base)
        for f in pages:
            path = '/' if f.name == 'index.html' else '/' + f.name
            self.assertEqual(http('GET', base + path, cookie=cookie)[0], 200, f.name)
```
