```js
// Shared helpers for every page (spec ui §1). Ported from mockup apps.html:158-171.
const ESC = { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' };
export const esc = v => String(v ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ESC[c]);

class Safe { constructor(s) { this.s = s; } toString() { return this.s; } }
const part = v => (v instanceof Safe ? v.s : Array.isArray(v) ? v.map(part).join('')
  : v == null || v === false ? '' : esc(v));
export function html(strings, ...vals) {
  return new Safe(strings.reduce((acc, s, i) => acc + part(vals[i - 1]) + s));
}

export const $ = (s, r = document) => r.querySelector(s);
export const $$ = (s, r = document) => [...r.querySelectorAll(s)];

export async function api(method, path, body) {
  const init = { method, credentials: 'same-origin', headers: {} };
  if (method !== 'GET') {
    init.headers['Content-Type'] = 'application/json';
    init.body = JSON.stringify(body ?? {});
  }
  const r = await fetch(path, init);
  const data = (r.headers.get('Content-Type') || '').startsWith('application/json') ? await r.json() : null;
  if (r.status === 401 && location.pathname !== '/login.html') {
    location.href = '/login.html';
    throw Object.assign(new Error('login required'), { status: 401 });
  }
  if (!r.ok) throw Object.assign(new Error(data?.error || `HTTP ${r.status}`), { status: r.status, data });
  return data;
}

export async function act(method, path, body, okMsg) {
  try {
    const d = await api(method, path, body);
    if (okMsg) toast(okMsg);
    return d;
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.status !== 401) toast(e.message, 'error');
    throw e;
  }
}

export function toast(msg, kind = 'ok') {
  const t = $('#toast');
  if (!t) return;
  t.querySelector('span').textContent = msg;
  t.dataset.kind = kind;
  t.classList.add('show');
  clearTimeout(t._h);
  t._h = setTimeout(() => t.classList.remove('show'), 2600);
}

export function logStream(jobId, onText, onEnd) {
  const es = new EventSource(`/api/jobs/${Number(jobId)}/log`);
  es.onmessage = e => onText(e.data + '\n');
  es.addEventListener('end', e => { es.close(); onEnd(e.data); });
  return es;
}

const rtf = new Intl.RelativeTimeFormat([], { numeric: 'auto' });
export const fmt = {
  abs: iso => (iso ? new Date(iso).toLocaleString([], { dateStyle: 'medium', timeStyle: 'short' }) : '—'),
  rel: iso => {
    if (!iso) return '—';
    const s = Math.round((Date.parse(iso) - Date.now()) / 1000), a = Math.abs(s);
    if (a < 60) return rtf.format(s, 'second');
    if (a < 3600) return rtf.format(Math.round(s / 60), 'minute');
    if (a < 86400) return rtf.format(Math.round(s / 3600), 'hour');
    return rtf.format(Math.round(s / 86400), 'day');
  },
  bytes: n => {
    if (n == null) return '—';
    const u = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB', 'TB'];
    let i = 0;
    while (n >= 1024 && i < u.length - 1) { n /= 1024; i++; }
    return `${i ? n.toFixed(1) : n} ${u[i]}`;
  },
  gb: n => (n == null ? '—' : (n / 1e9).toFixed(1)),
  duration: (a, b) => {
    if (!a) return '—';
    const s = Math.max(0, Math.round(((b ? Date.parse(b) : Date.now()) - Date.parse(a)) / 1000));
    if (s < 60) return `${s}s`;
    if (s < 3600) return `${Math.floor(s / 60)}m ${s % 60}s`;
    return `${Math.floor(s / 3600)}h ${Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60)}m`;
  },
};

export function every(ms, fn) {
  const tick = () => { if (document.visibilityState === 'visible') fn(); };
  const h = setInterval(tick, ms);
  document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', tick);
  return () => { clearInterval(h); document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', tick); };
}

export function ask(dlg) {
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    dlg.returnValue = '';
    dlg.addEventListener('close', () => resolve(dlg.returnValue), { once: true });
    dlg.showModal();
  });
}

export const STATUS = {
  run: { label: 'Building', cls: 'b-info' },
  fail: { label: 'Failed', cls: 'b-err' },
  upd: { label: 'Update available', cls: 'b-warn' },
  new: { label: 'Not built', cls: 'b-mute' },
  ok: { label: 'Up to date', cls: 'b-ok' },
};

export function itemStatus(it) {
  if (it.active_job) return 'run';
  if (it.last_build && it.last_build.status === 'failed') return 'fail';
  if (it.upstream && it.upstream.latest_tag && it.upstream.latest_tag !== it.upstream.downloaded_tag) return 'upd';
  if (!it.last_success) return 'new';
  return 'ok';
}

export function iconTile(it, cls = '') {
  const letters = (it.label || it.package || '?').split(/[\s·]+/).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 2)
    .map(w => w[0]).join('').toUpperCase();
  return html`<span class="ico ${cls}" aria-hidden="true" data-l="${letters}"><img src="${it.icon_url}" alt="" loading="lazy" style="width:100%;height:100%;border-radius:inherit" data-fallback></span>`;
}

// One delegated handler: a broken icon becomes its letter tile (spec ui §2 "Letter tile when no icon").
document.addEventListener('error', e => {
  const img = e.target;
  if (img.tagName === 'IMG' && img.hasAttribute('data-fallback')) img.replaceWith(document.createTextNode(img.parentNode.dataset.l));
}, true);

const root = document.documentElement;
export function toggleTheme() {
  root.dataset.theme = root.dataset.theme === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
  try { localStorage.setItem('relume-theme', root.dataset.theme); } catch (_) { /* private mode */ }
}

async function logout() {
  try { await api('POST', '/api/auth/logout'); } finally { location.href = '/login.html'; }
}

document.addEventListener('click', e => {
  if (e.target.closest('[data-theme-toggle]')) toggleTheme();
  if (e.target.closest('[data-logout]')) logout();
  if (e.target.closest('[data-nav-toggle]')) document.body.classList.toggle('nav-open');
  else if (document.body.classList.contains('nav-open') && !e.target.closest('.side')) document.body.classList.remove('nav-open');
  $$('details.dd[open]').forEach(d => { if (!d.contains(e.target)) d.open = false; });
});
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  if (e.key !== 'Escape') return;
  document.body.classList.remove('nav-open');
  $$('details.dd[open]').forEach(d => { d.open = false; });
});

export function tabs(scope = document) {
  $$('[role=tablist]', scope).forEach(tl => {
    const list = $$('[role=tab]', tl);
    const sel = t => {
      list.forEach(x => {
        const on = x === t;
        x.setAttribute('aria-selected', on);
        x.tabIndex = on ? 0 : -1;
        document.getElementById(x.getAttribute('aria-controls')).hidden = !on;
      });
      t.focus();
    };
    list.forEach((t, i) => {
      t.addEventListener('click', () => sel(t));
      t.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
        const d = e.key === 'ArrowRight' ? 1 : e.key === 'ArrowLeft' ? -1 : 0;
        if (d) sel(list[(i + d + list.length) % list.length]);
      });
    });
  });
}

export function shell() {
  const here = location.pathname === '/index.html' ? '/' : location.pathname;
  $$('.side .nav a').forEach(a => {
    if (a.getAttribute('href') === here) a.setAttribute('aria-current', 'page');
    else a.removeAttribute('aria-current');
  });
  tabs();
  $$('[data-host]').forEach(el => { el.textContent = location.hostname; });
  const box = $('[data-shell-status]');
  if (!box) return;
  const load = async () => {
    const [run, queued, s] = await Promise.all([
      api('GET', '/api/jobs?status=running&limit=100'),
      api('GET', '/api/jobs?status=queued&limit=100'),
      api('GET', '/api/settings'),
    ]);
    const n = run.jobs.length, m = queued.jobs.length, d = s.server.disk;
    box.innerHTML = html`<span class="row" style="gap:.5rem;flex-wrap:nowrap"><span class="dot ${n ? 'on' : ''}" aria-hidden="true"></span>${n ? 'Worker busy' : 'Idle'} · ${n} running · ${m} queued</span><span class="mono">Disk ${fmt.gb(d.total - d.free)} / ${fmt.gb(d.total)} GB · ${s.server.arch}</span>`;
  };
  load().catch(() => {});
  every(15000, () => load().catch(() => {}));
}
```

#### Task P6.2: Login page

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/static/login.html`, `local/webui/static/login.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET /api/health`, `POST /api/auth/login {password, remember}`; `app.js` `api`, `$`.

**Behavior (spec L1073–1085, L333–335, §0.6 R2, R5):**
- Markup = `M/login.html:163-175` with these changes: the username field (`#u` and its hint) is removed; the hint under the password reads `The admin password is printed once in the server log: docker compose -f local/docker-compose.yml logs app | grep 'admin password'`; the "Preview error state" button is removed; the forgot-password line reads `Forgot the password? On the host run docker compose -f local/docker-compose.yml exec app python3 -m webui reset-password`; the checkbox gets `id="remember"` and stays checked by default with the label "Keep me signed in on this device (30 days)"; the `<form>` has `id="f"` and no inline `onsubmit`; the server `<dl>` becomes `<dl … id="srv" aria-label="Server"></dl>`; the footer text `Relume v1.0` stays static under the form; the `.auth` rule of `M/login.html:148` goes into the page `<style>`.
- `login.js`: show/hide toggle (port of the mockup inline script); submit → busy state (`aria-busy`, text `Signing in…`) → `api('POST','/api/auth/login',{password, remember})`; 200 → `location.href = '/'`; 401 → alert text `Wrong password. N attempts left before a 5-minute lockout.` and `aria-invalid="true"` on the field; 429 → `Too many attempts. Try again in M min S s.` and the submit button disabled until `retry_after` has passed; other errors → `Server unreachable`.
- Server summary from `GET /api/health`: rows `host` = `location.host`, `status` = `Server up · <app>`, `version` = `<version>`, `up since` = `fmt.abs(started_at)`; health failure → one row `status` = `Server unreachable`. No other fields (spec L1080).

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `node --check local/webui/static/login.js` exit 0; `grep -c 'id="u"\|relume reset-password\|Preview error' local/webui/static/login.html` prints `0`.
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` — the login page row of `test_every_page_and_asset` passes (index row still fails until P6.3).

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing check** — `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` → FAIL (`AssertionError: [] is not true`).

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement markup** — build `login.html` from `M/login.html` lines 1–2 (doctype/head start with title `Sign in · Relume`) + head items of the frontend rules + body lines 163–175 edited as listed; replace the trailing inline `<script>` with nothing (logic moves to `login.js`).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/static/login.js`

```js
import { $, api, fmt, html } from '/static/app.js';

const form = $('#f'), pw = $('#p'), err = $('#err'), go = $('#go');

$('#pwt').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const hidden = pw.type === 'password';
  pw.type = hidden ? 'text' : 'password';
  e.currentTarget.textContent = hidden ? 'Hide' : 'Show';
  e.currentTarget.setAttribute('aria-pressed', hidden);
});

function showError(text) {
  err.querySelector('span').textContent = text;
  err.hidden = false;
  pw.setAttribute('aria-invalid', 'true');
}

function busy(on) {
  go.disabled = on;
  go.setAttribute('aria-busy', on);
  go.lastChild.textContent = on ? 'Signing in…' : 'Sign in';
}

form.addEventListener('submit', async e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  busy(true);
  try {
    await api('POST', '/api/auth/login', { password: pw.value, remember: $('#remember').checked });
    location.href = '/';
  } catch (ex) {
    busy(false);
    if (ex.status === 401) {
      showError(`Wrong password. ${ex.data.attempts_left} attempts left before a 5-minute lockout.`);
    } else if (ex.status === 429) {
      const s = ex.data.retry_after;
      showError(`Too many attempts. Try again in ${Math.floor(s / 60)} min ${s % 60} s.`);
      go.disabled = true;
      setTimeout(() => { go.disabled = false; }, s * 1000);
    } else {
      showError('Server unreachable');
    }
  }
});

(async () => {
  const srv = $('#srv');
  try {
    const h = await api('GET', '/api/health');
    srv.innerHTML = html`<dt>host</dt><dd style="margin:0">${location.host}</dd><dt>status</dt><dd style="margin:0">Server up · ${h.app}</dd><dt>version</dt><dd style="margin:0">${h.version}</dd><dt>up since</dt><dd style="margin:0">${fmt.abs(h.started_at)}</dd>`;
  } catch (_) {
    srv.innerHTML = html`<dt>status</dt><dd style="margin:0">Server unreachable</dd>`;
  }
})();
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run** — `node --check local/webui/static/login.js` → exit 0; the grep acceptance prints `0`.

```bash
git add local/webui/static/login.html local/webui/static/login.js && git commit -m "feat(webui): login page with remember and server summary" -- local/webui/static/login.html local/webui/static/login.js
```

#### Task P6.3: App list — markup, rendering, tallies, filters, item actions

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/static/index.html`, `local/webui/static/index.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET /api/catalog`, `GET /api/upstreams`, `POST /api/upstreams/check`, `POST /api/upstreams/{id}/pull {"rebuild":true}`, `POST /api/items/{id}/build`, `POST /api/builds {item_ids}`, `GET /api/items/{id}/artifacts`, `GET /api/artifacts/{id}/download`, `GET /api/groups/{id}/download`, `GET /api/devices`, `POST /api/devices/{id}/ota {item_ids}`; `app.js` helpers.
- Produces: `index.js` functions used by P6.4 in the same file: `state` (`{catalog, upstreams, filter:{s, q}, dragging}`), `load()`, `render()`.

**Behavior (spec L1086–1107, L1222, L1564):**
- Markup = `M/apps.html` body lines 172–318 ported by the frontend rules; the crumbs/title stay "Home / App list"; the top bar keeps "Check upstream" (`id="check"`) and "Add app" (`id="add"`); the tally section keeps its four `button.card.stat` with `data-f` = `all|upd|run|fail` and inner `<b>`/`.xs` emptied (ids `n-all`, `n-upd`, `n-run`, `n-fail`, `s-all`, `s-upd`, `s-run`, `s-fail`); search `#q`, status select `#sf`, "New group" button `id="newgrp"`; `<div class="stack" id="groups"></div>` replaces the sample groups; an amber banner `<div class="alert warn" id="banner" hidden><span></span></div>` sits directly under the tallies; `#nores` and the keyboard hint paragraph stay; the second `<style>` block `M/apps.html:149-157` goes into the page head.
- `load()` = `GET /api/catalog` + `GET /api/upstreams` in parallel, then `render()`; `every(5000, load)` while visible and not `state.dragging` and no `<dialog open>` and no `details.dd[open]`.
- Group card (`<details class="card group" data-group="<id>" draggable="true">`, Ungrouped = `data-group="0"`, rendered last, not draggable, no rename/delete): handle, chevron, select-all checkbox, `<h2>` name, colour dot `<span class="gdot" style="--gc:<color>">` when `color`, count badge, `.gsum` per-status counts, "Update all" (one pull per distinct `upstream.id` of the group's items), "Rebuild all" (`POST /api/builds` with the checked items, or every enabled item when none is checked), dropdown with "OTA group to devices", "Download all (zip)" (`<a role="menuitem" href="/api/groups/<id>/download" download>` with text "Download all (zip) · newest successful build of each item"), "Rename…", "Delete group" (named groups only). A group with `color` gets `data-color` and `style="--gc:<color>"` (left border rule from `app.css`). Open/closed state per group id in `localStorage['relume-open']` (JSON array of open ids); default closed (spec L1095).
- Item row (`<div class="item" data-item="<id>" data-s="<status>" draggable="true">`): handle, `iconTile(item)`, link `/item.html?id=<id>` with `item.label`, source badge (`.src`) = `source_name`, plus `Xposed · LSPatch` / `Xposed · NPatch` badge when `engine` ≠ `morphe`; package line `package · abis.join(', ')`; "Built app" = `last_success.app_version` or `—`; "Patches · local → upstream" = `downloaded_tag → latest_tag` with `<span class="newer">` on the latest tag when they differ; status badge from `itemStatus` + `STATUS`: `run` shows `Building <pct> %` (`pct = round(100 * step_index / step_count)`, `Queued` when `active_job.status === 'queued'`), `fail` shows `Failed #<job_id>` and a `.why` line `<reason> · <a href="/builds.html?job=<id>">Log</a>`; actions: Update (disabled when `latest_tag === downloaded_tag`; title "Updates the <source_name> source for all its items" or "Updates module <repo> for all its apps" for Xposed), Rebuild (label Retry when `fail`, Build when `new`, disabled when `run`), Download (menu built on open from `GET /api/items/<id>/artifacts` restricted to the newest `job_id`; one entry per artifact `<a href="/api/artifacts/<aid>/download" download>` labelled `<variant> · <abi> · <size>`; disabled with title "No build yet" when no `last_success`).
- Tallies: all = item count, sub-line `<groups> groups · <sources> patch sources`; updates = count `upd`, sub-line `upstream checked <fmt.rel(max checked_at)>`; building = count `run`, sub-line `+<queued> queued`; failed = count `fail`, sub-line = label of the newest failed item. Click on a tally or `#sf` change sets `state.filter.s`; `#q` filters by `label`, `package`, `source_name` (lowercase substring). Filter port = `applyFilter` of `M/apps.html:325-329`.
- Banner: shown when any row of `GET /api/upstreams` has `check_error`; text `Upstream check problems: <repo> — <check_error>; …` (max 3, then `and N more`).
- "Check upstream" → `act('POST','/api/upstreams/check',{},'Upstream checked')` then `load()`.
- "OTA group to devices": `GET /api/devices`; for each device whose `assignments` include group items: `POST /api/devices/<id>/ota {item_ids:[those items]}`; toast `OTA queued on N devices` or `No device has items of this group assigned`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `node --check local/webui/static/index.js` exit 0; `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` exit 0 (now both pages exist).
- [ ] `grep -c 'Download all (zip)' local/webui/static/index.js` ≥ 1; `grep -c '/api/upstreams/check' local/webui/static/index.js` ≥ 1; `grep -c 'rebuild: true' local/webui/static/index.js` ≥ 1.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing check** — `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` → FAIL (index.html missing: `/` returns 404 with a session).

- [ ] **Step 2: Implement markup** — `index.html` per Behavior.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/static/index.js` (rendering and actions; P6.4 appends reorder and dialogs)

```js
import { $, $$, act, api, every, fmt, html, iconTile, itemStatus, shell, STATUS, toast } from '/static/app.js';

export const state = { catalog: null, upstreams: [], filter: { s: 'all', q: '' }, dragging: false };
const openIds = () => new Set(JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('relume-open') || '[]'));
const allItems = () => [...state.catalog.groups.flatMap(g => g.items), ...state.catalog.ungrouped];
const groupsWithUngrouped = () => [...state.catalog.groups,
  { id: 0, name: 'Ungrouped', color: null, items: state.catalog.ungrouped }];

function badge(it, s) {
  if (s === 'run') {
    const j = it.active_job;
    const txt = j.status === 'queued' ? 'Queued'
      : `Building ${j.step_count ? Math.round((100 * j.step_index) / j.step_count) : 0} %`;
    return html`<span class="badge b-info">${txt}</span>`;
  }
  if (s === 'fail') {
    const b = it.last_build;
    return html`<span class="badge b-err">Failed #${b.job_id}</span><div class="why xs"><span class="muted">${b.reason || ''}</span> · <a href="/builds.html?job=${b.job_id}">Log</a></div>`;
  }
  return html`<span class="badge ${STATUS[s].cls}">${STATUS[s].label}</span>`;
}

function itemRow(it) {
  const s = itemStatus(it), up = it.upstream || {};
  const current = !up.latest_tag || up.latest_tag === up.downloaded_tag;
  const xposed = it.engine !== 'morphe';
  const updTitle = xposed ? `Updates module ${up.repo} for all its apps` : `Updates the ${it.source_name} source for all its items`;
  const rebuildLabel = s === 'fail' ? 'Retry' : s === 'new' ? 'Build' : 'Rebuild';
  return html`<div class="item" draggable="true" data-item="${it.id}" data-s="${s}">
<span class="handle" aria-label="Reorder ${it.label}; Alt+arrow keys" role="button" tabindex="0"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="9" cy="6" r="1"/><circle cx="15" cy="6" r="1"/><circle cx="9" cy="12" r="1"/><circle cx="15" cy="12" r="1"/><circle cx="9" cy="18" r="1"/><circle cx="15" cy="18" r="1"/></svg></span>${iconTile(it)}
<div style="min-width:0"><div class="row" style="gap:.375rem"><a href="/item.html?id=${it.id}" style="font-weight:600;text-decoration:none">${it.label}</a><span class="src">${it.source_name}</span>${xposed ? html`<span class="badge b-mute plain">Xposed · ${it.engine === 'lspatch' ? 'LSPatch' : 'NPatch'}</span>` : ''}</div>
<div class="xs muted mono" style="overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${it.package} · ${(it.abis || []).join(', ')}</div></div>
<div class="ver col-built"><small>Built app</small><span class="mono">${it.last_success ? it.last_success.app_version : '—'}</span></div>
<div class="ver col-up"><small>Patches · local → upstream</small><span class="mono">${up.downloaded_tag || '—'} → ${current ? (up.latest_tag || '—') : html`<span class="newer">${up.latest_tag}</span>`}</span></div>
<div>${badge(it, s)}</div>
<div class="acts">
<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" type="button" data-act="pull" title="${updTitle}" ${current ? html`disabled` : ''}>Update</button>
<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" type="button" data-act="build" ${s === 'run' ? html`disabled` : ''}>${rebuildLabel}</button>
<details class="dd"><summary class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm btn-icon" aria-label="Download ${it.label}" title="${it.last_success ? 'Download' : 'No build yet'}" data-act="dl" ${it.last_success ? '' : html`aria-disabled="true"`}><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M21 15v4a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-4M7 10l5 5 5-5M12 15V3"/></svg></summary><div class="menu" role="menu" data-dl-menu><span class="xs muted" style="padding:.5rem">Loading…</span></div></details>
</div></div>`;
}

function groupCard(g, open) {
  const counts = {};
  g.items.forEach(it => { const s = itemStatus(it); counts[s] = (counts[s] || 0) + 1; });
  const named = g.id !== 0;
  const colorAttr = g.color ? html`data-color style="--gc:${g.color}"` : '';
  return html`<details class="card group" data-group="${g.id}" ${named ? html`draggable="true"` : ''} ${open ? html`open` : ''} ${colorAttr}>
<summary>${named ? html`<span class="handle" aria-label="Reorder group ${g.name}; Alt+arrow keys" role="button" tabindex="0"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="9" cy="6" r="1"/><circle cx="15" cy="6" r="1"/><circle cx="9" cy="12" r="1"/><circle cx="15" cy="12" r="1"/><circle cx="9" cy="18" r="1"/><circle cx="15" cy="18" r="1"/></svg></span>` : ''}<svg class="chev" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M9 6l6 6-6 6"/></svg>
<input type="checkbox" class="cb" aria-label="Select all in ${g.name}" data-act="selall">${g.color ? html`<span class="gdot" style="--gc:${g.color}" aria-hidden="true"></span>` : ''}<h2>${g.name}</h2><span class="badge b-mute plain">${g.items.length}</span>
<span class="gsum xs hide-m" aria-label="Group status">${Object.entries(counts).map(([s, n]) => html`<span class="${STATUS[s].cls}"><i></i>${n} ${STATUS[s].label.toLowerCase()}</span>`)}</span><span class="grow"></span>
<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm hide-m" type="button" data-act="gpull">Update all</button>
<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm hide-m" type="button" data-act="gbuild">Rebuild all</button>
<details class="dd"><summary class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm btn-icon" aria-label="More actions for ${g.name}"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" aria-hidden="true"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="1"/><circle cx="19" cy="12" r="1"/><circle cx="5" cy="12" r="1"/></svg></summary>
<div class="menu" role="menu"><button role="menuitem" type="button" data-act="gota">OTA group to devices</button><a role="menuitem" href="/api/groups/${g.id}/download" download>Download all (zip) · newest successful build of each item</a>${named ? html`<button role="menuitem" type="button" data-act="grename">Rename…</button><button role="menuitem" type="button" data-act="gdelete">Delete group</button>` : ''}</div></details>
</summary><div data-list>${g.items.map(itemRow)}</div></details>`;
}

function renderTallies(items) {
  const by = s => items.filter(it => itemStatus(it) === s);
  const sources = new Set(items.map(it => it.source_id));
  const checked = items.map(it => it.upstream && it.upstream.checked_at).filter(Boolean).sort().pop();
  const queued = items.filter(it => it.active_job && it.active_job.status === 'queued').length;
  const failed = by('fail');
  $('#n-all').textContent = items.length;
  $('#s-all').textContent = `${state.catalog.groups.length} groups · ${sources.size} patch sources`;
  $('#n-upd').textContent = by('upd').length;
  $('#s-upd').textContent = `upstream checked ${fmt.rel(checked)}`;
  $('#n-run').textContent = by('run').length;
  $('#s-run').textContent = `+${queued} queued`;
  $('#n-fail').textContent = failed.length;
  $('#s-fail').textContent = failed.length ? failed[0].label : 'none';
}

function renderBanner() {
  const bad = state.upstreams.filter(u => u.check_error);
  const b = $('#banner');
  b.hidden = !bad.length;
  if (!bad.length) return;
  const shown = bad.slice(0, 3).map(u => `${u.repo} — ${u.check_error}`).join('; ');
  b.querySelector('span').textContent = `Upstream check problems: ${shown}${bad.length > 3 ? `; and ${bad.length - 3} more` : ''}`;
}

export function applyFilter() {
  const { s, q } = state.filter;
  let n = 0;
  $$('[data-item]').forEach(el => {
    const ok = (s === 'all' || el.dataset.s === s) && el.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q);
    el.hidden = !ok;
    if (ok) n++;
  });
  $$('[data-group]').forEach(g => {
    const any = g.querySelector('[data-item]:not([hidden])');
    g.hidden = !any && (s !== 'all' || q !== '');
    if (any && (s !== 'all' || q)) g.open = true;
  });
  $$('[data-f]').forEach(b => b.setAttribute('aria-pressed', b.dataset.f === s));
  $('#sf').value = s;
  $('#nores').hidden = n > 0;
}

export function render() {
  const open = openIds();
  $('#groups').innerHTML = html`${groupsWithUngrouped().map(g => groupCard(g, open.has(g.id)))}`;
  renderTallies(allItems());
  renderBanner();
  applyFilter();
}

export async function load() {
  const [catalog, ups] = await Promise.all([api('GET', '/api/catalog'), api('GET', '/api/upstreams')]);
  state.catalog = catalog;
  state.upstreams = ups.upstreams;
  render();
}

const itemById = id => allItems().find(it => it.id === Number(id));
const groupById = id => groupsWithUngrouped().find(g => g.id === Number(id));

async function pullAll(items) {
  const ids = [...new Set(items.map(it => it.upstream && it.upstream.id).filter(Boolean))];
  await Promise.all(ids.map(id => act('POST', `/api/upstreams/${id}/pull`, { rebuild: true })));
  toast(`Update queued for ${ids.length} upstream${ids.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
}

async function otaGroup(g) {
  const ids = new Set(g.items.map(it => it.id));
  const { devices } = await api('GET', '/api/devices');
  let n = 0;
  for (const d of devices) {
    const mine = d.assignments.map(a => a.item_id).filter(id => ids.has(id));
    if (mine.length) { await act('POST', `/api/devices/${d.id}/ota`, { item_ids: mine }); n++; }
  }
  toast(n ? `OTA queued on ${n} device${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}` : 'No device has items of this group assigned');
}

async function fillDownloads(menu, it) {
  const { artifacts } = await api('GET', `/api/items/${it.id}/artifacts`);
  const newest = artifacts.length ? artifacts[0].job_id : null;
  const rows = artifacts.filter(a => a.job_id === newest);
  menu.innerHTML = html`${rows.length
    ? rows.map(a => html`<a role="menuitem" href="/api/artifacts/${a.id}/download" download>${a.variant} · ${a.abi} · ${fmt.bytes(a.size)}</a>`)
    : html`<span class="xs muted" style="padding:.5rem">No artifact</span>`}`;
}

document.addEventListener('click', async e => {
  const el = e.target.closest('[data-act]');
  if (!el) return;
  const row = el.closest('[data-item]'), card = el.closest('[data-group]');
  const it = row && itemById(row.dataset.item), g = card && groupById(card.dataset.group);
  const a = el.dataset.act;
  if (a === 'grename' || a === 'gdelete') return;   // handled by the dialog code below
  if (a === 'selall') { e.stopPropagation(); $$('[data-item]', card).forEach(r => r.classList.toggle('selected', el.checked)); return; }
  if (a === 'dl') { if (!it.last_success) { e.preventDefault(); return; } fillDownloads(el.nextElementSibling, it).catch(() => {}); return; }
  e.preventDefault();
  try {
    if (a === 'pull') await act('POST', `/api/upstreams/${it.upstream.id}/pull`, { rebuild: true }, `Update queued: ${it.source_name}`);
    if (a === 'build') await act('POST', `/api/items/${it.id}/build`, {}, `Build queued: ${it.label}`);
    if (a === 'gpull') await pullAll(g.items);
    if (a === 'gbuild') {
      const picked = $$('[data-item].selected', card).map(r => Number(r.dataset.item));
      const ids = picked.length ? picked : g.items.filter(x => x.enabled).map(x => x.id);
      await act('POST', '/api/builds', { item_ids: ids }, `Queued ${ids.length} builds`);
    }
    if (a === 'gota') await otaGroup(g);
    await load();
  } catch (_) { /* act() already showed the error */ }
});

document.addEventListener('toggle', e => {
  const g = e.target.closest && e.target.matches('details[data-group]') ? e.target : null;
  if (!g) return;
  const ids = openIds();
  if (g.open) ids.add(Number(g.dataset.group)); else ids.delete(Number(g.dataset.group));
  localStorage.setItem('relume-open', JSON.stringify([...ids]));
}, true);

$$('[data-f]').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => { state.filter.s = b.dataset.f; applyFilter(); }));
$('#sf').addEventListener('change', e => { state.filter.s = e.target.value; applyFilter(); });
$('#q').addEventListener('input', e => { state.filter.q = e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase(); applyFilter(); });
$('#check').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  try { await act('POST', '/api/upstreams/check', {}, 'Upstream checked'); await load(); } catch (_) { /* shown */ }
});

shell();
load().catch(() => {});
every(5000, () => {
  if (state.dragging || document.querySelector('dialog[open], details.dd[open]')) return;
  load().catch(() => {});
});
```

The `.selected` class needs one rule in the page `<style>`: `.item.selected{background:hsl(var(--accent)/.08)}`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add local/webui/static/index.html local/webui/static/index.js && git commit -m "feat(webui): app list with groups, tallies, filters and item actions" -- local/webui/static/index.html local/webui/static/index.js
```

#### Task P6.4: App list — reorder, group dialogs with colour, Add app and New source

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/static/index.html` (add three `<dialog>` elements), `local/webui/static/index.js` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PUT /api/groups/order {ids}`, `PUT /api/groups/{id}/items/order {item_ids}` (id 0 = Ungrouped), `POST /api/groups {name, color}`, `PUT /api/groups/{id} {name, color}`, `DELETE /api/groups/{id}`, `GET /api/sources`, `POST /api/sources {id, name, engine, repo, channel, tag?, asset_regex?, keystore, cli_upstream_id}`, `POST /api/items {source_id, package, group_id, display_name?}`, `GET /api/upstreams`.

**Behavior (spec L1094, L1100, L1102, L1104 group colour row, L1218, L1568, §0.6 R6):**
- Reorder: port of `M/apps.html:320-347` (native DnD for mouse, pointer events for touch/pen, Alt+↑/↓ on a handle). `state.dragging = true` during a drag. On drop/move end: if a group moved → `PUT /api/groups/order {ids:[named group ids in DOM order]}`; if an item moved → `PUT /api/groups/<gid>/items/order {item_ids:[DOM order]}` for the destination list, and for the source list when it differs; then toast `Order saved` and `load()`. The Ungrouped card cannot be dragged and stays last; dropping a group after it inserts before it.
- Group dialog `<dialog id="dlg-group"><form method="dialog" class="card">`: name input (`required maxlength=60`), `<input type="color" id="g-color">` with a checkbox `id="g-nocolor"` "No colour" (checked = send `color:null`), buttons Cancel (`value="cancel"`) / Save (`value="ok"`). New group → `POST /api/groups`; Rename… → prefilled, `PUT /api/groups/<id>`. Delete group → `confirm('Delete group "<name>"? Its items move to Ungrouped.')` → `DELETE`.
- Add app dialog `<dialog id="dlg-add">`: source `<select id="a-src">` from `GET /api/sources` (label `name · engine`) plus last option `New source…`; package input (`pattern` = spec package regex, `required`); group select (named groups + Ungrouped → `group_id` null); optional display name. Save → `POST /api/items` → toast `Added; the icon appears after the next fetch` → `load()`. Choosing `New source…` opens `<dialog id="dlg-src">`: id (slug pattern), name, engine select (Morphe/LSPatch/NPatch), repo (`owner/name` pattern), channel select (stable/prerelease/tag) + tag input (shown for tag), asset regex (required when engine ≠ morphe, hint "must match exactly one asset" for modules), keystore input with `<datalist>` of `morphe.keystore`, `lspatch-signing.p12`, `fiorenmas.ks`, engine upstream select from `GET /api/upstreams` rows with `kind === 'cli'` (label `repo · channel/tag`). Save → `POST /api/sources` → reload sources into `#a-src` and select the new id. Server 400 messages are shown as toast (via `act`).

```js
// ---- reorder (port of mockup apps.html:320-347) ----
let dragEl = null, fromList = null;
const listGroupId = list => Number(list.closest('[data-group]').dataset.group);

async function saveOrder(el, srcList) {
  try {
    if (el.hasAttribute('data-group')) {
      const ids = $$('#groups > [data-group]').map(g => Number(g.dataset.group)).filter(id => id !== 0);
      await api('PUT', '/api/groups/order', { ids });
    } else {
      const lists = new Set([el.closest('[data-list]'), srcList]);
      for (const list of lists) {
        if (!list) continue;
        const item_ids = $$('[data-item]', list).map(r => Number(r.dataset.item));
        await api('PUT', `/api/groups/${listGroupId(list)}/items/order`, { item_ids });
      }
    }
    toast('Order saved');
  } catch (e) {
    toast(e.message, 'error');
  }
  state.dragging = false;
  load().catch(() => {});
}

function placeOver(el, x, y, target) {
  const isItem = el.hasAttribute('data-item');
  let over = isItem ? target.closest('[data-item]') : target.closest('[data-group]');
  const list = isItem ? target.closest('[data-list]') : null;
  if (!isItem && over && over.dataset.group === '0') over = null;   // Ungrouped stays last
  if (over && over !== el) {
    const r = over.getBoundingClientRect();
    over.parentNode.insertBefore(el, y - r.top > r.height / 2 ? over.nextSibling : over);
    return true;
  }
  if (!over && list) { list.appendChild(el); return true; }
  if (!isItem && !over && target.closest('[data-group="0"]')) {
    $('#groups').insertBefore(el, $('#groups > [data-group="0"]'));
    return true;
  }
  return false;
}

document.addEventListener('dragstart', e => {
  dragEl = e.target.closest('[data-item]') || e.target.closest('[data-group]:not([data-group="0"])');
  if (!dragEl) return;
  e.stopPropagation();
  state.dragging = true;
  fromList = dragEl.closest('[data-list]');
  dragEl.classList.add('dragging');
  e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
  e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', '');
});
document.addEventListener('dragover', e => {
  if (!dragEl) return;
  if (placeOver(dragEl, e.clientX, e.clientY, e.target)) e.preventDefault();
});
document.addEventListener('dragend', () => {
  if (!dragEl) return;
  dragEl.classList.remove('dragging');
  const el = dragEl;
  dragEl = null;
  saveOrder(el, fromList);
});

let tEl = null, tFrom = null;
document.addEventListener('pointerdown', e => {
  const h = e.target.closest('.handle');
  if (!h || e.pointerType === 'mouse') return;
  tEl = h.closest('[data-item]') || h.closest('[data-group]');
  e.preventDefault();
  state.dragging = true;
  tFrom = tEl.closest('[data-list]');
  tEl.classList.add('touch-drag');
  try { h.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId); } catch (_) { /* capture unsupported */ }
});
document.addEventListener('pointermove', e => {
  if (!tEl) return;
  tEl.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
  const t = document.elementFromPoint(e.clientX, e.clientY);
  tEl.style.pointerEvents = '';
  if (t) placeOver(tEl, e.clientX, e.clientY, t);
});
const tEnd = () => {
  if (!tEl) return;
  tEl.classList.remove('touch-drag');
  const el = tEl;
  tEl = null;
  saveOrder(el, tFrom);
};
document.addEventListener('pointerup', tEnd);
document.addEventListener('pointercancel', tEnd);
document.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target.closest('summary .handle')) e.preventDefault(); }, true);
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  const h = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.handle');
  if (!h || !e.altKey || (e.key !== 'ArrowUp' && e.key !== 'ArrowDown')) return;
  const el = h.closest('[data-item]') || h.closest('[data-group]');
  const sib = e.key === 'ArrowUp' ? el.previousElementSibling : el.nextElementSibling;
  if (!sib || sib.dataset.group === '0') return;
  e.preventDefault();
  if (e.key === 'ArrowUp') el.parentNode.insertBefore(el, sib); else el.parentNode.insertBefore(sib, el);
  h.focus();
  saveOrder(el, el.closest('[data-list]'));
});

// ---- group dialogs (spec §0.6 R6) ----
async function editGroup(g) {
  const dlg = $('#dlg-group');
  $('#dg-t').textContent = g ? 'Rename group' : 'New group';
  $('#g-name').value = g ? g.name : '';
  $('#g-color').value = (g && g.color) || '#10b981';
  $('#g-nocolor').checked = !!g && !g.color;
  if (await ask(dlg) !== 'ok') return;
  const body = { name: $('#g-name').value.trim(), color: $('#g-nocolor').checked ? null : $('#g-color').value };
  try {
    if (g) await act('PUT', `/api/groups/${g.id}`, body, 'Group saved');
    else await act('POST', '/api/groups', body, 'Group created');
    await load();
  } catch (_) { /* shown */ }
}
$('#g-color').addEventListener('input', () => { $('#g-nocolor').checked = false; });
$('#newgrp').addEventListener('click', () => editGroup(null));
document.addEventListener('click', async e => {
  const el = e.target.closest('[data-act="grename"], [data-act="gdelete"]');
  if (!el) return;
  const g = groupById(el.closest('[data-group]').dataset.group);
  if (el.dataset.act === 'grename') return editGroup(g);
  if (!confirm(`Delete group "${g.name}"? Its items move to Ungrouped.`)) return;
  try { await act('DELETE', `/api/groups/${g.id}`, {}, 'Group deleted'); await load(); } catch (_) { /* shown */ }
});

// ---- Add app and New source (spec L1102, L1218, xposed L1568) ----
async function fillSources(selectId) {
  const { sources } = await api('GET', '/api/sources');
  $('#a-src').innerHTML = html`${sources.map(s => html`<option value="${s.id}">${s.name} · ${s.engine}</option>`)}<option value="__new">New source…</option>`;
  if (selectId) $('#a-src').value = selectId;
}

async function newSource() {
  const { upstreams } = await api('GET', '/api/upstreams');
  $('#s-cli').innerHTML = html`${upstreams.filter(u => u.kind === 'cli').map(u => html`<option value="${u.id}">${u.repo} · ${u.channel === 'tag' ? u.tag : u.channel}</option>`)}`;
  if (await ask($('#dlg-src')) !== 'ok') return null;
  const engine = $('#s-engine').value, ch = $('#s-ch').value;
  const body = {
    id: $('#s-id').value.trim(), name: $('#s-name').value.trim(), engine,
    repo: $('#s-repo').value.trim(), channel: ch, keystore: $('#s-ks').value.trim(),
    cli_upstream_id: Number($('#s-cli').value),
  };
  if (ch === 'tag') body.tag = $('#s-tag').value.trim();
  if ($('#s-re').value.trim()) body.asset_regex = $('#s-re').value.trim();
  const r = await act('POST', '/api/sources', body, 'Source created');
  return r.id;
}
$('#s-ch').addEventListener('change', e => { $('#s-tag-f').hidden = e.target.value !== 'tag'; });
$('#s-engine').addEventListener('change', e => {
  const mod = e.target.value !== 'morphe';
  $('[data-repo-label]').textContent = mod ? 'Module repo' : 'Patches repo';
  $('#s-re').required = mod;
  $('#s-re-h').textContent = mod ? 'Required: must match exactly one asset of the module release.' : 'Optional for Morphe (default \\.mpp$).';
  $('#s-ks').value = e.target.value === 'npatch' ? 'fiorenmas.ks' : mod ? 'lspatch-signing.p12' : 'morphe.keystore';
});
$('#a-src').addEventListener('change', async e => {
  if (e.target.value !== '__new') return;
  try { await fillSources(await newSource()); } catch (_) { await fillSources(); }
});
$('#add').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  try {
    await fillSources();
    $('#a-grp').innerHTML = html`${state.catalog.groups.map(g => html`<option value="${g.id}">${g.name}</option>`)}<option value="">Ungrouped</option>`;
    if (await ask($('#dlg-add')) !== 'ok' || $('#a-src').value === '__new') return;
    const body = { source_id: $('#a-src').value, package: $('#a-pkg').value.trim(),
      group_id: $('#a-grp').value ? Number($('#a-grp').value) : null };
    if ($('#a-name').value.trim()) body.display_name = $('#a-name').value.trim();
    await act('POST', '/api/items', body, 'Added; the icon appears after the next fetch');
    await load();
  } catch (_) { /* shown */ }
});
```

## Round 2 packages

Round 2 starts after the round-1 gate and reviewer sign-off (execution plan §2).

### P7 CI — headless commands, CI compose override, workflow

Spec: ci §1–§8 (L1230–1382), core §1 L207, core §3 L241, §0.5 L114, L125. Owner files (round 2, impl-ci): `local/webui/ci.py`, `local/webui/tests/test_ci.py`, `local/webui/tests/fixtures/ci/`, `local/docker-compose.ci.yml`, `.github/workflows/build.yml`. Depends on round 1: P1 (`run_inline`, `boot`), P2 (`upstream.check`, `catalog.seed_text`, seed import), P3 (compose env names X11, amd64 image), P4 (build handler, `artifacts`/`build_jobs` tables).

#### Task 7.1: `ci.py` labels, plan and export-seed

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/ci.py`
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_ci.py`
- Create: `local/webui/tests/fixtures/ci/release-all.json` (GitHub release JSON with `assets[].{name,label}`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P1 `db.conn()`, `db.DATA` (X4), `settings.github_token()`; P2 `upstream.check(upstream_id)` (updates the `upstream` row), `catalog.seed_text()` (X8); `__main__` calls `boot(start=False)` (X3) then `ci.main(argv)`.
- Produces:
  - `ci.label(name: str, app_version: str, tag: str) -> str` = `"<name> (<app_version>, patches <tag>)"`.
  - `ci.label_tag(label: str | None) -> str | None` (regex `, patches (\S+)\)$`).
  - `ci.asset_name(source_id: str, key: str, variant: str) -> str` = `"<source_id>-<key>-<variant>.apk"`.
  - `ci.CiFatal(Exception)`.
  - `ci.fetch_release(repo: str, token: str) -> dict[str, str | None]` (asset name → label; 404 → `{}`; 401/403/429 → `CiFatal`; patched in tests).
  - `ci.stale_items(source_id: str, assets: dict, latest_tag: str | None, force: bool) -> list[int]`.
  - `ci.plan(source: str = "all", force: bool = False) -> list[str]`.
  - `ci.main(argv: list[str]) -> int` with subcommands `ci-plan [--source <id>|all] [--force]`, `ci-build <source_id> [--force]`, `export-seed`.

**Behavior:**
- `plan`: sources in scope = sources with ≥ 1 enabled item, ordered by id, filtered by `--source` (unknown id → `CiFatal("unknown source <id>")`). For each distinct `source.upstream_id`: `upstream.check(id)`; then read rows. Every checked upstream with `check_error` starting `token rejected` or `rate limited` → `CiFatal("github unavailable for every upstream")`. `assets = fetch_release(os.environ["GITHUB_REPOSITORY"], settings.github_token())` (missing env → `CiFatal`). Source with upstream `check_error` → skipped, stderr `warning: <source>: <check_error>`. Else `--force` or `stale_items(...)` non-empty → planned.
- `stale_items`: enabled items of the source; with `force` all of them; else an item is stale when any `variants[].name` has no asset `asset_name(source, key, name)` or `label_tag(label) != latest_tag`.
- `main('ci-plan')`: all work runs with stdout redirected to stderr; then exactly one line `json.dumps(list)` on the real stdout; return 0. `CiFatal` → message on stderr, stdout empty, return 1.
- `main('export-seed')`: `sys.stdout.write(catalog.seed_text())`, return 0.

**Constraints:**
- stdlib only; GitHub call via `urllib.request` with `Authorization: Bearer <token>` when the token is non-empty, `Accept: application/vnd.github+json`, timeout 30 s.
- No job.json, runner or upstream logic in `ci.py` (spec L1241).

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_ci.py'` exit 0 with `test_label_round_trip`, `test_label_tag_none`, `test_plan_asset_missing_builds`, `test_plan_label_tag_differs_builds`, `test_plan_all_equal_skips`, `test_plan_check_error_skips`, `test_plan_force_builds`, `test_plan_source_filter`, `test_plan_stdout_one_json_line` (captured stdout = one line, parses to a list), `test_plan_release_403_fatal` (fake `fetch_release` raises `CiFatal` → return 1, stdout empty), `test_plan_every_upstream_rate_limited_fatal`, `test_export_seed_prints_seed_text`, `test_assets_from_release_json`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the fixture and the failing tests**

`local/webui/tests/test_ci.py`:
```python
import contextlib
import io
import json
import pathlib
from unittest import mock

from webui import catalog, ci, db, jobs, upstream
from webui.tests import helpers

FIX = pathlib.Path(__file__).parent / 'fixtures' / 'ci'
V = lambda *names: json.dumps([{'name': n, 'abi': n, 'download': {}, 'exclude_splits': None, 'strip_libs': None}
                               for n in names])


def seed():
    with db.tx() as c:
        for uid, kind, repo, tag in ((1, 'patches', 'MorpheApp/morphe-patches', 'v1.21.1'),
                                     (2, 'patches', 'crimera/piko', 'v3.0.0'),
                                     (3, 'cli', 'MorpheApp/morphe-desktop', 'v1.18.1')):
            c.execute('INSERT INTO upstream(id,kind,repo,channel,tag,asset_regex,latest_tag,downloaded_tag) '
                      "VALUES (?,?,?,'stable',NULL,'x',?,?)", (uid, kind, repo, tag, tag))
        for sid, uid in (('morphe', 1), ('piko', 2)):
            c.execute("INSERT INTO source(id,name,upstream_id,cli_upstream_id,engine,keystore) "
                      "VALUES (?,?,?,3,'morphe','morphe.keystore')", (sid, sid, uid))
        for pkg in ('com.google.android.youtube', 'com.twitter.android', 'com.reddit.frontpage'):
            c.execute('INSERT INTO app(package,display_name) VALUES (?,?)', (pkg, pkg))
        for iid, sid, pkg, key, variants, enabled in (
                (1, 'morphe', 'com.google.android.youtube', 'youtube', V('universal', 'arm64-v8a'), 1),
                (2, 'piko', 'com.twitter.android', 'twitter', V('universal'), 1),
                (3, 'morphe', 'com.reddit.frontpage', 'reddit', V('universal'), 0)):
            c.execute('INSERT INTO item(id,source_id,package,key,display_name,group_id,position,enabled,version_mode,'
                      "version_pin,allow_nearest,variants,patch_overrides,engine_options) "
                      "VALUES (?,?,?,?,NULL,NULL,?,?,'auto',NULL,0,?,'{}',NULL)", (iid, sid, pkg, key, iid, enabled, variants))


def release():
    return ci._assets(json.loads((FIX / 'release-all.json').read_text()))


class CiPlanTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        seed()
        self.check_errors = {}

        def fake_check(uid):
            with db.tx() as c:
                c.execute('UPDATE upstream SET check_error=? WHERE id=?', (self.check_errors.get(uid), uid))
        patches = [mock.patch.object(upstream, 'check', side_effect=fake_check),
                   mock.patch.object(ci, 'fetch_release', side_effect=lambda repo, token: release()),
                   mock.patch.dict('os.environ', {'GITHUB_REPOSITORY': 'FiorenMas/Relume'})]
        started = [p.start() for p in patches]
        for p in patches:
            self.addCleanup(p.stop)
        self.fetch = started[1]

    def main(self, *argv):
        out, err = io.StringIO(), io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(err):
            rc = ci.main(list(argv))
        return rc, out.getvalue(), err.getvalue()

    def test_label_round_trip(self):
        lbl = ci.label('morphe-youtube-universal.apk', '19.47.53', 'v1.21.1')
        self.assertEqual(lbl, 'morphe-youtube-universal.apk (19.47.53, patches v1.21.1)')
        self.assertEqual(ci.label_tag(lbl), 'v1.21.1')

    def test_label_tag_none(self):
        self.assertIsNone(ci.label_tag(None))
        self.assertIsNone(ci.label_tag('youtube-morphe.apk'))

    def test_assets_from_release_json(self):
        a = release()
        self.assertEqual(len(a), 4)
        self.assertIsNone(a['youtube-morphe.apk'])

    def test_plan_label_tag_differs_builds(self):
        self.assertEqual(ci.plan(), ['piko'])

    def test_plan_all_equal_skips(self):
        self.assertNotIn('morphe', ci.plan())

    def test_plan_asset_missing_builds(self):
        a = release()
        del a['morphe-youtube-arm64-v8a.apk']
        self.fetch.side_effect = lambda repo, token: a
        self.assertEqual(ci.plan(), ['morphe', 'piko'])

    def test_plan_check_error_skips(self):
        self.check_errors = {2: 'not found'}
        rc, out, err = self.main('ci-plan')
        self.assertEqual((rc, out), (0, '[]\n'))
        self.assertIn('warning: piko: not found', err)

    def test_plan_force_builds(self):
        self.assertEqual(ci.plan(force=True), ['morphe', 'piko'])

    def test_plan_source_filter(self):
        self.assertEqual(ci.plan('morphe', True), ['morphe'])
        self.assertEqual(ci.plan('morphe', False), [])

    def test_plan_stdout_one_json_line(self):
        rc, out, err = self.main('ci-plan', '--source', 'all', '--force')
        self.assertEqual(rc, 0)
        self.assertEqual(out.count('\n'), 1)
        self.assertEqual(json.loads(out), ['morphe', 'piko'])

    def test_plan_release_403_fatal(self):
        self.fetch.side_effect = ci.CiFatal('GitHub 403 on release "all"')
        rc, out, err = self.main('ci-plan')
        self.assertEqual((rc, out), (1, ''))
        self.assertIn('403', err)

    def test_plan_every_upstream_rate_limited_fatal(self):
        self.check_errors = {1: 'rate limited until 2026-10-19T10:00:00Z', 2: 'token rejected'}
        rc, out, err = self.main('ci-plan')
        self.assertEqual((rc, out), (1, ''))

    def test_export_seed_prints_seed_text(self):
        with mock.patch.object(catalog, 'seed_text', return_value='{"sources": []}\n'):
            rc, out, err = self.main('export-seed')
        self.assertEqual((rc, out), (0, '{"sources": []}\n'))
```

Expected: `ImportError: cannot import name 'ci' from 'webui'`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/ci.py`

```python
"""CI subcommands (spec ci 4). Called by webui.__main__ after boot(): schema + seed import + register() only."""
import argparse
import contextlib
import json
import os
import re
import shutil
import sys
import urllib.error
import urllib.request

from . import db, jobs, settings

GITHUB_API = 'https://api.github.com'
_TAG = re.compile(r', patches (\S+)\)$')
_FATAL_CHECK = ('token rejected', 'rate limited')


class CiFatal(Exception):
    pass


def label(name, app_version, tag):
    return f'{name} ({app_version}, patches {tag})'


def label_tag(lbl):
    m = _TAG.search(lbl or '')
    return m.group(1) if m else None


def asset_name(source_id, key, variant):
    return f'{source_id}-{key}-{variant}.apk'


def _assets(release):
    return {a['name']: a.get('label') for a in release.get('assets', [])}


def fetch_release(repo, token):
    headers = {'Accept': 'application/vnd.github+json'}
    if token:
        headers['Authorization'] = f'Bearer {token}'
    req = urllib.request.Request(f'{GITHUB_API}/repos/{repo}/releases/tags/all', headers=headers)
    try:
        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=30) as r:
            return _assets(json.load(r))
    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:
        if e.code == 404:
            return {}
        raise CiFatal(f'GitHub {e.code} on release "all" of {repo}')
    except OSError as e:
        raise CiFatal(f'GitHub unreachable: {e}')


def _repo():
    repo = os.environ.get('GITHUB_REPOSITORY')
    if not repo:
        raise CiFatal('GITHUB_REPOSITORY is not set')
    return repo


def _sources(source):
    rows = db.conn().execute('SELECT DISTINCT s.id, s.upstream_id, s.cli_upstream_id FROM source s '
                             'JOIN item i ON i.source_id = s.id AND i.enabled = 1 ORDER BY s.id').fetchall()
    if source != 'all':
        rows = [r for r in rows if r['id'] == source]
        if not rows:
            raise CiFatal(f'unknown source {source} (or it has no enabled item)')
    return rows


def stale_items(source_id, assets, latest_tag, force):
    out = []
    for r in db.conn().execute('SELECT id, key, variants FROM item WHERE source_id=? AND enabled=1 ORDER BY id',
                               (source_id,)):
        names = [asset_name(source_id, r['key'], v['name']) for v in json.loads(r['variants'] or '[]')]
        if force or any(n not in assets or label_tag(assets[n]) != latest_tag for n in names):
            out.append(r['id'])
    return out


def plan(source='all', force=False):
    from . import upstream
    rows = _sources(source)
    uids = sorted({r['upstream_id'] for r in rows})
    for uid in uids:
        upstream.check(uid)
    ups = {u: dict(db.conn().execute('SELECT latest_tag, check_error FROM upstream WHERE id=?', (u,)).fetchone())
           for u in uids}
    if uids and all((ups[u]['check_error'] or '').startswith(_FATAL_CHECK) for u in uids):
        raise CiFatal('GitHub refused every upstream check')
    assets = fetch_release(_repo(), settings.github_token())
    out = []
    for r in rows:
        u = ups[r['upstream_id']]
        if u['check_error']:
            print(f"warning: {r['id']}: {u['check_error']}", file=sys.stderr)
        elif force or stale_items(r['id'], assets, u['latest_tag'], False):
            out.append(r['id'])
    return out


def main(argv):
    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog='python3 -m webui')
    sub = p.add_subparsers(dest='cmd', required=True)
    a = sub.add_parser('ci-plan')
    a.add_argument('--source', default='all')
    a.add_argument('--force', action='store_true')
    b = sub.add_parser('ci-build')
    b.add_argument('source')
    b.add_argument('--force', action='store_true')
    sub.add_parser('export-seed')
    args = p.parse_args(argv)
    if args.cmd == 'export-seed':
        from . import catalog
        sys.stdout.write(catalog.seed_text())
        return 0
    try:
        if args.cmd == 'ci-plan':
            out = sys.stdout
            with contextlib.redirect_stdout(sys.stderr):
                result = plan(args.source, args.force)
            out.write(json.dumps(result) + '\n')
            return 0
        return build(args.source, args.force)  # build() is added in Task 7.2
    except CiFatal as e:
        print(f'error: {e}', file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
```

#### Task 7.2: `ci-build` publish and exit code

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/ci.py`
- Test: `local/webui/tests/test_ci.py` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P1 `jobs.run_inline(kind, target, trigger, options=None) -> dict` (job row with `id`, `status`); P4 tables `build_jobs(app_version, patches_version)`, `artifacts(job_id, variant, path)`.
- Produces: `ci.build(source_id: str, force: bool = False) -> int` (0 all built ok, 1 any failure or fatal); `/data/release/<asset name>` files; `/data/release/upload.list` lines `<asset name>#<label>`.

**Behavior:**
- Unknown source → stderr, return 1.
- `run_inline('upstream', {"upstream_id": source.upstream_id}, 'manual')` and, when `cli_upstream_id` is set and differs, the same for it; no `options` argument (spec L1266: no rebuild chaining). A failed pull does not stop the run; the builds then fail `upstream_missing` in P4's handler.
- Items: `stale_items(source, assets, latest_tag, force)`; `assets` from `fetch_release` only when not `force` (`CiFatal` → return 1).
- Per item: `row = run_inline('build', {"item_id": id}, 'manual')`; print `/data/logs/<row id>.log` to stdout (missing file → one line `no log`); `status == "success"` → for every `artifacts` row of that job copy `path` to `DATA/release/<asset_name(source, item.key, variant)>` and append `<name>#<label(name, app_version, patches_version)>\n` to `upload.list`.
- Return 0 when every build row is `success`, else 1 (after publishing successes).

**Constraints:**
- `shutil.copyfile`; release dir created with `mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)`.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_ci.py'` exit 0 incl. `test_build_publishes_success_only` (two items, fake `run_inline` succeeds for one, fails for the other → one file in release dir, `upload.list` == exactly `"<name>#<ci.label(name,'19.47.53','v1.21.1')>\n"`, return 1), `test_build_passes_no_options` (every fake `run_inline` call has no `options` argument), `test_build_pulls_source_and_cli_upstream`, `test_build_force_skips_release_fetch`, `test_build_prints_job_log`.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — append to `local/webui/tests/test_ci.py`

```python
class CiBuildTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        seed()
        with db.tx() as c:  # second enabled morphe item (reddit) that will fail
            c.execute("UPDATE item SET enabled=1, variants=? WHERE id=3", (V('universal'),))
            c.execute("UPDATE item SET variants=? WHERE id=1", (V('universal'),))
        self.calls, self.fail_items = [], {3}

        def fake_run_inline(*args, **kwargs):
            self.calls.append((args, kwargs))
            kind, target, trigger = args
            status = 'failed' if target.get('item_id') in self.fail_items else 'success'
            with db.tx() as c:
                jid = c.execute('INSERT INTO jobs(kind,status,trigger,target,options,created_at) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                                (kind, status, trigger, json.dumps(target), '{}', db.utcnow())).lastrowid
                if kind == 'build':
                    iid = target['item_id']
                    c.execute('INSERT INTO build_jobs(job_id,item_id,app_version,patches_version,cli_version) '
                              "VALUES (?,?,'19.47.53','v1.21.1','v1.18.1')", (jid, iid))
                    if status == 'success':
                        f = db.DATA / f'art-{jid}.apk'

                        c.execute('INSERT INTO artifacts(job_id,item_id,variant,abi,package,version_name,version_code,'
                                  'patches_version,signer_sha256,path,size,sha256,created_at) '
                                  "VALUES (?,?,'universal','universal','p','19.47.53',1,'v1.21.1','s',?,3,'h',?)",
                                  (jid, iid, str(f), db.utcnow()))
            (db.DATA / 'logs' / f'{jid}.log').write_text(f'10:00:00 log of job {jid}\n')
            return {'id': jid, 'status': status}

        patches = [mock.patch.object(jobs, 'run_inline', side_effect=fake_run_inline),
                   mock.patch.object(ci, 'fetch_release', side_effect=lambda repo, token: {}),
                   mock.patch.dict('os.environ', {'GITHUB_REPOSITORY': 'o/r'})]
        started = [p.start() for p in patches]
        for p in patches:
            self.addCleanup(p.stop)
        self.fetch = started[1]

    def build(self, *argv):
        out = io.StringIO()
        with contextlib.redirect_stdout(out), contextlib.redirect_stderr(io.StringIO()):
            rc = ci.main(['ci-build', *argv])
        return rc, out.getvalue()

    def test_build_publishes_success_only(self):

        self.assertEqual(rc, 1)
        rel = db.DATA / 'release'
        self.assertEqual(sorted(p.name for p in rel.iterdir()), ['morphe-youtube-universal.apk', 'upload.list'])
        name = 'morphe-youtube-universal.apk'
        self.assertEqual((rel / 'upload.list').read_text(), f"{name}#{ci.label(name, '19.47.53', 'v1.21.1')}\n")
        self.assertEqual((rel / name).read_bytes(), b'APK')

    def test_build_passes_no_options(self):

        self.assertTrue(self.calls)

        self.assertEqual([a for a, k in self.calls[:2]],
                         [('upstream', {'upstream_id': 1}, 'manual'), ('upstream', {'upstream_id': 3}, 'manual')])
        self.assertEqual([a[1] for a, k in self.calls[2:]], [{'item_id': 1}, {'item_id': 3}])

    def test_build_force_skips_release_fetch(self):
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — append to `local/webui/ci.py`

```python
def _publish(source_id, item_id, job_id, rel):
    c = db.conn()
    key = c.execute('SELECT key FROM item WHERE id=?', (item_id,)).fetchone()['key']
    bj = c.execute('SELECT app_version, patches_version FROM build_jobs WHERE job_id=?', (job_id,)).fetchone()
    with open(rel / 'upload.list', 'a', encoding='utf-8') as f:
        for a in c.execute('SELECT variant, path FROM artifacts WHERE job_id=? ORDER BY id', (job_id,)):
            name = asset_name(source_id, key, a['variant'])
            shutil.copyfile(a['path'], rel / name)
            f.write(f"{name}#{label(name, bj['app_version'], bj['patches_version'])}\n")


def build(source_id, force=False):
    """Spec ci 4.2: pull the source and CLI upstreams, build stale items, publish successes, exit 1 on any failure."""
    c = db.conn()
    src = c.execute('SELECT id, upstream_id, cli_upstream_id FROM source WHERE id=?', (source_id,)).fetchone()
    if src is None:
        print(f'error: unknown source {source_id}', file=sys.stderr)
        return 1
    jobs.run_inline('upstream', {'upstream_id': src['upstream_id']}, 'manual')
    if src['cli_upstream_id'] and src['cli_upstream_id'] != src['upstream_id']:
        jobs.run_inline('upstream', {'upstream_id': src['cli_upstream_id']}, 'manual')
    latest = c.execute('SELECT latest_tag FROM upstream WHERE id=?', (src['upstream_id'],)).fetchone()['latest_tag']
    assets = {} if force else fetch_release(_repo(), settings.github_token())
    rel = db.DATA / 'release'
    rel.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    ok = True
    for item_id in stale_items(source_id, assets, latest, force):
        row = jobs.run_inline('build', {'item_id': item_id}, 'manual')
        log = db.DATA / 'logs' / f"{row['id']}.log"
        sys.stdout.write(log.read_text(encoding='utf-8', errors='replace') if log.exists() else 'no log\n')
        sys.stdout.flush()
        if row['status'] == 'success':
            _publish(source_id, item_id, row['id'], rel)
        else:
            ok = False
    return 0 if ok else 1
```

#### Task 7.3: CI compose override and workflow

**Files:**

- Produces: workflow `Build` (jobs `plan`, `build`), override file.

**Behavior:**
- Workflow = spec L1282–1335 verbatim except: both `$FORCE` arguments are written `${FORCE:+"$FORCE"}` (actionlint SC2086; empty → no argument, same behaviour).
- Override: `services.app.command: ["sleep","infinity"]`, `services.app.healthcheck.disable: true`.

**Constraints:**
- `actionlint` is a host tool (not in the test image).

**Acceptance criteria:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Run the checks before the files exist (red)**

Run: `actionlint .github/workflows/build.yml; echo RC=$?`

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the compose override**

`local/docker-compose.ci.yml`:
```yaml
# CI only (spec ci §3): app stays idle so ci-build runs through `exec`; sidecars start as usual.
services:
  app:
    command: ["sleep", "infinity"]
    healthcheck:
      disable: true
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the workflow**

`.github/workflows/build.yml`:
```yaml
name: Build
on:
  schedule:
    - cron: "0 9 * * *"
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      source: {description: "source id or all", type: string, default: all}
      force:  {description: "build even if up to date", type: boolean, default: false}
permissions:
  contents: write
concurrency:
  group: build
env:
  SOURCE: ${{ inputs.source || 'all' }}
  FORCE: ${{ inputs.force && '--force' || '' }}
jobs:
  plan:
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    outputs:
      sources: ${{ steps.plan.outputs.sources }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7.0.1
      - id: plan
        env: {GITHUB_TOKEN: "${{ github.token }}"}
        run: |
          printf '%s' "$GITHUB_TOKEN" > "$RUNNER_TEMP/github_token"
          out=$(docker run --rm -e GITHUB_REPOSITORY -e PYTHONPATH=/repo/local \
            -v "$PWD:/repo:ro" -v "$RUNNER_TEMP/github_token:/run/secrets/github_token:ro" \
            -v "$RUNNER_TEMP/data:/data" python:3.12-slim \
            python3 -m webui ci-plan --source "$SOURCE" ${FORCE:+"$FORCE"})
          echo "sources=$out" >> "$GITHUB_OUTPUT"
  build:
    needs: plan
    if: needs.plan.outputs.sources != '[]'
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    strategy:
      fail-fast: false
      matrix:
        source: ${{ fromJSON(needs.plan.outputs.sources) }}
    env:
      RELUME_NETWORK_MODE: host
      GH_TOKEN: ${{ github.token }}
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v7.0.1
      - run: echo "RELUME_GITHUB_TOKEN_FILE=$RUNNER_TEMP/github_token" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
      - run: |
          printf '%s' "$GH_TOKEN" > "$RELUME_GITHUB_TOKEN_FILE"
          mkdir -p local/data
          echo "RELUME_UID=$(id -u)" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"; echo "RELUME_GID=$(id -g)" >> "$GITHUB_ENV"
      - run: docker compose -f local/docker-compose.yml -f local/docker-compose.ci.yml up -d --build
      - run: docker compose -f local/docker-compose.yml -f local/docker-compose.ci.yml exec -T app python3 -m webui ci-build "${{ matrix.source }}" ${FORCE:+"$FORCE"}
      - if: ${{ !cancelled() && hashFiles('local/data/release/upload.list') != '' }}
        working-directory: local/data/release
        run: xargs -d '\n' -a upload.list gh release upload all --clobber
```

### P8 Xposed items — engine branch and catalog validation

Spec: xposed §1–§12 (L1389–1597), catalog §4 L497, build §4 step 5e L786. Owner files (round 2, impl-xposed): `src/build/{build-item,utils,test-build-item}.sh`, `src/build/test-fixtures/`, `local/webui/catalog.py`, `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_xposed.py`. Reference values: `git show 9fd15dc:src/build/Xposed.sh` (deleted by P11) and `git show 9fd15dc:src/build/utils.sh` lines 797–843 (`lspatch`/`npatch`, removed from `utils.sh` by P3 in round 1).

#### Task 8.1: Runner engine branch and keystore table

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/build/utils.sh` (`patch_variant` `lspatch|npatch)` branch)
- Modify: `src/build/build-item.sh` (`keystore_creds` table; `engine_sigbypass`/`engine_injectdex` reads)
- Modify: `src/build/test-build-item.sh` (replace the `runner_engine_stub` case with engine cases)
- Modify: `src/build/test-fixtures/bin/java` (engine mode)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes (P3, merged plan Tasks P3.2/P3.3, X14): `patch_variant <in> <out> <strip>` with locals `in`, `out`, `strip` and globals `engine`, `cli_jar`, `patches_files`, `keystore`, `RELUME_REPO`; failure contract = set `FAIL_CODE` (empty = `patch_failed`) and `LAST_ERR`, return 1, the runner prints the one `::fail::${FAIL_CODE:-patch_failed}::$LAST_ERR` line; `build-item.sh` reads fields with `j() { jq -r "$1 // empty" "$job"; }` (`$job` = absolute job.json path); harness `new_env`, `make_job '<jq filter>'`, `run_runner`, `fail_lines`, `pass`, `fail`, counter `fails`; shims log to `$SHIM_LOG`.
- Produces: `keystore_creds <file>` prints `storepass alias aliaspass`, rc 1 for unknown; `patch_variant` handles `engine=lspatch|npatch` per spec L1519–1533.

**Behavior:**
- Command: `java -jar "$cli_jar" "$in" -k "$RELUME_REPO/src/keystore/$keystore" <storepass> <alias> <aliaspass> -l <sigbypass> [--injectdex] -m <file>… -o "${out%.apk}.d" -f`; the `.d` dir is created empty first; exactly one `*.apk` in it → moved to `$out`, dir removed; 0 or ≥ 2 → `LAST_ERR="engine produced <n> apks"` (code `patch_failed`); engine non-zero exit → `LAST_ERR="engine exit <rc>"` (code `patch_failed`).
- Keystore table: `lspatch-signing.p12 → morphe fiorenmas morphe`, `fiorenmas.ks → fiorenmas morphe fiorenmas`; other file → `FAIL_CODE=internal`, `LAST_ERR="unknown keystore"`.
- `engine_sigbypass` must match `^[0-4]$`, else `FAIL_CODE=internal`, `LAST_ERR="bad engine_options"`; `engine_injectdex == true` adds `--injectdex`.

**Constraints:**
- No `eval`; every expansion quoted; argv array; shellcheck clean.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_SH src/build/test-build-item.sh` prints `PASS xposed_lspatch_argv`, `PASS xposed_npatch_argv`, `PASS xposed_0_apks`, `PASS xposed_2_apks`, `PASS xposed_unknown_keystore`, `PASS xposed_bad_sigbypass`, no `runner_engine_stub` line, exit 0.
- [ ] `shellcheck -S warning src/build/build-item.sh src/build/utils.sh src/build/test-build-item.sh src/build/test-fixtures/bin/java` exit 0; `grep -nw eval src/build/build-item.sh src/build/utils.sh` prints nothing.

```bash
*.apk)
	# Engine mode (LSPatch/NPatch): <input.apk> -k ... -o <dir> -f
	odir="" prev=""
	for a in "$@"; do
		[ "$prev" = "-o" ] && odir=$a
		prev=$a
	done
	for ((i = 1; i <= ${SHIM_ENGINE_APKS:-1}; i++)); do
		cp "$1" "$odir/in-$i-lspatched.apk"
	done
	exit "${SHIM_ENGINE_RC:-0}"
	;;
```

```bash
XP_LS='.engine="lspatch" | .engine_options={"sigbypass":3,"injectdex":true} | .keystore="lspatch-signing.p12" | .recipe.variants=[.recipe.variants[0] | .name="arm64-v8a" | .abi="arm64-v8a" | .strip_libs=null]'
```

```bash
# Keystore credentials for Xposed engines (spec xposed 5.3; values from utils.sh:811,835 at 9fd15dc, already public).
keystore_creds() {
	case "$1" in
	lspatch-signing.p12) echo "morphe fiorenmas morphe" ;;
	fiorenmas.ks) echo "fiorenmas morphe fiorenmas" ;;
	*) return 1 ;;
	esac
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Replace the engine stub in `patch_variant`** — `src/build/utils.sh`, the `lspatch|npatch)` branch becomes:

```bash
	lspatch | npatch)
		# spec xposed 5.3: module(s) embedded by the engine; output = the only APK in an empty dir
		local creds odir a rc
		local -a ks cmd apks
		: "${engine_sigbypass:=}" "${engine_injectdex:=false}" # set by build-item.sh from job.json engine_options
		if ! creds=$(keystore_creds "$keystore"); then
			FAIL_CODE=internal
			LAST_ERR="unknown keystore"
			return 1
		fi
		if [[ ! "$engine_sigbypass" =~ ^[0-4]$ ]]; then
			FAIL_CODE=internal
			LAST_ERR="bad engine_options"
			return 1
		fi
		read -r -a ks <<<"$creds"
		odir="${out%.apk}.d"
		rm -rf "$odir"
		mkdir -p "$odir"
		cmd=(java -jar "$cli_jar" "$in" -k "$RELUME_REPO/src/keystore/$keystore" "${ks[0]}" "${ks[1]}" "${ks[2]}"
			-l "$engine_sigbypass")
		[ "$engine_injectdex" = "true" ] && cmd+=(--injectdex)
		for a in "${patches_files[@]}"; do
			cmd+=(-m "$a")
		done
		cmd+=(-o "$odir" -f)
		"${cmd[@]}"
		rc=$?
		if [ "$rc" -ne 0 ]; then
			LAST_ERR="engine exit $rc"
			return 1
		fi
		shopt -s nullglob
		apks=("$odir"/*.apk)
		shopt -u nullglob
		if [ "${#apks[@]}" -ne 1 ]; then
			LAST_ERR="engine produced ${#apks[@]} apks"
			return 1
		fi
		mv "${apks[0]}" "$out"
		rm -rf "$odir"
		;;
```

#### Task 8.2: Catalog Xposed validation

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/catalog.py`
- Create: `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_xposed.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P2 route handlers (X9), P1 `helpers.serve`/`helpers.http`/`helpers.login` (X4), tables `source.engine`, `source.cli_upstream_id`, `item.engine_options`, `catalog.job_input`, upstream pull handler `rebuild` chaining (P2).
- Produces (all raise `HttpError`): `catalog.validate_engine_options(engine: str, value) -> dict | None`, `catalog.check_xposed_variants(variants) -> None`, `catalog.xposed_item_rules(body: dict, item_id: int | None = None) -> None` (normalizes `body['engine_options']` in place), `catalog.xposed_source_rules(body: dict, source_id: str | None = None) -> None`, `catalog.require_patches_item(item_id) -> None` (409 `{"error":"not a patches item"}`), `catalog.check_version_mode(item_id, body) -> None` (400 `{"error":"auto version not available for module items"}`). Each is called as the first statement of the P2 handler bound to the route it guards (Step 3 table).

**Behavior:**
- `validate_engine_options`: `morphe` → value must be `None` (else 400 `engine_options must be null for patches items`); `lspatch`/`npatch` → dict with exactly keys `sigbypass` (int, not bool, 0–3 lspatch, 0–4 npatch) and `injectdex` (bool); missing/unknown key or wrong type → 400.
- `check_xposed_variants`: each variant `exclude_splits` and `strip_libs` must be `null`, `download.type` ∈ `apk|bundle` (`bundle_extract` → 400).
- Applied in `POST/PUT /api/items` for items of `lspatch`/`npatch` sources; `POST/PUT /api/sources` with `engine != 'morphe'` requires `asset_regex` (400 `asset_regex required for module sources`) and stores the upstream row with `kind='module'` (P2 dedup function).
- Patches routes: item's source engine ≠ `morphe` → 409 before any bundle lookup. Version route: `version_mode == "auto"` for such items → 400.

**Constraints:**
- No change to `upstream.py` (P2's; X10). No new table, column, route or job kind (spec L1417).

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `T_PY 'test_catalog_xposed.py'` exit 0 with `test_auto_rejected`, `test_sigbypass_4_lspatch_rejected_npatch_ok`, `test_engine_options_shape`, `test_strip_libs_rejected`, `test_bundle_extract_rejected`, `test_morphe_item_engine_options_must_be_null`, `test_patches_routes_409`, `test_module_source_needs_asset_regex`, `test_job_input_xposed` (`engine`, module path in `patches_files`, engine jar in `cli_jar`, `options_file_json` null). The two module-row upstream cases of spec L1590 (ambiguous asset, rebuild of exactly that source's items) are P2 tests (X10).
