- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** — `local/webui/tests/test_catalog_xposed.py`

```python
import json

from webui import catalog, db
from webui.tests import helpers

XV = {'name': 'arm64-v8a', 'abi': 'arm64-v8a', 'exclude_splits': None, 'strip_libs': None,
      'download': {'kind': 'apkmirror', 'type': 'bundle', 'arch': 'arm64-v8a', 'dpi': '320-640dpi',
                   'minver': 'Android 11+', 'url': None}}


class XposedCatalogTest(helpers.DataTestCase):
    def setUp(self):
        super().setUp()
        with db.tx() as c:
            for uid, kind, repo, tag in ((1, 'patches', 'MorpheApp/morphe-patches', 'v1.21.1'),
                                         (2, 'cli', 'MorpheApp/morphe-desktop', 'v1.18.1'),
                                         (3, 'module', 'gnadgnaoh/NexAlloy', 'v1.1'),
                                         (4, 'cli', 'JingMatrix/LSPatch', 'v1.2'),
                                         (5, 'cli', '7723mod/NPatch', 'v1.0.5')):
                c.execute('INSERT INTO upstream(id,kind,repo,channel,tag,asset_regex,latest_tag,downloaded_tag) '
                          "VALUES (?,?,?,'stable',NULL,'.*',?,?)", (uid, kind, repo, tag, tag))
            for sid, uid, cli, engine, ks in (('morphe', 1, 2, 'morphe', 'morphe.keystore'),
                                              ('nexalloy-lspatch', 3, 4, 'lspatch', 'lspatch-signing.p12'),
                                              ('nexalloy-npatch', 3, 5, 'npatch', 'fiorenmas.ks')):
                c.execute('INSERT INTO source(id,name,upstream_id,cli_upstream_id,engine,keystore) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?,?)',
                          (sid, sid, uid, cli, engine, ks))
            for pkg in ('com.facebook.katana', 'com.zhiliaoapp.musically', 'com.google.android.youtube'):
                c.execute('INSERT INTO app(package,display_name) VALUES (?,?)', (pkg, pkg))
            for iid, sid, pkg, key, eo, variants in (
                    (1, 'nexalloy-lspatch', 'com.facebook.katana', 'facebook-arm64-v8a',
                     '{"sigbypass":3,"injectdex":true}', json.dumps([XV])),
                    (2, 'morphe', 'com.google.android.youtube', 'youtube', None, '[]')):
                c.execute('INSERT INTO item(id,source_id,package,key,display_name,group_id,position,enabled,version_mode,'
                          "version_pin,allow_nearest,variants,patch_overrides,engine_options) "
                          "VALUES (?,?,?,?,NULL,NULL,?,1,'latest',NULL,0,?,'{}',?)", (iid, sid, pkg, key, iid, variants, eo))
        self.base = helpers.serve(self, ['catalog'])
        self.cookie = helpers.login(self.base)

    def call(self, method, path, body=None):
        st, _, raw = helpers.http(method, self.base + path, body, self.cookie)
        return st, json.loads(raw) if raw else None

    def test_auto_rejected(self):
        st, body = self.call('PUT', '/api/items/1/version', {'version_mode': 'auto', 'version_pin': None, 'allow_nearest': False})
        self.assertEqual((st, body), (400, {'error': 'auto version not available for module items'}))
        st, _ = self.call('PUT', '/api/items/1/version', {'version_mode': 'latest', 'version_pin': None, 'allow_nearest': False})
        self.assertLess(st, 300)

    def test_sigbypass_4_lspatch_rejected_npatch_ok(self):
        base = {'package': 'com.zhiliaoapp.musically', 'group_id': None, 'variants': [dict(XV, name='universal', abi='universal')],
                'engine_options': {'sigbypass': 4, 'injectdex': False}}
        st, body = self.call('POST', '/api/items', dict(base, source_id='nexalloy-lspatch'))
        self.assertEqual((st, body), (400, {'error': 'sigbypass must be 0-3 for lspatch'}))
        st, _ = self.call('POST', '/api/items', dict(base, source_id='nexalloy-npatch'))
        self.assertLess(st, 300)

    def test_engine_options_shape(self):
        for eo in (None, {'sigbypass': 1}, {'sigbypass': 1, 'injectdex': False, 'x': 1}, {'sigbypass': True, 'injectdex': False},
                   {'sigbypass': 1, 'injectdex': 'yes'}):
            st, _ = self.call('POST', '/api/items', {'source_id': 'nexalloy-npatch', 'package': 'com.zhiliaoapp.musically',
                                                     'group_id': None, 'variants': [XV], 'engine_options': eo})
            self.assertEqual(st, 400, eo)

    def test_strip_libs_rejected(self):
        st, body = self.call('PUT', '/api/items/1', {'variants': [dict(XV, strip_libs='arm64-v8a')]})
        self.assertEqual((st, body), (400, {'error': 'exclude_splits and strip_libs must be null for module items'}))

    def test_bundle_extract_rejected(self):
        v = dict(XV, download=dict(XV['download'], type='bundle_extract'))
        st, body = self.call('PUT', '/api/items/1', {'variants': [v]})
        self.assertEqual((st, body), (400, {'error': 'download.type must be apk or bundle for module items'}))

    def test_morphe_item_engine_options_must_be_null(self):
        st, body = self.call('PUT', '/api/items/2', {'engine_options': {'sigbypass': 0, 'injectdex': False}})
        self.assertEqual((st, body), (400, {'error': 'engine_options must be null for patches items'}))

    def test_patches_routes_409(self):
        self.assertEqual(self.call('GET', '/api/items/1/patches'), (409, {'error': 'not a patches item'}))
        self.assertEqual(self.call('PUT', '/api/items/1/patches', {'overrides': {}}), (409, {'error': 'not a patches item'}))

    def test_module_source_needs_asset_regex(self):
        body = {'id': 'revenge-lspatch', 'name': 'Revenge', 'engine': 'lspatch', 'repo': 'revenge-mod/revenge-xposed',
                'channel': 'stable', 'keystore': 'lspatch-signing.p12', 'cli_upstream_id': 4}
        st, err = self.call('POST', '/api/sources', body)
        self.assertEqual((st, err), (400, {'error': 'asset_regex required for module sources'}))
        st, _ = self.call('POST', '/api/sources', dict(body, asset_regex='^app-release\\.apk$'))
        self.assertLess(st, 300)
        kind = db.conn().execute("SELECT u.kind FROM source s JOIN upstream u ON u.id = s.upstream_id "
                                 "WHERE s.id = 'revenge-lspatch'").fetchone()['kind']
        self.assertEqual(kind, 'module')

    def test_job_input_xposed(self):
        mod = db.DATA / 'upstream' / '3' / 'v1.1'
        jar = db.DATA / 'upstream' / '4' / 'v1.2'
        mod.mkdir(parents=True)
        jar.mkdir(parents=True)
        (mod / 'NexAlloy-refactor-release-v1.1.apk').write_bytes(b'MOD')
        (jar / 'lspatch-v1.2-487-release.jar').write_bytes(b'JAR')
        j = catalog.job_input(1)
        self.assertEqual(j['engine'], 'lspatch')
        self.assertEqual(j['engine_options'], {'sigbypass': 3, 'injectdex': True})
        self.assertEqual(j['patches_files'], [str(mod / 'NexAlloy-refactor-release-v1.1.apk')])
        self.assertEqual(j['cli_jar'], str(jar / 'lspatch-v1.2-487-release.jar'))
        self.assertIsNone(j['options_file_json'])
        self.assertEqual((j['upstream_tag'], j['cli_tag'], j['keystore']), ('v1.1', 'v1.2', 'lspatch-signing.p12'))
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — append to `local/webui/catalog.py` (it already imports `db` and `HttpError`)

```python
# --- Xposed module items (P8, spec xposed 5.1) ---
_ENGINE_MAX = {'lspatch': 3, 'npatch': 4}


def _engine_of_source(source_id):
    r = db.conn().execute('SELECT engine FROM source WHERE id=?', (source_id,)).fetchone()
    return r['engine'] if r else None


def _engine_of_item(item_id):
    r = db.conn().execute('SELECT s.engine FROM item i JOIN source s ON s.id = i.source_id WHERE i.id=?',
                          (item_id,)).fetchone()
    return r['engine'] if r else None


def validate_engine_options(engine, value):
    if engine not in _ENGINE_MAX:
        if value is not None:
            raise HttpError(400, 'engine_options must be null for patches items')
        return None
    if not isinstance(value, dict) or set(value) != {'sigbypass', 'injectdex'}:
        raise HttpError(400, 'engine_options needs exactly sigbypass and injectdex')
    sb, inj = value['sigbypass'], value['injectdex']
    if isinstance(sb, bool) or not isinstance(sb, int) or not 0 <= sb <= _ENGINE_MAX[engine]:
        raise HttpError(400, f'sigbypass must be 0-{_ENGINE_MAX[engine]} for {engine}')
    if not isinstance(inj, bool):
        raise HttpError(400, 'injectdex must be true or false')
    return {'sigbypass': sb, 'injectdex': inj}


def check_xposed_variants(variants):
    for v in variants if isinstance(variants, list) else []:
        if not isinstance(v, dict):
            continue  # shape errors are answered by P2's variant validation
        if v.get('exclude_splits') is not None or v.get('strip_libs') is not None:
            raise HttpError(400, 'exclude_splits and strip_libs must be null for module items')
        if (v.get('download') or {}).get('type') not in ('apk', 'bundle'):
            raise HttpError(400, 'download.type must be apk or bundle for module items')


def xposed_item_rules(body, item_id=None):
    """POST /api/items (item_id None) and PUT /api/items/{id}; normalizes body['engine_options'] in place."""
    if not isinstance(body, dict):
        return
    if body.get('source_id') is not None:
        engine = _engine_of_source(body['source_id'])
    else:
        engine = _engine_of_item(item_id) if item_id is not None else None
    if engine is None:
        return  # unknown source or item: P2's own checks answer 400/404
    if item_id is None or 'engine_options' in body:
        body['engine_options'] = validate_engine_options(engine, body.get('engine_options'))
    if engine in _ENGINE_MAX and 'variants' in body:
        check_xposed_variants(body['variants'])


def xposed_source_rules(body, source_id=None):
    """POST /api/sources (source_id None) and PUT /api/sources/{id}."""
    if not isinstance(body, dict):
        return
    engine = body.get('engine') or (_engine_of_source(source_id) if source_id else 'morphe')
    if engine != 'morphe' and not body.get('asset_regex') and (source_id is None or 'repo' in body):
        raise HttpError(400, 'asset_regex required for module sources')


def require_patches_item(item_id):
    if _engine_of_item(item_id) in _ENGINE_MAX:
        raise HttpError(409, 'not a patches item')


def check_version_mode(item_id, body):
    if isinstance(body, dict) and body.get('version_mode') == 'auto' and _engine_of_item(item_id) in _ENGINE_MAX:
        raise HttpError(400, 'auto version not available for module items')
```

Then add one call as the first statement of each P2 handler bound to these routes in `catalog.ROUTES`:

| route | first statement |
|---|---|
| `POST /api/items` | `xposed_item_rules(req.json)` |
| `PUT /api/items/{id}` | `xposed_item_rules(req.json, req.params['id'])` |
| `POST /api/sources` | `xposed_source_rules(req.json)` |
| `PUT /api/sources/{id}` | `xposed_source_rules(req.json, req.params['id'])` |
| `GET /api/items/{id}/patches` | `require_patches_item(req.params['id'])` |
| `PUT /api/items/{id}/patches` | `require_patches_item(req.params['id'])` |
| `PUT /api/items/{id}/version` | `check_version_mode(req.params['id'], req.json)` |

### P9 Pages A — App detail, Builds (round 2; also owner of `app.css`, `app.js`, `test_ui_static.py` in round 2)

Spec: ui §2 App detail L1108–1132, Builds L1133–1147; ui §3 rows L1216–1217; xposed §8 L1565–1567; catalog §4.1 L504–513, §4.2 L514–521, §6 L576; core §8 L355; build §3.2 L733; §0.6 R3 L144, R4 L145. Frontend rules and shapes: see P6 header and `ui-api-shapes.md`. P10 sends any change request for `app.css`/`app.js` to impl-pages-a, who applies it and replies with the commit hash.

#### Task P9.1: App detail — header, installed-on, Patches and Options tabs, save bar

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/static/item.html`, `local/webui/static/item.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET /api/items/{id}`, `GET /api/items/{id}/patches`, `PUT /api/items/{id}/patches {overrides}`, `PUT /api/items/{id}/version`, `POST /api/items/{id}/build`, `POST /api/upstreams/{id}/pull {"rebuild":true}`, `GET /api/items/{id}/artifacts`, `GET /api/devices`; `app.js` helpers.
- Produces (inside `item.js`, used by P9.2): `S` state object (`item`, `patches` (GET body or null), `saved` (overrides map derived on load), `edit` (current overrides map), `version` (saved `{version_mode, version_pin, allow_nearest}`), `vEdit`), `dirty()`, `refreshSaveBar()`.

**Behavior:**
- `item.html` = `M/app-detail.html` lines 1–2 head (title set at runtime) + frontend head rules + body lines 171–223 ported; second style block `M/app-detail.html:149-156` into the page head. Containers: header `#hdr` (icon, name, source, badge, mono line), header buttons `#b-pull`, `#b-build`, download menu `#b-dl` (same menu pattern as the App list), header menu `<details class="dd">` with "Edit app…" (`#m-app`), "Refresh icon" (`#m-icon`), "Item settings…" (`#m-item`); installed-on footer `#inst` + link `/devices.html` "Manage devices"; tablist with tabs `t1` Patches (`#tcount`), `t2` Options (`#ocount`), `t3` Version, `t4` History, `t5` Module (hidden for Morphe items); panels `p1`–`p5`; patch list `#plist`, filter `#pf`, show segment `[name=pshow]` (`all|in|ex|opt`), counter `#pinc` / `#ptotal`, `[data-all]` buttons, `#reset`, file line `#pfiles`, diff `<pre class="log" id="diff">` with summary text "Pending change · saved settings"; options container `#opts`, raw JSON `<pre class="log" id="raw">`; save bar `<div class="card savebar" id="savebar" hidden>` with `#sbtxt`, `#discard`, `#save`, `#saverun`. Locked "required" patches (deferred F2) are not rendered; the text "Options are shared by every item from the morphe source" becomes "These options apply to this item only".
- Load: `GET /api/items/{id}` (id from `?id=`), then for Morphe items `GET /api/items/{id}/patches`; a 409/404 from patches, or `tag` null → panel text "Patch list unavailable — run Update upstream" and patch edits disabled (catalog §9).
- `saved` overrides = for each patch: `enabled` when `enabled !== default_enabled`; `options[key] = value` when `value !== default` (deep compare via `JSON.stringify`). `edit` starts as a deep copy.
- Patch row: switch (checked = effective enabled), name, description, tag text (`default on` / `default off` / `included by you` / `excluded by you`, `.chg` class when user-changed), "N options" link jumping to the fieldset when the patch has options. Counter `#pinc` = enabled count, `#ptotal` = patch count, tab text `n/total`. "All on/All off" set every switch; "Reset to defaults" sets `edit = {}`.
- Options tab: one fieldset per patch with options (`id="opt-<index>"`), legend = patch name, a "kept but unused" badge when the patch is off; per option a field by `type`: `values` non-empty → `<select>` with the preset values; boolean → switch; `int`/`long`/`float` (type name contains `int`, `long`, `float` or `double`, case-insensitive) → `<input type="number">` (`step="any"` for float/double); list types (type name contains `list` or `[]`) → `<textarea>` one value per line; otherwise text input. Badge with the type, "changed" badge when value ≠ default, hint `Default: <default>`. Colour picker (R3): a string option whose current value or default matches `^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}([0-9A-Fa-f]{2})?$` gets `<input type="color">` next to the text field; picker → text keeps the alpha prefix of an 8-digit value (`#AARRGGBB`: picker edits the last 6 digits); text → picker shows the RGB part.
- Diff `#diff` (textContent lines): `+ include <patch>`, `− exclude <patch>`, `~ <patch>.<key> = <JSON value>` for each difference between `edit` and `saved`, else `no unsaved changes`. Raw JSON `#raw` = `JSON.stringify([{patches: edit}], null, 2)`.
- Save bar: visible when `edit` ≠ `saved` or `vEdit` ≠ `version`; text `<n> unsaved change(s) · applies to next build`; Discard → reload; Save → `PUT …/patches {overrides: edit}` (when changed) then `PUT …/version` (when changed), toast `Saved`; Save & rebuild → same, then `POST /api/items/{id}/build`, toast `Saved · build queued`.
- Header: icon (`iconTile(item,'ico-lg')`), label, source badge, badge `<latest_tag> available` when `latest_tag !== downloaded_tag`, mono line `<package> · <repo> (<channel or tag>) · built <last_success.app_version> with <last_success.patches_version>` (or `not built yet`). Update upstream → pull with `rebuild:true` (title as on the App list); Rebuild → `POST …/build`; Download menu → newest job's artifacts.
- Installed on: `GET /api/devices`; for each device with an assignment of this item: online dot, device name, `installed.version_name` (or `not installed`), `(behind)` when `pending`; none → "Not assigned to any device".

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `node --check local/webui/static/item.js` exit 0; `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` exit 0.
- [ ] `grep -c 'type="color"\|colorInput' local/webui/static/item.js` ≥ 1; `grep -c 'patchName\|src/options\|shared by every item' local/webui/static/item.html local/webui/static/item.js` prints `0` for both files.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing check** — `ls local/webui/static/item.html` → `No such file or directory`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Markup** — `item.html` per Behavior.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/static/item.js` (part 1)

```js
import { $, $$, act, api, ask, fmt, html, iconTile, shell, toast } from '/static/app.js';

const ID = Number(new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('id'));
const COLOR = /^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}([0-9A-Fa-f]{2})?$/;
const same = (a, b) => JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b);
const clone = v => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(v));
export const S = { item: null, patches: null, saved: {}, edit: {}, version: null, vEdit: null };

function overridesFrom(p) {
  const out = {};
  for (const x of p.patches) {
    const o = {};
    if (x.enabled !== x.default_enabled) o.enabled = x.enabled;
    const opts = {};
    for (const op of x.options) if (!same(op.value, op.default)) opts[op.key] = op.value;
    if (Object.keys(opts).length) o.options = opts;
    if (Object.keys(o).length) out[x.name] = o;
  }
  return out;
}

const effEnabled = x => (S.edit[x.name] && 'enabled' in S.edit[x.name] ? S.edit[x.name].enabled : x.default_enabled);
const effValue = (x, op) => {
  const o = S.edit[x.name] && S.edit[x.name].options;
  return o && op.key in o ? o[op.key] : op.default;
};

function setEnabled(x, on) {
  const o = S.edit[x.name] || {};
  if (on === x.default_enabled) delete o.enabled; else o.enabled = on;
  if (Object.keys(o).length) S.edit[x.name] = o; else delete S.edit[x.name];
}

function setValue(x, op, v) {
  const o = S.edit[x.name] || {};
  const opts = o.options || {};
  if (same(v, op.default)) delete opts[op.key]; else opts[op.key] = v;
  if (Object.keys(opts).length) o.options = opts; else delete o.options;
  if (Object.keys(o).length) S.edit[x.name] = o; else delete S.edit[x.name];
}

export const dirty = () => !same(S.edit, S.saved) || !same(S.vEdit, S.version);

export function refreshSaveBar() {
  let n = 0;
  if (S.patches) {
    for (const x of S.patches.patches) {
      if (effEnabled(x) !== (S.saved[x.name] && 'enabled' in S.saved[x.name] ? S.saved[x.name].enabled : x.default_enabled)) n++;
      for (const op of x.options) {
        const sv = S.saved[x.name] && S.saved[x.name].options && op.key in S.saved[x.name].options ? S.saved[x.name].options[op.key] : op.default;
        if (!same(effValue(x, op), sv)) n++;
      }
    }
  }
  if (!same(S.vEdit, S.version)) n++;
  $('#savebar').hidden = !n;
  $('#sbtxt').textContent = `${n} unsaved change${n === 1 ? '' : 's'} · applies to next build`;
}

function tagOf(x) {
  const on = effEnabled(x);
  if (on === x.default_enabled) return [x.default_enabled ? 'default on' : 'default off', false];
  return [on ? 'included by you' : 'excluded by you', true];
}

function renderPatches() {
  const p = S.patches;
  $('#pfiles').textContent = p.files.join(', ');
  $('#plist').innerHTML = html`${p.patches.map((x, i) => {
    const [tag, chg] = tagOf(x);
    return html`<label class="prow row" data-i="${i}" ${x.options.length ? html`data-hasopt` : ''}><span class="switch"><input type="checkbox" ${effEnabled(x) ? html`checked` : ''} aria-label="${x.name}" ${S.locked ? html`disabled` : ''}><span></span></span><div class="grow" style="min-width:0"><div style="font-weight:500">${x.name}</div><div class="xs muted">${x.description || ''}</div></div>${x.options.length ? html`<a href="#opt-${i}" class="xs" data-opt="opt-${i}">${x.options.length} option${x.options.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}</a>` : ''}<span class="tag ${chg ? 'chg' : ''}">${tag}</span></label>`;
  })}`;
  if (p.stale_overrides.length) {
    $('#pstale').hidden = false;
    $('#pstale').querySelector('span').textContent = `Kept but not in this bundle: ${p.stale_overrides.join(', ')}`;
  }
  updatePatchMeta();
}

function updatePatchMeta() {
  const p = S.patches;
  const on = p.patches.filter(effEnabled).length;
  $('#pinc').textContent = on;
  $('#ptotal').textContent = p.patches.length;
  $('#tcount').textContent = `${on}/${p.patches.length}`;
  $$('#plist .prow').forEach(r => {
    const x = p.patches[Number(r.dataset.i)];
    const [tag, chg] = tagOf(x);
    const t = r.querySelector('.tag');
    t.textContent = tag;
    t.classList.toggle('chg', chg);
  });
  const lines = [];
  for (const x of p.patches) {
    const was = S.saved[x.name] && 'enabled' in S.saved[x.name] ? S.saved[x.name].enabled : x.default_enabled;
    if (effEnabled(x) !== was) lines.push(`${effEnabled(x) ? '+ include' : '− exclude'} ${x.name}`);
    for (const op of x.options) {
      const sv = S.saved[x.name] && S.saved[x.name].options && op.key in S.saved[x.name].options ? S.saved[x.name].options[op.key] : op.default;
      if (!same(effValue(x, op), sv)) lines.push(`~ ${x.name}.${op.key} = ${JSON.stringify(effValue(x, op))}`);
    }
  }
  $('#diff').textContent = lines.length ? lines.join('\n') : 'no unsaved changes';
  $('#raw').textContent = JSON.stringify([{ patches: S.edit }], null, 2);
  const changedOpts = p.patches.reduce((n, x) => n + x.options.filter(op => !same(effValue(x, op), op.default)).length, 0);
  $('#ocount').textContent = changedOpts ? `· ${changedOpts} changed` : '';
  $$('#opts fieldset').forEach(f => {
    const x = p.patches[Number(f.dataset.i)];
    f.querySelector('[data-unused]').hidden = effEnabled(x);
  });
  filterPatches();
  refreshSaveBar();
}

function filterPatches() {
  const q = $('#pf').value.trim().toLowerCase(), m = $('[name=pshow]:checked').value;
  $$('#plist .prow').forEach(r => {
    const on = r.querySelector('input').checked;
    r.hidden = !(r.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q)
      && (m === 'all' || (m === 'in' && on) || (m === 'ex' && !on) || (m === 'opt' && r.hasAttribute('data-hasopt'))));
  });
}

function fieldFor(x, op, i, j) {
  const id = `o-${i}-${j}`, v = effValue(x, op), t = String(op.type || 'string');
  const head = html`<label class="lbl" for="${id}">${op.title || op.key} <span class="badge plain b-mute">${t}</span>${same(v, op.default) ? '' : html` <span class="badge plain b-warn">changed</span>`}</label>`;
  const hint = html`<p class="hint">Default: ${JSON.stringify(op.default)}</p>`;
  const data = html`data-i="${i}" data-j="${j}"`;
  if (op.values && op.values.length) {
    return html`<div class="field">${head}<select class="input" id="${id}" ${data} data-kind="select">${op.values.map(pv => html`<option value="${JSON.stringify(pv)}" ${same(pv, v) ? html`selected` : ''}>${String(pv)}</option>`)}</select>${hint}</div>`;
  }
  if (/bool/i.test(t)) {
    return html`<label class="row sm" style="align-self:center"><span class="switch"><input type="checkbox" id="${id}" ${data} data-kind="bool" ${v ? html`checked` : ''}><span></span></span>${op.title || op.key} <span class="badge plain b-mute">${t}</span></label>`;
  }
  if (/list|\[\]/i.test(t)) {
    return html`<div class="field">${head}<textarea class="input mono" id="${id}" rows="3" ${data} data-kind="list">${(v || []).join('\n')}</textarea>${hint}</div>`;
  }
  if (/int|long|float|double/i.test(t)) {
    return html`<div class="field">${head}<input class="input mono" type="number" ${/float|double/i.test(t) ? html`step="any"` : ''} id="${id}" ${data} data-kind="num" value="${v ?? ''}">${hint}</div>`;
  }
  const isColor = COLOR.test(String(v ?? '')) || COLOR.test(String(op.default ?? ''));
  return html`<div class="field">${head}<div class="row" style="flex-wrap:nowrap">${isColor ? html`<input type="color" data-color-for="${id}" value="${String(v ?? op.default).replace(/^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{2}(?=[0-9A-Fa-f]{6}$)/, '#')}" aria-label="Pick colour">` : ''}<input class="input mono" id="${id}" ${data} data-kind="text" value="${v ?? ''}"></div>${hint}</div>`;
}

function renderOptions() {
  const p = S.patches;
  $('#opts').innerHTML = html`${p.patches.map((x, i) => (x.options.length ? html`<fieldset id="opt-${i}" data-i="${i}" class="stack" style="border-width:1px;border-radius:var(--radius);padding:1rem;margin:0"><legend class="sm" style="font-weight:600;padding:0 .375rem">${x.name} <span class="badge plain b-mute" data-unused hidden>kept but unused</span></legend><div class="grid2">${x.options.map((op, j) => fieldFor(x, op, i, j))}</div></fieldset>` : ''))}`;
}

function readField(el) {
  const k = el.dataset.kind;
  if (k === 'bool') return el.checked;
  if (k === 'select') return JSON.parse(el.value);
  if (k === 'num') return el.value === '' ? null : Number(el.value);
  if (k === 'list') return el.value.split('\n').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
  return el.value;
}

// colour picker <-> text sync (spec §0.6 R3, ui L1123)
function colorInput(picker) {
  const text = document.getElementById(picker.dataset.colorFor);
  const old = text.value;
  text.value = /^#[0-9A-Fa-f]{8}$/.test(old) ? old.slice(0, 3) + picker.value.slice(1) : picker.value;
  text.dispatchEvent(new Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
}

function wirePatchEvents() {
  $('#plist').addEventListener('change', e => {
    const r = e.target.closest('.prow');
    if (!r) return;
    setEnabled(S.patches.patches[Number(r.dataset.i)], e.target.checked);
    updatePatchMeta();
  });
  $('#plist').addEventListener('click', e => {
    const a = e.target.closest('[data-opt]');
    if (!a) return;
    e.preventDefault();
    document.getElementById('t2').click();
    const f = document.getElementById(a.dataset.opt);
    f.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' });
    f.classList.add('flash');
    setTimeout(() => f.classList.remove('flash'), 1500);
  });
  $('#pf').addEventListener('input', filterPatches);
  $$('[name=pshow]').forEach(r => r.addEventListener('change', filterPatches));
  $$('[data-all]').forEach(b => b.addEventListener('click', () => {
    S.patches.patches.forEach(x => setEnabled(x, b.dataset.all === '1'));
    renderPatches();
  }));
  $('#reset').addEventListener('click', () => {
    S.edit = {};
    renderPatches();
    renderOptions();
    updatePatchMeta();
    toast('Reset to bundle defaults (save to apply)');
  });
  const onField = e => {
    if (e.target.matches('[data-color-for]')) return colorInput(e.target);
    const el = e.target.closest('[data-kind]');
    if (!el) return;
    const x = S.patches.patches[Number(el.dataset.i)], op = x.options[Number(el.dataset.j)];
    setValue(x, op, readField(el));
    const pick = document.querySelector(`[data-color-for="${el.id}"]`);
    if (pick && COLOR.test(el.value)) pick.value = '#' + el.value.slice(-6);
    updatePatchMeta();
  };
  $('#opts').addEventListener('input', onField);
  $('#opts').addEventListener('change', onField);
}

function renderHeader() {
  const it = S.item, up = it.upstream || {};
  document.title = `${it.label} · Relume`;
  const newer = up.latest_tag && up.latest_tag !== up.downloaded_tag;
  const built = it.last_success ? `built ${it.last_success.app_version} with ${it.last_success.patches_version}` : 'not built yet';
  $('#hdr').innerHTML = html`${iconTile(it, 'ico-lg')}<div class="grow" style="min-width:220px"><div class="row"><h2 style="font-size:1.125rem">${it.label}</h2><span class="src">${it.source_name}</span>${newer ? html`<span class="badge b-warn">${up.latest_tag} available</span>` : ''}</div><p class="sm muted mono">${it.package} · ${up.repo} (${up.channel === 'tag' ? up.tag : up.channel}) · ${built}</p></div>`;
  $('#b-pull').disabled = !newer;
  $('#b-pull').title = it.engine === 'morphe' ? `Updates the ${it.source_name} source for all its items` : `Updates module ${up.repo} for all its apps`;
  $('#b-build').disabled = !!it.active_job;
  $('#t5').hidden = it.engine === 'morphe';
  $('#t1').hidden = $('#t2').hidden = it.engine !== 'morphe';
  if (it.engine !== 'morphe') document.getElementById('t5').click();
}

async function renderInstalled() {
  const { devices } = await api('GET', '/api/devices');
  const rows = devices.flatMap(d => d.assignments.filter(a => a.item_id === ID).map(a => ({ d, a })));
  $('#inst').innerHTML = html`${rows.length
    ? rows.map(({ d, a }) => html`<span class="row sm" style="gap:.375rem"><span class="dot ${d.online ? 'on' : 'off'}" aria-hidden="true"></span>${d.name} <span class="mono ${a.pending ? 'newer' : ''}">${a.installed ? a.installed.version_name : 'not installed'}</span>${a.pending ? html`<span class="xs muted">(behind)</span>` : ''}</span>`)
    : html`<span class="sm muted">Not assigned to any device</span>`}`;
}

export async function save(rebuild) {
  try {
    if (!same(S.edit, S.saved)) await api('PUT', `/api/items/${ID}/patches`, { overrides: S.edit });
    if (!same(S.vEdit, S.version)) await api('PUT', `/api/items/${ID}/version`, S.vEdit);
    if (rebuild) await api('POST', `/api/items/${ID}/build`);
    toast(rebuild ? 'Saved · build queued' : 'Saved');
    await load();
  } catch (e) {
    toast(e.message, 'error');
  }
}

export async function load() {
  S.item = await api('GET', `/api/items/${ID}`);
  S.version = { version_mode: S.item.version.mode, version_pin: S.item.version.pin, allow_nearest: S.item.version.allow_nearest };
  S.vEdit = clone(S.version);
  S.patches = null;
  S.locked = false;
  if (S.item.engine === 'morphe') {
    try {
      S.patches = await api('GET', `/api/items/${ID}/patches`);
      if (!S.patches.tag) throw new Error('no tag');
    } catch (_) {
      S.patches = null;
    }
  }
  renderHeader();
  if (S.patches) {
    S.saved = overridesFrom(S.patches);
    S.edit = clone(S.saved);
    $('#punavail').hidden = true;
    renderPatches();
    renderOptions();
    updatePatchMeta();
  } else if (S.item.engine === 'morphe') {
    $('#punavail').hidden = false;
  }
  renderVersion();          // P9.2
  renderHistory();          // P9.2
  renderModule();           // P9.2
  renderInstalled().catch(() => {});
  refreshSaveBar();
  if (location.hash === '#version') document.getElementById('t3').click();
}

$('#discard').addEventListener('click', () => load().catch(() => {}));
$('#save').addEventListener('click', () => save(false));
$('#saverun').addEventListener('click', () => save(true));
$('#b-build').addEventListener('click', () => act('POST', `/api/items/${ID}/build`, {}, 'Build queued').then(load).catch(() => {}));
$('#b-pull').addEventListener('click', () => act('POST', `/api/upstreams/${S.item.upstream.id}/pull`, { rebuild: true }, 'Update queued').catch(() => {}));
$('#b-dl').addEventListener('toggle', async e => {
  if (!e.target.open) return;
  const { artifacts } = await api('GET', `/api/items/${ID}/artifacts`);
  const newest = artifacts.length ? artifacts[0].job_id : null;
  const rows = artifacts.filter(a => a.job_id === newest);
  e.target.querySelector('.menu').innerHTML = html`${rows.length ? rows.map(a => html`<a role="menuitem" href="/api/artifacts/${a.id}/download" download>${a.variant} · ${a.abi} · ${fmt.bytes(a.size)}</a>`) : html`<span class="xs muted" style="padding:.5rem">No artifact</span>`}`;
});
window.addEventListener('beforeunload', e => { if (dirty()) e.preventDefault(); });
```

`item.html` additionally holds, inside panel `p1` above `#plist`: `<div class="alert warn" id="punavail" hidden><span>Patch list unavailable — run Update upstream. Builds still use the saved settings.</span></div>` and `<div class="alert warn" id="pstale" hidden><span></span></div>`.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run** — after P9.2 (the functions `renderVersion`, `renderHistory`, `renderModule` are added there): `node --check local/webui/static/item.js` → exit 0.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit** — together with P9.2 (one commit for the page; see P9.2 Step 5).

#### Task P9.2: App detail — Version, History, Module tabs, dialogs

**Files:**
- Modify: `local/webui/static/item.html`, `local/webui/static/item.js` (append)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET /api/jobs?kind=build&item_id=N&limit=20`, `GET /api/settings` (`artifact_retention`), `GET /api/sources`, `GET /api/upstreams`, `GET /api/projects` (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-23-follow-project-design.md`), `GET /api/groups`, `PUT /api/items/{id}`, `DELETE /api/items/{id}`, `PUT /api/apps/{pkg}`, `POST /api/apps/{pkg}/icon/refresh`.

**Behavior:**
- Version tab (spec L1127–1129, L514–521, R4): radio cards `auto` (hidden for Xposed items, explanation line "Modules list no compatible versions"), `pinned`, `latest` (warning `#latestwarn` shown when chosen); `auto` card subtitle `resolves to <compatible_versions[0]> today` or `no compatible version — the build will fail; pin a version or choose Latest` when the list is empty; pin = text input `#pv` (`list="pvl"`) enabled only for `pinned`, `<datalist id="pvl">`: each `compatible_versions` entry labelled `patches recommend` (first) / `compatible`, plus `· last built OK` when a successful build of this item had that `app_version`; then every `apkmirror.versions` entry newer than `compatible_versions[0]` (string compare by numeric segments) labelled `not in patch list ⚠`; hint `APKMirror checked <fmt.rel(checked_at)>` or `filled by the next build`; a pin outside `compatible_versions` shows the warning "Not in the bundle's compatible list; the build still runs (--continue-on-error)". "Allow nearest version" switch → `allow_nearest`. Changes update `S.vEdit` and the save bar.
- History tab (spec L1131): `GET /api/jobs?kind=build&item_id=<id>&limit=20`; table columns Build (`#id` link `/builds.html?job=<id>`), Started (`fmt.abs`), App ver. (`detail.app_version`), Patches (`detail.patches_version`), Result (badge + `reason` for failed), Duration (`fmt.duration(started_at, finished_at)`), Download (first artifact link of `detail.artifacts`, `Download (n)` menu when several); failed rows are clickable to `/builds.html?job=<id>`; header text `Keeping the last <artifact_retention> successful builds (Settings → Artifacts)`; link `All builds →` = `/builds.html?item=<id>`.
- Module tab (Xposed, spec L1565): read-only module repo, channel/tag, downloaded and latest tag (from `item.upstream`), engine (`LSPatch`/`NPatch`) and engine tag (`downloaded_tag` of the source's `cli_upstream_id` row from `GET /api/upstreams`, source from `GET /api/sources`); editable "Signature bypass level" `<select>` 0–3 (LSPatch) or 0–4 (NPatch) and "Inject loader dex" checkbox; Save module → `PUT /api/items/{id} {engine_options:{sigbypass, injectdex}}`.
- "Edit app…" dialog (spec L1216): display name, APKMirror list URL, example URL, example URL 2, APKPure URL (all optional text) → `PUT /api/apps/<package>`; "Refresh icon" → `POST /api/apps/<package>/icon/refresh` + toast.
- "Item settings…" dialog (spec L1217, L1567): enabled switch, display name (empty = default label), group select (`GET /api/groups` + Ungrouped), ABI chips `universal`, `arm64-v8a`, `armeabi-v7a`, `x86`, `x86_64` (pressed = a variant with that `abi` exists; adding a chip clones the first variant with `name = abi`, `abi`, `strip_libs = abi === 'universal' || engine !== 'morphe' ? null : abi`; removing deletes that variant; at least one must stay), dependency checkboxes from `GET /api/projects` (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-23-follow-project-design.md` §8) → `PUT /api/items/{id}` with `project_ids` with the changed fields; "Delete item" → `confirm('Delete <label>? Its build files are deleted too.')` → `DELETE /api/items/{id}` → `location.href = '/'` (409 shows the server message).

- [ ] `node --check local/webui/static/item.js` exit 0; `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` exit 0.
- [ ] `grep -c 'not in patch list' local/webui/static/item.js` ≥ 1; `grep -c 'sigbypass' local/webui/static/item.js` ≥ 1; `grep -c '/api/apps/' local/webui/static/item.js` ≥ 2.

```js
const verKey = v => String(v).split(/[^0-9]+/).filter(Boolean).map(Number);
const newer = (a, b) => {
  const x = verKey(a), y = verKey(b);
  for (let i = 0; i < Math.max(x.length, y.length); i++) {
    if ((x[i] || 0) !== (y[i] || 0)) return (x[i] || 0) > (y[i] || 0);
  }
  return false;
};
let builtOk = new Set();

function renderVersion() {
  const it = S.item, compat = (S.patches && S.patches.compatible_versions) || [];
  const xposed = it.engine !== 'morphe';
  $('[name=pin][value=auto]').closest('.rcard').hidden = xposed;
  $('#vauto-sub').textContent = compat.length ? `resolves to ${compat[0]} today`
    : 'no compatible version — the build will fail; pin a version or choose Latest';
  $(`[name=pin][value=${S.vEdit.version_mode === 'pinned' ? 'pin' : S.vEdit.version_mode}]`).checked = true;
  $('#pv').value = S.vEdit.version_pin || '';
  $('#pv').disabled = S.vEdit.version_mode !== 'pinned';
  $('#latestwarn').hidden = S.vEdit.version_mode !== 'latest';
  $('#near').checked = !!S.vEdit.allow_nearest;
  const mirror = ((it.apkmirror && it.apkmirror.versions) || []).filter(v => !compat.length || newer(v, compat[0]));
  $('#pvl').innerHTML = html`${compat.map((v, i) => html`<option value="${v}">${i ? 'compatible' : 'patches recommend'}${builtOk.has(v) ? ' · last built OK' : ''}</option>`)}${mirror.map(v => html`<option value="${v}">not in patch list ⚠</option>`)}`;
  $('#pvhint').textContent = it.apkmirror && it.apkmirror.checked_at ? `APKMirror checked ${fmt.rel(it.apkmirror.checked_at)}` : 'APKMirror versions: filled by the next build';
  checkPin();
}

function checkPin() {
  const compat = (S.patches && S.patches.compatible_versions) || [];
  const v = $('#pv').value.trim();
  $('#pvwarn').hidden = !(S.vEdit.version_mode === 'pinned' && v && compat.length && !compat.includes(v));
}

document.addEventListener('change', e => {
  if (e.target.name === 'pin') {
    const m = e.target.value === 'pin' ? 'pinned' : e.target.value;
    S.vEdit.version_mode = m;
    S.vEdit.version_pin = m === 'pinned' ? ($('#pv').value.trim() || null) : null;
    $('#pv').disabled = m !== 'pinned';
    $('#latestwarn').hidden = m !== 'latest';
    checkPin();
    refreshSaveBar();
  }
  if (e.target.id === 'near') { S.vEdit.allow_nearest = e.target.checked; refreshSaveBar(); }
});
$('#pv').addEventListener('input', () => { S.vEdit.version_pin = $('#pv').value.trim() || null; checkPin(); refreshSaveBar(); });

async function renderHistory() {
  const [{ jobs }, settings] = await Promise.all([
    api('GET', `/api/jobs?kind=build&item_id=${ID}&limit=20`), api('GET', '/api/settings')]);
  builtOk = new Set(jobs.filter(j => j.status === 'success' && j.detail).map(j => j.detail.app_version));
  $('#keep').textContent = `Keeping the last ${settings.artifact_retention} successful builds (Settings → Artifacts).`;
  $('#allb').href = `/builds.html?item=${ID}`;
  const badge = j => html`<span class="badge ${{ success: 'b-ok', failed: 'b-err', running: 'b-info', queued: 'b-mute', cancelled: 'b-mute' }[j.status]}">${j.status}</span>`;
  $('#hist tbody').innerHTML = html`${jobs.map(j => {
    const d = j.detail || {}, arts = d.artifacts || [];
    return html`<tr ${j.status === 'failed' ? html`data-href="/builds.html?job=${j.id}" style="cursor:pointer"` : ''}><td class="mono"><a href="/builds.html?job=${j.id}">#${j.id}</a></td><td>${fmt.abs(j.started_at || j.created_at)}</td><td class="hide-m mono">${d.app_version || '—'}</td><td class="hide-m mono">${d.patches_version || '—'}</td><td>${badge(j)}${j.status === 'failed' ? html`<div class="xs" style="color:hsl(var(--destructive))">${j.reason || ''}</div>` : ''}</td><td class="hide-m">${fmt.duration(j.started_at, j.finished_at)}</td><td>${arts.length ? html`<a class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" href="/api/artifacts/${arts[0].id}/download" download>${arts.length > 1 ? `Download (${arts.length})` : 'Download'}</a>` : ''}</td></tr>`;
  })}`;
  renderVersion();
}
$('#hist').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const tr = e.target.closest('tr[data-href]');
  if (tr && !e.target.closest('a')) location.href = tr.dataset.href;
});

async function renderModule() {
  const it = S.item;
  if (it.engine === 'morphe') return;
  const [{ sources }, { upstreams }] = await Promise.all([api('GET', '/api/sources'), api('GET', '/api/upstreams')]);
  const src = sources.find(s => s.id === it.source_id);
  const eng = upstreams.find(u => u.id === (src && src.cli_upstream_id)) || {};
  const up = it.upstream || {};
  $('#mod').innerHTML = html`<div><dt class="muted">Module</dt><dd class="mono">${up.repo}</dd></div><div><dt class="muted">Channel</dt><dd class="mono">${up.channel === 'tag' ? up.tag : up.channel}</dd></div><div><dt class="muted">Downloaded</dt><dd class="mono">${up.downloaded_tag || '—'}</dd></div><div><dt class="muted">Latest</dt><dd class="mono">${up.latest_tag || '—'}</dd></div><div><dt class="muted">Engine</dt><dd class="mono">${it.engine === 'lspatch' ? 'LSPatch' : 'NPatch'} ${eng.downloaded_tag || ''}</dd></div>`;
  const max = it.engine === 'npatch' ? 4 : 3, eo = it.engine_options || { sigbypass: 0, injectdex: false };
  $('#m-sig').innerHTML = html`${Array.from({ length: max + 1 }, (_, n) => html`<option value="${n}" ${n === eo.sigbypass ? html`selected` : ''}>${n}</option>`)}`;
  $('#m-dex').checked = !!eo.injectdex;
}
$('#m-save').addEventListener('click', () => act('PUT', `/api/items/${ID}`,
  { engine_options: { sigbypass: Number($('#m-sig').value), injectdex: $('#m-dex').checked } }, 'Module settings saved').then(load).catch(() => {}));

$('#m-icon').addEventListener('click', () => act('POST', `/api/apps/${encodeURIComponent(S.item.package)}/icon/refresh`, {}, 'Icon refresh queued').catch(() => {}));
$('#m-app').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const dlg = $('#dlg-app');
  $('#e-name').value = S.item.display_name || '';
  if (await ask(dlg) !== 'ok') return;
  const body = { display_name: $('#e-name').value.trim() || null };
  for (const [id, key] of [['#e-list', 'apkmirror_list_url'], ['#e-ex', 'apkmirror_example_url'], ['#e-ex2', 'apkmirror_example_url2'], ['#e-pure', 'apkpure_url']]) {
    if ($(id).value.trim()) body[key] = $(id).value.trim();
  }
  act('PUT', `/api/apps/${encodeURIComponent(S.item.package)}`, body, 'App saved').then(load).catch(() => {});
});

const ABIS = ['universal', 'arm64-v8a', 'armeabi-v7a', 'x86', 'x86_64'];
$('#m-item').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const it = S.item;
  const [{ groups }, { dependency_apps }] = await Promise.all([api('GET', '/api/groups'), api('GET', '/api/dependency-apps')]);
  let variants = clone(it.variants);
  $('#i-en').checked = it.enabled;
  $('#i-name').value = it.display_name || '';
  $('#i-grp').innerHTML = html`${groups.map(g => html`<option value="${g.id}" ${g.id === it.group_id ? html`selected` : ''}>${g.name}</option>`)}<option value="" ${it.group_id == null ? html`selected` : ''}>Ungrouped</option>`;
  const chips = () => {
    $('#i-abis').innerHTML = html`${ABIS.map(a => html`<button type="button" class="chip" data-abi="${a}" aria-pressed="${variants.some(v => v.abi === a)}">${a}</button>`)}`;
  };
  chips();
  $('#i-abis').onclick = e => {
    const b = e.target.closest('[data-abi]');
    if (!b) return;
    const a = b.dataset.abi, has = variants.some(v => v.abi === a);
    if (has && variants.length > 1) variants = variants.filter(v => v.abi !== a);
    if (!has) variants.push({ ...clone(variants[0]), name: a, abi: a, strip_libs: a === 'universal' || it.engine !== 'morphe' ? null : a });
    chips();
  };
  $('#i-deps').innerHTML = html`${dependency_apps.map(d => html`<label class="row sm"><input type="checkbox" class="cb" value="${d.id}" ${it.dependency_app_ids.includes(d.id) ? html`checked` : ''}> ${d.display_name} <span class="xs muted mono">${d.package}</span></label>`)}`;
  if (await ask($('#dlg-item')) !== 'ok') return;
  const body = {
    enabled: $('#i-en').checked,
    display_name: $('#i-name').value.trim() || null,
    group_id: $('#i-grp').value ? Number($('#i-grp').value) : null,
    variants,
    dependency_app_ids: $$('#i-deps input:checked').map(i => Number(i.value)),
  };
  act('PUT', `/api/items/${ID}`, body, 'Item saved').then(load).catch(() => {});
});
$('#i-del').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (!confirm(`Delete ${S.item.label}? Its build files are deleted too.`)) return;
  try { await act('DELETE', `/api/items/${ID}`, {}, 'Item deleted'); location.href = '/'; } catch (_) { /* shown */ }
});

shell();
wirePatchEvents();
load().catch(e => toast(e.message, 'error'));
```

Add the chip rule to the page `<style>` if `M/app-detail.html` has none: `.chip{border-width:1px;border-radius:999px;padding:.25rem .75rem;background:none;font:inherit}.chip[aria-pressed=true]{border-color:hsl(var(--accent));background:hsl(var(--accent)/.1)}`.

```bash
git add local/webui/static/item.html local/webui/static/item.js && git commit -m "feat(webui): app detail page with patches, options, version, history, module" -- local/webui/static/item.html local/webui/static/item.js
```

#### Task P9.3: Builds page — queue, live log, step tracker, history

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/static/builds.html`, `local/webui/static/builds.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET /api/jobs?kind=build&status=running|queued`, `GET /api/jobs?kind=build&status=&item_id=&before_id=&limit=25`, `GET /api/jobs/{id}`, `POST /api/jobs/{id}/cancel`, `GET /api/jobs/{id}/log` (via `logStream`), `GET /api/catalog` (item labels/icons), `GET /api/schedules` (schedule names), `POST /api/items/{id}/build`, `POST /api/upstreams/{id}/pull`.

**Behavior (spec L1133–1147, L355, L733):**
- `builds.html` = `M/build-log.html` body lines 176–213 ported; second style block `M/build-log.html:163-175` in the head. Containers: queue `#queue tbody`, `#qsum`; build card `#bico`, `#bt`, `#bsrc`, `#bst`, `#bsub`, `#cancel`, facts `#facts`, steps `<ol class="steps" id="steps">` with five `<li>`: `version` "Resolve version", `download` "Download APK", `patch` "Patch", `verify` "Verify", `store` "Store", progress `#pbar`; log toolbar `#follow`, `#wrap`, `#find`, `#hits`, `#lines`, `#copy`, `#dlog`; log `<div class="log" id="log" role="log">`; history `#hist tbody`, filter `#filt` (`""` All, `failed`, `success`, `item` This app — the last option only when `?item=`), `#older` "Load older".
- Queue (refresh 5 s while visible): rows of running + queued build jobs: `#id`, item icon + label + source, trigger text (`manual`; `schedule` → `schedule "<name>"`; `upstream` → `patch update`; `after_build` → `after build`), badge Running/Waiting, Remove (queued) / Cancel (running) → `POST /api/jobs/<id>/cancel`; `#qsum` = `<n> running · <m> waiting · 1 worker`.
- Selected build: `?job=N`, else the running build, else the newest history row. Header `Build #<id> · <label>`, source, status badge; `#bsub` = `Trigger: <trigger text> · started <fmt.abs> · <fmt.duration> elapsed|took`; Cancel enabled only while running/queued; facts (from `detail`): Patches `patches_version`, App `app_version`, CLI `cli_version`, Arch = item variant ABIs (`item.abis`), APK source = `download.kind` of the item's first variant (`GET /api/items/{id}` only for the selected build).
- Log: `logStream(id, onText, onEnd)`; each received line → one `<div>` (class `s` for `::step::` lines, `e` when the line contains `[-]` or `::fail::`, `w` for `[!]`, `ok` for `[+]`); text via `textContent` (first 9 chars `HH:MM:SS ` into a `<span class="t">`). Step tracker from lines matching `^(\d\d:\d\d:\d\d) ::step::(\w+)(?:::(.*))?$`: steps before the current are `done`, current `now` (or `fail` when the job failed), small text = time since the previous step mark (`m:ss`), the `patch` step shows its `<detail>` (`i/n`); progress bar = `(index + 0.5) / 5` while running, 100 % on success. `onEnd(status)` → badge, Cancel disabled, queue/history refresh. Follow: auto-scroll; a manual scroll up more than 24 px unchecks Follow (port of `M/build-log.html:241`); Wrap toggles `.nowrap`; Find highlights matching lines (`.hit`) and shows `<n> matches`; `#lines` = line count; Copy → `navigator.clipboard.writeText(text)`; Download .log → `Blob` of the received text, file name `build-<id>.log`.
- History (25 per page, Load older uses `before_id` = last id): columns #, item, trigger, result + reason (red), started, duration, size (sum of `detail.artifacts[].size`), View log (selects the row: `history.replaceState` to `?job=<id>`, reload the build card). Suggested fix on failed rows (spec L1146): `version_unresolved`/`patch_failed` → link `Pin a version` to `/item.html?id=<item>#version`; `upstream_missing` → button `Update upstream` (`POST /api/upstreams/<item upstream id>/pull {rebuild:true}`); other codes → button `Retry` (`POST /api/items/<item>/build`). The per-patch counter is not shown (spec L1142).

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `node --check local/webui/static/builds.js` exit 0; `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` exit 0.
- [ ] `grep -c 'logStream' local/webui/static/builds.js` ≥ 1; `grep -c '::step::' local/webui/static/builds.js` ≥ 1; `grep -c 'Patch 4 / 41\|patch update 1.10.0' local/webui/static/builds.html` prints `0`.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing check** — `ls local/webui/static/builds.js` → `No such file or directory`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Markup** — `builds.html` per Behavior.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/static/builds.js`

```js
import { $, $$, act, api, every, fmt, html, iconTile, logStream, shell, toast } from '/static/app.js';

const qs = new URLSearchParams(location.search);
const ITEM = qs.get('item') ? Number(qs.get('item')) : null;
const STEPS = ['version', 'download', 'patch', 'verify', 'store'];
const STEP_RE = /^(\d\d:\d\d:\d\d) ::step::(\w+)(?:::(.*))?$/;
const B = { items: new Map(), schedules: new Map(), sel: null, es: null, text: '', marks: [], last: null };

const triggerText = j => (j.trigger === 'schedule' ? `schedule "${B.schedules.get(j.schedule_id) || j.schedule_id}"`
  : j.trigger === 'upstream' ? 'patch update' : j.trigger === 'after_build' ? 'after build' : 'manual');
const itemOf = j => B.items.get(j.target.item_id) || { label: `item ${j.target.item_id}`, source_name: '', abis: [] };
const BADGE = { success: ['b-ok', 'Success'], failed: ['b-err', 'Failed'], running: ['b-info', 'Running'], queued: ['b-mute', 'Waiting'], cancelled: ['b-mute', 'Cancelled'] };

async function loadRefs() {
  const [cat, sch] = await Promise.all([api('GET', '/api/catalog'), api('GET', '/api/schedules')]);
  [...cat.groups.flatMap(g => g.items), ...cat.ungrouped].forEach(it => B.items.set(it.id, it));
  sch.schedules.forEach(s => B.schedules.set(s.id, s.name));
}

async function renderQueue() {
  const [r, q] = await Promise.all([api('GET', '/api/jobs?kind=build&status=running'), api('GET', '/api/jobs?kind=build&status=queued&limit=100')]);
  const rows = [...r.jobs, ...q.jobs.reverse()];
  $('#qsum').textContent = `${r.jobs.length} running · ${q.jobs.length} waiting · 1 worker`;
  $('#queue tbody').innerHTML = html`${rows.map(j => {
    const it = itemOf(j), [cls, label] = BADGE[j.status];
    return html`<tr><td class="mono" style="width:4rem"><a href="?job=${j.id}" data-sel="${j.id}">#${j.id}</a></td><td><div class="row" style="flex-wrap:nowrap">${iconTile(it, 'ico-sm')}<b style="font-weight:500">${it.label}</b><span class="src">${it.source_name}</span></div></td><td class="hide-m sm muted">${triggerText(j)}</td><td><span class="badge ${cls}">${label}</span></td><td style="text-align:right"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" type="button" data-cancel="${j.id}">${j.status === 'running' ? 'Cancel' : 'Remove'}</button></td></tr>`;
  })}`;
  return r.jobs[0];
}

function lineClass(t) {
  if (t.includes('::step::')) return 's';
  if (t.includes('[-]') || t.includes('::fail::')) return 'e';
  if (t.includes('[!]')) return 'w';
  if (t.includes('[+]')) return 'ok';
  return '';
}

function appendLines(chunk) {
  B.text += chunk;
  const log = $('#log');
  for (const t of chunk.split('\n')) {
    if (!t) continue;
    const d = document.createElement('div');
    d.className = lineClass(t);
    const s = document.createElement('span');
    s.className = 't';
    s.textContent = t.slice(0, 8);
    d.append(s, document.createTextNode(t.slice(9)));
    log.appendChild(d);
    const m = STEP_RE.exec(t);
    if (m) B.marks.push({ at: m[1], step: m[2], detail: m[3] || '' });
  }
  renderSteps();
  $('#lines').textContent = `${log.children.length} lines`;
  if ($('#follow').checked) log.scrollTop = log.scrollHeight;
  find();
}

const secs = t => { const [h, m, s] = t.split(':').map(Number); return h * 3600 + m * 60 + s; };
function renderSteps(final) {
  const status = final || (B.last && B.last.status);
  const cur = B.marks.length ? STEPS.indexOf(B.marks[B.marks.length - 1].step) : -1;
  $$('#steps li').forEach((li, i) => {
    const mark = B.marks.find(m => m.step === STEPS[i]);
    const next = B.marks.find(m => STEPS.indexOf(m.step) === i + 1);
    li.className = status === 'success' || i < cur ? 'done' : i === cur ? (status === 'failed' ? 'fail' : 'now') : '';
    let small = '—';
    if (mark && next) { const d = secs(next.at) - secs(mark.at); small = `${Math.floor(d / 60)}:${String(d % 60).padStart(2, '0')}`; }
    if (STEPS[i] === 'patch' && mark && mark.detail && i === cur) small = mark.detail;
    li.querySelector('small').textContent = small;
  });
  const pct = status === 'success' ? 100 : Math.max(0, Math.round(((cur + 0.5) / STEPS.length) * 100));
  $('#pbar').style.width = `${pct}%`;
  $('#pbar').parentNode.setAttribute('aria-valuenow', pct);
}

function find() {
  const q = $('#find').value.trim().toLowerCase();
  let n = 0;
  $$('#log > div').forEach(d => { const h = !!q && d.textContent.toLowerCase().includes(q); d.classList.toggle('hit', h); if (h) n++; });
  $('#hits').textContent = q ? `${n} match${n === 1 ? '' : 'es'}` : '';
}

async function select(id) {
  if (B.es) B.es.close();
  B.text = '';
  B.marks = [];
  $('#log').textContent = '';
  const j = await api('GET', `/api/jobs/${id}`);
  B.sel = id;
  B.last = j;
  const it = itemOf(j), d = j.detail || {}, [cls, label] = BADGE[j.status];
  const tpl = document.createElement('template');
  tpl.innerHTML = html`${iconTile(it)}`;
  tpl.content.firstChild.id = 'bico';
  $('#bico').replaceWith(tpl.content.firstChild);
  $('#bt').textContent = `Build #${j.id} · ${it.label}`;
  $('#bsrc').textContent = it.source_name;
  $('#bst').className = `badge ${cls}`;
  $('#bst').textContent = label;
  const live = j.status === 'running' || j.status === 'queued';
  $('#bsub').textContent = `Trigger: ${triggerText(j)} · started ${fmt.abs(j.started_at)} · ${fmt.duration(j.started_at, j.finished_at)} ${live ? 'elapsed' : 'took'}`;
  $('#cancel').disabled = !live;
  let full = null;
  try { full = await api('GET', `/api/items/${j.target.item_id}`); } catch (_) { /* item deleted */ }
  $('#facts').innerHTML = html`<div><dt class="muted">Patches</dt><dd class="mono">${d.patches_version || '—'}</dd></div><div><dt class="muted">App</dt><dd class="mono">${d.app_version || '—'}</dd></div><div><dt class="muted">Arch</dt><dd class="mono">${(it.abis || []).join(', ') || '—'}</dd></div><div><dt class="muted">CLI</dt><dd class="mono">${d.cli_version || '—'}</dd></div><div><dt class="muted">APK source</dt><dd class="mono">${full && full.variants.length ? full.variants[0].download.kind : '—'}</dd></div>`;
  renderSteps();
  B.es = logStream(id, appendLines, status => {
    B.last = { ...B.last, status };
    const [c2, l2] = BADGE[status] || ['b-mute', status];
    $('#bst').className = `badge ${c2}`;
    $('#bst').textContent = l2;
    $('#cancel').disabled = true;
    renderSteps(status);
    refresh();
  });
}

let oldest = null;
function fixFor(j) {
  const code = j.detail && j.detail.fail_code, it = itemOf(j);
  if (code === 'version_unresolved' || code === 'patch_failed') return html`<a class="xs" href="/item.html?id=${j.target.item_id}#version">Pin a version</a>`;
  if (code === 'upstream_missing' && it.upstream) return html`<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" type="button" data-pull="${it.upstream.id}">Update upstream</button>`;
  return html`<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" type="button" data-retry="${j.target.item_id}">Retry</button>`;
}

async function renderHistory(append) {
  const f = $('#filt').value;
  const p = new URLSearchParams({ kind: 'build', limit: '25' });
  if (f === 'failed' || f === 'success') p.set('status', f);
  if (f === 'item' && ITEM) p.set('item_id', ITEM);
  if (append && oldest) p.set('before_id', oldest);
  const { jobs } = await api('GET', `/api/jobs?${p}`);
  const done = jobs.filter(j => j.status !== 'queued' && j.status !== 'running');
  if (jobs.length) oldest = jobs[jobs.length - 1].id;
  const rows = html`${done.map(j => {
    const it = itemOf(j), [cls, label] = BADGE[j.status], size = ((j.detail && j.detail.artifacts) || []).reduce((n, a) => n + a.size, 0);
    return html`<tr data-id="${j.id}" ${j.id === B.sel ? html`aria-current="true"` : ''}><td class="mono">#${j.id}</td><td><div class="row" style="flex-wrap:nowrap">${iconTile(it, 'ico-sm')}<span>${it.label}</span></div></td><td class="hide-m sm muted">${triggerText(j)}</td><td><span class="badge ${cls}">${label}</span>${j.status === 'failed' ? html`<div class="xs" style="color:hsl(var(--destructive))">${j.reason || ''}</div>${fixFor(j)}` : ''}</td><td class="hide-m">${fmt.abs(j.started_at)}</td><td class="hide-m">${fmt.duration(j.started_at, j.finished_at)}</td><td class="hide-m">${size ? fmt.bytes(size) : '—'}</td><td><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" type="button" data-sel="${j.id}">View log</button></td></tr>`;
  })}`;
  if (append) $('#hist tbody').insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', html`${rows}`);
  else $('#hist tbody').innerHTML = html`${rows}`;
  $('#older').hidden = jobs.length < 25;
  return done[0];
}

async function refresh() {
  try { await renderQueue(); await renderHistory(false); } catch (_) { /* next tick retries */ }
}

document.addEventListener('click', async e => {
  const sel = e.target.closest('[data-sel]'), can = e.target.closest('[data-cancel]');
  const pull = e.target.closest('[data-pull]'), retry = e.target.closest('[data-retry]');
  try {
    if (sel) {
      e.preventDefault();
      history.replaceState(null, '', `?job=${sel.dataset.sel}${ITEM ? `&item=${ITEM}` : ''}`);
      await select(Number(sel.dataset.sel));
      $('[aria-labelledby=bt]').scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
    }
    if (can) await act('POST', `/api/jobs/${can.dataset.cancel}/cancel`, {}, 'Cancel requested');
    if (pull) await act('POST', `/api/upstreams/${pull.dataset.pull}/pull`, { rebuild: true }, 'Update queued');
    if (retry) await act('POST', `/api/items/${retry.dataset.retry}/build`, {}, 'Build queued');
    if (can || pull || retry) await refresh();
  } catch (_) { /* act() showed it */ }
});
$('#cancel').addEventListener('click', () => act('POST', `/api/jobs/${B.sel}/cancel`, {}, 'Cancel requested').then(refresh).catch(() => {}));
$('#log').addEventListener('scroll', () => { const l = $('#log'); if (l.scrollTop + l.clientHeight < l.scrollHeight - 24) $('#follow').checked = false; });
$('#wrap').addEventListener('change', e => $('#log').classList.toggle('nowrap', !e.target.checked));
$('#find').addEventListener('input', find);
$('#copy').addEventListener('click', () => navigator.clipboard.writeText(B.text).then(() => toast('Log copied')));
$('#dlog').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const a = document.createElement('a');
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([B.text], { type: 'text/plain' }));
  a.download = `build-${B.sel}.log`;
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);
});
$('#filt').addEventListener('change', () => renderHistory(false).catch(() => {}));
$('#older').addEventListener('click', () => renderHistory(true).catch(() => {}));

(async () => {
  shell();
  if (!ITEM) $('#filt option[value=item]').remove();
  else $('#filt').value = 'item';
  await loadRefs();
  const running = await renderQueue();
  const newest = await renderHistory(false);
  const first = qs.get('job') ? Number(qs.get('job')) : running ? running.id : newest ? newest.id : null;
  if (first) await select(first);
  every(5000, refresh);
})().catch(e => toast(e.message, 'error'));
```

```bash
git add local/webui/static/builds.html local/webui/static/builds.js && git commit -m "feat(webui): builds page with queue, live log, steps and history" -- local/webui/static/builds.html local/webui/static/builds.js
```

### P10 Pages B — Schedules, Devices, Settings (round 2)

Spec: ui §2 Schedules L1148–1167, Devices L1168–1190, Settings L1191–1205; ui §3 rows L1219–1221; core §6 L311–324, §8 L347–348; device §3.2 L922–941, §4.1 L956–964, D6 L875; §0.6 F1 L148, F3 L150. Frontend rules and shapes: P6 header and `ui-api-shapes.md`. Shared CSS/JS changes: ask impl-pages-a (P9).

#### Task P10.1: Schedules page

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/static/schedules.html`, `local/webui/static/schedules.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET/POST /api/schedules`, `PUT/DELETE /api/schedules/{id}`, `POST /api/schedules/preview {spec}`, `POST /api/schedules/{id}/run`, `GET /api/settings` (`server.tz`, `server.now`), `GET /api/sources`, `GET /api/catalog`, `GET /api/devices`.

**Behavior:**
- `schedules.html` = `M/schedules.html` body lines 173–223 ported; second style block `M/schedules.html:163-172` plus the media query of line 148 in the head. Remove: the Cron radio option, `#cronf`, `#cronout`, the "switch to Cron" text (F1), the "Skip devices with nothing new" option, and the "Order of a night" paragraph, which is replaced by `Kinds run in parallel. Order follows the schedule times: a pull with rebuild queues builds after its download, and phones with "Install after build" get OTA when a build succeeds. A run missed while the server was off runs once at startup.` (spec L1161). Containers: `#srvtime`, timeline `#tl` + legend `#tll`, list `#list`, editor fields `#sn`, `[name=act]` (`upstream|build|ota`), option blocks `[data-act=upstream]` (`#o-pull` checkbox "Also pull new patches", `#o-rebuild` checkbox "Rebuild affected items after pull"), `[data-act=build]` (`[name=onlych]` radio `changed|all`), `[data-act=ota]` (`#o-retries` number 0–5 "Retry offline devices N times, 60 s apart"); repeat `[name=f]` (`hours|day|week`), `#hh` (1/3/6/12/24 h and a number input `#hh-n` 1–168), `#tm` (`type=time`), day chips `#days input` (UI order Sun..Sat), target chip groups `#tg-upstream`, `#tg-build`, `#tg-ota`, notify `#notify`, summary `#human`, preview list `#nexts`, buttons `#del`, `#runnow`, `#cancel`, `#save`, `#new`.
- List cards (from `GET /api/schedules`): name, kind badge (Check / Rebuild / OTA), human sentence + target names, option line, `Next: <fmt.abs(next_run_at)> (<fmt.rel>)` or `paused`, `Last: <fmt.abs(last_run_at)>` with a badge from `last_counts` (`N success · N failed`, `N queued`/`N running` shown as well; empty → `never`), "Run now" (`POST …/run`, toast `Started: <name> — see Builds`), enable switch (`PUT {enabled}`); disabled cards dimmed (`opacity:.6`). Click on a card loads it into the editor.
- Timeline: one tick per `next_runs` entry inside the next 24 h from `server.now`, colour by kind; legend lists schedule names.
- Editor ↔ spec: Hourly → `{every:'hours', hours}`; Daily → `{every:'day', at}`; Weekly → `{every:'week', days, at}` where UI day index `u` (0 = Sun) maps to `(u + 6) % 7` (0 = Monday) and back with `(d + 1) % 7` (spec L1156). Targets: upstream → `{all:true}` or `{source_ids:[…]}`; build → `{all:true}`, `{group_ids:[…]}` (Ungrouped = 0) or `{item_ids:[…]}` ("Pick items…" opens a checkbox list of all items); ota → `{all:true}` or `{device_ids:[…]}`. Options: upstream `{pull, rebuild, notify}`, build `{only_changed, notify}`, ota `{retries, notify}` — only these keys (Plan-fixed "schedule option keys").
- Live summary: sentence (`Every day at 04:00`, `Mondays and Thursdays at 09:30`, `Every 6 hours`) + `POST /api/schedules/preview` (debounced 300 ms) → five times as `fmt.abs` with relative text. Server time line `Server time <now in TZ> · <tz>`.
- Save → `POST` (new) or `PUT` (existing) → toast `Schedule saved`; 400 → toast with the server message; Delete → `confirm` → `DELETE`; Run now → `POST …/run`; New schedule resets the editor (kind build, daily 04:00, all items, notify on).

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `node --check local/webui/static/schedules.js` exit 0; `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` exit 0.
- [ ] `grep -ciE 'cron' local/webui/static/schedules.html local/webui/static/schedules.js` prints `0` for both; `grep -c '(u + 6) % 7' local/webui/static/schedules.js` = 1.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing check** — `ls local/webui/static/schedules.js` → `No such file or directory`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Markup** — `schedules.html` per Behavior.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/static/schedules.js`

```js
import { $, $$, act, api, fmt, html, shell, toast } from '/static/app.js';

const KIND = { upstream: 'Check', build: 'Rebuild', ota: 'OTA' };
const DAYS = ['Mondays', 'Tuesdays', 'Wednesdays', 'Thursdays', 'Fridays', 'Saturdays', 'Sundays'];
const R = { list: [], sources: [], groups: [], items: [], devices: [], cur: null, pickItems: null };

const uiToSpec = u => (u + 6) % 7;
const specToUi = d => (d + 1) % 7;

function sentence(spec) {
  if (spec.every === 'hours') return spec.hours === 1 ? 'Every hour' : `Every ${spec.hours} hours`;
  if (spec.every === 'day') return `Every day at ${spec.at}`;
  const names = [...spec.days].sort().map(d => DAYS[d]);
  return `${names.length > 1 ? `${names.slice(0, -1).join(', ')} and ${names[names.length - 1]}` : names[0]} at ${spec.at}`;
}

function targetText(kind, t) {
  if (t.all) return kind === 'upstream' ? 'All sources' : kind === 'build' ? 'All items' : 'All devices';
  if (t.source_ids) return t.source_ids.join(', ');
  if (t.group_ids) return t.group_ids.map(id => (id === 0 ? 'Ungrouped' : (R.groups.find(g => g.id === id) || {}).name || `group ${id}`)).join(', ');
  if (t.item_ids) return `${t.item_ids.length} picked items`;
  return t.device_ids.map(id => (R.devices.find(d => d.id === id) || {}).name || `device ${id}`).join(', ');
}

function optionText(s) {
  const o = s.options;
  if (s.kind === 'upstream') return `${o.pull === false ? 'Check only' : 'Pull new patches'}${o.pull !== false && o.rebuild !== false ? ' · rebuild affected items' : ''}`;
  if (s.kind === 'build') return o.only_changed ? 'Only items whose patches changed' : 'Rebuild everything';
  return o.retries ? `Retry offline devices ${o.retries}×` : 'Skip offline devices';
}

function countsText(c) {
  const parts = Object.entries(c).map(([k, n]) => `${n} ${k}`);
  return parts.length ? parts.join(' · ') : 'never';
}

function renderList() {
  $('#list').innerHTML = html`${R.list.map(s => html`<div class="card sch" tabindex="0" data-id="${s.id}" ${R.cur && R.cur.id === s.id ? html`aria-current="true"` : ''} style="${s.enabled ? '' : 'opacity:.6'}"><div class="row between" style="flex-wrap:nowrap;align-items:flex-start"><div style="min-width:0"><div class="row"><h3>${s.name}</h3><span class="badge b-mute plain">${KIND[s.kind]}</span></div><p class="sm">${sentence(s.spec)} <span class="muted">· ${targetText(s.kind, s.target)}</span></p><p class="xs muted">${optionText(s)}</p><p class="xs muted" style="margin-top:.25rem">Next: <b>${s.enabled ? fmt.abs(s.next_run_at) : 'paused'}</b> ${s.enabled ? fmt.rel(s.next_run_at) : ''} · Last: ${s.last_run_at ? fmt.abs(s.last_run_at) : 'never'} <span class="badge ${s.last_counts.failed ? 'b-warn' : 'b-ok'}" style="height:1.125rem">${countsText(s.last_counts)}</span></p></div><div class="row" style="flex-wrap:nowrap"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" type="button" data-run="${s.id}">Run now</button><span class="switch"><input type="checkbox" data-en="${s.id}" ${s.enabled ? html`checked` : ''} aria-label="Enable ${s.name}"><span></span></span></div></div></div>`)}`;
}

function renderTimeline(now) {
  const t0 = Date.parse(now), span = 86400000;
  const ticks = R.list.filter(s => s.enabled).flatMap(s => s.next_runs.filter(r => Date.parse(r) - t0 < span).map(r => ({ s, r })));
  $('#tl').innerHTML = html`${ticks.map(({ s, r }) => html`<i class="k-${s.kind}" style="left:${Number(((Date.parse(r) - t0) / span) * 100).toFixed(2)}%" title="${s.name} ${fmt.abs(r)}"></i>`)}`;
  $('#tll').innerHTML = html`${R.list.filter(s => s.enabled).map(s => html`<li><i class="k-${s.kind}"></i>${s.name}</li>`)}`;
}

function chip(name, value, label, checked) {
  return html`<label class="chip"><input type="checkbox" name="${name}" value="${value}" ${checked ? html`checked` : ''}><span>${label}</span></label>`;
}

function renderTargets(t) {
  const has = (k, v) => !!(t && t[k] && t[k].includes(v));
  $('#tg-upstream').innerHTML = html`${chip('tu', 'all', `All sources (${R.sources.length})`, !t || t.all)}${R.sources.map(s => chip('tu', s.id, s.id, has('source_ids', s.id)))}`;
  $('#tg-build').innerHTML = html`${chip('tb', 'all', `All items (${R.items.length})`, !t || t.all)}${R.groups.map(g => chip('tb', `g${g.id}`, g.name, has('group_ids', g.id)))}${chip('tb', 'g0', 'Ungrouped', has('group_ids', 0))}<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" type="button" id="pick">Pick items…${t && t.item_ids ? ` (${t.item_ids.length})` : ''}</button>`;
  $('#tg-ota').innerHTML = html`${chip('to', 'all', 'All devices', !t || t.all)}${R.devices.map(d => chip('to', d.id, d.name, has('device_ids', d.id)))}`;
  R.pickItems = t && t.item_ids ? [...t.item_ids] : null;
}

const kindNow = () => $('[name=act]:checked').value;

function readSpec() {
  const f = $('[name=f]:checked').value;
  if (f === 'hours') return { every: 'hours', hours: Number($('#hh-n').value || $('#hh').value) };
  if (f === 'day') return { every: 'day', at: $('#tm').value };
  return { every: 'week', days: $$('#days input:checked').map(i => uiToSpec(Number(i.value))), at: $('#tm').value };
}

function readTarget(kind) {
  const vals = n => $$(`[name=${n}]:checked`).map(i => i.value);
  if (kind === 'upstream') { const v = vals('tu'); return v.includes('all') || !v.length ? { all: true } : { source_ids: v }; }
  if (kind === 'ota') { const v = vals('to'); return v.includes('all') || !v.length ? { all: true } : { device_ids: v.map(Number) }; }
  const v = vals('tb');
  if (R.pickItems && R.pickItems.length && !v.length) return { item_ids: R.pickItems };
  return v.includes('all') || !v.length ? { all: true } : { group_ids: v.map(x => Number(x.slice(1))) };
}

function readOptions(kind) {
  const notify = $('#notify').checked;
  if (kind === 'upstream') return { pull: $('#o-pull').checked, rebuild: $('#o-rebuild').checked, notify };
  if (kind === 'build') return { only_changed: $('[name=onlych]:checked').value === 'changed', notify };
  return { retries: Math.min(5, Math.max(0, Number($('#o-retries').value) || 0)), notify };
}

function showKind(kind) {
  $$('[data-act]').forEach(el => { el.hidden = el.dataset.act !== kind; });
  $$('[id^=tg-]').forEach(el => { el.hidden = el.id !== `tg-${kind}`; });
}

function showRepeat(f) {
  $('#every-h').hidden = f !== 'hours';
  $('#at').hidden = f === 'hours';
  $('#days').hidden = f !== 'week';
}

let pv = null;
function preview() {
  clearTimeout(pv);
  pv = setTimeout(async () => {
    const spec = readSpec();
    $('#human').textContent = `${sentence(spec)} · ${targetText(kindNow(), readTarget(kindNow()))}`;
    try {
      const { runs } = await api('POST', '/api/schedules/preview', { spec });
      $('#nexts').innerHTML = html`${runs.map(r => html`<li class="mono xs">${fmt.abs(r)} <span class="muted">${fmt.rel(r)}</span></li>`)}`;
    } catch (e) {
      $('#nexts').innerHTML = html`<li class="xs" style="color:hsl(var(--destructive))">${e.message}</li>`;
    }
  }, 300);
}

function edit(s) {
  R.cur = s;
  const kind = s ? s.kind : 'build', spec = s ? s.spec : { every: 'day', at: '04:00' }, o = s ? s.options : {};
  $('#sn').value = s ? s.name : '';
  $(`[name=act][value=${kind}]`).checked = true;
  $$('[name=act]').forEach(r => { r.disabled = !!s && r.value !== kind; });   // kind cannot change on PUT
  $('#o-pull').checked = o.pull !== false;
  $('#o-rebuild').checked = o.rebuild !== false;
  $(`[name=onlych][value=${o.only_changed ? 'changed' : 'all'}]`).checked = true;
  $('#o-retries').value = o.retries || 0;
  $('#notify').checked = o.notify !== false;
  $(`[name=f][value=${spec.every}]`).checked = true;
  if (spec.every === 'hours') { $('#hh').value = [1, 3, 6, 12, 24].includes(spec.hours) ? spec.hours : ''; $('#hh-n').value = spec.hours; }
  if (spec.at) $('#tm').value = spec.at;
  $$('#days input').forEach(i => { i.checked = spec.every === 'week' && spec.days.map(specToUi).includes(Number(i.value)); });
  renderTargets(s ? s.target : null);
  showKind(kind);
  showRepeat(spec.every);
  $('#del').hidden = $('#runnow').hidden = !s;
  $('#ed').textContent = s ? 'Edit schedule' : 'New schedule';
  renderList();
  preview();
}

async function load() {
  const [{ schedules }, settings] = await Promise.all([api('GET', '/api/schedules'), api('GET', '/api/settings')]);
  R.list = schedules;
  $('#srvtime').textContent = `Server time ${new Date(settings.server.now).toLocaleString([], { timeZone: settings.server.tz, weekday: 'short', month: 'numeric', day: 'numeric', hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' })} · ${settings.server.tz}`;
  renderList();
  renderTimeline(settings.server.now);
}

async function save() {
  const kind = kindNow();
  const body = { name: $('#sn').value.trim(), kind, spec: readSpec(), target: readTarget(kind), options: readOptions(kind) };
  try {
    if (R.cur) { const { kind: _k, ...rest } = body; await act('PUT', `/api/schedules/${R.cur.id}`, rest, 'Schedule saved'); }
    else { const r = await act('POST', '/api/schedules', body, 'Schedule saved'); R.cur = { id: r.id }; }
    await load();
    edit(R.list.find(s => s.id === R.cur.id) || null);
  } catch (_) { /* shown */ }
}

$('#edc').addEventListener('change', e => {
  if (e.target.name === 'act') showKind(e.target.value);
  if (e.target.name === 'f') showRepeat(e.target.value);
  if (e.target.id === 'hh' && e.target.value) $('#hh-n').value = e.target.value;
  if (e.target.name && /^t[ubo]$/.test(e.target.name)) {
    const all = $(`[name=${e.target.name}][value=all]`);
    if (e.target === all && all.checked) $$(`[name=${e.target.name}]`).forEach(i => { if (i !== all) i.checked = false; });
    else if (e.target !== all && e.target.checked) { all.checked = false; R.pickItems = null; }
  }
  preview();
});
$('#edc').addEventListener('input', e => { if (e.target.id === 'tm' || e.target.id === 'hh-n') preview(); });
$('#edc').addEventListener('click', async e => {
  if (e.target.id !== 'pick') return;
  const dlg = $('#dlg-items');
  $('#pick-list').innerHTML = html`${R.items.map(it => html`<label class="row sm"><input type="checkbox" class="cb" value="${it.id}" ${R.pickItems && R.pickItems.includes(it.id) ? html`checked` : ''}> ${it.label}</label>`)}`;
  dlg.showModal();
  dlg.addEventListener('close', () => {
    if (dlg.returnValue !== 'ok') return;
    R.pickItems = $$('#pick-list input:checked').map(i => Number(i.value));
    $$('[name=tb]').forEach(i => { i.checked = false; });
    e.target.textContent = `Pick items… (${R.pickItems.length})`;
    preview();
  }, { once: true });
});
$('#list').addEventListener('click', async e => {
  const run = e.target.closest('[data-run]'), en = e.target.closest('[data-en]'), card = e.target.closest('.sch');
  try {
    if (run) { const s = R.list.find(x => x.id === Number(run.dataset.run)); await act('POST', `/api/schedules/${s.id}/run`, {}, `Started: ${s.name} — see Builds`); return load(); }
    if (en) { await act('PUT', `/api/schedules/${en.dataset.en}`, { enabled: en.checked }); return load(); }
    if (card) edit(R.list.find(x => x.id === Number(card.dataset.id)));
  } catch (_) { load(); }
});
$('#save').addEventListener('click', save);
$('#cancel').addEventListener('click', () => edit(R.cur && R.cur.name ? R.cur : null));
$('#new').addEventListener('click', () => edit(null));
$('#runnow').addEventListener('click', () => act('POST', `/api/schedules/${R.cur.id}/run`, {}, 'Running now — see Builds').then(load).catch(() => {}));
$('#del').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  if (!confirm(`Delete schedule "${R.cur.name}"?`)) return;
  try { await act('DELETE', `/api/schedules/${R.cur.id}`, {}, 'Schedule deleted'); await load(); edit(null); } catch (_) { /* shown */ }
});

(async () => {
  shell();
  const [{ sources }, cat, { devices }] = await Promise.all([api('GET', '/api/sources'), api('GET', '/api/catalog'), api('GET', '/api/devices')]);
  R.sources = sources;
  R.groups = cat.groups;
  R.items = [...cat.groups.flatMap(g => g.items), ...cat.ungrouped];
  R.devices = devices;
  await load();
  edit(R.list[0] || null);
})().catch(e => toast(e.message, 'error'));
```

`schedules.html` also carries `<dialog id="dlg-items"><form method="dialog" class="card stack"><div class="card-h"><h2>Pick items</h2></div><div class="card-c stack-sm" id="pick-list" style="max-height:60vh;overflow:auto"></div><div class="card-f"><button class="btn btn-outline" value="cancel">Cancel</button><button class="btn" value="ok">Use</button></div></form></dialog>` and timeline tick colours in the page style: `.tl i{position:absolute;top:0;bottom:0;width:3px;border-radius:2px}.k-upstream{background:hsl(var(--warn))}.k-build{background:hsl(var(--info))}.k-ota{background:hsl(var(--accent))}` (keep the mockup `.tl` container rule).

- [ ] **Step 4: Run** — `node --check local/webui/static/schedules.js` → exit 0; `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` → `OK`; the cron grep prints `0`.
