```bash
git add local/webui/static/schedules.html local/webui/static/schedules.js && git commit -m "feat(webui): schedules page with editor, preview and timeline" -- local/webui/static/schedules.html local/webui/static/schedules.js
```

#### Task P10.2: Devices page

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/static/devices.html`, `local/webui/static/devices.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET /api/devices`, `GET /api/devices/discovered`, `POST /api/devices/pair`, `POST /api/devices/{id}/connect`, `PATCH /api/devices/{id}`, `DELETE /api/devices/{id}`, `PUT /api/devices/{id}/assignments`, `POST /api/devices/{id}/ota`, `POST /api/ota/{job_id}/force-reinstall {install_id}`, `POST /api/ota/{job_id}/downgrade {install_id}`, `GET /api/jobs?kind=ota[&device_id=]`, `GET /api/catalog`, `GET /api/settings` (`server.network_mode`).

**Behavior (spec L1168–1190, D4, D6, §4.1–4.2, F3):**
- `devices.html` = `M/devices.html` body lines 165–218 ported; second style block `M/devices.html:163-164` in the head; the "Preview host: Linux / macOS" toggle and any latency text are removed (spec L1173, F3). Containers: host line `#hostline`, device list `#devs`, "OTA all online" `#ota-all`, discovered list `#disc` with `#scan` status, manual form `#man` (fields `#m-host`, `#m-pport`, `#m-code` (6-box OTP group `#m-otp`), `#m-cport`), pair dialog `#dlg-pair` (title `#pt`, OTP `#d-otp`, connect port `#d-cport`), assignment dialog `#dlg-assign` (`#as-list`), OTA history `#ota tbody` with device filter `#of`.
- Host line: `Network mode: <host|bridge> · discovery runs on every platform; when nothing is found, enter the phone's IP and ports below` (spec L1172; no branch on the mode in JS).
- Device card per device: name (inline rename → `PATCH {name}`), model, `Android <android_release>` (`sdk` in title), `host:connect_port`, ABI (`abi_list` first entry), last seen (`fmt.rel`), online dot; switch "Install after build" (`PATCH {ota_after_build}`); assigned apps: per assignment item label, `installed.version_name → available.version_name`, ✓ when not `pending`; dependency rows: display name, `installed_tag` or `not installed`, `available_tag`, `required by <labels>`, text `installs first`; buttons "OTA now · <pending count> pending" (disabled offline, title = offline guidance), "Reconnect" (`POST …/connect {}`; 409 `{"error":"DEVICE_OFFLINE"}` → prompt for a new port with `prompt('New connect port shown on the phone')` and retry with `{connect_port}`), "Edit apps…" (dialog listing all catalog items with checkboxes → `PUT …/assignments {item_ids}`; a 400 shows the server message about two items with one package), "Unpair" (`confirm('Remove <name>? On the phone, also tap Forget under Wireless debugging.')` → `DELETE`). Offline cards show `Turn on Wireless debugging on the phone, then Reconnect` (spec L1177).
- Last OTA result on each card: `GET /api/jobs?kind=ota&device_id=<id>&limit=1` → outcome + time.
- Discovered (poll `GET /api/devices/discovered` every 10 s while visible, spec L884): pairing rows `name · host:port · Pairing mode` with "Pair" (opens `#dlg-pair` pre-filled), connect rows `name · host:port · Paired` (or `Known: <device name>`); empty → `No devices discovered — enter IP and ports below` (D4).
- OTP input (6 boxes): typing a digit moves focus to the next box, Backspace on an empty box moves back, pasting 6 digits fills all; value = the 6 digits.
- Pair (dialog or manual form): `POST /api/devices/pair {host, pair_port, code, connect_port?}` (`connect_port` omitted when empty); success → toast `Paired <device name>`, reload; failure → toast with the server `error` text (e.g. `PAIR_FAILED: <adb line>`).
- OTA history table (newest 50, filter by device): time, device, trigger, installed list (`installs` with `result`, e.g. `YouTube 20.12.46 ✓`, `already current, skipped` for `up_to_date`), `n / n`, outcome badge (`Skipped · offline` for `skipped_offline`); rows with an install `error_code === 'SIGNATURE_MISMATCH'` get "Uninstall & retry" → `confirm('Uninstall <package> and install the patched app? All app data on the phone is lost.')` → `POST /api/ota/<job>/force-reinstall {install_id}`; `VERSION_DOWNGRADE` rows get "Install with downgrade" → `POST /api/ota/<job>/downgrade {install_id}`.
- OTA all online → `POST /api/devices/<id>/ota {}` for every online device.

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `node --check local/webui/static/devices.js` exit 0; `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` exit 0.
- [ ] `grep -ciE 'latency|Preview host' local/webui/static/devices.html local/webui/static/devices.js` prints `0` for both; `grep -c 'force-reinstall' local/webui/static/devices.js` ≥ 1; `grep -c 'All app data on the phone is lost' local/webui/static/devices.js` = 1; `grep -c 'network_mode ===' local/webui/static/devices.js` prints `0`.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing check** — `ls local/webui/static/devices.js` → `No such file or directory`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Markup** — `devices.html` per Behavior; the OTP groups are six `<input class="input otp" inputmode="numeric" maxlength="1" aria-label="Digit N">` inside `<div class="row otpg" id="m-otp">` / `id="d-otp"`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/static/devices.js`

```js
import { $, $$, act, api, every, fmt, html, shell, toast } from '/static/app.js';

const D = { devices: [], items: new Map(), last: new Map() };
const label = id => (D.items.get(id) || {}).label || `item ${id}`;
const otpValue = g => $$('input', g).map(i => i.value).join('');

function wireOtp(g) {
  const boxes = $$('input', g);
  boxes.forEach((b, i) => {
    b.addEventListener('input', () => { b.value = b.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 1); if (b.value && boxes[i + 1]) boxes[i + 1].focus(); });
    b.addEventListener('keydown', e => { if (e.key === 'Backspace' && !b.value && boxes[i - 1]) boxes[i - 1].focus(); });
    b.addEventListener('paste', e => {
      const d = (e.clipboardData.getData('text') || '').replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 6);
      if (d.length !== 6) return;
      e.preventDefault();
      boxes.forEach((x, k) => { x.value = d[k]; });
      boxes[5].focus();
    });
  });
}

function card(d) {
  const pending = d.assignments.filter(a => a.pending).length;
  const last = D.last.get(d.id);
  return html`<section class="card" data-dev="${d.id}"><div class="card-h"><div class="row between"><div class="row"><span class="dot ${d.online ? 'on' : 'off'}" aria-hidden="true"></span><h2 contenteditable="true" spellcheck="false" data-rename title="Click to rename">${d.name}</h2><span class="xs muted">${d.model} · Android ${d.android_release}</span></div>
<label class="row sm"><span class="switch"><input type="checkbox" data-oab ${d.ota_after_build ? html`checked` : ''}><span></span></span>Install after build</label></div>
<p class="xs muted mono">${d.host}:${d.connect_port} · ${(d.abi_list || '').split(',')[0]} · last seen ${fmt.rel(d.last_seen_at)}</p>${d.online ? '' : html`<div class="alert warn"><span>Turn on Wireless debugging on the phone, then Reconnect.</span></div>`}</div>
<div class="card-c stack-sm"><ul class="alist">${d.assignments.map(a => html`<li><span>${a.pending ? '' : '✓'}</span><span>${label(a.item_id)} <span class="xs muted mono">${a.installed ? a.installed.version_name : 'not installed'}${a.available ? ` → ${a.available.version_name}` : ''}</span></span><span class="xs ${a.pending ? 'newer' : 'muted'}">${a.pending ? 'pending' : 'current'}</span></li>`)}</ul>
${d.dependencies.length ? html`<ul class="alist">${d.dependencies.map(x => html`<li><span></span><span>${x.display_name} <span class="xs muted mono">${x.installed_tag || 'not installed'} → ${x.available_tag || '—'}</span><span class="xs muted"> · required by ${x.required_by.map(label).join(', ')} · installs first</span></span><span></span></li>`)}</ul>` : ''}
<p class="xs muted">Last OTA: ${last ? html`${last.detail ? last.detail.outcome || last.status : last.status} · ${fmt.rel(last.finished_at || last.created_at)}` : 'none'}</p></div>
<div class="card-f"><button class="btn" type="button" data-ota ${d.online ? '' : html`disabled title="Device offline: turn on Wireless debugging, then Reconnect"`}>OTA now · ${pending} pending</button><button class="btn btn-outline" type="button" data-reconnect>Reconnect</button><button class="btn btn-ghost" type="button" data-assign>Edit apps…</button><button class="btn btn-ghost danger" type="button" data-unpair style="margin-left:auto">Unpair</button></div></section>`;
}

async function loadDevices() {
  const { devices } = await api('GET', '/api/devices');
  D.devices = devices;
  await Promise.all(devices.map(async d => {
    const { jobs } = await api('GET', `/api/jobs?kind=ota&device_id=${d.id}&limit=1`);
    D.last.set(d.id, jobs[0]);
  }));
  $('#devs').innerHTML = html`${devices.length ? devices.map(card) : html`<p class="sm muted">No paired devices yet.</p>`}`;
  $('#of').innerHTML = html`<option value="">All devices</option>${devices.map(d => html`<option value="${d.id}">${d.name}</option>`)}`;
}

async function loadDiscovered() {
  const r = await api('GET', '/api/devices/discovered');
  const rows = [...r.pairing.map(p => ({ ...p, mode: 'pair' })), ...r.connect.map(c => ({ ...c, mode: 'conn' }))];
  $('#scan').textContent = `Scanned ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}`;
  $('#disc').innerHTML = html`${rows.length ? rows.map(x => html`<li><span class="dot on" aria-hidden="true"></span><span>${x.name} <span class="xs muted mono">${x.host}:${x.port}</span> <span class="badge b-mute plain">${x.mode === 'pair' ? 'Pairing mode' : x.device_id ? `Known: ${(D.devices.find(d => d.id === x.device_id) || {}).name || x.device_id}` : 'Paired'}</span></span>${x.mode === 'pair' ? html`<button class="btn btn-sm" type="button" data-pair="${x.host}" data-port="${x.port}">Pair</button>` : html`<span></span>`}</li>`) : html`<li><span></span><span class="sm muted">No devices discovered — enter IP and ports below</span><span></span></li>`}`;
}

async function loadHistory() {
  const dev = $('#of').value;
  const { jobs } = await api('GET', `/api/jobs?kind=ota&limit=50${dev ? `&device_id=${dev}` : ''}`);
  $('#ota tbody').innerHTML = html`${jobs.map(j => {
    const d = j.detail || { installs: [] };
    const ok = d.installs.filter(i => i.result !== 'failed').length;
    const name = (D.devices.find(x => x.id === j.target.device_id) || {}).name || `device ${j.target.device_id}`;
    const outcome = d.outcome === 'skipped_offline' ? 'Skipped · offline' : d.outcome || j.status;
    return html`<tr><td>${fmt.abs(j.created_at)}</td><td>${name}</td><td class="sm muted">${j.trigger}${d.mode && d.mode !== 'normal' ? ` · ${d.mode}` : ''}</td><td class="sm">${d.installs.map(i => html`<div>${i.package} ${i.version_name || ''} ${i.result === 'installed' ? '✓' : i.result === 'up_to_date' ? '· already current, skipped' : html`<span style="color:hsl(var(--destructive))">✗ ${i.error_code}</span>`}${i.error_code === 'SIGNATURE_MISMATCH' ? html` <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm danger" type="button" data-force="${j.id}" data-install="${i.id}" data-pkg="${i.package}">Uninstall &amp; retry</button>` : ''}${i.error_code === 'VERSION_DOWNGRADE' ? html` <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" type="button" data-down="${j.id}" data-install="${i.id}">Install with downgrade</button>` : ''}</div>`)}</td><td class="mono">${ok} / ${d.installs.length}</td><td><span class="badge ${j.status === 'success' ? 'b-ok' : j.status === 'failed' ? 'b-err' : 'b-mute'}">${outcome}</span></td></tr>`;
  })}`;
}

async function pair(body) {
  try {
    const r = await api('POST', '/api/devices/pair', body);
    toast(`Paired ${r.device.name}`);
    await loadDevices();
  } catch (e) {
    toast(e.message, 'error');   // e.g. PAIR_FAILED: <adb line>
  }
}

async function reconnect(d) {
  try {
    await api('POST', `/api/devices/${d.id}/connect`, {});
    toast(`${d.name} connected`);
  } catch (e) {
    if (e.status === 409 && e.data && e.data.error === 'DEVICE_OFFLINE') {
      const port = prompt('New connect port shown on the phone (Wireless debugging → IP address & Port)');
      if (port) await act('POST', `/api/devices/${d.id}/connect`, { connect_port: Number(port) }, `${d.name} connected`).catch(() => {});
    } else {
      toast(e.message, 'error');
    }
  }
  await loadDevices();
}

document.addEventListener('click', async e => {
  const sec = e.target.closest('[data-dev]');
  const d = sec && D.devices.find(x => x.id === Number(sec.dataset.dev));
  try {
    if (e.target.closest('[data-ota]')) await act('POST', `/api/devices/${d.id}/ota`, {}, `OTA queued on ${d.name}`);
    if (e.target.closest('[data-reconnect]')) return reconnect(d);
    if (e.target.closest('[data-unpair]')) {
      if (!confirm(`Remove ${d.name}? On the phone, also tap Forget under Wireless debugging.`)) return;
      await act('DELETE', `/api/devices/${d.id}`, {}, 'Device removed');
    }
    if (e.target.closest('[data-assign]')) {
      const mine = new Set(d.assignments.map(a => a.item_id));
      $('#as-list').innerHTML = html`${[...D.items.values()].map(it => html`<label class="row sm"><input type="checkbox" class="cb" value="${it.id}" ${mine.has(it.id) ? html`checked` : ''}> ${it.label} <span class="xs muted mono">${it.package}</span></label>`)}`;
      const dlg = $('#dlg-assign');
      dlg.showModal();
      dlg.addEventListener('close', () => {
        if (dlg.returnValue !== 'ok') return;
        act('PUT', `/api/devices/${d.id}/assignments`, { item_ids: $$('#as-list input:checked').map(i => Number(i.value)) }, 'Apps saved').then(loadDevices).catch(() => {});
      }, { once: true });
      return;
    }
    const p = e.target.closest('[data-pair]');
    if (p) {
      $('#pt').textContent = `Pair ${p.dataset.pair}:${p.dataset.port}`;
      $$('#d-otp input').forEach(i => { i.value = ''; });
      const dlg = $('#dlg-pair');
      dlg.showModal();
      dlg.addEventListener('close', () => {
        if (dlg.returnValue !== 'ok') return;
        const body = { host: p.dataset.pair, pair_port: Number(p.dataset.port), code: otpValue($('#d-otp')) };
        if ($('#d-cport').value) body.connect_port = Number($('#d-cport').value);
        pair(body);
      }, { once: true });
      return;
    }
    const f = e.target.closest('[data-force]');
    if (f) {
      if (!confirm(`Uninstall ${f.dataset.pkg} and install the patched app? All app data on the phone is lost.`)) return;
      await act('POST', `/api/ota/${f.dataset.force}/force-reinstall`, { install_id: Number(f.dataset.install) }, 'Reinstall queued');
    }
    const dn = e.target.closest('[data-down]');
    if (dn) await act('POST', `/api/ota/${dn.dataset.down}/downgrade`, { install_id: Number(dn.dataset.install) }, 'Downgrade install queued');
    if (sec || f || dn) { await loadDevices(); await loadHistory(); }
  } catch (_) { /* act() showed it */ }
});

document.addEventListener('change', e => {
  const sec = e.target.closest('[data-dev]');
  if (sec && e.target.matches('[data-oab]')) act('PATCH', `/api/devices/${sec.dataset.dev}`, { ota_after_build: e.target.checked }, 'Saved').catch(loadDevices);
});
document.addEventListener('focusout', e => {
  const h = e.target.closest('[data-rename]');
  if (!h) return;
  const sec = h.closest('[data-dev]'), name = h.textContent.trim();
  const d = D.devices.find(x => x.id === Number(sec.dataset.dev));
  if (name && name !== d.name) act('PATCH', `/api/devices/${d.id}`, { name }, 'Renamed').then(loadDevices).catch(loadDevices);
});
$('#man').addEventListener('submit', e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const body = { host: $('#m-host').value.trim(), pair_port: Number($('#m-pport').value), code: otpValue($('#m-otp')) };
  if ($('#m-cport').value) body.connect_port = Number($('#m-cport').value);
  pair(body);
});
$('#ota-all').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const online = D.devices.filter(d => d.online);
  for (const d of online) await act('POST', `/api/devices/${d.id}/ota`, {}).catch(() => {});
  toast(`OTA queued on ${online.length} device${online.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
});
$('#of').addEventListener('change', () => loadHistory().catch(() => {}));

(async () => {
  shell();
  wireOtp($('#m-otp'));
  wireOtp($('#d-otp'));
  const [cat, s] = await Promise.all([api('GET', '/api/catalog'), api('GET', '/api/settings')]);
  [...cat.groups.flatMap(g => g.items), ...cat.ungrouped].forEach(it => D.items.set(it.id, it));
  $('#hostline').textContent = `Network mode: ${s.server.network_mode || 'unknown'} · discovery runs on every platform; when nothing is found, enter the phone's IP and ports below`;
  await loadDevices();
  await loadHistory();
  await loadDiscovered().catch(() => {});
  every(10000, () => loadDiscovered().catch(() => {}));
  every(15000, () => { if (!document.querySelector('dialog[open]') && !document.activeElement.closest('[data-rename]')) loadDevices().catch(() => {}); });
})().catch(e => toast(e.message, 'error'));
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Run** — `node --check local/webui/static/devices.js` → exit 0; `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` → `OK`; greps as listed.

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add local/webui/static/devices.html local/webui/static/devices.js && git commit -m "feat(webui): devices page with pairing, assignments, ota and history" -- local/webui/static/devices.html local/webui/static/devices.js
```

#### Task P10.3: Settings page

**Files:**
- Create: `local/webui/static/settings.html`, `local/webui/static/settings.js`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `GET/PUT /api/notify`, `POST /api/notify/test`, `GET /api/settings[?recheck=1]`, `PUT /api/settings {job_history_count, base_url, artifact_retention}`, `GET /api/catalog`, `GET /api/upstreams`, `POST /api/upstreams/{id}/pull`, `POST /api/auth/password {old, new}`.

**Behavior (spec L1191–1205, L1219–1221):**
- `settings.html` = `M/settings.html` body lines 166–200 ported; second style block `M/settings.html:163-165` in the head; the "Stored encrypted" text becomes "Secrets are write-only and shown masked"; the "Clean up now" button is removed (saving cleans, spec L1200); the password minimum hint says 10 characters. Containers: channel radio cards `[name=ch]` (`telegram|discord|ntfy`) with field panels `[data-ch=…]` (telegram `#tg-token`, `#tg-chat`; discord `#dc-url`; ntfy `#nt-url`, `#nt-topic`, `#nt-token`), `#n-en` enabled switch, event chips `[name=ev]` values `upstream.new_version`, `build.succeeded`, `build.failed`, `ota` (= `ota.done` + `ota.failed`), `device.offline` (default off), `#n-save`, `#n-test`, `#n-last`; artifacts card `#keep`, `#est`, `#hist-n` (job history count), `#base` (base URL), `#a-save`, disk bars `#disk`; GitHub card `#gh-state`, `#gh-hint`, `#gh-path`, `#gh-exp`, `#gh-scopes`, gauge `#gh-gauge` + `#gh-reset`, `#gh-60` (shown when unauthenticated), `#gh-recheck`, `#gh-cli` ("Update CLI"); password card `#pw-old`, `#pw-new`, `#pw-new2`, `#pw-meter`, `#pw-err`, `#pw-save`; appearance toggle `data-theme-toggle`.
- Notify: load `GET /api/notify`; secret fields show the masked value; saving sends the field values as shown (the masked value keeps the stored secret, Plan-fixed "notify secret round trip"); events chip `ota` maps to both `ota.done` and `ota.failed`; Save → `PUT /api/notify {type, enabled, config, events}`; Send test → `POST /api/notify/test` → toast `Delivered` or `Failed: <detail>`, then reload `last_test` (`Last test: <fmt.abs(at)> · delivered|failed`).
- Artifacts: keep count input 1–100; live estimate `≈ <items × variants × keep> APKs (~<fmt.bytes(that × disk.artifacts / artifacts_count)>)` with items/variants from `GET /api/catalog` (`abis.length` per item); job history count 1–100000; base URL; Save → `PUT /api/settings` (only changed keys), toast `Saved` (a changed keep count also runs cleanup server-side); disk bars APKs (`artifacts`), download cache (`apk_cache`), patches (`upstream`), logs, free.
- GitHub: state badge (`authenticated` ok, `unauthenticated` warn + 60/h hint, `rejected` err, `absent` mute + text "Token file not found at <path>"), `token_hint`, path, expiry (`expires_at` or `—`), scopes, gauge `remaining / limit` with `reset_at` relative; Re-check → `GET /api/settings?recheck=1`; Update CLI → `GET /api/upstreams` then pull each `kind === 'cli'` row (`POST …/pull {}`), toast `CLI update queued (<n>)`.
- Password: meter = score 0–4 (length ≥ 10, ≥ 14, mixed case, digit, symbol; capped 4) shown as `Weak|Fair|Good|Strong`; mismatch → `#pw-err` "Passwords do not match"; new < 10 → "At least 10 characters"; Save → `POST /api/auth/password` → toast `Password changed · <signed_out> other sessions signed out`; 403 → "Current password is wrong".

**Acceptance criteria:**
- [ ] `node --check local/webui/static/settings.js` exit 0; `T_PY 'test_ui_static.py'` exit 0.
- [ ] `grep -ciE 'stored encrypted|clean up now' local/webui/static/settings.html` prints `0`; `grep -c "'ota.done', 'ota.failed'" local/webui/static/settings.js` ≥ 1; `grep -c 'recheck=1' local/webui/static/settings.js` ≥ 1.

**Steps:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing check** — `ls local/webui/static/settings.js` → `No such file or directory`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Markup** — `settings.html` per Behavior.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** — `local/webui/static/settings.js`

```js
import { $, $$, act, api, fmt, html, shell, toast } from '/static/app.js';

const FIELDS = {
  telegram: { bot_token: '#tg-token', chat_id: '#tg-chat' },
  discord: { webhook_url: '#dc-url' },
  ntfy: { server_url: '#nt-url', topic: '#nt-topic', token: '#nt-token' },
};
const Z = { settings: null, items: [], notify: null };

function showChannel(t) {
  $$('[data-ch]').forEach(p => { p.hidden = p.dataset.ch !== t; });
}

async function loadNotify() {
  const n = await api('GET', '/api/notify');
  Z.notify = n;
  const type = n.type || 'telegram';
  $(`[name=ch][value=${type}]`).checked = true;
  showChannel(type);
  for (const [k, sel] of Object.entries(FIELDS[type])) $(sel).value = (n.config && n.config[k]) || '';
  $('#n-en').checked = !!n.enabled;
  const ev = new Set(n.type ? n.events : ['upstream.new_version', 'build.succeeded', 'build.failed', 'ota.done', 'ota.failed']);
  $$('[name=ev]').forEach(c => { c.checked = c.value === 'ota' ? ev.has('ota.done') || ev.has('ota.failed') : ev.has(c.value); });
  $('#n-last').textContent = n.last_test ? `Last test: ${fmt.abs(n.last_test.at)} · ${n.last_test.ok ? 'delivered' : `failed (${n.last_test.detail})`}` : 'Last test: never';
}

async function saveNotify() {
  const type = $('[name=ch]:checked').value;
  const config = {};
  for (const [k, sel] of Object.entries(FIELDS[type])) if ($(sel).value.trim()) config[k] = $(sel).value.trim();
  const events = $$('[name=ev]:checked').flatMap(c => (c.value === 'ota' ? ['ota.done', 'ota.failed'] : [c.value]));
  await act('PUT', '/api/notify', { type, enabled: $('#n-en').checked, config, events }, 'Notification channel saved');
  await loadNotify();
}

function renderEstimate() {
  const s = Z.settings, keep = Number($('#keep').value) || 0, d = s.server.disk;
  const apks = Z.items.reduce((n, it) => n + Math.max(1, (it.abis || []).length), 0) * keep;
  const avg = d.artifacts_count ? d.artifacts / d.artifacts_count : 0;
  $('#est').textContent = `≈ ${apks} APKs${avg ? ` (~${fmt.bytes(apks * avg)})` : ''}`;
}

function renderSettings() {
  const s = Z.settings, d = s.server.disk, g = s.github;
  $('#keep').value = s.artifact_retention;
  $('#hist-n').value = s.job_history_count;
  $('#base').value = s.base_url;
  renderEstimate();
  const parts = [['APKs', d.artifacts], ['Download cache', d.apk_cache], ['Patches', d.upstream], ['Logs', d.logs], ['Free', d.free]];
  $('#disk').innerHTML = html`${parts.map(([n, v]) => html`<div class="row between xs"><span>${n}</span><span class="mono">${fmt.bytes(v)}</span></div><div class="progress"><i style="width:${Number(d.total ? (100 * v) / d.total : 0).toFixed(1)}%"></i></div>`)}`;
  const cls = { authenticated: 'b-ok', unauthenticated: 'b-warn', rejected: 'b-err', absent: 'b-mute' }[g.state] || 'b-mute';
  $('#gh-state').className = `badge ${cls}`;
  $('#gh-state').textContent = g.state + (g.error ? ` · ${g.error}` : '');
  $('#gh-hint').textContent = g.token_hint || (g.state === 'absent' ? `Token file not found at ${g.path}` : 'empty token');
  $('#gh-path').textContent = g.path;
  $('#gh-exp').textContent = g.expires_at || '—';
  $('#gh-scopes').textContent = g.scopes || '—';
  $('#gh-gauge').style.width = `${g.limit ? Math.round((100 * g.remaining) / g.limit) : 0}%`;
  $('#gh-reset').textContent = g.limit ? `${g.remaining} / ${g.limit} left · resets ${fmt.rel(g.reset_at)}` : '—';
  $('#gh-60').hidden = g.state !== 'unauthenticated';
}

async function loadSettings(recheck) {
  Z.settings = await api('GET', `/api/settings${recheck ? '?recheck=1' : ''}`);
  renderSettings();
}

async function saveSettings() {
  const s = Z.settings, body = {};
  const keep = Number($('#keep').value), hist = Number($('#hist-n').value), base = $('#base').value.trim();
  if (keep !== s.artifact_retention) body.artifact_retention = keep;
  if (hist !== s.job_history_count) body.job_history_count = hist;
  if (base !== s.base_url) body.base_url = base;
  if (!Object.keys(body).length) return toast('Nothing changed');
  Z.settings = await act('PUT', '/api/settings', body, 'Saved');
  renderSettings();
}

function score(p) {
  let n = 0;
  if (p.length >= 10) n++;
  if (p.length >= 14) n++;
  if (/[a-z]/.test(p) && /[A-Z]/.test(p)) n++;
  if (/\d/.test(p)) n++;
  if (/[^A-Za-z0-9]/.test(p)) n++;
  return Math.min(4, n);
}

function checkPw() {
  const a = $('#pw-new').value, b = $('#pw-new2').value, s = score(a);
  $('#pw-meter').value = s;
  $('#pw-meter').title = ['Weak', 'Weak', 'Fair', 'Good', 'Strong'][s];
  $('#pw-meter-l').textContent = a ? ['Weak', 'Weak', 'Fair', 'Good', 'Strong'][s] : '';
  const err = a && a.length < 10 ? 'At least 10 characters' : b && a !== b ? 'Passwords do not match' : '';
  $('#pw-err').textContent = err;
  $('#pw-err').hidden = !err;
  $('#pw-save').disabled = !!err || !a || a !== b || !$('#pw-old').value;
}

$$('[name=ch]').forEach(r => r.addEventListener('change', () => showChannel(r.value)));
$('#n-save').addEventListener('click', () => saveNotify().catch(() => {}));
$('#n-test').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  try {
    const r = await api('POST', '/api/notify/test', {});
    toast(r.ok ? 'Delivered' : `Failed: ${r.detail}`, r.ok ? 'ok' : 'error');
  } catch (e) { toast(e.message, 'error'); }
  loadNotify().catch(() => {});
});
$('#keep').addEventListener('input', renderEstimate);
$('#a-save').addEventListener('click', () => saveSettings().catch(() => {}));
$('#gh-recheck').addEventListener('click', () => loadSettings(true).then(() => toast('Re-checked')).catch(() => {}));
$('#gh-cli').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const { upstreams } = await api('GET', '/api/upstreams');
  const cli = upstreams.filter(u => u.kind === 'cli');
  for (const u of cli) await act('POST', `/api/upstreams/${u.id}/pull`, {}).catch(() => {});
  toast(`CLI update queued (${cli.length})`);
});
['#pw-old', '#pw-new', '#pw-new2'].forEach(s => $(s).addEventListener('input', checkPw));
$('#pw-save').addEventListener('click', async () => {
  try {
    const r = await api('POST', '/api/auth/password', { old: $('#pw-old').value, new: $('#pw-new').value });
    toast(`Password changed · ${r.signed_out} other session${r.signed_out === 1 ? '' : 's'} signed out`);
    ['#pw-old', '#pw-new', '#pw-new2'].forEach(s => { $(s).value = ''; });
    checkPw();
  } catch (e) {
    toast(e.status === 403 ? 'Current password is wrong' : e.message, 'error');
  }
});

(async () => {
  shell();
  const cat = await api('GET', '/api/catalog');
  Z.items = [...cat.groups.flatMap(g => g.items), ...cat.ungrouped];
  await Promise.all([loadNotify(), loadSettings(false)]);
  checkPw();
})().catch(e => toast(e.message, 'error'));
```

The password meter in the markup is `<meter id="pw-meter" min="0" max="4" low="2" high="3" optimum="4" value="0"></meter><span class="xs muted" id="pw-meter-l"></span>`; `#pw-err` is `<p class="hint" id="pw-err" hidden style="color:hsl(var(--destructive))"></p>`.

```bash
git add local/webui/static/settings.html local/webui/static/settings.js && git commit -m "feat(webui): settings page with notifications, artifacts, token and password" -- local/webui/static/settings.html local/webui/static/settings.js
```

```markdown
#### Q: How do I change patches, patch options or the app version?

Patch selection, patch options and version pins live in the web UI ("Relume", `local/`): open an app, then use the Patches, Options and Version tabs. To make GitHub Actions build with the same settings, export the catalog and commit it:

`docker compose -f local/docker-compose.yml exec app python3 -m webui export-seed > local/webui/catalog-seed.json`
```
Lines 15–22 (fork, enable workflows, images 1 and 2) stay unchanged.

| after round 2, P7 merged and pushed | `workflow_dispatch` `source=hushfacebook` → asset + label on release `all`; rerun without `force` → `plan` `[]` | P7 |
| after round 2 | pull Revenge + LSPatch, build Discord, `apksigner verify --print-certs` shows the `lspatch-signing.p12` cert, one APK under `assets/lspatch/modules/`, OTA and open | P8 |

### Uncertainties

- Real Android mDNS replies (legacy unicast) are not captured; Task 5.3 uses constructed packets. T4 shows whether a real responder answers.
- `ro.serialno` may be empty on some phones under `adb shell`; Task 5.4 falls back to `ro.boot.serialno` and else answers 409 `DEVICE_OFFLINE`.
- Task 8.2 positive cases assert only `status < 300` for P2's create routes, because P2's success codes and full PUT body rules are P2's.
- P8 Task 8.1 is written against P3's merged-plan code (row X13); a later P3 rename of `new_env`/`make_job`/`run_runner` or of the `FAIL_CODE`/`LAST_ERR` contract changes Task 8.1 Steps 2, 4, 5.

## Execution partition (2 rounds)

The member list, models, owned files per round (disjoint within each round), start order, integration gates, reviewer checklists, user-run steps and the round-1 → round-2 hand-off are in `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-19-webui-execution.md`. That file is the single source for ownership; package sections above list the files each task creates or changes.

### Requirements → packages

| 3 Wireless ADB, one-click OTA, scheduled OTA | P5 (pair, OTA job), P1 (schedules kind `ota`), P3 (adb in image), P10 (Devices page, OTA schedules) |

| 4b Notifications: one of Telegram / Discord / ntfy | P5 (notifier), P10 (Settings channel card) |
| 4c Web editing of patch selection, options, version pin | P2 (patches/version routes, inspect), P9 (App detail tabs) |
| 4d Multiple phones, per-phone apps, dependency apps | P5 (`device_assignments`, D8), P2 (`dependency_app`, `item_dependency`, `item_dependencies`), P9 (item dependency dialog), P10 (assignment editor) |
| Deployment: macOS bridge + Linux host from one compose | P3 (compose, `.env.example`), P5 (mDNS empty result on Mac, manual form) |
| Single admin password login, LAN | P1 (auth), P6 (Login page) |
| Decision Q1 keep GitHub builds | P7 (+ P11 deletes old workflows) |
| Decision Q2 Xposed items | P2 (seed), P8 (engine + validation), P9 (Module tab) |
| Decisions Q3, Q5 seed rules | P2 |
| Decision Q4 auto rebuild | P2 (pull `rebuild`), P5 (after-build OTA) |
| Decision Q6 plain HTTP | P1 |

Result: every spec Part 1–7 and every requirement 1–4d maps to ≥ 1 package. Same-round file ownership has no overlap (tables above; `catalog.py`, `utils.sh`, `build-item.sh`, `test-build-item.sh`, `app.css`, `app.js` change owner between rounds, never within one).

## Open items for the detail planners (derived, not user decisions)

- Spec L225 (`core §3` code layout) assigns `.gitignore`, compose and Dockerfile to build; this plan gives them to P3 (runner), the build part's bash/container half. P4 (build service) owns only Python.
- Spec L230 lists "README download links"; ci §9 and the contract parking lot keep the 142-link rewrite as a one-time user step. P11 edits `README.md` only for the CI badge and the local-build instructions (planner-ops Task 11.1), not the download links.
- Spec L234 suggests `build.py` in round 2; this plan moves it to round 1 (Decisions above).
- Names fixed here (Plan-fixed names table) must be confirmed by the architect review.
- `catalog.resolve_items({"all":true})` order: P5 needs catalog item order for install order (spec L910); P2 returns ids in group position then item position order — detail planner writes this into P2's Produces block.

## Uncertainties (from spec §0.7 L154–164, settled by tests during implementation)

- mDNS legacy-unicast against a real Android responder (T4, user-run).
- Morphe CLI `options-create` / `list-patches` output layout, `--with-options` preset values: P2 captures real output once as a fixture (needs one network download of a CLI + bundle; user-run if it exceeds 150 s).
- APKEditor 1.4.9 `info` flags (P3, checked in the new image).
- Build duration vs the 60 min timeout; GitHub job limit for large sources (P4, P7 user-run).
- LSPatch v1.2 flags and the NexAlloy v1.1 pin (P8 user-run).
- APKMirror list-page title layout for `::apkm_versions::` (P3, from captured pages).
- GitHub token `expires_at`/`scopes` headers may be absent → null (P1).
